# Implementation Register — Workflow Engine Decoupling & Universal Workflow Runtime

> **Purpose.** One stitched, code-free knowledge layer over the entire `.planning/` history. **Read this before implementing any bug-fix or feature** so you do not (a) re-write logic that already exists as a registered capability, or (b) contradict a locked decision or resurrect deliberately-deleted code. Every claim points to the exact file (and heading) where the real detail lives — this register is the index, the phase folders are the source of truth.
>
> **How it was built.** The four root planning docs (`PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`) + `config.json` were read in full to produce the Overview; then an agent read **every file** in each `.planning/phases/<NN-…>/` folder in full and wrote one section in a fixed 8-part format. The per-section sources also live separately under `.planning/_register-parts/` (one file per phase) if you prefer to open just one.
>
> **Authoritative full specification:** `specs/003-workflow-engine-decoupling/plan.md`. **Running issues log:** `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` (live-pass + deep-investigation + UI-campaign findings, with statuses).

## How To Read This Register

1. **Start with the Overview** (next section): what the project is, SC-001, the architecture/invariants that bind every phase, the leak map, the phase table, requirement families, locked + open decisions, and the glossary.
2. **Jump to a phase** using the table below. Each phase section has the same 8 parts:
   `1` Goal & success criteria (PLANNED) · `2` per-plan implementation table (BUILT) · `3` capabilities/modules/schema/API added · `4` what was deleted/superseded (don't resurrect) · `5` locked decisions & constraints (don't contradict) · `6` verification & evidence (what HAPPENED) · `7` gotchas, live survivors & carry-forward · `8` full file index for the folder.
3. **Before changing the engine kernel**, re-read Overview §4 (hard constraints) + the relevant phase's §4 (deletions) and §5 (locked decisions). The kernel is name-free by design (INV-1) and protected by banned-pattern + import-linter CI gates.

## Project Overview & How To Use This Register

> **What this document is.** A single navigation + knowledge layer over `.planning/`. Read this BEFORE implementing a bug-fix or feature so you do not (a) write duplicate code that already exists as a registered capability, or (b) contradict a locked plan/phase decision or resurrect deliberately-deleted code. Every claim here points to where the real detail lives.
>
> **Source files this overview summarizes (all read in full):** `.planning/PROJECT.md`, `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md`, `.planning/ROADMAP.md`, `.planning/STATE.md`, `.planning/config.json`.
> **Authoritative full specification:** `specs/003-workflow-engine-decoupling/plan.md` — nothing in it may be dropped; every requirement traces back to it.
> **Per-phase detail:** the 22 sections that follow (Phase 1…22), each summarizing one `.planning/phases/<NN-…>/` folder and pointing to the exact file + heading for any detail.

### 1. What This Project Is

**Workflow Engine Decoupling & Universal Workflow Runtime** — a brownfield, strangler/incremental refactor (Q39) of Flowin's `agents/execution_engine/engine.py`. It turns the prototype-/PPT-/revision-coupled `ExecutionEngine` into a small, **workflow-agnostic runtime kernel** driven by **declarative workflow manifests** that compile (thin, no-DSL) to a typed `CompiledWorkflow`/`ExecutionPlan`. Every capability once hardwired for `prototype` (per-task sub-agent loop, validation + fix-loop, context injection, deliverable resolution, clarify defaults) becomes a **declared, registered capability** any workflow can opt into. The `Workspace`/`RuntimeEnvironment` abstraction is designed now (local impl only) so ECS/containers plug in later as a backend swap, not an engine rewrite.

Audience: engineers building/operating Flowin's agent workflows, and (via the dynamic composer) users composing custom workflows from an allow-listed capability palette. Builds on spec `002-deepagents-migration` (the `deepagents` runtime this restructures). Branch: `feature/003-workflow-engine-decoupling`. Repo is GitLab `hexaware-uki/flowin`.

### 2. Core Value — SC-001 (the one test that must always hold)

**A brand-new custom workflow can replicate `prototype` by manifest + AGENT.md only — with ZERO engine edits.** If everything else fails, this must hold: the kernel knows no workflow by name; all power lives in registered, declared capabilities.
**Status: PROVEN** (Phase 7 gap-closure `test_sc001_nonprototype_task_loop` — a non-prototype `task_loop` workflow produces `app.py` from manifest+AGENT.md with zero engine edits). Re-proven for fan-out (Phase 11 `sample_fanout`) and waves (Phase 12 `sample_wave`). → detail in the Phase 7 / 11 / 12 sections.

### 4. Architecture & Hard Constraints (apply to EVERY phase — do not violate)

- **Tech stack:** Python · FastAPI · PostgreSQL · LangGraph checkpointer — *extend, don't replace*.
- **INV-13 (runtime mandate):** every agent runs on LangChain `deepagents` — canonical `from deepagents import create_deep_agent` (PyPI `deepagents==0.6.7`); adapter id `langchain_deepagents`. No hand-rolled deep agent, no local `deepagents`/`langchain_deepagents` module, no re-implemented agent loop. **`create_deep_agent` is called ONLY inside the allow-listed `deep_agent_runner.py`.** Enforced by a banned-pattern CI gate (R15).
- **Ports & Adapters (hexagonal):** the kernel depends only on capability **ports**; concrete impls self-register into the `CapabilityRegistry` via `@register(kind,name)`. Adding a capability = add a module + register — **no kernel edit**. Enforced by import-linter (§31): 4 forbidden contracts (`agents.capabilities ↛ agents.execution_engine`, `agents.runtime ↛ [agents.execution_engine, app]`, kernel ↛ `app`, etc.); steady state is **lint-imports 4/0**.
- **Compiler is thin, no DSL (INV-5):** manifests are data; control flow lives inside strategies.
- **Persistence — additive migrations only (Q3):** every new table carries `owner_id` + `workspace_id`. Alembic chain: 0014 (artifacts/workspaces/run_events/run_capabilities) → 0015 (drop legacy `WorkflowArtifact`) → 0016 (validation_results/gate_events/hook_runs) → 0017 (repositories) → 0018 (exec_runs) → 0019 (subagent_runs) → 0020 (wave_runs) → **0021** (Phase 21 — user-authored saved workflows: +3 nullable cols `base_pipeline_type`/`model_overrides`/`description` on the **reused** `workflows` table, NOT a new table; the first migration since the planned milestone) → **0022** (Phase 22 — UXFIX-02: additive `deliverable_mimetype`/`deliverable_filename` on `WorkflowRun` for faithful history-reopen of binary deliverables).
- **Security defaults OFF:** `exec`/`network`/`secrets`/`spawn_subagents` default OFF. Code-exec lives behind the `security`+`approval` gates (N3 resolved Phase 10 — `10-SPEC.md` is the decision record); `network`/`secrets` remain gated-off; untrusted end-user exec stays out of scope.
- **INV-3 / INV-12 — no dual implementations:** a phase that adds an abstraction without deleting the code it supersedes is **not done** (move → rewire call-sites → delete; deletion is an exit gate, ratcheted by banned-pattern greps). The only sanctioned temporary duplication was the `accumulated_outputs` mirror (removed Phase 5).
- **Backward-compat (INV-3):** existing `prototype`/`od_*`/PPT/code-gen behavior stays deterministic-byte-identical + semantic-event-parity, proven by characterization tests (Phase 1). The single sanctioned non-byte-identical change is the Phase 3 token-trim (measured ≥50% reduction, semantic parity held).

**Global invariants (success constraints on every phase):** INV-1 kernel knows no workflow by name (zero `if pipeline_type ==` / `spec.id ==` branches) · INV-2 no per-run state on the singleton (per-run `ExecutionContext`; immutable kernel) · INV-3 semantic event parity, deliverables byte-identical where deterministic · INV-12 move-don't-copy · INV-13 LangChain `deepagents` only.

### 5. The Leak Map (what the refactor had to dissolve)

From plan §4: **16 verified couplings L1–L16** in `engine.py` (literal name frozensets, `_resolve_final_output`, carousel sanitize, revision seeding, skip-planner, clarify defaults, build-loop dispatch, HTML readback, build-loop internals, context injection, dead skeleton, singleton per-run state, untyped `accumulated_outputs`, unchecked parent seeding) and **5 factory leaks F1–F5** (inline prompt order, closed tool switch, inline skills/hooks, constitution no-op R12, hardcoded `create_deep_agent`). **D1** (dead `_handle_revision`) was **VOIDED** — it is LIVE (the `run_revision` PPT-revision handler at `app/api/websocket.py:625`). By milestone end L1–L16 + F1–F5 are resolved/deleted or kept as documented **live survivors** (CHECK ledger rows). The operational mirror with status + deleting commit SHA is `specs/003-…/migration-ledger.md`, asserted by `tests/test_migration_ledger.py`.

### 8. Key Locked Decisions (do NOT re-open without a decision record)

- Unify vocabulary on `workflow`; `pipeline_type` kept only as a temporary id-alias (Q1).
- Engineer-registered capabilities + user-composable manifests; per-capability `user_allowed` trust boundary + owner allow-list (Q2 / CAP-03).
- File-backed manifests now; DB-backed user workflows later (Q5) — **now realized**: Phase 21 added DB-backed user-authored saved workflows on the reused `workflows` table (`source="user"`, migration 0021); file-backed built-in manifests remain the source for engineer-authored workflows.
- `ExecutionStrategy` registry: `single_shot` / `task_loop` / `fanout_batch` / `wave_scheduler` (Q8).
- Canonical `Task` schema + parser adapters; `heading_tasks` + `json_tasks` parsers (Q11).
- Engine owns fan-out via the `spawn_subagents` request-emitter tool (Q13 / INV-7); the library `task` tool stays excluded; `spawn_subagents` is `user_allowed=False`.
- Deterministic topo wave-builder first; CP-SAT seam left (Q31/Q32).
- Per-sub-agent subdirs first; git worktrees for brownfield (Q34).
- Internal P0–P3 severities → external CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW via one `map_severity` (Q24).
- User per-agent model selection at the top of the resolution order (A12); global default stays Haiku.
- **Resolved decision records:** N2 isolation MVP (Phase 9 `LocalSandboxRuntime`) · N3 exec threat-model (Phase 10 — `10-SPEC.md`) · N5/N7 repo deliverable shape (Phase 9 diff-only `repo_diff`) · N6/N10 RepoIndex (Phase 9 grep-default + opt-in tree-sitter).
- **Still-OPEN decision records (confirm before any related future work):** **N4** git hosting (GitHub vs GitLab; repo is GitLab) · **N8** long-job substrate. *(**N9** artifact retention + **N11** model/premium policy — formerly open — were **resolved in Phase 22**: N9 → **keep-by-default** (run_ttl/`days:N` opt-in overrides), N11 → **premium open to all tiers** (global default Haiku + fallback chain; picker tier-filter dropped). ⚠ N9 follow-up: the code/config default still reads `run_ttl` in ~4 sites — see Phase 22 §7.)* → `STATE.md` (Blockers/Concerns), `PROJECT.md` (Key Decisions).

### 9. Glossary of Recurring Tags

- **L#** = leak in `engine.py` (§5 leak map). **F#** = factory leak. **D#** = plan decision/dead-code item; **D-0x** = a phase-local design decision. **Q#** = locked decision. **N#** = open decision record. **A#** = answered/locked decision. **R#** = risk. **INV-#** = global invariant. **§#** = section of `plan.md`. **Tier#** = validator tier.
- **CR-xx / WR-xx / IN-xx** = code-review Critical / Warning / Info findings (per-phase `NN-REVIEW*.md`).
- **_KNOWN N→M** = the capability-registry name counter, growing monotonically as capabilities land (final ≈ 61 declared capability names by Phase 12).
- **migration 00NN** = an Alembic revision. **SC-001** = the core-value zero-engine-edit proof.

### 10. Per-Phase File Convention (where detail lives in every phase folder)

Each `.planning/phases/<NN-…>/` folder contains:
- `NN-NN-PLAN.md` + `NN-NN-SUMMARY.md` — one pair per plan (planned vs built).
- `NN-CONTEXT.md` — phase entry context. `NN-DISCUSSION-LOG.md` — Q&A/decisions. `NN-PATTERNS.md` — reusable patterns mapped to analogs.
- `NN-SPEC.md` (some) — scoped contract. `NN-RESEARCH.md` (some) — pre-plan research.
- `NN-REVIEW.md` / `NN-REVIEW-FIX.md` (+ `.iter2/.iter3`) — code-review findings + fixes.
- `NN-SECURITY.md` (some) — threat model + mitigations. `NN-VALIDATION.md` (some) — Nyquist/test coverage.
- `NN-VERIFICATION.md` — goal-backward verification verdict. `NN-UAT.md` (some) — user-acceptance evidence. `deferred-items.md` (some) — out-of-scope carry-forward.

The 22 sections that follow summarize each folder and point to the exact file + heading for any detail.

---

## Phase 01 — Safety Net + Deletion Guard [0A]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/01-safety-net-deletion-guard-0a/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-06)  ·  **Plans:** 4/4  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 0A
**Requirements delivered:** SAFE-01, SAFE-02, SAFE-03, SAFE-04, SAFE-05, SAFE-06, SAFE-07, DEL-01, DEL-02, DEL-03, DEL-04
**One-line outcome:** Locked the current byte-and-event behavior of all five named pipeline families with offline characterization golden snapshots, and armed (and finally CI-executed) the migration-ledger ratchet, banned-pattern gate, import-linter contract, and dead-code scan — all tests/CI-config only, ZERO runtime change.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Phase 0A is the safety net that must exist BEFORE any engine refactor: record current behavior as characterization snapshots and stand up the CI enforcement gates the rest of the migration ratchets against — adding NO runtime behavior change (tests + CI scaffolding only). → 01-CONTEXT.md (Phase Boundary). Depends-on: none (this is the first execution phase; it only reads `engine.py`/`factory.py`, never edits them).
Success criteria (→ 01-VERIFICATION.md, Observable Truths): (1) deliverable byte-snapshots + semantic event snapshots recorded and green for `prototype`, `od_prototype`, `prototype_revision`, `ppt`/`od_ppt`, and one code-gen pipeline (`app_builder`); (2) the migration-ledger CI guard, import-linter contract, and hand-rolled-deep-agent banned-pattern gate all run in CI, starting green/empty and tightening as items delete; (3) per-run event `seq` asserted contiguous with no runtime behavior change. All three verified 3/3.

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
Phase 0A registered NO runtime capabilities, ports, migrations, endpoints, or event types — it is the pre-refactor safety net. What it added is test/CI infrastructure (names only; pointers below):
- **Characterization harness extensions** (in existing test code): `_scripts_for` branches for `od-ppt-brief-analyst`/`od-ppt-composer`/`od-ppt-validator`; an `_OD_ALIAS_FOR_LOOKUP` map (`od_prototype→prototype`, `ppt→od_ppt`) used for agent lookup ONLY in `_drive`, mirroring the production WS handler; extended `od_context` seeding to cover `od_ppt`/`ppt`. → 01-01-SUMMARY.md (What Was Built).
- **Snapshot helpers (new test modules):** `characterization/__init__.py` and `characterization/_normalize.py` with `VOLATILE_SENTINEL = "<normalized>"`, `_canonical_order` (order-canonical multiset), `assert_seq_contiguous`. `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES` / `_REQUIRED_DATA_KEYS` are IMPORTED from `test_phase3_cutover_verify` (single source of truth, never copied). → 01-02-SUMMARY.md (`_normalize()` field list).
- **CI ratchet tests:** `test_migration_ledger.py`, `test_banned_patterns.py` (both establish a NEW grep-the-tree ratchet pattern with no prior in-repo analog). → 01-PATTERNS.md (No Analog Found).
- **Single-source-of-truth doc:** `specs/003-workflow-engine-decoupling/migration-ledger.md` (operational mirror of plan §31; 20 rows; `Status` + `Deleting SHA` columns).
- **Tooling pins added** (tech-stack delta): `import-linter==2.11`, `vulture==2.16`. → 01-04-SUMMARY.md (Grounding verification). Registry `_KNOWN` name-count counter: NOT touched in this phase (no capabilities registered).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
Phase 0A is **pure-additive** — it DELETES nothing and edits no runtime module. `engine.py` and `factory.py` were read-only the entire phase (verified `git diff 04e9105..HEAD` on both → empty). → 01-VERIFICATION.md (Behavioral Spot-Checks, "No runtime code change"). Instead it **ARMS** the deletion machinery that later phases use:
- The migration-ledger ratchet: all 20 rows start `☐` (nothing deleted yet, D-10) → the guard is green/empty now and becomes a permanent ban the instant a row flips to `☑`. Owning-phase flips are pre-assigned: L14/L16/D1 (Phase 0B); L13 (0C→2); L1, L2/L9, L3, L4/L8, L5, L6, L7, L10, L11, L12 (Phase 2); L15 (1A→1B); F1–F5 (Phase 3). → 01-03-SUMMARY.md (Next Phase Readiness) and migration-ledger.md.
- The banned-pattern gate (INV-13/R15): hard-bans `class DeepAgent`, `def deep_agent`, any NEW local `deepagents`/`langchain_deepagents` module under our source, and bespoke `for _ in range(max_iterations)` agent loops — allow-listing the SINGLE sanctioned `from deepagents import create_deep_agent` site in `app/agents/deep_agent_runner.py`. → 01-04-SUMMARY.md (Grounding verification).
- The import-linter contract (scaffold): a `forbidden` contract that `agents.execution_engine.engine` must not import `app.api` (the real, already-respected dependency-inversion boundary), `root_packages = ["agents", "app"]`. → 01-04-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made).
- **No symbol was deleted** because Phase 0A precedes all extraction; do not interpret any ledger row as "already removed" — all rows are still `☐`.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01/D-02 — reuse, don't rebuild the harness:** all characterization drives go through the existing `_scripted_model.py::_drive()`; extend `_scripts_for`, never fork `_drive`. → 01-CONTEXT.md (Characterization harness).
- **D-04/D-06 — hand-rolled golden files (no syrupy); `VOLATILE_SENTINEL = "<normalized>"`** is the single-sourced replacement value for volatile-but-required keys (duration, `*_tokens`); strip non-required volatile keys; keep `type` + `_REQUIRED_DATA_KEYS`. → 01-02-SUMMARY.md (`_normalize()` field list).
- **D-08/D-09/D-10 — ledger is the operational SoT, mirrors §31 verbatim; ☑-rows enforce grep→0; ALL rows ☐ in Phase 1.** CHECK-rows (L16, F4, F5) carry a literal `CHECK:`/`test:` marker → classified as `(item, None)` → skipped (their enforcement lives in dedicated tests in their owning phase, not the grep ratchet). → 01-03-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made).
- **D-12 — import-linter contract is a green scaffold designed to TIGHTEN not rewrite.** Intended Phase 7/8 final form (kernel must not import `agents.factory`/`engine` internals once `kernel.py` + `capabilities/base.py` exist) is documented inline; Phase 8 only adds, never rewrites. Note: any `agents.*` forbidden target is BROKEN today because everything is transitively reachable from `engine.py` via factory — hence the `app.api` boundary was chosen. → 01-04-SUMMARY.md (import-linter contract anchor).
- **D-15 — INV-1 reservation (`if pipeline_type ==` / `spec.id ==`) is WARN-ONLY in Phase 1** (10 matches, recorded ceiling 16); it still legitimately lives in `engine.py`. Hard-fail (→0) is deferred to Phase 7 when L7 is deleted. Do NOT make it hard-fail before then. → 01-CONTEXT.md (Banned-pattern gate) / 01-04-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).
- **D-16 (critical finding, now closed) — `tests/agents` was NOT run in CI before this phase** (`backend:test` ran only `tests/unit`); the new offline `backend:characterization` job is what actually executes the safety net. Do not assume agent tests run via the old job. → 01-CONTEXT.md (CI wiring).
- **Invariant relied on:** INV-3 (byte + semantic-event parity) is the whole point of the snapshots; Phase 0C is the ONE sanctioned place deliverable bytes may legitimately change, so the event snapshot is text-drift-robust by design (multiset + sentinel). INV-13 enforced by the banned-pattern gate; INV-12 made machine-enforceable by the ledger ratchet.

Full gate suite → 21 passed, 1 skipped. `engine.py`/`factory.py` diff empty. → 01-VERIFICATION.md (Behavioral Spot-Checks).
- **Code-review findings:** no separate NN-REVIEW*.md in this phase; review was folded into per-plan SUMMARY "Deviations" — 3 auto-fixed bugs total (see §7). No CR/WR/IN register exists.
- **Security:** no NN-SECURITY.md file; each plan carried an inline STRIDE register, all threats `mitigate`/`accept` and closed (false-green/vacuous-gate threats T-01..T-04, all proven non-vacuous by injected-fixture/known-present-token guards). No network/data/auth surface added.
- **Key commit SHAs:** 01-01: `839ad8b` (Task1), `ed8cb18` (Task2). 01-02: `1fceb13` (helper), `eeead30` (tests+goldens). 01-03: `982f20b` (ledger), `66e9b9d` (test). 01-04: `35f8107` (pins/config), `cd28d6a` (banned-patterns), `2accdb5` (CI). Baseline before phase: `04e9105`.

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **Live survivor — INV-1 reservation in `engine.py`:** `if pipeline_type ==` / `spec.id ==` (10 matches) is LIVE and warn-only here; it is NOT a leak to remove in 0A. It dies with L7 in Phase 7. → 01-04-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).
- **Order-canonical multiset deviation (accepted):** the event snapshot is sorted by canonical JSON (`_canonical_order`), NOT strict positional order, because the prototype/app build loop interleaves `tool_result`/`task_progress`/retry `tool_call` nondeterministically across runs of the UNCHANGED engine — a raw positional snapshot was flaky. Strict emission order stays covered by `assert_seq_contiguous` + the existing positional sequence tests in `test_phase3_cutover_verify.py`. A future agent must keep the multiset framing, not "fix" it back to positional. → 01-02-SUMMARY.md (Deviations) / 01-VERIFICATION.md (Gaps Summary, Accepted deviation).
- **`assert_seq_contiguous` is vacuous today** — the engine stamps no `seq`; the function is wired in all 5 modules and proven to raise on a synthetic gap, so it activates automatically if a `seq` is ever added. INFO-severity, not debt. → 01-VERIFICATION.md (Anti-Patterns Found).
- **od_ alias gotcha:** `od_prototype` and `ppt` have NO AGENT.md of their own (aliases of `prototype` / `od_ppt`); `get_pipeline_agents` returns ∅ for them. The harness resolves the alias for agent LOOKUP only and forwards the original label to `execute()` — mirror this if extending characterization, do not give aliases their own agents. → 01-01-SUMMARY.md (Auto-fixed Issues #1).
- **Pre-existing non-fatal `sqlite3.IntegrityError (FOREIGN KEY) … INSERT INTO workflows`** logs during `_drive` (offline harness lacks the parent users row). It does NOT interrupt the run or affect snapshots — expected noise, not a bug to chase. → 01-01-SUMMARY.md (Notes) / 01-02-SUMMARY.md (Notes).
- **CHECK-row classification is by explicit `CHECK:`/`test:` marker, NOT a metacharacter heuristic** — a single-token grep pattern (e.g. `SKIP_PLANNER_FOR_PROTOTYPE`) was being misclassified as prose and silently dropped. When adding ledger rows, mark prose gates explicitly or the ratchet may skip them. → 01-03-SUMMARY.md (Deviations).
- **Deferred to later phases (not 0A scope):** tightening the import-linter contract to its final kernel form (Phase 7/8); flipping ledger rows to ☑ + enabling their grep enforcement (each owning phase); INV-1 hard-fail (Phase 7); pre-commit mirrors of the CI gates (opportunistic). → 01-CONTEXT.md (Deferred Ideas).

### 8. File Index (every file in this folder)
- `01-CONTEXT.md` — phase boundary, all D-01..D-17 implementation decisions, canonical refs, deferred items. Open first to understand WHY each gate is shaped the way it is.
- `01-DISCUSSION-LOG.md` — audit trail of the `--auto` option selections (alternatives considered for harness/snapshot/ledger/gates/CI). Open only to see rejected alternatives; not planning input.
- `01-PATTERNS.md` — pattern map: every new file's in-repo analog, the S1–S5 shared patterns (the `_drive` capture mechanism, `_scripts_for` extension, event-contract dicts, sandbox reads, offline=unmarked), and concrete authoring templates for each gate. Open before writing any new characterization test or CI gate.
- `01-01-PLAN.md` — planned: byte-snapshot harness + 5 modules + PPT scripting. Open for the must-haves/threat-model of SAFE-01.
- `01-01-SUMMARY.md` — built: deliverable-extraction mechanism per pipeline (all via `pipeline_complete.final_output`), golden byte sizes, od_-alias fix. Open to see how each pipeline's deliverable is captured.
- `01-02-PLAN.md` — planned: `_normalize()` + contiguous-seq + event snapshots (SAFE-02/03). Open for the volatile-field contract.
- `01-02-SUMMARY.md` — built: full `_normalize()` keep/strip/sentinel table, the order-canonical multiset deviation, agent_chunk handling. Open before changing normalization or the event golden.
- `01-03-PLAN.md` — planned: migration ledger + ratchet test (DEL-01..04, SAFE-04). Open for the parser/CHECK-row spec.
- `01-03-SUMMARY.md` — built: ledger SoT, ratchet classification convention, non-vacuity token (`_PROTOTYPE_PIPELINE_TYPES`), repo-root depth `parents[3]`. Open before editing the ledger or adding a ☑ row.
- `01-04-PLAN.md` — planned: static gates + CI wiring (SAFE-05/06/07). Open for the import-linter scaffold + banned-pattern spec.
- `01-04-SUMMARY.md` — built: `import-linter==2.11`/`vulture==2.16`, the `app.api` contract anchor + intended final form, vulture allow-list mechanism, final CI job shapes. Open before touching `pyproject.toml`/`.gitlab-ci.yml` gates.
- `01-VERIFICATION.md` — the PASSED 3/3 verdict: truth-by-truth evidence, artifact table (all golden sizes), key-link checks, behavioral spot-checks, SAFE/DEL requirements coverage, accepted deviation. Open to confirm what was proven and the exact commands/evidence.

## Phase 02 — ExecutionContext + Ownership [0B]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/02-executioncontext-ownership-0b/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-07)  ·  **Plans:** 3/3  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 0B
**Requirements delivered:** CTX-01, CTX-02, CTX-03, CTX-05  ·  **Deferred:** CTX-04 (voided — `_handle_revision` is live)
**One-line outcome:** Lifted every per-run `self._*` datum off the `ExecutionEngine` singleton onto a new threaded per-run `ExecutionContext` (kernel now stateless after `__init__`) and added the explicit cross-owner `parent_run` ownership gate — with zero behavior change (0A snapshots byte-identical, L14 grep → 0).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Pure state-relocation + one security point-fix + one (intended) dead-code deletion — **no manifests, no typed artifacts, no new capabilities, no engine file-split** (→ 02-CONTEXT.md "Phase Boundary"; 02-SPEC.md "Goal"). Depends-on Phase 1 [0A] which provides the golden-snapshot characterization suite (`_scripted_model._drive`), the migration-ledger CI ratchet, and the import-linter kernel→ports scaffold (→ 02-01-SUMMARY.md frontmatter `requires`).

Five locked requirements (ambiguity score 0.09; → 02-SPEC.md "Requirements"):
- **CTX-01 / L14 / INV-2** — all enumerated `self._*` per-run state moves onto a per-run `ExecutionContext`; acceptance = L14 grep returns 0.
- **CTX-02 / NFR-001 / INV-2** — kernel singleton holds NO per-run attributes after construction (immutable kernel).
- **CTX-03 / L16 / INV-8** — explicit ownership check on `parent_run` seeding rejects cross-owner access; cross-owner denial test passes.
- **CTX-04 / D1** — delete dead `_handle_revision`. **VOIDED during execution** (→ 02-SPEC.md "Requirements §4").
- **CTX-05 / INV-3** — zero behavior change; deliverables byte-identical, events at semantic parity, `seq` contiguous (the hard gate on the whole phase).

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)

| Plan | Title (short) | What it built (names only) | Key modules/files touched | Status | Detail |
|------|---------------|-----------------------------|----------------------------|--------|--------|
| 02-01 | ExecutionContext state lift | `ExecutionContext` dataclass; `ectx` param threaded through 6 read-site methods; all per-run `self._*` writes deleted | `context.py` (new), `engine.py`, `test_execution_context.py` (new) + 4 white-box test files | Complete | → 02-01-PLAN.md (planned) / 02-01-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 02-02 | Delete `_handle_revision` (D1) + flip ledger | **RE-SCOPED**: delete VOIDED (method is live); flipped ONLY L14 → ☑; fixed `_parse_rows` escaped-pipe bug; corrected D1 ledger row | `migration-ledger.md`, `test_migration_ledger.py` (NO engine.py change) | Complete (re-scoped) | → 02-02-PLAN.md (planned) / 02-02-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 02-03 | Parent-run ownership check (L16 / CTX-03) | `authz.py` + pure `assert_owns`; `_derive_parent_owner`; ownership gate wired above seed try; 9 ownership tests; L16 → ☑ | `authz.py` (new), `engine.py`, `test_parent_run_ownership.py` (new), `migration-ledger.md` | Complete | → 02-03-PLAN.md (planned) / 02-03-SUMMARY.md (built) |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
No registered capabilities, no Alembic migrations, no API endpoints, no event types (pure infra/refactor phase — → 02-SPEC.md "Constraints": "no migrations required this phase"). New code symbols:
- **`ExecutionContext`** — mutable `@dataclass` in new `backend/agents/execution_engine/context.py` (the engine-level per-run state container; DISTINCT from factory's per-agent `AgentContext` — do not conflate). D-01 minimal field set: `run_id`, `owner_id` (required); `od_context`, `completed_tasks`, `gate_agent_ids`, `parent_run_id`, `checkpointer`, `current_task_block`, the FLAT `revision_*` group (`revision_original_html`/`revision_instruction`/`revision_baseline_static`/`revision_baseline_console`), `accumulated_outputs`, `cancel_event`, `disk_skills`, `depth`. NO speculative later-phase fields (`plan`/`workspace`/`artifacts`/`budget`/`models`/`workspace_id`) — INV-12 (→ 02-01-SUMMARY.md "Final ExecutionContext field layout"). Stdlib-only imports (import-direction clean).
- **`assert_owns(owner_id, parent_run_id, parent_owner_id) -> None`** — pure free function in new `backend/agents/execution_engine/authz.py`; raises builtin `PermissionError` iff `parent_owner_id != owner_id`; no I/O (Phase-5-relocatable). The D-06 seam Phase 5 AUTHZ-02 absorbs as a MOVE (→ 02-03-SUMMARY.md "Task 1").
- **`ExecutionEngine._derive_parent_owner(user_id, parent_run_id)`** — by-convention 0B parent-owner derivation, returns `user_id or "anon"`; Phase 5 replaces with a real store lookup (→ 02-03-SUMMARY.md "By-convention derivation note").
- **`ectx` parameter** added (positional, last) to `_run_agent`, `_run_build_task_loop`, `_should_gate`, `_build_context_message`, `_write_build_reference_files`; `_run_validation_fix_loop` got a keyword-only `checkpointer=` arg (NOT a full `ectx` — named `ectx` everywhere to avoid shadowing its existing `ctx: AgentContext` param) (→ 02-01-SUMMARY.md "New ectx parameter added").
- Registry counter `_KNOWN`: not applicable (no capability registration this phase).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
- **L14 dissolved** — every per-run `self._*` write DELETED from the `ExecutionEngine` singleton; only `_resolver`/`_store`/`_state_machine` (the 3 `__init__` construction-time singletons) survive. Relocated: `_od_context`, `_user_id`, `_gate_agent_ids`, `_parent_run_id`, `_checkpointer`, `_completed_tasks`, `_current_task_block`, the four `_revision_*`, `_disk_skills` → all now on `ectx` (→ 02-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments").
- **Ledger row L14 flipped ☐→☑** (deleting SHA `8b90fd2`) — its grep ratchet `self\._(od_context|completed_tasks|current_task_block|revision_|gate_agent_ids)` is now ARMED and enforced by CI; reappearance fails the build permanently (→ 02-02-SUMMARY.md "What was delivered").
- **Ledger row L16 flipped ☐→☑** as a CHECK row (NOT a grep ratchet — enforcement lives in `test_parent_run_ownership.py`) (→ 02-03-SUMMARY.md "Ledger / orchestrator coordination").
- **D1 / `_handle_revision` NOT deleted** — the planned deletion was VOIDED (see §5/§7). D1 row stays ☐ (deferred, with a `‡` finding note in the ledger).
- **No Alembic migration, no import-linter contract added** this phase (state move is in-memory; `context.py`/`authz.py` keep the existing Phase-1 kernel→ports scaffold green by importing only stdlib).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01** minimal field set; do NOT add `plan`/`workspace`/`artifacts`/`budget`/`models`/`workspace_id` to `ExecutionContext` (added in owning phases 4/9/5/11/6; `workspace_id` → Phase 5) (→ 02-CONTEXT.md "ExecutionContext scope"; 02-01-SUMMARY.md).
- **D-02** `current_task_block` is a **DELIBERATE Phase-7-TEMPORARY home** on `ExecutionContext` — the only known-temporary field; **Phase 7 [2] must reclaim it into `TaskLoopStrategy`** (→ 02-01-SUMMARY.md "D-02 flag for Phase 7").
- **D-03** explicit `ectx` parameter threading — NO `contextvars`/ambient state (fan-out phases 5/6 would have to undo it) (→ 02-CONTEXT.md "Threading mechanism").
- **D-04** `owner_id = user_id or "anon"`; `"anon"` is a REAL owner principal, never None — it cannot bypass the ownership check. The formal `anon:<session_id>` synthetic owner is deferred to Phase 5 / AUTHZ-03 (→ 02-CONTEXT.md "Owner identity").
- **D-05** `RunSandbox(user_id or "anon", parent_run_id)` disk keying is BYTE-IDENTICAL — do NOT swap in `ectx.owner_id` at the keying call (keeps 0A paths identical) (→ 02-CONTEXT.md "Owner identity"; verified grep still matches).
- **D-06** `assert_owns` kept small/pure in `authz.py` so Phase 5 AUTHZ-02 relocates it as a MOVE, not a rewrite (→ 02-03-SUMMARY.md "key-decisions").
- **D-07** denial = builtin `PermissionError`, raised LEXICALLY ABOVE the graceful-degrade `try:` so it is NOT swallowed by the broad `except Exception as _seed_exc`; do NOT widen that except to catch `PermissionError`. Same-owner missing/TTL-swept parents still degrade gracefully (→ 02-03-SUMMARY.md "Wiring point (D-07)").
- **CTX-05 / INV-3** is the binding gate: any future engine edit here must keep 0A snapshots byte-identical + semantic-event parity.

### 6. Status, Verification & Evidence (what HAPPENED)
- **Verdict: PASSED — 4/4 must-have truths verified** (→ 02-VERIFICATION.md "Goal Achievement"). Evidence: `ExecutionContext(` at engine.py:485; `assert_owns(` at engine.py:598 (inside `if parent_run_id:` line 587, above `try:` line 603); only 3 `self._` assignments in engine.py (all `__init__`); L14 grep → 0; full 0A suite **278 passed, 18 skipped**; ledger guard **4 passed, 1 skipped** (L16 CHECK-row skip is correct); 9/9 ownership tests pass (5 unit + 4 end-to-end).
- **Code review: 2 Critical, 3 Warning, 4 Info (9 total), status `issues_found`** (→ 02-REVIEW.md). The two Criticals are SECURITY-relevant and **as-designed for 0B, not fixed**:
  - **CR-01** — ownership check is reached only when `existing_html` is truthy (nested inside `if pipeline_type == "prototype_revision":` → `if existing_html:`); a `parent_run_id` supplied with no HTML block skips both check and seeding. Impact limited in 0B (seeding is gated by the same condition, so no cross-boundary copy), but the guard does not unconditionally cover the `parent_run_id` parameter. Reviewer recommends hoisting the check to guard the param unconditionally.
  - **CR-02** — `_derive_parent_owner` always returns the caller's own identity, so `assert_owns` is a **no-op in all real 0B production invocations** (only test monkey-patching exercises the deny path). **L16 ☑ implies enforcement that does not exist until Phase 5 AUTHZ-02 lands** — a hard carry-forward constraint. (CR/WR/IN findings appear to be advisory; no `02-REVIEW-FIX.md` exists in the folder.)
- **Security threats**: T-02-01..10 registered across the three plans (info-disclosure on shared singleton, cross-owner seeding, anon-bypass, graceful-degrade re-widening, regression) all dispositioned `mitigate`; package-install threats `accept` (stdlib only) (→ 02-01/02/03-PLAN.md threat_model). No standalone `02-SECURITY.md` in folder.
- **Key commits**: `5a71931` (02-01 Task 1 context.py), `8b90fd2` (02-01 Task 2 state lift; also the L14 deleting SHA), `f1f194c` (02-03 Task 1 authz.py), `d91e4d7` (02-03 Task 2 wire gate + L16).

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **`_handle_revision` is a LIVE SURVIVOR — do NOT delete it.** Despite the spec/D1 calling it "dead," it is the live handler for the frontend `run_revision` PPT-revision message (`DashboardLayout.tsx:385` → `app/api/websocket.py:625` → `engine.py::_handle_revision`), with a dedicated `backend/tests/unit/test_revision_intelligence.py` suite. The inline `prototype_revision` pipeline (which 0A snapshots characterize) is a SEPARATE mechanism — the spec conflated them. Deleting would break PPT revision (CTX-05 violation). D1/CTX-04 deferred pending a product decision to retire `run_revision` (→ 02-02-SUMMARY.md "The defect"; 02-VERIFICATION.md "Deferred Items").
- **L16 gate is structurally wired but provides ZERO real enforcement in 0B production** (CR-02) — `_derive_parent_owner` returns the caller's own id. Real protection requires Phase 5 AUTHZ-02 to land. Do not assume cross-owner seeding is actually blocked yet.
- **`accumulated_outputs` field on `ExecutionContext` is a sanctioned DEAD mirror (L15)** — the live map is a local dict in `execute()` (engine.py:878); `ectx.accumulated_outputs` is never written. Reading it returns an empty dict (WR-03). Removed in Phase 1B.
- **`current_task_block` carry-forward (D-02)** — Phase 7 must reclaim it into `TaskLoopStrategy`.
- **`_parse_rows` escaped-pipe bug fixed in 02-02** — ledger gate cells holding grep alternations must escape pipes as `\|` for the markdown table; the parser un-escapes before `grep -E`. L14 was the first alternation-pattern row to flip, surfacing this latent Phase-1 bug. Later alternation rows (L3/L10/L11) now flip correctly (→ 02-02-SUMMARY.md "What was delivered" #3).
- **Stray gitignored `backend/backend/.local/`** (192 MB python3.13 pip-target) vendored alembic whose `self._revision_map` false-matched the L14 `revision_` alternation; removed (user-approved), never affected CI (→ 02-02-SUMMARY.md). The L14 ratchet greps with `--include=*.py` over git-tracked source.
- **Pre-existing unrelated test failures NOT caused by this phase** (do not chase): `tests/unit/test_logout.py` x7 (JWT/DB auth) and `tests/unit/test_pipeline_cancel.py::test_cancel_marks_workflow_cancelled_and_sends_ack` (expired AWS Bedrock token; the test stubs the engine so real `execute()` never runs) (→ deferred-items.md).
- **`ExecutionContext` vs `AgentContext`**: engine-level per-RUN vs factory per-AGENT — distinct objects, same dataclass idiom; `_run_validation_fix_loop` carries both (its `ctx: AgentContext` plus relocated state via args).

## Phase 03 — Token-Trim (measured change) [0C]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/03-token-trim-measured-change-0c/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-07)  ·  **Plans:** 2/2  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 0C
**Requirements delivered:** COMPACT-01, COMPACT-02, COMPACT-03 (all three map exclusively to this phase → REQUIREMENTS.md "Context Compaction / Token-Trim (Phase 0C)")
**One-line outcome:** Wired the previously-dead `_extract_html_skeleton` helper into the `prototype-build` task-2+ prompt path so the build sub-agent now receives a ~1–3k-char skeleton state-map instead of the full current HTML (proven ≥50% smaller, semantic parity held) — the one sanctioned non-byte-identical change in the migration.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Wire the dead `_extract_html_skeleton` (ledger item **L13**) as build-task-2+ context compaction: in `_build_context_message`, for `is_build_task_2_plus`, replace the full current-HTML block (up to 120k chars) with the compact skeleton; prove a **≥50% reduction** in the task-2+ prompt input (deterministic offline CI gate) plus a real-run token delta (SUMMARY evidence), while holding **semantic parity** (same pages/routes, equal-or-better validation). This is the **one sanctioned INV-3 exception** — gated on the semantic event snapshot + measured token delta, NOT deliverable byte-identity (→ 03-SPEC.md "Goal" / "Constraints"; §25 lines 806-809; §3 line 252 INV-3). Covers both `prototype` and `od_prototype` (od alias → same `prototype-build` path).
Success criteria (→ 03-SPEC.md "Acceptance Criteria", 8 items): skeleton invoked on task-2+ & full-HTML block absent there; offline ≤50% gate on a ≥2-page HTML; SUMMARY records a real token/cost delta; `prototype`/`od_prototype` semantic event snapshots green with no golden edit & `seq` contiguous; the two deliverable byte-goldens re-recorded while all other pipelines stay byte-identical; pages/routes + equal-or-better-validation assertion; `read_file` access preserved with a read-before-edit instruction; **L13 stays `☐`** (wired, not deleted).
**Depends on:** Phase 02 (ectx threading through `_build_context_message`) and Phase 00 (offline `_scripted_model._drive` harness + characterization snapshot split) (→ 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Dependency graph").

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)
| Plan | Title (short) | What it built (names only) | Key modules/files touched | Status | Detail |
|------|---------------|----------------------------|---------------------------|--------|--------|
| 03-01 | Wire skeleton + ≥50% CI gate | `is_build_task_2_plus` branch in `_build_context_message` calling `self._extract_html_skeleton`; new offline test module `test_phase3_compaction.py` (≥50% gate, marker presence/absence, task-1 control, read_file-access, revert-ratchet, + faithfulness unit test added in review) | `agents/execution_engine/engine.py`; `tests/agents/test_phase3_compaction.py` | Complete | → 03-01-PLAN.md (planned) / 03-01-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 03-02 | Parity proof + golden re-baseline + live evidence | `test_phase3_parity.py` (pages/routes + validation parity for `prototype`/`od_prototype`); `test_phase3_token_delta_live.py` (opt-in dual-gated live token-delta evidence); `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE=1` re-baseline of the two deliverable goldens (confirmed NO-OP diff) | `tests/agents/test_phase3_parity.py`; `tests/agents/test_phase3_token_delta_live.py`; `characterization/golden/prototype.html` + `od_prototype.html` | Complete | → 03-02-PLAN.md (planned) / 03-02-SUMMARY.md (built) |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **No new registered capability, no port/Protocol, no `CompactionStrategy`, no manifest, no Alembic migration, no API endpoint, no new event type.** This phase is a single in-place edit to an existing private method plus test scaffolding (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Phase Boundary"; 03-VERIFICATION.md "Anti-Patterns Found" confirms no `CompactionStrategy` match in engine.py).
- The only production symbol touched: `ExecutionEngine._build_context_message` — its `is_build_task_2_plus` HTML branch now calls the existing `self._extract_html_skeleton(current_html)` and emits a distinctly-marked skeleton block; the helper's own extraction logic is byte-unchanged (→ 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Files Created/Modified"; 03-VERIFICATION.md "Required Artifacts").
- Skeleton block marker (chosen per D-01): `=== CURRENT PROTOTYPE (skeleton — call read_file('prototype.html') for full content before editing) ===` … `=== END CURRENT PROTOTYPE ===` — visually distinct from the old `--- CURRENT HTML (modify this …) ---` block and carrying the `read_file` pointer (→ 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made").
- Registry counter (_KNOWN N→M): **not applicable** — no capability registered this phase.

No AGENT.md edit (Req 7 already satisfied by `prototype-build/AGENT.md` lines 40/78 which already mandate `read_file` + "NEVER rebuild") (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Implementation Decisions" D-01; 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments").

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01 (locked):** distinct skeleton-map marker + literal `read_file('prototype.html')` pointer at the injection site; honest "state-map not editable source" framing; keep the empty/`[Error:` guard; NO AGENT.md edit (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Skeleton injection framing"; 03-DISCUSSION-LOG.md).
- **D-02 / D-02a (locked):** the deterministic CI gate is a message-level `len(compacted) ≤ 0.5 × len(fullhtml)` assertion exercising the real `_build_context_message`, with the full-HTML baseline reconstructed inline (capped at 120k); fixture is an inline multi-page HTML literal, NOT the tiny 84-byte committed golden (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Token-reduction gate test design"; 03-PATTERNS.md "≥50% deterministic gate").
- **D-03 (locked):** re-baseline ONLY the `prototype` + `od_prototype` deliverable goldens via `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE=1`; hold the semantic `*.events.json` snapshots green with no edit; add a focused pages/routes + equal-or-better-validation assertion (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Parity assertion approach").
- **D-04 (locked):** the real-run token delta is captured by an opt-in, dual-gated (`RUN_LIVE_BEDROCK=1` + AWS STS probe) live test, evidence-only, NEVER a CI gate; documented manual-run procedure is the accepted fallback if no live model is available (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Real-run token-delta evidence"; matches MEMORY "Defer live verification").
- **INV-3 sanctioned exception (the load-bearing constraint):** Phase 0C is the ONLY phase permitted to move a deterministic deliverable's bytes; it is gated on semantics + token delta, never byte-identity. Every other migration phase stays byte-identical-where-deterministic. A future agent must NOT treat 0C as license to break byte-parity elsewhere (→ 03-SPEC.md "Constraints"; §3 line 252).
- **Reduction floor = 50%** of the task-2+ context-message size (chars, input-token proxy), measured on a ≥2-page HTML (→ 03-SPEC.md "Constraints").
- **Discretion items resolved:** keep the `=== TEMPLATE COMPLIANCE ===` reminder for task-2+; leave `_extract_html_skeleton` extraction logic as-is unless a concrete parity gap surfaces; on empty/`[Error:` HTML emit NO block (→ 03-CONTEXT.md "Claude's Discretion").

The ≥50% gate measured a **96.9% reduction (ratio 0.031)** on a 29,749-char fixture (419-char skeleton; 941-char compacted message vs 30,227-char full-HTML message). Revert-ratchet confirmed non-vacuous (reverting the engine edit fails 4/5 of the original tests) (→ 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Measured token reduction"; 03-VERIFICATION.md "Behavioral Spot-Checks").

Resolution (commit `f89be6e`, scope tests): **WR-01 FIXED** (live-test monkeypatch had keyword-only params vs the engine's positional call → would `TypeError`; now positional) and **WR-04 ADDRESSED** (added `test_extract_html_skeleton_is_faithful_to_source` so the offline suite actually covers skeleton fidelity rather than relying on the prompt-independent scripted model). **WR-02/WR-03/WR-05 accepted/deferred to Phase 7** (pre-existing `_extract_html_skeleton` robustness gaps — final-section drop with no `</body>`, nested-brace route maps, no engine-side read-before-edit enforcement). **IN-01/IN-02/IN-03 deferred** (dead `_fullhtml_baseline_len`, duplicated full-HTML marker strings across 4 sites, magic `_build_task_number`/`_build_task_total` control keys mutated on the shared `accumulated_outputs` dict) (→ 03-REVIEW.md "Resolution").

No standalone SECURITY.md.

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **`_extract_html_skeleton` is a deliberate live survivor at end-of-phase** — it is now CALLED, not dead; vulture (`min_confidence=80`) does not flag it and NO allow-list entry was added (and none must be). Do not delete it within Phase 3's frame; its deletion is Phase 7's job (→ 03-01-SUMMARY.md "Verification Results"; 03-SPEC.md "Constraints").

Therefore (a) the deliverable golden re-baseline is a no-op diff, (b) the offline parity test (`test_phase3_parity.py`) only proves output-preservation, not skeleton fidelity — it cannot detect a corrupted skeleton (WR-04). Real byte/token change is only observable under a live model run (→ 03-PATTERNS.md "deliverable re-baseline" Caveat; 03-REVIEW.md WR-04).

- **Deferred robustness debt on the now-load-bearing helper** (track when touching it in Phase 7): WR-02 final-section drop on missing `</body>`, WR-03 nested-brace `:root`/`const routes` truncation, WR-05 no engine-side read-before-edit guard (correctness hinges on agent compliance with the prompt pointer) (→ 03-REVIEW.md WR-02/03/05).

## Phase 04 — Manifest + Compiler [1A]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/04-manifest-compiler-1a/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-07)  ·  **Plans:** 5/5  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 1A
**Requirements delivered:** MAN-01, MAN-02, MAN-03, MAN-04, MAN-05, API-01 (→ 04-SPEC.md "Requirements"; 04-VERIFICATION.md "Requirements Coverage")
**One-line outcome:** Introduced the declarative workflow layer — `agents/workflows/` (manifest loader + thin no-DSL compiler + typed `CompiledWorkflow`) and `agents/capabilities/` (Protocol ports + name registry) — and routed every dispatchable pipeline to execute from its compiled plan, reducing `pipeline_type` to a run-entry id-alias, with zero L1–L13 behavioral branch deleted and all Phase-0A characterization snapshots green.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Phase 4 is the **declaration + routing seam only** — it builds the manifest→compiler→`CompiledWorkflow` pipeline and makes the engine source structure (agent sequence, deliverable, clarify defaults, planner-skip) from the compiled plan, while artifacts still flow through the legacy `accumulated_outputs` mirror and **no L1–L13 kernel branch is deleted** (those are Phase 7). Concrete capability impls (Phase 7), typed `ArtifactGraph`/persistence (Phase 5/1B), model policy (Phase 6/1C), and trust flags/self-registration (Phase 8/3) are all explicitly out of scope. (→ 04-SPEC.md "Goal"/"Background"; 04-CONTEXT.md "Phase Boundary")

Must-be-TRUE criteria (→ 04-SPEC.md "Acceptance Criteria"; 04-VERIFICATION.md "Observable Truths"): a malformed manifest raises a typed error naming the field (MAN-01); the compiler is thin/no-DSL with no workflow-name branch (MAN-02/INV-1/INV-5); unknown capability refs are rejected naming the bad ref (MAN-03/INV-4); every `PIPELINE_AGENTS` pipeline + `od_*` alias runs from its `CompiledWorkflow` with no legacy dispatch fallback (MAN-04); `pipeline_type` is consumed only by the id-alias resolver for routing (MAN-05); `GET /api/workflows` + `/{id}` return manifest-derived metadata, 404 on unknown (API-01); Phase-0A snapshots + import-linter + migration-ledger stay green (INV-3/SC-001). **Depends on:** Phase 03 (token-trim 0C) — provides `od_prototype=alias→prototype` precedent + live import-linter/vulture/migration-ledger gates (→ 04-01-SUMMARY.md "requires").

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)
| Plan | Title (short) | What it built (names only) | Key modules/files touched | Status | Detail |
|------|---------------|----------------------------|---------------------------|--------|--------|
| 04-01 | Capability ports + name registry | `CapabilityRegistry` (`is_registered`, `resolve_alias`, `_KNOWN` 14 pairs); 6 `runtime_checkable` Protocol ports `ExecutionStrategy`/`Validator`/`DeliverableResolver`/`ContextProvider`/`GateHandler`/`TaskParser` | `agents/capabilities/{__init__,base,registry}.py`; `tests/agents/test_registry_capabilities.py`; `pyproject.toml` (vulture) | Complete | → 04-01-PLAN.md / 04-01-SUMMARY.md |
| 04-02 | Declaration data layer | `CompiledWorkflow`/`Step`/`Task` dataclasses + inert forward stub types; `WorkflowManifest` + `ManifestValidationError` + `load_manifest`; `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS` strict-key rejection | `agents/workflows/{__init__,plan,manifest}.py`; `tests/agents/test_manifest.py` | Complete | → 04-02-PLAN.md / 04-02-SUMMARY.md |
| 04-03 | Thin no-DSL compiler + 15 manifests + parity tests | `WorkflowCompiler.compile` + `CompilerError` (name-resolve + topo-validate); 15 `workflow.yaml`; coverage/parity/edge tests | `agents/workflows/compiler.py`; 15 `agents/workflows/<id>/workflow.yaml`; `tests/agents/test_{compiler,manifest_coverage,manifest_parity,edge_manifests}.py` | Complete | → 04-03-PLAN.md / 04-03-SUMMARY.md |
| 04-04 | Engine routing seam — run from CompiledWorkflow | `resolve_alias` + `compile_for_run` module fns; `_WORKFLOWS_DIR`/`_CAPABILITY_REGISTRY`/`_WORKFLOW_COMPILER` constants; 4-concern routing seam in `execute()`; removed `_pipeline_defaults` dict + `SKIP_PLANNER_FOR_PROTOTYPE` import as SOURCES | `agents/execution_engine/engine.py`; `tests/agents/test_{id_alias_resolver,pipeline_type_routing,compiled_plan_runs}.py`; `deferred-items.md` | Complete | → 04-04-PLAN.md / 04-04-SUMMARY.md |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **New registered capability NAMES (names only, no impls — Phase 7 binds impls):** `strategy:single_shot`, `strategy:task_loop`, `validator:html_static`, `validator:html_render`, `deliverable:single_file`, `deliverable:serialized_sandbox`, `deliverable:streamed_text`, `deliverable:ppt`, `context_provider:opendesign`, `context_provider:previous_run`, `task_parser:heading_tasks`, `gate:human`, `gate:validation`, `compaction:html_skeleton` — exactly 14 `(kind, name)` pairs in `_KNOWN` (→ 04-01-PLAN.md "artifacts_produced"; 04-RESEARCH.md "D-07 — Authoritative Capability Name Set"). `_KNOWN N→M`: this phase establishes the counter at **14** (drift-guarded by `test_registered_count_is_exactly_fourteen`).
- **Ports/Protocols added:** `ExecutionStrategy`, `Validator`, `DeliverableResolver`, `ContextProvider`, `GateHandler`, `TaskParser` in `agents/capabilities/base.py` — first `typing.Protocol`-port precedent in the repo; interface-only, no bodies (→ 04-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments").
- **Key classes/modules introduced:** `CapabilityRegistry` (`agents/capabilities/registry.py`); `CompiledWorkflow`/`Step`/`Task` + forward stub dataclasses `ToolPermissions`/`ModelPolicy`/`TaskSource`/`FixPolicy`/`FanoutSpec`/`RetryPolicy`/`RepoSpec`/`Limits`/`DeliverableSpec`/`ClarifySpec` (`agents/workflows/plan.py`); `WorkflowManifest`/`ManifestValidationError`/`load_manifest` (`agents/workflows/manifest.py`); `WorkflowCompiler`/`CompilerError` (`agents/workflows/compiler.py`); `resolve_alias`/`compile_for_run` (module-level in `engine.py`) (→ 04-VERIFICATION.md "Required Artifacts").
- **Alembic migrations:** NONE — Phase 4 is additive-only with **zero DB schema change** (Q3); `accumulated_outputs` mirror untouched, no `owner_id`/`workspace_id` tables added (→ 04-SPEC.md "Constraints"; 04-VERIFICATION.md truth #8).
- **New/changed API endpoints:** `GET /api/workflows` (manifest list, was run-history list) + `GET /api/workflows/{id}` (full compiled step config; 404 on unknown) — manifest-derived, no DB query. Run-history **relocated** to `GET /api/runs`, `GET /api/runs/{id}`, `GET /api/runs/{id}/chain-context`, `DELETE /api/runs/{id}`, `POST /api/runs/export-pptx` (→ 04-05-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments").
- **New event types:** NONE — semantic-event multiset kept byte-identical for INV-3 (the deliverable routing concern is consumed via a DEBUG log, NOT a yielded event) (→ 04-04-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made").

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
This phase is **predominantly additive** (declaration layer is net-new), with **two narrow source-deletions** in `engine.py` — the hardcoded *sources* of two routing concerns, replaced (not duplicated) by `CompiledWorkflow` reads:
- **`_pipeline_defaults` dict** (the function-local clarify-defaults dict at the old engine.py:752-763) — DELETED from executable code; clarify defaults now sourced from `compiled.clarify.defaults` (→ 04-04-SUMMARY.md "Files Created/Modified"). Its verbatim lists were copied into the 15 manifests' `clarify.defaults` (parity trap #2).
- **`SKIP_PLANNER_FOR_PROTOTYPE` / `is_prototype_pipeline` import** (from `agents.prototype.pipeline`) — removed as the planner-skip ROUTING source; `skip_planner` now `== (compiled.planner == "skip")`, which is `False` everywhere because all manifests declare `planner: run` (byte-identical, since `SKIP_PLANNER_FOR_PROTOTYPE = False` today) (→ 04-04-SUMMARY.md "key-decisions").
- **NOT deleted (deliberately survive — see §7):** all L1–L13 behavioral branches; `get_pipeline_agents` (demoted from agent-list SOURCE to a 1A drift-check reference + manifest-derivation source); the `accumulated_outputs` mirror (L15, untouched, deleted in Phase 5/1B).
- **Migration-ledger:** L1–L13 rows stay un-flipped (`☐`); only the future-Phase-7 deletions flip them. `test_migration_ledger.py` green (4 passed, 1 skipped); pinned L7 (`spec.id == "prototype-build"`) and L10 (`pipeline_type in ("od_prototype", "prototype")`) lines kept byte-identical (→ 04-04-SUMMARY.md "Self-Check"; 04-VERIFICATION.md truth #11).
- **No new banned-pattern/grep gate armed this phase.** Import-linter contracts for the new packages were RECOMMENDED but NOT added (see IN-01 in §6) — the only live contract remains `engine → app.api` forbidden.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01/D-02 [user override]:** `/api/workflows` reclaimed for manifest definitions; run-history moved to NEW `/api/runs` router — **clean break, no 308-redirect aliases** (RESEARCH D-02 confirmed JWT-only, no external API-key consumer). `od_prototype` is reachable via `GET /api/workflows/{id}` but NOT in the list (alias, not a `PIPELINE_AGENTS` key) (→ 04-CONTEXT.md "API surface"; 04-DISCUSSION-LOG.md "A").
- **D-03/D-04:** 15 manifests, one per `PIPELINE_AGENTS` key; revisions get their own manifest (distinct agents). `od_prototype` is the SOLE id-alias → `prototype` (no own dir). `od_ppt`/`od_ppt_revision` are REAL keys (own manifests; OpenDesign flavor declared via `context_providers: [opendesign]` but actual injection stays in surviving L12) (→ 04-CONTEXT.md "Manifest coverage & aliases").
- **D-05 [user override]:** Placeholder manifests for both edge cases, ZERO coverage-test exemptions — `reverse_engineer` = `steps: []` empty-plan stub; `chat` = full 7-agent manifest that loads+compiles but the engine never dispatches (ChatRunner-driven, in `_INTERNAL_PIPELINES`) (→ 04-CONTEXT.md D-05; 04-DISCUSSION-LOG.md "D").
- **D-06:** Full §6 typed contract declared NOW; forward fields (`model`, `tools`, `validators`, `fix`, `compaction`, `fanout`, `on_conflict`, `retry`, `injects`, `repo`, `limits`) are declared-but-INERT with safe defaults — **no Phase-4 behavior may key off an inert field.** Forward types are minimal stub dataclasses (chosen over `dict|None`) (→ 04-CONTEXT.md D-06; 04-02-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made").
- **D-07:** Names-only registry now; `@register`/`discover()`/`user_allowed`/owner allow-list are Phase 8 (registering names then converting to self-registration is an evolution, not a dual impl — INV-12 respected) (→ 04-CONTEXT.md D-07).
- **D-08/INV-5:** Manifests are pure data; `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS` strict-key rejection means a `when:`/`if:`/`for:`/`${}`/`{{}}` field has nowhere to live; compiler has no `if pipeline_type` branch and no `eval`/`exec` (→ 04-CONTEXT.md D-08).
- **D-09/MAN-05:** Engine sources the FOUR routing concerns from `CompiledWorkflow`; `pipeline_type` consumed only by the id-alias resolver. The 8 surviving behavioral `pipeline_type` branches + 3 `spec.id` branches are explicitly allow-listed as Phase-7-scoped (full classification → 04-RESEARCH.md "D-09 — Engine pipeline_type / spec.id Inventory") — pinned by `test_pipeline_type_routing.py`.
- **D-10:** Loader is `@dataclass` + `yaml.safe_load` + manual per-field validation mirroring `agents/loader.py` — **NOT Pydantic** (Pydantic is only at the API boundary). Errors NAME the offending field (→ 04-CONTEXT.md D-10; 04-PATTERNS.md "Dataclass-not-Pydantic").
- **Two PARITY TRAPS locked by dedicated tests (do not break):** (1) every dispatchable manifest declares `planner: run` (NOT `skip` — `SKIP_PLANNER_FOR_PROTOTYPE = False`); (2) each manifest's `clarify.defaults` reproduces the engine `_pipeline_defaults` lists verbatim (→ 04-03-SUMMARY.md "patterns-established"; 04-RESEARCH.md "Common Pitfalls" 1 & 2).

This same quirk is the root of OPEN review finding WR-01.

## Phase 05 — Typed Artifacts + Persistence + Ownership [1B]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/05-typed-artifacts-persistence-ownership-1b/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-08)  ·  **Plans:** 7/7  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 1B
**Requirements delivered:** ART-01, ART-02, ART-03, ART-04, PERSIST-01, PERSIST-02, PERSIST-03, AUTHZ-01, AUTHZ-02, AUTHZ-03, AUTHZ-04, CAPRUN-01, API-04, API-05 (14 reqs) — confirmed in → 05-SPEC.md (Requirements) and → 05-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage).
**One-line outcome:** Replaced the two competing artifact mechanisms — the untyped per-run `accumulated_outputs` mirror (L15) and the legacy DB `ArtifactStore` "thin store" — with ONE typed, sha256-content-addressed, owner-scoped `ArtifactGraph`/`ArtifactRef` persisted via additive §18 schema with a single default-deny `ScopedStore`, then deleted both legacy stores at byte-identical + semantic-event parity.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Replace `accumulated_outputs: dict[str,str]` AND the thin DB store with one typed content-addressed owner-scoped `ArtifactGraph`/`ArtifactRef`; land the additive §18 persistence schema (`0014+`); enforce default-deny ownership at one store-layer helper; durably log events with a per-run `seq`; expose two owner-scoped read endpoints; then dual-write → migrate reads → DELETE both legacy stores, gated on Phase-0A byte-identical deliverables + semantic-event parity (INV-3/INV-12 — adding the graph without deleting what it supersedes = phase NOT done). → 05-SPEC.md (Goal), → 05-CONTEXT.md (Phase Boundary). Ambiguity 0.14 (gate ≤0.20). **Depends on:** Phase 0A (characterization parity gate + `_normalize.py` strip-keys), Phase 2 (the pure `assert_owns` predicate this phase relocates + `user_id or "anon"` disk-keying it preserves), Phase 4 (`/api/runs.py` router, `runtime=langchain_deepagents`, `plan_id` forward field). → 05-CONTEXT.md (Canonical References).
Key "must be TRUE" criteria (→ 05-SPEC.md Acceptance Criteria): `grep accumulated_outputs` → 0 non-test; thin-store artifact methods + `WorkflowArtifact` + `workflow_artifacts` gone, HITL half kept; `0014` up/downgrade clean from `0013`; every new table carries `owner_id`+`workspace_id`; cross-owner parent/artifact/workspace reads denied (404); anon run persists `anon:<session_id>` never `None`; `run_events` contiguous `seq`+unique `event_id`; one `run_capabilities` row `runtime=langchain_deepagents`; 0A characterization (5 pipelines) byte-identical + semantic-event parity.

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)

| 05-02 | §18 persistence schema | ORM `ArtifactRef`/`Workspace`/`RunEvent`/`RunCapabilities`; extend `WorkflowRun` + `WorkflowDefinition`; migration `0014` + default-workspace backfill | `app/models/{artifact_ref,workspace,run_event,run_capabilities,workflow,workflow_definition,__init__}.py`, `alembic/versions/0014_typed_artifacts_persistence.py`, `tests/unit/test_migration_0014.py` | Complete | → 05-02-PLAN.md / 05-02-SUMMARY.md |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **Typed substrate (no registered capabilities — this phase is data/persistence, not strategies):** `ArtifactGraph` (per-run in-memory registry; owns sha256 hashing + per-`(run_id, kind)` versioning; `write_ref`/`get`/`list_by_kind`/`consumed_for` typed-routing-by-kind-equality/`lineage`/`tree`); `ArtifactRef` dataclass (plan §6:454–466 field set + D-01 inline `content`); `ARTIFACT_KINDS` closed snake_case vocabulary (spec|plan|task_list|html_file|file_bundle|repo_inventory|repo_diff|context_pack|validation_report|merge_conflict|summary|patch). → 05-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments). Kernel-pure: stdlib only, NO `app.*` imports.
- **Store-layer port:** `ScopedStore` in `agents/authz.py` — the SINGLE enforced default-deny read/write path constructed with `(owner_id, workspace_id, session=None)`; `_scope_with_visibility` (ArtifactRef: `owner_id == :o AND (workspace_id == :ws OR visibility IN ('workspace','public'))`) + `_scope_owner_ws` (run/workspace/event/caps). Relocated `assert_owns` is now a real store lookup (D-07). → 05-03-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
- **Alembic migrations:** `0014_typed_artifacts_persistence` (additive, down_revision `0013`) — creates `artifact_refs` (carries owner_id/workspace_id/kind/producer_step/producer_agent/task_id/inline content/content_hash/location/version/parents/derived_from/visibility/retention; idx `(run_id,kind)` + `(content_hash)`), `workspaces` (kind/runtime/repo_id/ttl; `workspace_id == own id`), `run_events` (seq/event_id/type/payload_json; idx `(run_id,seq)`), `run_capabilities` (runtime + nullable model_overrides/skills/hooks/integrations/mcp_servers/versions); extends `workflow_runs` (+owner_id/workspace_id/source_run_id/plan_id/budget_snapshot_json) and existing `workflows`/`WorkflowDefinition` (+owner_id/workspace_id/source/manifest_json/version); inline default-workspace backfill scopes every historical run. `0015_drop_thin_artifact_store` (destructive, down_revision `0014`, sequenced LAST) — `op.drop_table("workflow_artifacts")`, reversible downgrade recreates table+index. → 05-02-SUMMARY.md, 05-07-SUMMARY.md.
- **API endpoints (owner-scoped, `async def`, cross-owner→404):** `GET /api/runs/{id}/artifacts` (nested lineage tree, content excluded by default / `?include=content`), `GET /api/runs/{id}/events?after=<seq>` (rows seq>after ascending, each with event_id; `after: int` FastAPI-coerced→422 on non-int). → 05-05-SUMMARY.md.
- **New event fields:** every emitted event gains `seq` (monotonic per-run, single `itertools.count(1)` at the `execute()` emit boundary) + `event_id` (uuid) — both stripped from the 0A snapshot multiset. → 05-04-SUMMARY.md.
- **Registry counter (_KNOWN):** not moved this phase — no new registered capabilities (this phase adds data/persistence, not strategies/validators).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
This phase is the strangler cutover; it deleted substantial code. A future agent must NOT resurrect any of it:
- **`ExecutionContext.accumulated_outputs` field + the local prior-output dict (leak L15)** — DELETED; the typed `ctx.artifacts` (`ArtifactGraph`) is the SOLE prior-agent output source. The L15 grep ratchet (`accumulated_outputs` → 0 in `backend/ *.py`) is now a permanent armed CI gate. Ledger **L15 ☑** (SHA aa68dc9). → 05-07-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments), → migration-ledger.md.
- **Thin-store artifact-persistence half** — DELETED from `agents/artifact_store/store.py`: `store`/`retrieve_latest`/`retrieve_version`/`list_by_type`/`list_lineage`/`_store_in_memory`/`_to_dict` + `_use_db`/`_mem_*` fields + `ArtifactStoreWriteError`. → 05-07-SUMMARY.md.
- **`app/models/artifact.py` `WorkflowArtifact` model + `workflow_artifacts` table** — DELETED (`0015` DROP); the `WorkflowRun.artifacts` relationship + the `__init__.py` import removed. The **D2** thin-store deletion gate greps `from app\.models\.artifact import` → 0 (NOT the bare table name `workflow_artifacts`, which is load-bearing in the 0010 CREATE + 0015 DROP migrations — see Gotchas). Ledger **D2 ☑** (SHA 26863bc). → 05-07-SUMMARY.md (Deviations #1), → migration-ledger.md.
- **`agents/execution_engine/authz.py`** (Phase-2 pure `assert_owns` predicate) — DELETED, relocated UP to `agents/authz.py` and grown into `ScopedStore.assert_owns` (real lookup). The engine's old `_derive_parent_owner` by-convention helper is superseded. → 05-03-SUMMARY.md.
- **`tests/unit/test_artifact_store_fk.py`** + the unused `conftest.fk_session`/`make_fk_sqlite_engine` fixtures — DELETED (orphaned by the table drop). → 05-07-SUMMARY.md (Deviations #2).
- **KEPT on purpose (do NOT delete):** the in-memory `asyncio.Event` HITL half of `store.py` (`get_resume_event`/`set+get_questionnaire_responses`/`get_review_event`/`set+get_review_response` + `get_artifact_store` singleton) — Phase 8 owns HITL. → 05-07-SUMMARY.md.
- **Ledger flip-set** assertion in `test_migration_ledger.py` updated to `{D2, L14, L15, L16}`; the old hard-coded `["L14","L16"]` assertion removed. L16 stays a CHECK row (green). → 05-07-PLAN.md (Task 3), → migration-ledger.md.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01: `artifact_refs` keeps INLINE `content: Text`** + records `content_hash = sha256(content.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()` + `location` (sandbox-relative path for file-backed, logical `artifact_refs/{id}` for string artifacts). Content is self-contained in the row so it survives the 48h sandbox TTL; the helper stays pure-DB. → 05-CONTEXT.md (Area A).
- **D-02: Package layout per plan §32 — `agents/artifacts/` (sibling of kernel, NOT inside execution_engine/).** Overrides SPEC ART-01 prose which said `agents/execution_engine/artifacts/`. Dataclass↔ORM mapping lives in the store helper, never in the kernel-pure graph. → 05-01-SUMMARY.md (Decisions).
- **D-03/D-13: split migration chain, parity-gated deletion** — `0014` additive, `0015` destructive lands LAST after read-cutover + parity green. Sequence: add → dual-write → migrate reads → delete. Never ship with the mirror alive (fix-forward). → 05-CONTEXT.md (Area B / Dual-write cutover sequencing).
- **D-06/D-07: ONE default-deny `ScopedStore` is the single enforced read path** (§19 "not scattered in callers"); `assert_owns` does a real UNSCOPED-by-owner parent lookup then compares, preserving the Phase-2 `PermissionError` message shape. → 05-03-SUMMARY.md.
- **D-08 (corrected by RESEARCH #2): the DB is FULLY SYNC — no `AsyncSession`.** `ScopedStore` reuses the sync-`SessionLocal()`-inside-`async def` idiom; accepts an injected `Session` (endpoint path) or opens its own (engine path). Do NOT introduce `create_async_engine`/`AsyncSession`. → 05-RESEARCH.md (#2).
- **D-09 byte-identity guard (CRITICAL): `ctx.disk_principal = user_id or "anon"` is DECOUPLED from `ctx.owner_id = user_id or f"anon:{session_id or pipeline_run_id}"`.** Disk keying (`RunSandbox`/`create_runner`) MUST use `disk_principal`, NEVER `owner_id` — else anon disk paths change and break the 0A snapshot (CTX-05). For authed runs they are equal; only anon runs differ. → 05-04-SUMMARY.md (Decisions), → 05-SECURITY.md (T-5-CTX05).
- **D-11 (corrected by RESEARCH #4): there was NO existing per-run `seq`.** Introduce exactly ONE monotonic counter at the single `execute()` emit boundary (NOT in `ndjson_adapter.py`, NOT a DB-side `MAX(seq)+1`/Postgres sequence). `seq`/`event_id` added to `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` so the canonical-JSON multiset is unperturbed; `assert_seq_contiguous` (deltas==1, SAFE-03) is now non-vacuous. → 05-04-SUMMARY.md, → 05-RESEARCH.md (#4).
- **`workflows` table collision (RESEARCH #5): EXTEND the existing `WorkflowDefinition` (`__tablename__="workflows"`), do NOT create a second `workflows` table.** DB-authored user workflows are v2; the table lands as file-backed manifest metadata only. → 05-02-SUMMARY.md (Decisions).
- **`visibility="workspace"` on cross-run-readable producer writes** (planning_context, each `produces` kind, revision write, clarifications) — the dataclass default `"private"` would starve a same-owner cross-run `_handle_revision` read (which threads no workspace_id) and break INV-3. The revision ref inherits `original.workspace_id` (NOT NULL). → 05-06-SUMMARY.md (Deviations #2). NOTE this is a deliberate override of the §6:465 `"private"` dataclass default for these specific persisted rows.
- **Owner/AUTHZ invariants enforced:** `owner_id` never `None` (AUTHZ-03); `owner_id`+`workspace_id` NOT NULL on the 4 new tables (nullable+backfilled on extended tables); `write_ref` rejects a falsy resolved owner; `assert_owns` propagates `PermissionError` (L16 intact). → 05-SECURITY.md (Threat Register).
- **Locked Q3/INV constraints honored:** additive migrations only (sole sanctioned destructive change = the `0015` thin-store DROP, reversible); import-linter stays green (`agents/artifacts/` stdlib-only, `agents/authz.py` imports `app.models` only never `app.api`); `engine.py`→`kernel.py` split deferred to Phase 7 (kept the Phase-1 import scaffold). → 05-CONTEXT.md (Established Patterns).
- **Deferred (do NOT build here):** retention SWEEP/janitor (P9; field-only here), `RuntimeEnvironment`/`Workspace` runtime port + `LocalSandboxRuntime` (P9; `workspaces` schema-only), content dedup/replay-reuse on hash (P12), `model_overrides` population (P6), `budget_snapshot_json` population (P11), `repositories`/`subagent_runs`/`wave_runs`/`validation_results`/`gate_events`/`hook_runs` tables (P9/P11/P12/P8), `GET /api/runs/{id}/diff` (P9), historical `workflow_artifacts`→`artifact_refs` content backfill (recorded decision: NOT done — pre-cutover lineage is best-effort), deleting kernel leaks L1–L13 + prototype-as-manifest parity (P7). → 05-CONTEXT.md (Deferred Ideas).

Round 3 → CLEAN (0 Critical/0 Warning), 3 Info (IN-01/IN-02 then fixed `38077bf`/`8be60ae`; IN-03 revision `pipeline_complete` constants = intentional Phase-3 stub, skipped). → 05-REVIEW.md / .iter2 / .iter3 + 05-REVIEW-FIX.md / .iter2 / .iter3. Notable round-1 fixes a future agent should know: clarifications multi-round versioning now uses `force_db_version=True` so the DB is authoritative (was always v1); `_build_lineage_tree` attaches each node under one non-cycle-forming parent (diamond renders under one parent only); best-effort DB-write catches narrowed to `SQLAlchemyError` (re-raise anything else); `restore_non_terminal_runs` only re-arms `waiting_for_user`, marks other interrupted runs `failed`; `0014` backfill is idempotent (`WHERE workspace_id IS NULL`).

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **`_handle_revision` is a LIVE survivor — do NOT delete it.** It is the `run_revision` PPT handler (ledger D1 voided). It reads the PARENT run's artifacts cross-run, so it MUST use persisted `ScopedStore` against `artifact_refs` (the per-run in-memory `ArtifactGraph` cannot serve cross-run); it is `assert_owns(parent_run_id)`-gated ABOVE the reads (T-5-SEED). It is NOT routed through `execute()` — it has its own `_stamped_send` seq counter and its own deferred `_RunEventSink` arming (armed only after `original.workspace_id` is resolved). → 05-06-SUMMARY.md, → 05-RESEARCH.md (Thin-store consumer map).
- **The D2 deletion gate greps the deleted MODEL IMPORT PATH `from app\.models\.artifact import`, NOT the table name** — `workflow_artifacts` is load-bearing in the `0010` CREATE + `0015` DROP migrations and 0013/0014 prose, so a bare-name grep can never reach 0. Do not "fix" the gate to the table name. → 05-07-SUMMARY.md (Deviations #1).
- **Clarifications PAYLOAD now lives in `artifact_refs` (kind=clarifications, visibility=workspace), read back via `ScopedStore.list_refs`** — the literal `retrieve_latest("clarifications")` is gone from websocket. The read is ownership-gated. → 05-06-SUMMARY.md.
- **Build-loop scratch keys `_build_task_number`/`_build_task_total` are NOT artifacts** — relocated off the deleted dict onto `ectx.build_task_number`/`build_task_total` (a non-artifact home, the documented temporary home for Phase 7's `TaskLoopStrategy`); do not model them as ArtifactRefs. → 05-07-SUMMARY.md.
- **Live survivors that LOOK like leaks but are deliberately kept:** the HITL `asyncio.Event` half of `store.py` (Phase 8); the `run_revision` revision path (`_handle_revision`); the build-loop scratch on `ectx`; `_run_validation_fix_loop`/`run_revision`/`REVISION_FILE_NAME` are Phase-7/8 concerns, untouched here.
- **`ScopedStore.assert_owns` allows an ABSENT parent (returns None)** by design (TTL-swept / same-owner degrade) — a non-existent `parent_run_id` passes the check (no leak, the subsequent read finds nothing). IN-01 documented; not a defect. → 05-REVIEW.iter2.md (IN-01).
- **Revision `pipeline_complete` reports constant `total_duration=0.0` / `agents_completed=1`** — intentional Phase-3 stub (the full DeepAgent revision loop is not yet wired); replace with measured values when that loop lands. IN-03, skipped. → 05-REVIEW-FIX.md.
- **CR-01 reopened/re-scoped the revision path twice** (WR-06 → iter2 CR-01) — the lesson: the offline characterization harness has no DB schema so it passes trivially; revision-run scoping/persistence is a REAL-DB-only concern. The regression test `test_revision_run_events_persist_and_resolve_on_real_db` builds an in-memory SQLite with full ORM metadata + monkeypatches `SessionLocal` to catch this. Any future change to revision-run creation must keep `owner_id`+`workspace_id` set (the main pipeline `workflow_runs` row is NOT scope-written-back — a pre-existing out-of-scope condition). → 05-REVIEW-FIX.iter2.md, → 05-REVIEW.iter3.md.
- **Pre-existing failures NOT to chase:** `test_logout.py` (×7, self-registration disabled→403), `test_pipeline_cancel.py` (×1, expired AWS Bedrock token); pre-existing ruff `B905`/`B023`/`F841` in `_normalize.py`/`websocket.py`/`clarify_engine.py`. → deferred-items.md.
