## Phase 06 — Model Policy [1C]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/06-model-policy-1c/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-08)  ·  **Plans:** 5/5  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 1C
**Requirements delivered:** MODEL-01, MODEL-02, MODEL-03, MODEL-04, MODEL-05, plus the additive AGENT.md optional `model` field (supports MODEL-01) — source plan §20. (REQUIREMENTS.md lines 60–64 map MODEL-01..05 to Phase 6 → 06-VERIFICATION.md "Requirements Coverage".)
**One-line outcome:** Made the inert Phase-4/5 model-policy scaffolding live — a `ModelResolver` on `ExecutionContext` selects each agent's effective model by a 5-tier precedence, a `ModelCatalog` becomes the single source of model metadata, per-agent `model_overrides` are validated + persisted, and an engine-level model-switch fallback advances on transient throttles — all with INV-3 byte/semantic parity for existing single-model runs.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
A `ModelResolver` carried on `ExecutionContext` resolves the effective model per agent invocation by fixed precedence (user per-agent override > `step.model` > AGENT.md agent default > `workflow.model` > run default = session `model_id` else global Haiku), drives `build_model` through a `ModelPolicy` (id + `cost_class` metadata + ordered `fallback`), switches to the next chain model on a sustained throttle, and persists the per-run `model_overrides` map — with no-override runs resolving to today's Haiku/session outcome unchanged (→ 06-SPEC.md "Goal"). Backend-only; the `GET /api/capabilities` endpoint + frontend picker are Phase 8, `cost_class`→BudgetManager enforcement is Phase 11, manifests authoring `step.model` is Phase 7 (→ 06-SPEC.md "Boundaries"). Ambiguity score 0.138 ≤ 0.20 (→ 06-SPEC.md "Ambiguity Report"). 13 falsifiable acceptance checks (→ 06-SPEC.md "Acceptance Criteria"). **Depends-on:** Phase 4 (the inert `ModelPolicy`/`Step.model`/`CompiledWorkflow.model` fields + name-only registry pattern D-07) and Phase 5 (the per-run-helper-on-ctx pattern `scoped_store`, `ScopedStore.record_capabilities`, the reserved `run_capabilities.model_overrides` column) (→ 06-CONTEXT.md "Canonical References").

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)

| Plan | Title (short) | What it built (names only) | Key modules/files touched | Status | Detail |
|------|---------------|----------------------------|---------------------------|--------|--------|
| 06-01 | ModelCatalog single-source data capability | `ModelCatalog` + frozen `ModelEntry` (5 entries); `("model_catalog","default")` in `_KNOWN`; `AVAILABLE_MODELS`/`_VALID_MODEL_IDS` as derived projections | `agents/capabilities/model_catalog.py` (new), `registry.py`, `app/api/settings.py`, `tests/agents/test_model_catalog.py` (new) | Complete | → 06-01-PLAN.md (planned) / 06-01-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 06-02 | AgentSpec.model loader field | `AgentSpec.model: str \| None`; `_build_spec` optional-string type-guard parse | `agents/loader.py`, `tests/agents/test_loader.py` | Complete | → 06-02-PLAN.md (planned) / 06-02-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 06-03 | ModelResolver + throttle predicate | `ModelResolver` (`resolve`/`chain_for`/`set_chain`/`current`/`advance`); `_is_transient_throttle`; `ExecutionContext.model_resolver`+`model_overrides`; resolver construct at `execute()` entry; `_resolve_model` helper; 3 `_run_agent` sites rewired | `agents/model_policy.py` (new), `context.py`, `engine.py`, `tests/unit/test_model_resolver.py` (new) | Complete | → 06-03-PLAN.md (planned) / 06-03-SUMMARY.md (built) |
| 06-04 | model_overrides ingress + persistence | `_validate_model_overrides` allow-list gate; `model_overrides` payload read + `execute()`/`_execute_impl()` param + ctx seed; persist `(ectx.model_overrides or None)` via `record_capabilities` | `app/api/websocket.py`, `engine.py`, `tests/unit/test_run_pipeline_validation.py`, `tests/unit/test_run_capabilities.py` | Complete | → 06-04-PLAN.md (planned) / 06-04-SUMMARY.md (built) |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **Registered capability (name-only):** `model_catalog:default` — added to `CapabilityRegistry._KNOWN` as pure membership (no instance, no `@register`, no trust until Phase 8) (→ 06-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments"). **_KNOWN counter moved 14→15** (docstring count bumped) (→ 06-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments"; carryover test fixed — see §7).
- **New kernel modules/classes:**
  - `ModelCatalog` + frozen `ModelEntry` dataclass — kernel-pure data capability, methods `list()`/`get()`/`is_allowed()`/`ids()`; 5 seeded entries (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.5, Opus 4.6) each with `id`/`label`/`description`/`tier`/`cost_class`/`provider`/`context_window`/`user_allowed`; all `user_allowed=true` incl. Opus, no gate (N11). No `app.*` import (→ 06-01-SUMMARY.md "Files Created/Modified"). No new Protocol in `base.py` (catalog is data, not behavior — → 06-RESEARCH.md "MUST RESOLVE #3").
  - `ModelResolver` in `agents/model_policy.py` — `resolve(spec, step)` 5-tier precedence; `chain_for(id)` tier-descent default chains (Opus→[Sonnet,Haiku], Sonnet→[Haiku], Haiku→[]); `set_chain`/`current`/`advance` cursor for the 06-05 loop (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md frontmatter `provides`).
  - module-level `_is_transient_throttle(exc)` predicate — layered Bedrock/Anthropic classifier, co-located in `model_policy.py` (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments").
- **ExecutionContext fields:** `model_resolver: object | None = None` + `model_overrides: dict = field(default_factory=dict)` — typed `object` import-pure exactly like `scoped_store` (no new import) (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md "Files Created/Modified").
- **engine.execute()/_execute_impl():** new `model_overrides: dict[str,str] | None = None` param (+ forward); resolver constructed after `compile_for_run()` (→ 06-04-SUMMARY.md; 06-03 key-decisions). `_resolve_model(ectx, spec, model_id)` helper added (resolver-or-fallback).
- **Alembic migrations:** **NONE added.** The `run_capabilities.model_overrides` JSON column already exists (migration **0014**, head 0015); Phase 6 only begins writing it (→ 06-04-PLAN.md acceptance `grep -c 001[6-9]` → 0; 06-RESEARCH.md "D-08"). `run_capabilities` already carries `owner_id`+`workspace_id`.
- **New API endpoints:** NONE (`GET /api/capabilities` is explicitly Phase 8 — confirmed absent in 06-VERIFICATION.md truth #13). `/api/settings` response shape unchanged (`{id,name,description,tier}`).
- **New event types:** `agent_model_fallback` WS event — emitted only on a throttle-driven model switch; carries `fallback_model`, `attempt`, and `reset_output: true` (WR-03 fix); never fires on the no-throttle path so does not affect characterization parity; the WS drainer forwards unknown types verbatim (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md "Issues Encountered"; 06-REVIEW.md WR-03).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
- **The hand-maintained `AVAILABLE_MODELS` literal list (settings.py:40–71) was DELETED** and replaced by a derived projection over `ModelCatalog().list()`; `_VALID_MODEL_IDS` became `set(ModelCatalog().ids())`. `ModelCatalog` is now the single source — INV-12 enforced by a single-source grep test asserting model-id literals (`claude-haiku/sonnet/opus-4`) live ONLY in `model_catalog.py` (→ 06-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments"; 06-SECURITY.md T-06-02). A future agent must NOT reintroduce a second model list anywhere under `app/api` — add models to `model_catalog.py` only.
- **The runner's `astream_events` exception swallow was SUPERSEDED (scoped):** `deep_agent_runner.py` now re-raises `_is_transient_throttle` matches (B1) instead of swallowing them into `{"type":"error"}`; every other exception keeps the exact prior swallow (surgical parity boundary). This is the planned "promote to raise" the runner docstring already anticipated, scoped strictly to transient throttles (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments"; patterns-established).
- **No L#/F# leaks flipped, no migration-ledger rows changed.** Phase 6 is largely additive/wiring; it deletes no engine leak. The migration-ledger ratchet stays unchanged (Phase 6 flips no L-rows) (→ 06-CONTEXT.md "Canonical References" migration-ledger note; 06-RESEARCH.md "CI gates" migration-ledger row).
- **Gates relied on (not newly armed by this phase):** banned-pattern (INV-13 deepagents-only) stayed green — fallback rebuilds via `create_runner`, `engine.py` `create_deep_agent` call-count remained 0 (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md "Parity + invariants verified"). import-linter contract "agents.capabilities must not import the execution kernel or the web layer" kept the new catalog kernel-pure (3 kept / 0 broken throughout).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-02 precedence (LOCKED):** override > `step.model` > `AgentSpec.model` > `workflow.model` > (session `model_id` else Haiku), None tiers skipped. Tier-5 default = `session_model_id or haiku_default` is the **INV-3 parity anchor** — never drop the session id, or every existing run silently falls to Haiku (→ 06-CONTEXT.md "Area A"; 06-RESEARCH.md "Pitfall 2").
- **D-05 → APPROACH B (LOCKED by research, NOT `with_fallbacks`):** `RunnableWithFallbacks` lacks `bind_tools` and does not compose through the deepagents `astream_events` graph; the runner swallows model exceptions. The engine owns the bounded rebuild-and-retry loop; `build_model` stays the unchanged single source. Do NOT attempt `build_model().with_fallbacks(...)` (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md key-decisions; 06-RESEARCH.md "MUST RESOLVE #1"; verified absent by `grep with_fallbacks` in 06-VERIFICATION.md).
- **D-03/D-04 (LOCKED):** `ModelCatalog` is kernel-pure (no `app.*` import — would break import-linter and the resolver's ability to import it); registry adds name-only membership; self-registration / `user_allowed` trust / `/api/capabilities` are Phase 8 evolution, NOT a dual implementation (→ 06-CONTEXT.md "Area B").
- **N11 (LOCKED, answered):** Haiku global default; all models incl. Opus `user_allowed=true`, no gate; `cost_class` Haiku=cheap / Sonnet=standard / Opus=premium; default tier-descent fallback (→ 06-SPEC.md "Interview Log").
- **Q2 allow-list trust (LOCKED):** model selection is catalog-only — never an arbitrary string. Validated at `model_overrides` ingress (websocket) AND for AGENT.md `model` at resolve time (→ 06-CONTEXT.md "Established Patterns"; 06-SECURITY.md T-06-03/T-06-06).
- **Q3 additive-only (LOCKED):** no new migration — column exists (0014); persist `(ectx.model_overrides or None)` so `{}`→SQL NULL (INV-3 row parity for legacy rows — never write `{}`) (→ 06-04-PLAN.md acceptance; 06-RESEARCH.md "D-08").
- **`ModelPolicy.max_tokens` stays doc-only** — the resolver threads NO runtime cap; cap remains `settings.MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` in `build_model` (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md key-decisions; SPEC constraint).
- **SmartPlanner left on the session `model_id`** (engine.py ~1432) — it has no `agent_id` and builds its own client; routing it through the resolver risks an INV-3 break. Do NOT rewire it (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md key-decisions; 06-RESEARCH.md "D-01 SmartPlanner note").
- **AGENT.md `model` validation is RESOLVE-time, not load-time:** the loader enforces only a string-or-null type guard (no catalog import); catalog-membership validation fires in `ModelResolver.resolve` — and (per WR-01 fix) ONLY when tier-3 is the selected tier (→ 06-02-SUMMARY.md key-decisions; 06-REVIEW.md WR-01).
- **Scope:** `model_overrides` threaded into the main `run_pipeline` execute ONLY, not the revision path (`_handle_revision`) — per RESEARCH Open Q2 (→ 06-04-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made").

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **`_resolve_model` resolver-or-fallback helper (live survivor):** direct unit-style `_run_agent`/`_run_build_task_loop` invocations build an `ExecutionContext` WITHOUT going through `execute()`, so `model_resolver is None`. `_resolve_model` returns the threaded `model_id` in that case (parity-safe). Do NOT assume the resolver is always present in `_run_agent` (→ 06-03-SUMMARY.md "Deviations from Plan"). The resolver-absent path runs exactly one attempt — no fallback (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md key-decisions).
- **Chain must be armed on the resolved id STRING, not `ctx.model`:** 06-05 captures `_resolved_model_id` BEFORE `create_runner` (because `create_runner` may replace `ctx.model` with a model OBJECT); arming on the object yields an empty chain. A future change to the fallback loop must preserve this ordering (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md "Deviations" #1).
- **Chain exhaustion does NOT raise out of `_run_agent`** — the last throttle escapes into the engine's existing recoverable `agent_error` path (visible WS event, no silent blank), matching the engine's fault-degradation contract. The "no live Bedrock / re-raise" wording in earlier plan text means "surfaced, not swallowed", not "raised exception" (→ 06-05-SUMMARY.md "Deviations" #2 + key-decisions).
- **CR-02 live-checkpointer fallback restart — deferred item now CLOSED.** The `{base}:retry{n}` fresh-thread fix was proven offline (InMemory, 4/4); live confirmation against a Postgres checkpointer + Bedrock throttle was deferred, then **`06-UAT.md` status flipped to `complete` — closed by the 2026-06-12 milestone-end live re-pass**. The item is also carried in **Phase 22's consolidated LIVE-01 sweep** (re-confirm consolidated to the milestone-end live pass) (→ 06-UAT.md frontmatter; Phase 22 §6 / 22-LIVE-01-EVIDENCE.md). No outstanding item remains for the phase.
- **Pre-existing environmental test failures (NOT this phase):** ~7–9 failures in `tests/unit/test_logout.py` (HTTP 403 "Self-registration is disabled") and `tests/unit/test_pipeline_cancel.py` (expired AWS SSO token / `ExpiredTokenException`) recur across all 5 plans. Out of scope, not fixed (→ deferred-items.md). A future agent should ignore these when assessing regressions.
- **Carryover fixed mid-phase:** 06-01 registering `model_catalog` bumped `_KNOWN` to 15, breaking `test_registry_capabilities.py::test_registered_count_is_exactly_fourteen` (was a hard-coded `== 14`); the assertion was bumped to 15 and the registry test now passes (→ deferred-items.md "06-03"; 06-VERIFICATION.md truth #10).
- **`context_window` numerics are `[ASSUMED]` metadata** (200000 default, 1000000 for Sonnet 4.6) — they do not drive resolution/fallback (which key off `id`/`cost_class`). Confirm if ever surfaced numerically (→ 06-01-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made"; 06-RESEARCH.md A1).
- **`max_tokens` doc-only survivor:** `ModelPolicy.max_tokens` exists but is intentionally never threaded into the runtime cap. Do not wire it to `build_model` (→ §5).

## Phase 07 — Prototype as Manifest — Parity Proof (SC-001) [2]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/07-prototype-as-manifest-parity-proof-sc-001-2/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-09)  ·  **Plans:** 11/11  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 2
**Requirements delivered:** PARITY-01, PARITY-02, PARITY-03, PARITY-04, PARITY-05, PARITY-06, PARITY-07, PARITY-08, PARITY-09 (the 9 PARITY-* reqs; the SPEC's "11 locked" = these 9 + the 2 belt-and-suspenders re-verifications of L14/L15/L16 closure). → 07-SPEC.md (Requirements) · 07-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage)
**One-line outcome:** Made the 9 Phase-4-registered-by-name-only capability families LIVE behind their ports, routed the engine through them off the compiled manifest, then DELETED kernel leaks L1–L13 + the `agents/prototype/` package — so the kernel knows no workflow by name (SC-001/INV-1, ratchet-locked).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Re-express `prototype`/`od_prototype`/`prototype_revision` (and keep `ppt`/code-gen at parity) with ALL capability behavior sourced from the compiled manifest — `single_shot`/`task_loop` strategies, `single_file`/`serialized_sandbox`/`streamed_text`/`ppt` resolvers, `opendesign`/`previous_run` providers, `heading_tasks` parser, `html_skeleton` compaction — then delete the hardcoded kernel leaks L1–L13 so `grep` for `if pipeline_type`/`spec.id ==` and the L1–L13 patterns returns 0 in `agents/execution_engine/`, while every pipeline holds deliverable byte + semantic-event parity vs the post-0C baseline. This is the SC-001 core-value proof. → 07-SPEC.md (Goal) · 07-CONTEXT.md (Phase Boundary).
Success = the 9-truth verification table (kernel name-free dispatch, both strategies behind the port, four resolvers, opendesign byte-identical to the acd1636 oracle, all 5 pipelines at parity, L1–L13 deleted, ledger flipped + banned-pattern hard-fail, and a NON-prototype `task_loop` workflow runs from manifest+AGENT.md with zero engine edits). → 07-VERIFICATION.md (Observable Truths).
**Depends on:** Phase 04 (CapabilityRegistry + `_KNOWN` + the 6 Protocol ports in `base.py`; authored manifests), Phase 05 (`ExecutionContext` object-typed per-run fields + `ScopedStore.assert_owns`), Phase 06 (`model_resolver` object-typed precedent), Phase 03 (0C `_extract_html_skeleton` + the ≥50% reduction gate). → 07-01-SUMMARY.md (Dependency graph).

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
**New registered capability impls (`kind:name`)** — all bound via `registry.install()`/`_register_builtins()` into the `_IMPLS` map (D-02 seam). → 07-01/02/03/10-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments):
- `strategy:single_shot` (`SingleShotStrategy`), `strategy:task_loop` (`TaskLoopStrategy`)
- `deliverable:single_file`, `deliverable:serialized_sandbox`, `deliverable:streamed_text`, `deliverable:ppt`
- `context_provider:opendesign` (`OpenDesignProvider`), `context_provider:previous_run` (`PreviousRunProvider`)
- `task_parser:heading_tasks` (`HeadingTasksParser`)
- `compaction:html_skeleton` (`HtmlSkeletonCompaction`) — duck-typed `compact(html)->str`, NO Protocol port (intentional; a port would be a `base.py` edit, Phase 8). → 07-03-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made)
- `post_step:revision_validation` — NEW capability KIND added this phase (07-10); the only NEW `_KNOWN` membership entry. → 07-10-SUMMARY.md
**New ports/Protocols:** `PostStep` Protocol added to `capabilities/base.py` (07-10). The other 6 ports pre-existed (Phase 4).
**Resolution seam (D-02):** `CapabilityRegistry.resolve(kind,name)` (static dict lookup over `_IMPLS`; checks `_KNOWN` first, raises on unknown/unbound — NOT `getattr`/`eval`/`importlib`), `install()`/`_register_builtins()` (explicit local-import binding, lazy on first call; NO `@register`/`discover()` — that is Phase 8). `is_registered`/`_KNOWN`/`resolve_alias` untouched. → 07-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
**Runner handle (D-03):** `ExecutionContext.runner` object-typed field; concrete `KernelServices` (`execution_engine/kernel_services.py`) — the ONLY kernel module capabilities reach `_run_agent`/sandbox/`static_check`/`render_check`/typed reads/OD reads/parent-run read/`run_validation_fix_loop`/`persist_task_html` through. KernelServices DELEGATES to the engine's existing `_run_agent` (which wraps `create_deep_agent` via `langchain_deepagents` — INV-13, no hand-rolled loop). → 07-04-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
**Manifest/compiled-model fields made live or added:** `strategy`, `task_source.parser`, `deliverable.strategy`/`deliverable.name`, `compaction`, `context_providers`, `clarify.mode`, `seed_files` (Phase-4 inert fields, now consumed); NEW declared: `DeliverableSpec.revises_existing`, `Step.post_step`, `TaskSource.source_step`/`spec_step` (07-10/07-11). → 07-10/07-11-SUMMARY.md.
**Migrations:** NONE — Phase 7 is additive engine-internal + capability code only, no DB table / no Alembic revision (per SPEC Out-of-scope). → 07-SPEC.md (Boundaries).
**API endpoints / event types:** NONE new — the WS event vocabulary is byte/multiset-preserved (strategies re-yield the engine's existing event dicts). → 07-PATTERNS.md (Yield-the-engine's-event-dicts).
**Registry counter (_KNOWN):** entered at 15 (kind,name) pairs (Phase 4 + Phase 6 model_catalog); the strategies/resolvers/providers/parser/compaction names were ALREADY in `_KNOWN` (only their impls were added to `_IMPLS`). `post_step:revision_validation` (07-10) is the one NEW `_KNOWN` entry → 15→16; the drift guard `test_registry_capabilities` was updated 15→16. → 07-10-SUMMARY.md (Deviations, auto-fix 1).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01:** §32 capability subpackage tree under `agents/capabilities/{strategies,deliverables,context_providers,task_parsers,compaction,post_steps}/`; one impl per module; `name` attr == registry key == manifest reference (snake_case). → 07-CONTEXT.md (Area A).
- **D-02:** Resolution seam EXTENDS `CapabilityRegistry` (separate `_IMPLS` map + `resolve()`); explicit `install()` registration, module-level singleton (capabilities stateless, per-run state on `ExecutionContext`). Evolution to `@register`/`discover()`/trust is Phase 8 — do NOT add decorators here. → 07-CONTEXT.md (Area B).
- **D-03:** `ExecutionStrategy.run(step,ctx)` OWNS the step loop; the kernel run-primitive/sandbox/validators are reached ONLY via the object-typed `ctx.runner` handle. Capabilities MUST NOT import `agents.execution_engine.*` or `app.*` (import-linter). `ctx`/`step` typed `Any`/`object`; capabilities MAY import `agents.workflows.plan`. → 07-CONTEXT.md (Area C) · 07-PATTERNS.md (Shared Patterns).
- **D-04:** opendesign re-home is move-don't-copy; the 3 loaders live in `od_context.py` (a kernel-package boundary module that legitimately imports `app.services.od_loader`) — NOT inside the capability (that would break import-linter). The provider composes blocks FROM the boundary `od_context` dict + handle (Assumption A6). → 07-02-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).

- **INV-1 is a KERNEL property** — legitimate non-kernel `pipeline_type`/name refs (registry `REVISION_BASE_MAP`/`PIPELINE_AGENTS`, `app/core/entitlements.py`, `app/api/runs.py`/`websocket.py` display, tests, the move-don't-copy capability homes) are RETAINED; the grep gates are kernel-scoped. → 07-SPEC.md (Req 9, Out of scope).
- **Revision behavior keys off the DECLARED `deliverable.revises_existing` flag** (NOT the `"previous_run" in context_providers` proxy, NOT the workflow name); the CompiledWorkflow is compiled at run ENTRY so the flag is available to per-run setup. → 07-10-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).
- **WR-04 fix (locked):** `previous_run.assert_owns` now FAILS CLOSED on any non-`PermissionError` store error (returns `{}`, no seed); `PermissionError` still propagates (L16, never swallowed). → 07-VERIFICATION.md (WR-04 Fix Verification).
- **D1 VOIDED** (`_handle_revision`/`run_revision` is a LIVE PPT-revision handler, not dead code — not touched). → 07-SPEC.md (Background, Out of scope).

## Phase 08 — Capabilities Hardened: Registry, Gates, Tool Perms, Runtime [3]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/08-capabilities-hardened-registry-gates-tool-perms-runtime-3/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-09)  ·  **Plans:** 8/8  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 3
**Requirements delivered:** CAP-01, CAP-02, CAP-03, GATE-01, GATE-02, GATE-03, TOOLPERM-01, TOOLPERM-02, TOOLPERM-03, VALID-01, VALID-02, VALID-03, VALID-04, VALID-05, AGENTRT-01, AGENTRT-02, AGENTRT-03, AGENTRT-04, AGENTRT-05, AGENTRT-06, SKILL-01, HOOK-01, HOOK-02, HOOK-03, HOOK-04, OBS-02, API-02, API-03, API-06 (29 IDs / 16 locked requirements) → 08-SPEC.md (Requirements), 08-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage).
**One-line outcome:** The factory-side analog of Phase 7 — turned the half-formed capability layer into a self-registering trust-flagged `CapabilityRegistry` with `tool`/`skill`/`hook`/`runtime` kinds, first-class gates, least-privilege tool permissions, a validator registry + generic fix-loop, executable hooks + OTel, and a `/api/capabilities` palette, while DELETING all five factory leaks F1–F5 and fixing the R12 constitution no-op at strict INV-3 parity.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Harden the Phase-7 capability seam: replace explicit `install()`/`_register_builtins()` with `@register`/`discover()` self-registration + per-owner `user_allowed` trust flags; make `human`/`validation`/`approval`/`security` first-class registry-driven `GateHandler`s (with a real `Validation_Gate`); enforce least-privilege `ToolPermissions` (intersection; AGENT.md may only lower); stand up a `Validator` registry + generic `FixPolicy` fix-loop + P0–P3 severity (migrate `html_static`/`html_render`, land Tier#4/5/6); lift the factory's hardcoded prompt-order/tool-switch/skills-hooks/constitution/runtime (F1–F5) behind `PromptAssemblyPolicy` + provider registries + `AgentRuntimeAdapter` and DELETE them; add executable hooks + persistence. All at strict INV-3 parity → 08-SPEC.md (Goal), 08-CONTEXT.md (Phase Boundary). **Must be TRUE:** registry self-registering with no central if/elif; trust flag enforced under user/db manifests; four gate kinds resolve at the step boundary; effective perms = intersection; F1–F5 ledger rows all ☑ + the 5 characterization snapshots byte/event-identical (no re-baseline) → 08-SPEC.md (Acceptance Criteria), ROADMAP Phase 8 (4 SCs). **Depends-on:** Phase 7 (the explicit `install()`/`resolve()` seam + `KernelServices` handle + base.py ports), Phase 5 (`ScopedStore` + additive-migration pattern), Phase 4 (compiler INV-4 path + inert `ToolPermissions`/`Step` fields).

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **New registered capabilities (`kind:name`):** `gate:human`, `gate:validation`, `gate:approval`, `gate:security` (→ 08-02-SUMMARY Accomplishments); `tool:workspace`, `tool:prototype`, `tool:prototype_emit_only`, `tool:planning` (→ 08-03-SUMMARY); `validator:html_static`, `validator:html_render`, `validator:design_quality` (app-side), `validator:spec_plan_coverage`, `validator:task_done_when` (kernel-side) (→ 08-04-SUMMARY); `runtime:langchain_deepagents`, `prompt:default`, `skill:ui|disk|template|repo`, `hook:behavioral` (→ 08-05-SUMMARY); `hook:secret_scan`, `hook:otel_tracing` (→ 08-07-SUMMARY).
- **New ports/Protocols (base.py, 08-01):** `PromptAssemblyPolicy`, `AgentRuntimeAdapter`, `HookHandler`, `ToolProvider`, `SkillProvider`, `HookProvider` — one-method `@runtime_checkable`, stdlib-typing-only (→ 08-01-SUMMARY).
- **Registry machinery (registry.py, 08-01):** `register(kind, name, *, user_allowed=False)` decorator binding `(kind,name)→impl` at import; `discover()` explicit-subpackage importer (incl. `app/agents/validators/`), guarded by `_DISCOVERED`, never at compiler import; `_TRUST` map + `is_user_allowed`; new `tool`/`skill`/`hook`/`runtime` KIND strings (free-string-keyed `_KNOWN`, no central if/elif).
- **Key classes/dataclasses:** `DeliverableContext` + `FixPolicy` (kernel-side, reached via handle factories `runner.deliverable_context`/`runner.make_fix_policy`) (→ 08-04-SUMMARY); `GateOutcome` (→ 08-02); `ExecutionPolicy` default-deny seam + `intersect_permissions`/`_and_mask`/`ToolPermissions.lowered_by` in plan.py (→ 08-03); `map_severity` single canonical P0→CRITICAL/…/P3→LOW in validators/severity.py (VALID-03 single source, the ONLY definition in the tree) (→ 08-01).
- **Alembic migration 0016 (`0016_capability_hardening_tables.py`, down_revision "0015"):** additive, adds `validation_results` (run_id, step, validator, severity, attempt, issues), `gate_events` (run_id, step, gate, outcome, detail), `hook_runs` (run_id, hook, event, outcome, detail) — each carries `owner_id` + `workspace_id` NOT NULL, §18 indexes, written via Phase-5 `ScopedStore` (`record_gate_event`/`record_validation_result`/`record_hook_run` on authz.py) (→ 08-02-SUMMARY, D-10).
- **New API endpoint:** `GET /api/capabilities` (auth via `Depends(get_current_user)`, registry-reflective palette: kind/name/`user_allowed`/`config_schema`={} + expanded `model_catalog`) (→ 08-08-SUMMARY).
- **New event types (additive, flow through the generic websocket.py forward — NO websocket.py edit):** `validator_result`, `validation_warning`, `gate_*` (→ 08-08-SUMMARY, API-03).
- **New external dependency:** `opentelemetry-api==1.42.1` + `opentelemetry-sdk==1.42.1` (the ONLY runtime dep added across the whole phase; OTLP exporter optional/lazy) — human-verified at a blocking checkpoint (→ 08-07-SUMMARY Decisions, 08-SECURITY AR-08-01).
- **Registry counter movement (`_KNOWN`, drift-guard count in test_registry_capabilities.py):** 16 → 18 (08-02 gates approval/security) → 22 (08-03 four tool sets) → 25 (08-04 three Tier validators) → 32 (08-05 runtime/prompt/skill/hook pairs) → 34 (08-07 secret_scan/otel_tracing). Each bump was a lockstep `_EXPECTED_NAMES` + count edit, NOT a snapshot re-baseline.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
- **`install()` + `_register_builtins` alias** (registry.py) DELETED in 08-01 — `discover()` is the single successor; the 11 Phase-7 built-in impl classes now self-register via `@register` decorators. `grep "def install|_register_builtins"` → 0; `test_install_is_deleted` enforces absence (→ 08-01-SUMMARY, 08-VERIFICATION Truth 3).
- **F2 — `factory._build_runner_tools`** (closed name→toolset switch) DELETED in 08-03; replaced by `_resolve_runner_tools` + `tool_provider` registry; `grep "_build_runner_tools"` → 0 backend-wide; F2 ledger row ☑ (→ 08-03-SUMMARY).
- **F1 — `blocks.append` inline prompt order** DELETED in 08-05; `PromptAssemblyPolicy.assemble` (order `injects→guardrails→skills→hooks→constitution→prompt_body`, `"\n\n"` join) drives it; `grep "blocks\.append"` → 0 backend-wide; F1 ledger row ☑ (→ 08-05-SUMMARY).
- **F3 — inline `_inject_skills`/`_inject_hooks`** DELETED in 08-05; replaced by `skill_provider` (versioned) + behavioral `hook_provider`; `grep "_inject_skills|_inject_hooks"` → 0; F3 ledger row ☑. The legacy prompt-only hook SURVIVES as `hook:behavioral` (non-executable, still renders `## Active Behavioral Hooks`) (→ 08-05-SUMMARY).
- **F4/R12 — the `_inject_constitution` `_mem`-only event-loop-running branch** (plus the dead `loop.run_until_complete` sync-context path) DELETED in 08-06; single sync-safe pre-warmed read remains; `grep "_mem.get"` → 0; F4 ledger row ☑ (→ 08-06-SUMMARY).
- **F5 — hardcoded `create_deep_agent`** confined to the allow-listed `app/agents/deep_agent_runner.py` in 08-05; the `AgentRuntimeAdapter` WRAPS (never calls) it via a `RuntimeBuildContext` build seam; F5 ledger row ☑. After 08-06, ALL F1–F5 rows are ☑.
- **Gates armed (permanent ratchets):** the F1/F3 `grep`, F2 `_build_runner_tools` `grep`, F4 `_mem.get` `grep`, F5 CHECK + INV-13 banned-pattern allow-list (`_ALLOWED_CREATE_DEEP_AGENT`), migration-ledger ratchet (`test_migration_ledger.py`), import-linter 3 contracts (`agents.capabilities ↛ {execution_engine, app}`). NOTE: the sanctioned `langchain_deepagents` capability module is allow-listed in the local-module ban (`_ALLOWED_LOCAL_MODULE_PATHS`) — it is import-clean of the library + `create_deep_agent` + any agent loop.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01/D-02 (CAP-01/02/03):** `@register`/`discover()` replaces `install()` (no dual-impl); `_KNOWN` stays the declared impl-free literal allow-list (`@register` adds to it) so the compiler's membership path stays impl-free; `discover()` runs at engine import / first `execute()`, NEVER at compiler import; trust threaded as a `compile(trust='file')` kwarg defaulting to file/trusted so the single engine call-site keeps Phase-4/7 parity with zero edits → 08-CONTEXT.md (Area A/B), 08-01-SUMMARY (Decisions Made).
- **Trust posture:** privileged caps (`gate:security`, `gate:approval`, `runtime:langchain_deepagents`) register `user_allowed=False`; validators/gates `human`/`validation`, the four tool sets, prompt/skill providers, `hook:secret_scan`/`hook:otel_tracing` register `user_allowed=True`. A user/db manifest referencing a `user_allowed=False` cap → `CompilerError` naming `(kind,name)`; `exec`/`secrets`/`spawn_subagents` collapse OFF via `intersect_permissions` (AND-mask, can only LOWER) → 08-SECURITY (T-08-01-EoP), 08-03-SUMMARY (Decisions).
- **D-06 LOCKED — `revision_validation` STAYS a post_step** (NOT converted to a `gates:[validation]` step): the generic validation gate does not replicate the revision-baselined fix sub-agent loop; converting would break byte/event parity (decided with snapshot evidence). Future agents must not "finish" this conversion → 08-04-SUMMARY (Decisions), 08-CONTEXT.md D-06.
- **D-06 — the fix-loop is the engine's SINGLE `_run_validation_fix_loop`**, made config-driven via `FixPolicy` (deliverable name + `max_attempts=2` default reproducing Phase-7 byte-for-byte). Do NOT build a new loop and do NOT move validation into the gate; `task_loop` owns inline validation (runs registered validators additively + event-free after the byte-identical loop) → 08-04-SUMMARY, 08-CONTEXT.md D-06.
- **D-04 backbone (hexagonal):** heavy-dep validators live app-side (`app/agents/validators/`), import the kernel PORT + `@register` (legal `app→capabilities` direction), and reach `static_check`/`render_check` + audit writers ONLY via the `KernelServices` handle (`target.runner.…`). The kernel NEVER imports `app.*`; `tool_provider` impls emit string KEYS (not concrete tools) and the factory (composition root) resolves them. Strategies reach kernel-built objects via handle factories, never importing the kernel → 08-04-SUMMARY, 08-03-SUMMARY (Decisions), import-linter.
- **INV-3 strict (locked this phase):** the 5 characterization snapshots (prototype / app_builder / od_ppt / od_prototype / prototype_revision) stay byte/event-identical, NO re-baseline; new validator/gate/hook events are additive only; every F-leak deletion is parity-gated (prove routed path identical BEFORE deleting) → 08-SPEC.md (Constraints), every SUMMARY.
- **F4/R12 parity trap (do not be confused by it):** fixing the constitution no-op is a production behavior change, but the characterization fixtures set NO Constitution (A4 verified by grep), so snapshots stay byte-identical; `test_constitution_prod.py` is a NEW additive test → 08-06-SUMMARY (Parity Trap Verified).
- **Q37 boundary:** only the R12 *bug fix* lands; prompt-content changes (constitution consolidation, clarify write-back, recommended-defaults) are a separate later PR → 08-CONTEXT.md, 08-SPEC.md (Out of scope).

Core security model judged sound (no trust bypass, intersection AND-only, security gate fail-closed, no IDOR, auth enforced). The 4 functional findings ALL remediated 2026-06-10: **CR-01** (secret_scan never fired — no `before_write` caller) + **WR-03** (otel_tracing fired globally on every step) fixed by DECLARATION-DRIVEN hook firing via new `Step.hooks` + a real `before_write` seam in `_run_agent` (`a9f6038`); **WR-01** (validation gate passed raw `ExecutionContext` instead of a `DeliverableContext`) fixed via `ctx.runner.deliverable_context(...)` (`6ebc7d9`); **WR-02/IN-03** (unguarded otel import could de-register secret_scan; `discover()` only caught `ModuleNotFoundError`) fixed by guarded otel import + reordered `hooks/__init__` (secret_scan first) + broadened `except ImportError` (`d8270fc`). **WR-04/WR-05** (overclaiming docstrings on `ExecutionPolicy.check` / `ToolPermissions.lowered_by` / factory grant-binding) downgraded to honest forward-surface wording (`c4990a9`). IN-01/IN-02/IN-04 documented only, no code change → 08-REVIEW.md, 08-07-SUMMARY (Post-Review Remediation).

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **Live survivors (look deletable, are NOT):** `hook:behavioral` non-executable sub-type still renders `## Active Behavioral Hooks` (F3 kept this on purpose); the `revision_validation` post_step is LIVE and deliberately NOT a gate (D-06); the Phase-7 `human` gate path (`_should_gate` → `_run_review_gate` + `review_gate_*`) is unchanged — the registered `gate:human` DELEGATES to it (no second HITL mechanism). Do not "consolidate" any of these.
- **Forward-surface helpers NOT wired this phase (Phase-9 carry-forward, honestly documented after WR-04/WR-05):** `ExecutionPolicy.check`, `ToolPermissions.lowered_by`, and the factory grant-gating in `_resolve_runner_tools` have NO production caller this phase — real exec/secrets denial comes from the compiler's `intersect_permissions` + the `security` gate (both ACTIVE). When the first privileged tool set / `LocalSandboxRuntime` lands (Phase 9), these call sites MUST be wired and re-audited → 08-SECURITY.md (Auditor Notes), 08-REVIEW.md WR-04/WR-05.
- **Hook firing is DECLARATION-DRIVEN (post-CR-01):** only `step.hooks` are resolved/fired. A legacy step (prototype/od_/ppt/code-gen) declares no hooks → fires NOTHING (this is what keeps snapshots identical AND fixes the global-otel regression). `otel_tracing` is NOT a global default — do not re-wire it to fire on every step. `pre_commit`/`on_validation`/`before_after_merge` are accepted bind targets but their producers are later-phase (git=P9, exec=P10).
- **secret_scan is NOT complete DLP (IN-04):** documented known false-negatives (JWTs, unquoted/opaque long tokens, GCP/Azure connection strings). Do not rely on it as a full DLP control.
- **Registry test-fixture pitfall (D-12 fold + Issues):** import-side-effect `@register` binding is one-shot per process — any registry save/restore test fixture MUST call `discover()` BEFORE snapshotting `_IMPLS`, else restore permanently drops built-ins. Grep-gate literals (`install`/`pkgutil`/`blocks.append`/`_build_runner_tools`) in docstring prose trip the whole-backend ratchets — keep prose off the bare tokens → 08-01-SUMMARY (Issues), 08-03-SUMMARY (Issues).
- **`config_schema` is an intentional empty `{}` stub** in `/api/capabilities` (ports declare no per-cap schema in Phase 8) — the documented stable slot for a future per-capability JSON schema. Not a bug → 08-08-SUMMARY (Known Stubs), 08-VERIFICATION (Anti-Patterns note).
- **Deferred / out-of-scope (do not treat as Phase-8 gaps):** `tests/agents/test_capability_resolution.py` fails at COLLECTION (imports the deleted `install` symbol — pre-existing, needs migration to `discover()`); `tests/unit/test_logout.py` (403 self-registration) + `tests/unit/test_pipeline_cancel.py` (CancelledError) fail identically on the pre-fix tree (auth/cancel subsystems, untouched) → deferred-items.md. Live checks deferred to end-of-milestone live pass: API-06 panel render (developer-approved at the 08-08 checkpoint) + real OTLP span export (logging/console fully functional now) → 08-VERIFICATION.md (Human Verification), VALIDATION.md (Manual-Only).

## Phase 09 — Local Workspace Runtime + Repo Workflows (no exec) [4A]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/09-local-workspace-runtime-repo-workflows-no-exec-4a/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-10)  ·  **Plans:** 6/6  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 4A
**Requirements delivered:** RUNTIME-01, RUNTIME-02, RUNTIME-03, REPO-01, REPO-02, REPO-03, REPO-04, REPO-05, MCP-01, MCP-02, MCP-03, MCP-04, INTEG-01, INTEG-02 (14 locked) → 09-SPEC.md (Requirements); verified all SATISFIED → 09-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage)
**One-line outcome:** Introduced the workflow-agnostic `RuntimeEnvironment`/`Workspace` port layer (`LocalSandboxRuntime`) and shipped the first net-new brownfield repo workflow class end-to-end offline (clone→branch→inventory→read/edit→diff, exec OFF), plus the net-new MCP **client** + catalog + integration-provider bridge — all additive, prototype byte/event-parity held, zero engine edits to run the new workflow (SC-001).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
First net-new workflow class (brownfield repo work) and first use of the `Workspace`/`RuntimeEnvironment` abstraction the plan reserved as a forward seam. A file-backed sample repo workflow runs locally with no execution; a live MCP **client** (`langchain-mcp-adapters`) and GitHub/GitLab/Jira/Slack integration providers become reachable from the unified `create_runner` path; prototype's 5-pipeline characterization stays byte/event-identical → 09-SPEC.md (Goal / Background).
- Must be TRUE (16 acceptance criteria → 09-SPEC.md "Acceptance Criteria"): 4 runtime/workspace ports kernel-side (import-linter 3→4 kept/0 broken); `LocalSandboxRuntime` clone/branch/read/write/search/diff with `exec_command` DENIED; `RunSandbox` runs as `Workspace(has_git=False, exec=off)` with 5-pipeline byte/event parity and no `if repo:` engine fork; migration 0017 adds `repositories`; `RepoInventory`/`RepoIndex`(tree-sitter)/`ContextPack`/`repo_diff` capabilities; sample brownfield workflow with exec=off everywhere; `McpClientAdapter` binds stub tools into deepagents; catalog with scoped per-owner creds; compiler `server.tool` validation; `security`+`secrets` gating; integration providers from `create_runner`; `integrations` scopes default none recorded in `run_capabilities`.
- Ambiguity 0.16 (gate ≤0.20) → 09-SPEC.md (Ambiguity Report). Depends-on: Phase 8 (capability registry/gates/tool-perms/`ExecutionPolicy` enforcement point), Phase 7 (`KernelServices` handle / deliverable-resolver seam), Phase 5 (`ScopedStore`/additive migrations/`UserGithubCredential` PAT precedent) → 09-CONTEXT.md (Canonical References / Prior phase context).

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **New registered capabilities** (`kind:name`, all via `@register`/`discover()`): `runtime_env:local` (→ 09-01-SUMMARY.md Accomplishments); `repo_inventory:default`, `repo_index:tree_sitter`, `context_pack:default`, `context_provider:repo` (→ 09-03-SUMMARY.md Accomplishments); `deliverable:repo_diff` (→ 09-04-SUMMARY.md); `mcp_server:github|gitlab|jira|slack` (`user_allowed=True`, read-scoped) + `mcp_server:filesystem|postgres` (`user_allowed=False`, powerful) (→ 09-05-SUMMARY.md); `integration_provider:github|gitlab|jira|slack` (→ 09-06-SUMMARY.md). New free-string registry kinds: `runtime_env`, `repo_inventory`, `repo_index`, `context_pack`, `mcp_server`, `integration_provider`.
- **New ports/Protocols:** `RuntimeEnvironment`, `Workspace`, `ExecutionPolicy`, `IsolationProvider` (kernel-side `agents/runtime/base.py`, stdlib-typing-only, `@runtime_checkable`) → 09-01-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made); `IntegrationProvider` port added to `agents/capabilities/base.py` → 09-06-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments). Note `RepoIndex` is an OPTIONAL registered capability exposing `search()` (grep/glob default, always available) + `symbol_query()` (only after in-memory per-run `build()`); kernel-pure dataclasses `Match`/`SymbolHit` → 09-03-SUMMARY.md.
- **Alembic migration 0017** (`revision="0017"`, `down_revision="0016"`, additive, SQLite upgrade→downgrade→upgrade reversible): `repositories` (id, owner_id, workspace_id, provider [free String github/gitlab/local], url, default_branch, auth_ref [scoped-cred pointer], created_at) + `workspaces.repo_id` FK; `mcp_credentials` (owner/workspace-scoped, Fernet-encrypted PAT-style, added in 09-05) → 09-02-SUMMARY.md / 09-05-SUMMARY.md (Deviation 3). Every new table carries `owner_id`+`workspace_id` (Q3). `workspaces.kind` accepts `repo` with NO enum widening (already free String since 0014).
- **New models:** `Repository` (`app/models/repository.py`), `McpCredential` (`app/models/mcp_credential.py`); new `ScopedStore` methods `create_repository`/`get_repository`/`assert_repo_owned` + `write_mcp_credential`/`read_mcp_credential`/`assert_mcp_cred_owned` (all default-deny) → 09-02 / 09-05 SUMMARYs.
- **API:** no new endpoint — `GET /api/capabilities` surfaces the new catalog/providers reflectively (zero endpoint edit) → 09-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments). **Event types:** none new; `run_capabilities` row now records active integration scopes + MCP servers + runtime per run (CAPRUN-01) → 09-06-SUMMARY.md.
- **New deps (behind ports, pinned in `requirements.txt` not pyproject.toml):** `tree-sitter==0.25.2` + per-language grammar wheels `tree-sitter-python`/`-javascript`/`-typescript` (NOT `tree-sitter-language-pack` — see §7); `langchain-mcp-adapters>=0.2.2,<0.3` (resolved 0.2.2) + transitive `mcp==1.27.2` + pinned-down `sse-starlette==3.0.2`.
- **Registry counter (`_KNOWN`):** _KNOWN 34→50 across the phase (34→35 in 09-01; 35→39 in 09-03; 39→40 in 09-04; 40→46 in 09-05; 46→50 in 09-06). Drift guard + `_EXPECTED_NAMES` in `test_registry_capabilities.py` bumped in lockstep → each NN-SUMMARY (patterns-established).

Tracked by ledger row R1 as a **CHECK row (prose), not a grep row** — the consumed surface survives byte-identical so a bare grep would false-fire; the gate is the 5-pipeline byte/event parity + persistence test → 09-02-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).

See §7 — a future agent must NOT delete these as "dead handoff code."

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01 (provisioner + facade split):** `RuntimeEnvironment` provisions (`create_workspace`/`teardown`); `Workspace` is the fs+git+exec+`ExecutionPolicy` facade carrying `owner_id`/`workspace_id` + back-ref to its runtime. This is the ECS-swap seam (§27) — a later `EcsRuntime.create_workspace()` returns a remote-backed `Workspace` with ZERO engine change → 09-CONTEXT.md (Area A). Ports kernel-side; `LocalSandboxRuntime` app-side (touches `app.core.config.settings.RUNS_ROOT` + `app/agents/sandbox.py` disk-safety), reached via the `ctx.runner` handle, mirroring the Phase-8 Validator split → 09-RESEARCH.md (R-A).
- **D-02 (RunSandbox refold in-place):** name survives as a thin facade; call sites untouched (parity-safest). `LocalWorkspace` is the **single git-subprocess owner**; `RunSandbox` keeps the disk primitives (`root`/`path_for`) and delegates `read`/`write`/`cleanup` (wrapping a separate RunSandbox would be circular). `serialize_sandbox_deliverable` keeps its **raw-bytes** read (NOT `read_text`) for CRLF byte-equivalence — load-bearing → 09-02-SUMMARY.md / 09-PATTERNS.md (RunSandbox refold).
- **D-04 (RepoIndex):** optional, in-memory per-run, built only on `RepoSpec.index` opt-in OR `file_count > REPO_INDEX_FILE_THRESHOLD` (N6 = 2000); **grep/glob is the default search**; no new table; embeddings OUT. The build DECISION lives in the capability (`should_build_index`); the compiler only RECORDS the flag (INV-5) → 09-03-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).
- **D-05/D-08 (ONE external-tool mechanism = MCP):** `integration_provider` is a thin DATA bridge mapping a granted `integrations` scope onto a catalog `mcp_server`'s tools via `resolve_integration_scopes()` → the EXACT `mcp_server_configs`/`mcp_exposed_tools` the MCP prewarm consumes. **NO parallel SDK path** (no PyGithub/python-gitlab/slack_sdk) — gated by grep → 09-06-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions). A future agent adding an integration must bridge MCP, not import a vendor SDK.
- **D-07 (async→sync binding):** MCP tools are bound by awaiting `McpClientAdapter.get_tools()` ONCE at the async engine run-entry (mirrors `prewarmed_constitution`), stashed on `AgentContext.prewarmed_mcp_tools`; the sync factory only reads+unions — **NO `asyncio.run`/`nest_asyncio`/`run_until_complete` in the sync factory** (double-loop hazard, gated by grep → 0) → 09-05-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions). MCP tools AUGMENT `create_deep_agent`, never replace it (INV-13).
- **Locked invariants this phase enforced:** INV-1 (kernel knows no workflow by name — branches default `main`/`work`, never a workflow-name branch; sample workflow runs with zero engine edit, SC-001); INV-3 (5-pipeline byte/event parity, `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` UNSET, no re-baseline); INV-5 (compiler records the index flag + scopes, control flow lives in capabilities); INV-8 (default-deny ownership — `ScopedStore`, cross-owner `PermissionError`); INV-9 (exec/network/secrets default OFF, mcp/integrations default none); INV-12 (move-don't-copy deletions); INV-13 (all agents on `deepagents`; tree-sitter/mcp-adapters are libraries, not runtimes — banned-pattern green) → 09-SPEC.md (Constraints) / 09-SECURITY.md.
- **No-exec invariant (this phase):** the whole repo path runs exec OFF; `exec_command` exists in the port but `ExecutionPolicy` denies it (`PermissionError`) until N3/Phase 4B. RepoSpec/repo binding is injected at RUN ENTRY by the host (the §15 seam), NOT a manifest top-key (manifests are pure data, INV-5) → 09-04-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward

## Phase 10 — Safe Local Exec (gated on N3) [4B]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/10-safe-local-exec-gated-on-n3-4b/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-10)  ·  **Plans:** 5/5  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 4B
**Requirements delivered:** EXEC-01 (constrained exec behind security gate + ExecutionPolicy: allow/deny, caps, ephemeral creds, default-deny egress), EXEC-02 (compile/test/lint validators land + sample passes). Resolves open decision **N3** (local exec threat model) and closes the Phase 9 review trio IN-01/IN-02/IN-03. → 10-SPEC.md (Requirements), 10-CONTEXT.md (spec_lock).
**One-line outcome:** Code execution flips from unconditionally-denied (four OFF layers from Phase 9) to a constrained, audited, engineer-only argv subprocess profile — hardened `exec_command` (no-shell/scrubbed-env/rlimits/wall-clock-kill/64KB-truncation) behind a three-tier gate (compiler trust ceiling → security+approval gate → workspace policy), with three code validators proving the whole stack offline by manifest only (SC-001 held, zero engine edits).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

The phase enables a constrained `exec` profile only behind the `security` gate plus a first-exec-per-run human `approval` gate: argv-only subprocess, Python-toolchain allow-list (`python`/`python3`/`pytest`/`ruff`), scrubbed minimal env (the v1 "ephemeral creds" posture), POSIX rlimits + wall-clock kill, egress denied at policy+allow-list+env level — proven by compile/test/lint validators passing against a local Python fixture repo with zero engine edits. The SPEC itself **is the N3 decision record** (resolved by its locked decisions; STATE/PROJECT flip N3 to decided at phase end). → 10-SPEC.md (Goal). 9 requirements locked at ambiguity 0.14: EXEC-PROFILE, EXEC-POLICY, EGRESS-DENY, GRANT-PATH, GATES, AUDIT, VALIDATORS, VALIDATOR-DENY, DEBT+PARITY; 14 pass/fail acceptance criteria, all offline-runnable. → 10-SPEC.md (Requirements / Acceptance Criteria). **Depends on:** Phase 9 (runtime ports + `LocalWorkspace`/`LocalExecutionPolicy` + the `exec=False` deny default T-09-01-02) and Phase 8 (gate registry + validator framework + ScopedStore writer pattern). → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (Dependency graph).

Cap defaults are LOCKED (never manifest-tunable): `cpu_seconds=60`, `mem_mb=512`, wall-clock `120s`, output truncation 64KB/stream; **deny beats allow**. → 10-SPEC.md (Constraints).

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

- **New registered capabilities** (`kind:name`): `validator:code_compile`, `validator:code_test`, `validator:code_lint` — each `@register("validator", name, user_allowed=True)`, kernel-side, reach exec only via `target.runner.workspace.exec_command(argv)`. → 10-04-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
- **Registry counter:** `_KNOWN` **50 → 53**; lockstep drift-guard in `test_registry_capabilities.py` bumped to 53; three modules added to `registry.discover()`'s `_builtin_modules`. → 10-04-SUMMARY.md (Files Created/Modified).
- **Port signature change:** `Workspace.exec_command(command: str)` → `exec_command(argv: list[str])` (single form, no shell), in `agents/runtime/base.py` — port + impl + all callers moved together (no dual signature). → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (provides).
- **New runtime symbols** (`app/agents/runtime/local.py`): module consts `DEFAULT_EXEC_PROFILE` (allow-list `python`/`python3`/`pytest`/`ruff`; cpu=60/mem=512/wall=120) and `_SCRUBBED_ENV_KEYS` (`PATH`/`HOME`/`TMPDIR`); grown `LocalExecutionPolicy` fields `exec_allow`/`exec_deny`/`cpu_seconds`/`mem_mb`/`wall_seconds`; `LocalWorkspace._recorder`; `create_workspace(exec=, recorder=)` kwargs now live. → 10-01-PLAN.md (artifacts_this_phase_produces).

Reversible offline against SQLite. → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions) / 10-SPEC.md (Requirement 6).
- **New ScopedStore methods** (`agents/authz.py`): `record_exec_run` (default-deny writer, mirrors `record_hook_run`), `read_exec_runs` (default-deny scoped reader). → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (provides).
- **New KernelServices members**: `record_exec_run` (best-effort handle, degrades to `None` offline — audit must never abort the run), `read_gate_events` (best-effort, offline → `[]`), `workspace` attr (declared None in 10-01, bound at §15 host seam in 10-03); `run_human_gate` parameterized with optional `payload` kwarg. → 10-01-SUMMARY.md, 10-03-SUMMARY.md (provides).
- **New event types:** NONE net-new — the D-04 approval payload deliberately rides the existing `review_gate_ready.data.output` field (the generic WS forward, no frontend rebuild); gate evaluations write existing `gate_events` rows. → 10-03-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions, "payload supersedes output").
- **New API endpoints:** NONE — no HTTP surface added; HITL approve/reject rides the existing review-gate WS path (`_run_review_gate` UNCHANGED).

This dissolved the INV-12 dual policy surface; the single live exec-allow decision is now `LocalExecutionPolicy.allows` + the 10-01 pre-spawn allow/deny check. `ToolPermissions`/`intersect_permissions`/`_and_mask` left UNCHANGED. → 10-02-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made). **Do NOT resurrect `ExecutionPolicy.check` or `runtime_host` — they are gone by design.**
- **DELETED — IN-02 `shell=True` exec surface**: `subprocess.run(command, shell=True)` in `exec_command` replaced by argv-list `shell=False` (Popen + `os.killpg` after the WR-01 fix). The grep ratchet over `backend/app/agents/runtime/` + `backend/agents/` returns 0. → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
- **DELETED — superseded compiler collapse**: the unconditional `workflow_ceiling = ToolPermissions()` (which forced `exec=False` for EVERY manifest) is gone, replaced by the trust-conditional `ToolPermissions(exec=trusted)` ceiling. → 10-02-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **D-01 (acquisition):** an exec-granting step MUST declare `gates: [security, approval]` in its manifest; the compiler raises `CompilerError` if `tools.exec` is granted without BOTH gates. NO auto-injection — the compiled plan mirrors the authored manifest (INV-5). The SecurityGate double-checks `approval` is declared (cheap defense-in-depth). → 10-CONTEXT.md (Decisions, D-01).
- **D-02 (pause/resume):** the `approval` gate delegates to the ONE durable HITL mechanism (`ctx.runner.run_human_gate` → engine `_run_review_gate`, the human-gate precedent). NO sibling `run_approval_gate` method, NO second resume surface. `_run_review_gate` stays UNCHANGED. → 10-CONTEXT.md (D-02), 10-03-SUMMARY.md (patterns-established).
- **D-03 (first-exec memory):** subsequent exec steps in the same run don't re-prompt — a prior `gate="approval"`/`outcome=pass` `gate_events` row (read via `read_gate_events`) short-circuits to silent PASS. Durable across restarts, NO new state field, NO migration — the audit trail IS the memory. **Exec authorization is run-scoped (one approval opens exec for the whole run), not per-step** — explicitly SPEC-locked (WR-03 documented). → 10-CONTEXT.md (D-03), 10-REVIEW.md (WR-03).
- **D-04 (approval payload):** the pause payload is the exec POLICY snapshot (step/agent id, allow-list, caps, scrubbed-env note, egress-denied) — **no creds, no dynamic argv preview** (argv is constructed at exec time; the allow-list IS the honest bound). → 10-CONTEXT.md (D-04).
- **Exec profile is runtime/host-owned, NEVER manifest-tunable** — `DEFAULT_EXEC_PROFILE` lives as module constants in `local.py`; a manifest may grant `tools.exec: true` but can never widen the allow-list or raise the caps. → 10-SPEC.md (Requirement 2).
- **Trust-conditional grant ceiling:** `file`/`builtin` trust may grant exec (survives `intersect_permissions`); `user`/`db` granting exec/network/secrets → `CompilerError`. Exec is engineer-only — nothing exec-related is `user_allowed=True`. `network`/`secrets` stay OFF in the ceiling for BOTH trust levels (still runtime gate-blocked, out of scope). → 10-02-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions).
- **`network` and `secrets` remain BLOCKED at the security gate this phase** — only the exec branch was split to a conditional PASS; the network/secrets BLOCK path is byte-identical. → 10-03-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
- **Three-layer enforcement, each independently testable, each dormant unless the plan grants exec:** compiler ceiling → security+approval gate → workspace policy. This dormancy is what preserves INV-3 parity (no existing manifest grants exec). → 10-PATTERNS.md (Shared Patterns).
- **Accepted residual risks (do not re-litigate):** RISK-1 — an allow-listed interpreter can still open sockets (OS-level netns enforcement is the v2 ECS seam; engineer-only exec); RISK-2 — D-04 payload discloses only policy (no creds/argv); RISK-3 — the exec-granting `sample_exec_workflow` lives under `tests/.../fixtures/` only, never a shipped/user palette. → 10-SECURITY.md (Accepted Risks Log).
- **N3 resolved** by citing 10-SPEC.md as the decision record (its 9 locked requirements ARE the N3 decision), not a separate ADR. → 10-05-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made).

### 6. Status, Verification & Evidence (what HAPPENED)

- **Verdict: PASSED — 14/14 must-have truths verified, 0 gaps.** → 10-VERIFICATION.md (status: passed).
- **Evidence:** targeted offline behavior suite **217 passed / 5 skipped** (local_runtime, exec_runs, compiler_trust, gates, code_validators, repo_diff + characterization + migration-ledger + banned-patterns + registry); `grep shell=True` over runtime+agents = **0**; `lint-imports` **4 kept / 0 broken** (171 files, 364 deps); `grep ExecutionPolicy\.check` = 0; direct-spawn grep over the 3 validator files = 0; 5 characterization snapshots **byte/event-identical** with `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset (10 tests). Migration 0018 reversible offline. → 10-VERIFICATION.md (Behavioral Spot-Checks), 10-UAT.md.
- **Code review:** standard depth, 28 files. Findings **1 Critical + 4 Warning + 2 Info**. CR-01/WR-01/WR-02/WR-04 **FIXED**; WR-03 **DOCUMENTED**; IN-01/IN-02 **ACCEPTED**. → 10-REVIEW.md (Fix Resolution).
  - **CR-01** (live exec-audit recorder never invoked — signature + sync/async mismatch broke audit AND the deny contract): fixed via `_make_exec_recorder` sync→async adapter at the host seam (`engine.py`, `loop.create_task` bridge) + single keyword-only step-free `_noop_recorder` contract. Commit `67abe33`. **The whole Phase-10 test suite hid this because every test injected a hand-shaped sync lambda recorder — a future agent touching the recorder contract MUST keep the engine `_make_exec_recorder` adapter in sync with `LocalWorkspace._recorder` call sites.**
  - **WR-01** (wall-clock timeout didn't kill the process group — forking runaway escaped): fixed via `Popen` + `communicate(timeout=)` + `os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), SIGKILL)`. Commit `3be966d`.
  - **WR-02** (empty argv → unaudited `IndexError`): fixed via empty-argv deny guard that records denial + raises `PermissionError`. Commit `6eedf71`.
  - **WR-04** (`_evaluate_gates` only observed an outcome when the gate emitted ≥1 event — a zero-event block silently passed): fixed via terminal `(None, outcome)` sentinel after the event loop. Commit `1328430`.
  - **WR-03** (exec grant is run-global, not per-step): documented as SPEC-locked D-03 run-scoped authorization at the host seam + `exec_granted`. Commit `dd3de5e`.
  - **IN-02** (accepted): SecurityGate's runtime trust check `getattr(step, "trust", "file")` always resolves to `"file"` because the compiled `Step` carries NO `trust` attribute — the runtime trust check is intentional defense-in-depth that cannot fire; the PRIMARY enforcement is the compiler `_TRUSTED_SOURCES` check. → 10-REVIEW.md (IN-02), 10-SECURITY.md (RISK-5).
- **Security:** `threats_open: 0` — **31/31 threats CLOSED** (26 mitigate + 5 accept); ASVS L1; status verified. → 10-SECURITY.md (Sign-Off).
- **Key commit SHAs:** 10-01 `a272ffb`/`926d9d7`; 10-02 `1a47f93`/`11a801f`; 10-03 `73453b8`/`3b77221`; 10-04 `9028e30`/`0ba4486`; 10-05 `2285941`(test)/`fc3efff`(feat)/`0fcbc84`(docs); review fixes `67abe33`/`3be966d`/`6eedf71`/`1328430`/`dd3de5e`. → SUMMARY Task Commits + 10-REVIEW.md.

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward

- **Live survivor — `exec_command` returns stdout-only (str contract).** `proc.stderr` + `proc.returncode` are passed to the recorder/audit but NOT returned. Validators therefore surface verdicts via STDOUT: `code_compile` drives an inline `python3 -c py_compile.compile(doraise=True)` driver printing `COMPILE_OK`/`COMPILE_FAIL`; `code_test` parses pytest `-q` summary; `code_lint` parses ruff `--output-format=concise`. **A future agent must NOT change `exec_command`'s return type to expose exit code without re-baselining `test_local_runtime.py` and risking a characterization re-baseline** — the stdout contract is load-bearing. → 10-04-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made / Deviations).
- **Gotcha — `python` is NOT on the scrubbed PATH; only `python3`/`python3.11`.** The compile driver uses `python3` (both are allow-listed). → 10-04-SUMMARY.md (Issues Encountered).
- **Gotcha — `import subprocess` survives in the 3 validator files but only for the `subprocess.TimeoutExpired` exception type** — NOT for spawning. Grep for `subprocess.run(`/`subprocess.Popen(` in those files = 0. This is by design (the type-only import). → 10-VERIFICATION.md (Anti-Patterns Found).
- **Gotcha — scrubbed-env test asserts the security PROPERTY (no host-credential-CLASS key survives), not an exact 3-key allow-list.** macOS / the python launcher injects benign keys (CPATH/SDKROOT/LC_CTYPE/…) into the child AFTER the env dict is constructed — a darwin platform artifact, not a leak. The constructed env dict is still PATH/HOME/TMPDIR only. → 10-01-SUMMARY.md (Deviations).
- **Gotcha — `RLIMIT_AS` is best-effort on darwin (dev), hard on linux (prod).** Tests assert the *mechanism* (`preexec_fn` calls `setrlimit`); the kill test uses the portable wall-clock timeout, never a memory bomb. → 10-RESEARCH.md (Pitfall 4).
- **Carry-forward DEFERRED (pre-existing, NOT a Phase-10 regression):** `tests/unit/test_alembic.py::test_upgrade_then_check_reports_no_drift` was ALREADY FAILING on clean HEAD — `alembic check` wants to drop `fk_workspaces_repo_id_repositories` because the `Workspace.repo_id` ORM column (Phase 9 / migration 0017) is declared without `ForeignKey(...)`. 10-01 confirmed it introduced NO new drift (`exec_runs.run_id` FK matches its ORM). **Revisit when the Phase 9 FK declaration is reconciled.** → deferred-items.md (10-01).
- **Carry-forward — REQUIREMENTS.md traceability table still shows EXEC-01/EXEC-02 "Pending"** (stale row ~line 246); the requirement definitions (~lines 114-115) are marked done and are authoritative. → 10-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage note).

Non-exec runs never provision a workspace → snapshot parity. → 10-03-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made).
- **Offline-suite caveat:** full backend pytest hangs offline (Chromium/Bedrock/Postgres-gated); always verify with the targeted parity suite + `lint-imports` at `/opt/homebrew/bin/lint-imports`. → every SUMMARY (Issues Encountered).

## Phase 11 — Engine-Owned Fan-Out + Merge [5]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/11-engine-owned-fan-out-merge-5/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-11)  ·  **Plans:** 5/5  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 5
**Requirements delivered:** FANOUT-01, FANOUT-02, FANOUT-03, FANOUT-04, FANOUT-05, FANOUT-06, FANOUT-07, FANOUT-08, FANOUT-09, FANOUT-10, FANOUT-11, OBS-01, RESUME-01 (13 locked; SC-001 proven for fan-out) → 11-SPEC.md (Requirements)
**One-line outcome:** Built the engine-owned fan-out subsystem — one kernel `run_fanout` spawn path (declarative `fanout_batch` strategy + runtime `spawn_subagents` tool both funnel through it), per-worker isolation (`sub_sandbox`/`worktree`), a 4-strategy `MergeStrategy` registry with first-class conflicts, a reserve-before-spawn `BudgetManager`, owner-scoped `subagent_runs` (0019) persistence, and cooperative cancellation with teardown — all dormant for existing workflows (INV-3 parity held).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

A granted step fans out N workers (self-copies or named from `allowed_workers`) into engine-selected isolated workspaces, parallel (capped) or sequential, under engine-enforced budgets; both the declarative `step.fanout` and the runtime `spawn_subagents` tool funnel through ONE kernel `run_fanout`; results merge deterministically via a registered `MergeStrategy`; conflicts are first-class (`merge_conflict` artifact + event + 4 `on_conflict` policies); every child gets an owner-scoped `subagent_runs` row; cancellation propagates to children with isolated-workspace teardown. The engine alone decides isolation/caps/merge (INV-7). → 11-SPEC.md (Goal), 11-CONTEXT.md (Phase Boundary).

"What must be TRUE" — the 13 acceptance criteria → 11-SPEC.md (Acceptance Criteria): single spawn path reached by both entry points; `spawn_subagents` permission-bound (`user_allowed=False`) + spawn-free; self×N / named worker selection with pre-spawn rejection (zero rows); parallel ≤ cap 4 / sequential ordered; isolated writes no cross-contamination; 4 merge strategies registered, `copy_disjoint` deterministic + overlap→conflict; 4 `on_conflict` policies per spec (human_gate via the ONE durable HITL, merge_agent ≤ 2, partial, abort); reserve-before-spawn with module-constant defaults 8/4/2/900s; per-workspace ceiling + `budget_snapshot_json` persistence; 0019 reversible + cross-owner read = ∅; cancel teardown zero residue; 5 characterization snapshots byte/event-identical; SC-001 (a fanning workflow needs zero engine edits). **Depends on:** Phase 10 (exec_runs 0018 recipe + ScopedStore None-degrade), Phase 09 (IsolationProvider port + LocalWorkspace single git-subprocess owner), Phase 08 (`@register`/`discover()` + tool_provider/perms-intersection + ONE durable `run_human_gate`), Phase 07 (KernelServices `ctx.runner` handle + `task_loop` TaskSource precedent + SC-001 proof pattern), Phase 05 (ArtifactGraph + `ScopedStore.write_ref` + allowed `merge_conflict` kind). → 11-CONTEXT.md (Canonical References), 11-01-SUMMARY.md (Dependency graph).

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (what was BUILT)

Plus the 0014 forward column `workflow_runs.budget_snapshot_json` finally **written** (no migration needed). → 11-01-PLAN.md (Task 1), 11-PATTERNS.md (Pattern 4).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **D-01 worker shape:** one child = ONE isolated `KernelServices.run_agent` invocation (NOT a nested mini-run); per-worker `thread_id = {run_id}:{step}:{worker_i}` (+ `:d{depth}` segment for nested fan-out). Workers carry NO gates, NO per-worker fix-loop. Children are `subagent_runs` rows, NEVER `workflow_runs` rows. → 11-CONTEXT.md (D-01).
- **D-02 declarative entry = `fanout_batch` ExecutionStrategy** (the Q8/base.py reserved name); runtime entry = `spawn_subagents` tool whose request the engine derives via the `report_task_complete` post-loop tool-event-derivation pattern (`_derive_fanout`) — keeps INV-13, tool body spawn-free. → 11-CONTEXT.md (D-02), 11-RESEARCH.md (Pattern 3).
- **D-03 child events lifecycle-only:** parent stream carries `subagent_*`/`merge_*`/`budget_warning` ONLY; worker chunk/tool events NOT forwarded (workers run silent). `subagent_chunk` tagged streaming is deferred. → 11-CONTEXT.md (D-03).
- **INV-7 (engine decides, never the manifest):** isolation scope derived SOLELY from `base_workspace.has_git` (worktree if True else sub_sandbox); merge strategy engine-selected keyed on the scope (worktree→git_3way, sub_sandbox→copy_disjoint). A user manifest can NEVER downgrade isolation or name the merge strategy on the isolated path. → 11-02/11-03-SUMMARY.md (Decisions).
- **Budget defaults are LOCKED module constants** (not suggestions): max_subagents=8, max_concurrency=4, max_depth=2, wall_clock=900s, merge_agent_attempts=2, warn@80%. `reserve()` gates countable units (subagents/concurrency/depth) before spawn; tokens/wall-clock are check-at-boundary (D-05 dual shape — cannot pre-reserve unknown token spend). depth enforces `depth+1 > max_depth`. `arm()` idempotent (first deadline wins). → 11-04-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made), 11-SPEC.md (Constraints).
- **Trust-conditional `Limits` (and `spawn_subagents`):** file/builtin manifests may RAISE a Limits cap; user/db manifests may only LOWER (raising any → `CompilerError`). `max_tokens` excluded from the raise-rejection set (no module ceiling — declaring it only self-constrains). `spawn_subagents` rides the SAME trust ceiling as `exec` (file may grant, user/db collapses OFF + CAP-03 rejects). Compiler defines its OWN `_LIMITS_DEFAULT_CEILING` constants (mirror of budget.py) because import-linter forbids `agents.workflows → agents.execution_engine`. → 11-04/11-05-SUMMARY.md (Decisions).
- **ONE durable HITL preserved (D-02, Phase-10):** merge `human_gate` delegates to `run_human_gate` → `_run_review_gate`; NO sibling `run_merge_gate`. Conflict-as-first-class: an overlapping target is EVICTED from the applied set (never silently overwritten). merge_agent bounded at 2 then human_gate fallback (no oscillation). → 11-03-SUMMARY.md, 11-SECURITY.md (T-11-03-04).
- **`write_fragment_artifact` is the SINGLE producer of typed fragment refs (INV-12)** — 11-04 (BudgetExceeded abort) and 11-05 (cancel) CONSUME it, they do NOT re-introduce it. Both fragment + merge_conflict writes delegate to the engine's single `_dual_write_artifact` path. → 11-03-PLAN.md (dependency_note).
- **D-08 SC-001 fixture is test-scoped:** the `sample_fanout` MANIFEST lives at the real home (`agents/workflows/sample_fanout/workflow.yaml` — loaded by `compile_for_run` with zero engine edit) but the worker AGENT.md specs are FIXTURE-scoped (`tests/agents/fixtures/sc001_fanout/`), NOT `agents/prompts/`, because a prompt-home `pipeline_type: sample_fanout` AGENT.md would break `test_loader.py`'s `SUPPORTED_PIPELINE_TYPES` schema gate (the sc001_task_loop/sample_brownfield precedent). → 11-05-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made / File-placement deviation).
- **`accumulated_outputs` mirror is the ONLY sanctioned temporary duplication project-wide** (removed Phase 1B) — unrelated to this phase but a global INV-12 constraint a future agent must respect. → CLAUDE.md (Constraints).

## Phase 12 — Wave Scheduler + Durable Resume [6]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/12-wave-scheduler-durable-resume-6/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-11)  ·  **Plans:** 10/10  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** 6
**Requirements delivered:** WAVE-01, WAVE-02, WAVE-03, RESUME-02, RESUME-03, RESUME-04 (6 IDs; → 12-VERIFICATION.md "Requirements Coverage", REQUIREMENTS.md lines 133–142 / traceability 250–252)
**One-line outcome:** Added a registered `wave_scheduler` strategy that topo-sorts `json_tasks` into deterministic parallel waves driven through the unmodified kernel `run_fanout`, plus a full durable-resume tier (idempotent per-step retry, WS `after_seq` replay, step-granular in-process auto-resume incl. mid-wave) and the §22 FE wave/subagent tree panel — all dormant for existing workflows (5/10 characterization snapshots byte/event-identical).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)
Goal: run disjoint multi-file tasks in deterministic parallel waves through the EXISTING kernel `run_fanout`, persist `wave_runs`, and harden durable resume (retry, reconnect replay, step-granular restart) — prototype stays sequential and byte/event-identical, CP-SAT stays a pure-function seam (→ 12-SPEC.md "Goal"; 12-CONTEXT.md "Phase Boundary"). Depends-on: Phase 11 (single `run_fanout` spawn path + `subagent_runs` 0019 recipe + fragments-persist-before-merge), Phase 5 (typed `ArtifactGraph`/`content_hash` + `run_events` durable replay semantics), Phase 6 (`_is_transient_throttle`), Phase 08-08 (deferred wave-tree FE panel, now cleared). Success criteria (→ 12-SPEC.md "Acceptance Criteria", ROADMAP Phase 12): `is_registered("strategy","wave_scheduler")` + `is_registered("task_parser","json_tasks")`; deterministic wave builder (diamond DAG, repeat-identical, conflict-key split, cycle/unknown-ref raises pre-spawn with zero rows); sample multi-file workflow ≥2 waves / ≥2 parallel workers from manifest+AGENT.md only with `grep <sample> agents/execution_engine/` = 0; `0020` reversible offline single head, cross-owner read = ∅; transient retry ≤`max_attempts` then visible error, non-transient no retry, hash-match reuse zero extra agent calls; WS reconnect `after_seq` replays exactly `seq>N` once each idempotent by `event_id` incl. after restart; `restore_non_terminal_runs` resumes mid-wave to `completed` (completed workers not re-invoked), `waiting_for_user` still gates, stateless runs keep WR-05; FE tree renders + reconnect adopts `after_seq`; 5 characterization snapshots byte/event-identical + lint-imports 4/0 + banned-pattern + migration-ledger green.
