### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added
- **New registered capabilities** (→ 12-01-SUMMARY.md "Accomplishments"): `strategy:wave_scheduler` (`user_allowed=True`, matches `fanout_batch` family); `task_parser:json_tasks` (default trust, matches `heading_tasks`). Registry `_KNOWN` lockstep **59 → 61** (→ registry.py `_KNOWN`; test_registry_capabilities.py lockstep assertion). CP-SAT is NOT a capability kind — it is the pure `build_waves(tasks) -> list[list[Task]]` seam inside wave_scheduler.py (D-03; no `_KNOWN` churn beyond the two real registrations).
- **Pure seam:** `build_waves` (Kahn topological levels over `depends_on`, stable tie-break by task id, within-level split so overlapping `conflict_keys` never co-schedule; `conflict_keys` defaults to `targets` when empty — documented Pitfall-6 default) + `WaveBuildError` raised pre-spawn on cycle / unknown ref / duplicate id (zero rows) (→ 12-PATTERNS.md "build_waves pure seam").
- **Migration 0020** (`0020_wave_runs.py`, `down_revision="0019"`, single head, reversible offline, free-String status no `sa.Enum`, named FK): table **`wave_runs`** carries id, run_id, **owner_id, workspace_id** (AUTHZ-01 NOT NULL), step, wave_index, task_ids (JSON), status, created_at; index `ix_wave_runs_run`. `WaveRun` ORM on `Base.metadata` (→ 12-01-PLAN.md Task 2; 12-PATTERNS.md "0020_wave_runs").
- **Ports/store methods:** `ScopedStore.record_wave_run`/`update_wave_run`/`read_wave_runs` (default-deny, cloned from `*_subagent_run`); `KernelServices.record_wave_run`/`update_wave_run`/`read_wave_runs`/`read_subagent_runs` (best-effort None-degrading) (→ authz.py, kernel_services.py).
- **Engine helpers (additive):** `_compute_step_input_hash`, `_find_reused_completion`, `_dispatch_step_with_retry`, module-level `_retry_sleep` (12-02); `resume_run`, `_compute_resume_offset`, `_first_incomplete_step`, `_recover_workspace_id`, `_hydrate_artifacts_from_store`, `_is_resumable_in_flight`, `_stamp_resume_marker`, `_fire_resume_cleanup` (12-03/05/09); `ExecutionContext.is_resuming`; `ArtifactGraph.adopt`.
- **New event types (additive families, ride the single seq/event_id emit boundary — zero websocket.py forwarding edits):** `wave_started`, `wave_completed`, `wave_failed` (12-01); `step_retry`, `step_reused`, `step_completed` (12-02); `run_resuming` resume marker (12-03). `subagent_spawned`/`subagent_result` GREW to carry flat `data.wave_index` (number) + `data.step` (string) alongside existing `data.worker` (number) — the FE emit contract (→ 12-06-SUMMARY.md "FE Event Contract").
- **WS protocol grew:** `reconnect_pipeline` message accepts optional `after_seq` (int-coerced); `pipeline_reconnected` reply carries `live`/`status`/`replayed_through_seq`. No new REST endpoint (events-only data plane, D-13). Injected engine→WS bridge over the EXISTING `_PIPELINE_TASKS`/`_PIPELINE_QUEUES` registries, wired ONLY in `app/main.py` (12-09).
- **FE:** `WaveTreePanel.tsx` (props-driven sibling panel, lifecycle statuses only); `wsReplayState.ts` pure dedup/reset helper; `pipeline_reconnected` handler in `useWorkflow.ts`.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)
- **INERT forward-fields went LIVE with ONE consumer each (no parallel config surface):** `Task.depends_on`/`conflict_keys`/`targets` — consumed only by `build_waves`/`json_tasks`; `Step.retry`/`RetryPolicy` (previously declared-but-INERT, "consumed nowhere") — consumed only by `_dispatch_step_with_retry`. `RetryPolicy.on` field added (→ 12-SPEC.md "Background"; migration-ledger INERT-fields-go-live watch).
- **`restore_non_terminal_runs` WR-05 abandoned→failed path was SUPERSEDED only where durable resume state exists** — branch (c) keeps the verbatim message/behavior for `waiting_for_user`-absent stateless runs; branch (b) (resumable in-flight) is the new auto-resume. No forked dispatch loop: `resume_run` re-enters `_execute_impl` at a `_resume_from` offset; the grep gate `for i, spec in enumerate(ordered_agents)` stays **exactly 1** (→ 12-03-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made"; 12-VERIFICATION.md key-link verification).
- **CR-03 deleted the unsafe prefix skip** in wave_scheduler.py: the `_completed_worker_count` tally, the `wave[_remaining_completed:]` slice, and `_workers_seen_in_completed_waves` bookkeeping were removed (grep `_remaining_completed` = 0 matches) and replaced with whole-in-flight-wave re-run (→ 12-06-SUMMARY.md "Decisions Made"). Do NOT reintroduce the prefix-by-count skip — it silently drops incomplete parallel tasks (data loss). The dead-mount of `WaveTreePanel` in unrouted `WorkflowComposer.tsx:299` was NOT deleted (left as a separate composer-routing concern; the live mount is in `DashboardLayout.tsx`).
- **Ledger/gates armed:** migration-ledger row `WAVE-PERSIST` added + flipped ☑ (0019→0020 reversible CHECK); registry lockstep ratcheted 59→61; characterization, banned-pattern (INV-13 — workers spawn only via existing `run_fanout`/`create_runner`), import-linter (4 kept / 0 broken — capabilities import-pure, kernel reached only via `ctx.runner`; engine never imports `app.api`) all stayed green (→ 12-VERIFICATION.md "Behavioral Spot-Checks").
- Otherwise this phase is **purely additive** (new tables, helpers, events, FE) — `heading_tasks.py` has an empty git diff (SPEC req-2 acceptance), prototype/`fanout_batch`/flat-fanout events are byte-identical.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)
- **D-01..D-05 (wave execution):** wave loop lives INSIDE the strategy (control flow in strategies, INV-5); ONE `ctx.runner.run_fanout` call per wave (kernel `run_fanout` is the single spawn path, INV-12 — NOT modified as an entry point); per-wave merge into base BEFORE next wave; CP-SAT = pure `build_waves` function (not a capability kind); `wave_runs` one row per executed wave via best-effort handle (→ 12-CONTEXT.md "Area A").
- **D-06..D-09 (restart re-entry):** dedicated `resume_run` re-entering the SAME dispatch loop at the first incomplete step; "first incomplete step" derived from EXISTING durable surfaces — **NO new step-status table** (§18 is a closed list); three-way startup classification with WR-05 kept verbatim; checkpointer reuse where `{run_id}:{agent}`/`:task` thread matches else idempotent re-run (→ 12-CONTEXT.md "Area B").
- **D-10..D-12 (retry/hash):** ONE retry wrapper at the single engine dispatch home (not in strategies, not per-worker in `run_fanout`); `input_hash` = sha256 over SORTED upstream `content_hash`es + resolved input string, **no timestamp/uuid/unsorted** (cross-restart hash stability is mandatory — restart re-runs depend on hash equality, Pitfall 1); stored in the **`run_events` payload**, NOT a new ArtifactRef field (D-11 researcher resolution, Open Q1); transient classification reuses 06-03 `_is_transient_throttle` (one classifier home) (→ 12-CONTEXT.md "Area C"; 12-RESEARCH.md "D-11 resolution").
- **D-13..D-15 (FE):** events-only data plane (no REST tree endpoint); lifecycle statuses ONLY (no `subagent_chunk`); additive props-driven sibling panel; FE sends `after_seq` + dedupes by `event_id` (→ 12-CONTEXT.md "Area D").
- **Gap-closure locked decisions:** workspace_id is RECOVERED from a durable owner-scoped row on resume, NOT re-minted via `create_workspace` (a fresh id makes owner+workspace-scoped reads resolve to ∅ — binding drift, Pitfall 2; the bug recurred at offset-0 in 12-05); resume seq counter seeded `max(seq)+1` so resumed events continue the monotonic per-run seq (CR-01); WS replay/CR-01 owner-gate workspace_id always recovered server-side from a row filtered by `owner_id == user.id`, never from the client payload (T-12-05-TENANT); `set_run_scope` is the SINGLE workspace stamper (no parallel stamper, INV-3/INV-12) and FAILS LOUD on non-`SQLAlchemyError` / `PermissionError` principal drift (WR-03 fix); engine→WS bridge is an INJECTED callback (engine must NOT import `app.api`, import-linter §31); per-task mid-wave skip is DEFERRED until `subagent_runs` carries task identity (CR-03-followup, → 12-CONTEXT.md "Deferred Ideas").
- **SC-001 held:** `sample_wave` is a manifest+AGENT.md-only workflow using only registered capabilities; `grep -rn sample_wave backend/agents/execution_engine/` = 0 (zero engine edits) — the kernel knows no workflow by name (→ 12-10-SUMMARY.md; 12-VERIFICATION.md).

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward
- **Live survivors (look removed/dead but are intentional):** the WR-05 abandoned→failed path SURVIVES verbatim as branch (c) of `restore_non_terminal_runs` — do not delete it (stateless runs still need it). `heading_tasks.py` SURVIVES byte-untouched (json_tasks is a sibling, not a replacement). `single_file` / `serialized_sandbox` / `copy_disjoint` deliverable+merge strategies are reused unchanged. The `run_pipeline` legacy WS registration path is unchanged (bridge is purely additive for the resume path).
- **Gotchas a future agent must respect:** (1) `input_hash` serialization MUST stay timestamp/uuid-free and sorted — any drift breaks restart-resume artifact reuse (Pitfall 1). (2) On resume, recover `workspace_id` from a durable owner-scoped row keyed on `_is_resume` (NOT `_resume_from > 0`) — an offset-0 resume is still a resume; minting a fresh workspace makes owner+workspace-scoped reads resolve to ∅ (Pitfall 2; the 12-05 Rule-1 bug). (3) Mid-wave resume re-runs the WHOLE in-flight wave (only TERMINAL-completed waves are skipped, step-filtered by `step == step_id`); per-task skip is impossible until `subagent_runs` carries task identity. (4) The engine MUST NOT import `app.api` — the WS bridge is an injected callback; reaching the WS registry any other way breaks import-linter §31. (5) FE `event_id` dedup must stay at the TOP of `handleWebSocketMessage` for ALL event types (the FE always sends `after_seq`, so the backend replay branch is always entered and would double-deliver non-wave events otherwise — CR-05); reset `lastSeqRef`/`seenEventIdsRef`/`waveGroups` per run on `pipeline_start` (WR-03).
- **Phase was REOPENED post-verification:** the initial pass (6/6) was STALE — the milestone-end live UAT then found 3 gaps; plans 12-05..10 + the review fixes closed them and verification was re-run to PASSED 15/15. A future agent reading an old "passed 6/6" must use the re-verified 12-VERIFICATION.md (re_verification block).
- **Deferred / carry-forward (→ 12-CONTEXT.md "Deferred Ideas"):** CP-SAT (or smarter) wave builder behind the `build_waves` seam; **CR-03-followup** per-task mid-wave skip (needs an additive migration adding task_id/worker_index to subagent_runs — replaces the wasteful whole-wave re-run); `subagent_chunk` live worker streaming; per-worker retry inside `run_fanout`; durable queue/worker substrate (N8 v2); cross-node/distributed resume locks; prototype fragment-merge parallelism (MERGE-01 v2, Q33); REST waves/subagents tree endpoint; FE repo-diff viewer (08-08 remainder); real € price table. **Folder:** `.planning/phases/13-live-verification-gap-closure/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-12) — verified PASSED (6/6 at the offline boundary); **security-audited `threats_open: 0`; full code-review backlog actioned — Critical + all 7 Warnings FIXED (`13-REVIEW-FIX.md` all_fixed)**; only 6 Info advisories + the live re-pass remain (the deferred revision-loop feature is now closed by **Phase 14**)  ·  **Plans:** 6/6  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** (post-milestone, no plan-id)

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Goal: close the product gaps surfaced by the 2026-06-11 post-milestone **live-Bedrock** verification pass — every workflow + custom workflows with all options on real AWS Bedrock Claude **Haiku 4.5** (`eu.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5-20251001-v1:0`), with semantic review of every agent's output by 8 parallel **Opus 4.8** reviewer agents. v1.0 had been marked complete on offline (scripted-model) evidence; this live pass found 8 gaps the offline suite could not catch. → REPORT.md "Live-Bedrock Verification Pass — 2026-06-11"; ROADMAP "### Phase 13" (Goal). **Depends on: Phase 12.** Wave structure: Wave 1 = 13-01/02/03/04 (independent, `depends_on: []`); Wave 2 = 13-05 (`depends_on: [13-01]` — shares the engine.py diff base); Wave 3 = 13-06 (`depends_on: [13-05]` — its total-failure branch composes with 13-05's `final_output and results` deliverable-write guard).

The 6 ROADMAP success criteria (one per finding-cluster) — each verified TRUE at the offline boundary; live re-verification deferred per project convention:

1. Declared `gates:[human]` step surfaces `review_gate_ready` BEFORE awaiting the response; client approves; run proceeds (F1). → ROADMAP SC1; closed by **13-01**.
2. `run_revision` with the FE-exact payload (`ppt_output`/`od_ppt_output`) against a completed parent passes FR-014 and drives a revision (F2). → ROADMAP SC2; closed by **13-05**.
3. All-agents-failed runs terminate as a visible failure / fail fast at ingress — never `pipeline_complete` with empty deliverable (F3). → ROADMAP SC3; closed by **13-06**.
4. Text-only agents emit no fabricated tool-call XML; handoff coder reliably returns parseable JSON (F4). → ROADMAP SC4; closed by **13-02**.
5. All 15 app_builder agents role-conformant; the 3 migration-templated prompts re-templated; devops emits files not narration (F5). → ROADMAP SC5; closed by **13-03**.
6. `sample_fanout` resolves its declared deliverable (no `single_file` fallback); `test_phase3_token_delta_live` runs against the current engine; phase-8 budget recalibrated (F6/F7). → ROADMAP SC6; closed by **13-04**.

Findings register (root cause + file:line artifact) — F1–F7 are the ROADMAP-named set; F8 is environment-only (no code plan). Full definitions → REPORT.md "Findings register" + 13-UAT.md "Gaps":

- **F1 (major, product)** — Declared-gate events undeliverable. `HumanGate.evaluate` consumed the whole `run_human_gate` delegate into a list; `_evaluate_gates` yielded events only post-resolution → `review_gate_ready` reached the client only after approval; a UI run hung at the first declared gate. → REPORT.md F1; 13-UAT.md Test 1 / Gaps[0]. **Closed by 13-01.**
- **F2 (major, product)** — `run_revision` dead end-to-end. FE sends `target_artifact_type: "ppt_output"`/`"od_ppt_output"`; FR-014 lookup `list_refs(kind=target)` matched no persisted kind (`grep ppt_output backend/` = 0 writers); revision characterization tests masked it by seeding the exact target kind. → REPORT.md F2; 13-UAT.md Test 2 / Gaps[1]. **Closed by 13-05.**
- **F3 (major, product)** — Inject-missing agents halt silently but the run still "completes". Bare `prototype`/`ppt` (no od_context) → injects-declaring agents `agent_error` and halt, yet pipeline ends `pipeline_complete` with empty/improvised output (ws-ppt deck authored solo by the validator — role inversion). → REPORT.md F3; 13-UAT.md Test 3 / Gaps[2]. **Closed by 13-06.**
- **F4 (major, model robustness)** — Live Haiku fabricates tool-call XML (`<function_calls>`/`<invoke>`/`write_todos`) as text when prompts imply tool actions without bound tools (handoff coder returned XML not JSON in 1/2 runs; XML leaked into user_stories/custom/app_builder/dotnet prose). The od_ppt instance was ALREADY FIXED pre-phase (commit `8ba8f626`); remaining = text-only prompt hygiene + handoff hardening. → REPORT.md F4; 13-UAT.md Test 4 / Gaps[3]. **Closed by 13-02.**
- **F5 (major, agent config)** — app_builder prompt defects (Opus 15-agent audit): `app-code-compliance`/`app-test-compliance`/`app-sdlc-governance` carry Java/.NET *migration* template language → stalls/empties; `app-devops` narration-only; `app-system-design` stalls on clarifying questions; auth impl↔test contract mismatch; `/api` vs `/api/v1` drift. → REPORT.md F5; 13-UAT.md Test 5 / Gaps[4]. **Closed by 13-03.**
- **F6 (minor, samples)** — `sample_fanout` declares `single_file: merged.txt` which no step produces → fallback degrades output to one worker's text. Same quirk 12-10 fixed for sample_wave (commit `8ffae37c`); fanout was out of scope then. → REPORT.md F6; 13-UAT.md Test 6 / Gaps[5]. **Closed by 13-04.**
- **F7 (test-infra)** — `live_harness` bit-rot already FIXED pre-phase (commit `6e68d9f4`); `test_phase3_token_delta_live` monkeypatched the deleted `_build_context_message` seam (→ AttributeError at setup); phase-8 `LIVE_BUDGET_USD=5.0` < measured $5.94. → REPORT.md F7; 13-UAT.md Tests 7 & 8 / Gaps[6][7]. **Closed by 13-04** (seam repair + budget recalibration).
- **F8 (environment, NO code plan)** — migration pipelines need source-repo inputs the bare-brief harness lacks; Bedrock daily token quota exhausted late in the pass (dotnet/chat captures degraded, od_ppt retest blocked). Re-run / environment-fidelity follow-up, EXCLUDED from the gap-closure plans (deferral note in 13-UAT.md frontmatter). → REPORT.md F8. **DEFERRED — no plan; re-run after quota reset with repo inputs.**

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

- **Gate streaming protocol** — `evaluate_stream(step, ctx)` async-generator on `HumanGate` + `ApprovalGate` (kind:gate); engine duck-types it via `getattr(gate, "evaluate_stream")`. Non-streaming gates (validation/security) keep the awaited path byte-identically. → 13-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments); 13-VERIFICATION.md (Observable Truths #1).
- **New artifact kind** — `"deliverable"` member of the closed `ARTIFACT_KINDS` vocabulary in `artifacts/graph.py`; a completion-time `kind="deliverable"` artifact_ref now persists the resolved `final_output` (visibility `workspace`, additive — no migration, additive to the existing typed-ref store from Phase 1B; carries owner_id+workspace_id via the existing `_dual_write_artifact`/ScopedStore path). → 13-05-SUMMARY.md (Task 1); 13-VERIFICATION.md (Observable Truths #2).
- **FR-014 fallback chain** — `_handle_revision` now resolves the original via exact `target_artifact_type` → `deliverable` → `summary`, `assert_owns` FIRST; one INFO log names the matched link; FR-014 ValueError message byte-unchanged. No new WS event. → 13-05-SUMMARY.md (Task 2).
- **New terminal WS event `pipeline_failed`** — data {pipeline_type, pipeline_run_id, total_duration, agents_completed:0, agents_total, agents_failed, error, timestamp}; plus additive conditional `status:"degraded"`+`agents_failed` keys on `pipeline_complete` (absent on clean runs → characterization byte-parity). → 13-06-SUMMARY.md (Task 1).
- **New WS ingress rejection code `missing_template_context`** — in `_handle_workflow_execution`; rejects template-inject-declaring pipelines with no loadable `template_body` BEFORE engine/checkpointer/sandbox spin-up; condition mirrors `factory._compose_injection`'s `TemplateMissingError` exactly, keyed on declared `spec.injects` (no pipeline-name branch). → 13-06-SUMMARY.md (Task 2).
- **Prompt-composition slot** — `"tool_availability"` placed FIRST in `PromptAssemblyPolicy.DEFAULT_ORDER`; conditional, so tool-having compositions are byte-identical. Runner gains `_strip_fabricated_tool_xml`; handoff coder gains `_MAX_PARSE_ATTEMPTS`/`_CORRECTIVE_SUFFIX`/`_FABRICATED_TOOL_XML_RE`. → 13-02-SUMMARY.md (Task 1/Task 2).
- **Manifest-only swap** — `sample_fanout/workflow.yaml` deliverable strategy `single_file` → `serialized_sandbox` (data-only, no engine edit). → 13-04-SUMMARY.md (Task 1).
- **FE changes** — `pipeline_failed` added to both WS unions in `types/index.ts`, a teardown case in `useWorkflow.ts`, and terminal handling in `dashboard/page.tsx`; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. → 13-06-SUMMARY.md (Task 3).
- **_KNOWN movement:** none — Phase 13 registered no new named capabilities (the gate-streaming surface is a method added to existing registered gates; the deliverable kind is a vocabulary member, not a capability). The capability-registry name counter is unchanged by this phase.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)

Phase 13 is **almost entirely additive** (gap-closure on a completed milestone), so there are no single-impl collapses or table drops. The move-don't-copy discipline still shows up in three places:

- **`evaluate()` collapsed onto `evaluate_stream`** — the old list-buffering gate body was *replaced* (not duplicated): `evaluate()` is now a thin collector over the single streaming implementation (INV-12, no dual gate logic). → 13-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments).
- **Dead manifest deliverable name removed** — `sample_fanout`'s `name: merged.txt` (a file no step ever wrote) is gone, eliminating the `single_file` fallback path. → 13-04-SUMMARY.md (Task 1).
- **Bit-rotted test seams retired** — `test_phase3_token_delta_live` no longer references the deleted `_build_context_message`/`_extract_html_skeleton` (grep 0/0); it targets the current `_compose_context_message` + the `html_skeleton` capability, with a module-level seam-existence assert so a future engine rename fails at OFFLINE collection, not a paid live run. → 13-04-SUMMARY.md (Task 2).
- **Migration-template prose stripped** — the 3 app_builder compliance/governance prompts had all Java/.NET/Maven/NuGet/legacy/parallel-run language removed (grep 0); `app-test-implementation`'s Java/.NET framework pins were de-templated in a Rule-2 deviation. → 13-03-SUMMARY.md (Deviations). No frontmatter, registry, or agent-id renames. INV-3 gates stayed armed: zero golden/snapshot files modified across all six plans; banned-pattern (`if pipeline_type ==` → 0) and import-linter (4 contracts kept) green throughout.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **SC-001 zero-engine-edit / kernel-name-free clauses (INV-1), per plan:**
  - 13-01: streaming branch keys purely on the gate object's capability surface (`getattr evaluate_stream`) — no workflow/agent-id literal added to `agents/execution_engine/`.
  - 13-05: the only new literal in the kernel is the generic kind `"deliverable"` (and `"summary"` in the chain) — `grep ppt_output\|od_ppt_output agents/execution_engine/` = 0; no `*_output` workflow literal.
  - 13-06: failure tracking keys on counters only; the `missing_template_context` ingress guard keys on declared `spec.injects` membership of `"template"` — NOT on any pipeline name (generic, INV-1). 13-04 is a manifest-data-only fix (zero `agents/execution_engine/` edits in its commits).
- **D-13-06-a (product decision, recorded as a code comment at the guard):** bare `prototype`/`ppt` remain ADMISSIBLE pipeline types but REQUIRE template context — they fail fast with `missing_template_context` instead of silently collapsing; the FE always sends `od_*` aliases, so no user-visible flow changes except the broken ones becoming visible. → 13-06-SUMMARY.md (decisions).
- **D-13-05-a:** the FR-014 deliverable ref uses `visibility="workspace"` (Phase 05-06 producer-write policy) so the same-owner cross-run `_handle_revision` read passes the owner+visibility scope filter; `assert_owns(parent_run_id)` stays FIRST (T-5-SEED ordering, L16 ownership). → 13-05-SUMMARY.md (Task 1/2).
- **D-13-03-a:** API prefix standardized on the literal `/api/v1`; default app_builder stack is Node.js/TypeScript when upstream context does not pin one. → 13-03-SUMMARY.md (Decisions Made).
- **INV-13 (deepagents-only):** no new agent-loop code; the handoff coder constructs a FRESH `DeepAgentRunner` per retry attempt (independent one-shots, no re-implemented loop). → 13-02-SUMMARY.md (Task 2); 13-VERIFICATION.md (Invariant Spot-Checks).
- **Generic (no-workflow-name) approach pattern:** the `missing_template_context` guard demonstrates the INV-1 idiom — gate ingress on a *declared inject* (`template` in `spec.injects`), never on a pipeline name; the `pipeline_failed`/degraded branches gate on observed `agent_error` counts, never on `spec.id`.

## Phase 14 — run_revision Real Revision Loop (F2 end-to-end) (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/14-run-revision-real-revision-loop-f2-end-to-end/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-12) — VERIFIED 4/4 must-haves; **SC4 now LIVE-CONFIRMED** at the milestone-end live re-pass (2026-06-12, real Bedrock Haiku 4.5): the run_revision MECHANICS passed live; SC4 *content* ("revised deck in the preview") was blocked by NEW finding **LV-02** (a parent-deliverable issue OUTSIDE Phase 14's surface) and handed to **Phase 15**, which CLOSED it live (2026-06-13).  ·  **Plans:** 4/4  ·  **Plan-id → source plan §25:** (post-milestone, no plan-id)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — completes the **live-verification finding F2** (`run_revision`) end-to-end, beyond its 13-05/WR-06 scoped fixes (validation + FE routing). NOTE on numbering spaces: this is the **live-finding F2** (run_revision deliverable was the Phase-3 stub), NOT the glossary factory-leak F2 — the two F-number spaces are distinct; and the **LV-xx** findings below are a THIRD, distinct space (milestone-end live re-pass, 2026-06-12, `REPORT-2026-06-12.md`). No REQUIREMENTS.md row maps to Phase 14 (the lone `(F2)` in REQUIREMENTS.md belongs to the unrelated AGENTRT-04). → ROADMAP.md "### Phase 14" / 14-VERIFICATION.md (Requirements Coverage)
**One-line outcome:** `run_revision` now drives the registry's real revision pipeline agents through the normal `execute()` chokepoint (a model actually runs — proven live: 118-frame revision stream) and persists the agents' **revised** artifact under the exact target kind with `derived_from` lineage — replacing the Phase-3 echo stub that wrote the composed context back as the "revision" with a fake `total_duration: 0.0` completion.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

**Closes the Phase-13 deferred feature.** Phase 13 (live finding F2) only closed the *routing + contract* half: `_handle_revision` resolved the parent via the FR-014 chain, composed a three-section revision context, then wrote that CONTEXT back as the "revision" and emitted a hand-rolled `pipeline_complete` with `total_duration: 0.0` — **no model ever ran** (the "Phase-3 stub"). The register's Phase-13 "deferred feature" note (run_revision deliverable still the Phase-3 stub) is **SUPERSEDED here**. → 14-04-SUMMARY.md (Implementation Register Note); ROADMAP.md "### Phase 14" (Goal, engine.py:3656 cited)

**Goal:** dispatch the registry's real revision pipelines (`ppt_revision` / `od_ppt_revision`, resolved via the WR-06 generic alias) through `execute()` with the composed context as `user_message`, and persist the run's actual deliverable as the `derived_from` artifact. → ROADMAP.md "### Phase 14" (Goal)

**Four success criteria** (each → ROADMAP.md "### Phase 14" Success Criteria):
1. SC1 — `run_revision` against a completed parent drives the real revision agents (model runs observed) and the final deliverable is a revised artifact, not the instruction/context blob.
2. SC2 — the revision artifact persists with `derived_from` lineage to the parent original, owner/workspace-scoped, and revision-of-revision still resolves (FR-014 chain link 1).
3. SC3 — stub-pinning tests (`test_revision_intelligence`, `test_run_revision_fe_contract`) updated to the real-dispatch contract; non-revision characterization goldens stay byte/event-identical (INV-3); no workflow-name literal added to the kernel (SC-001).
4. SC4 — live-confirmed on real Bedrock as part of the **milestone-end live pass** (FE-exact `run_revision` frame → revised deck in the preview). At phase-close (offline) scored ✓ by confirming the deferral was **recorded**; the milestone-end re-pass (2026-06-12) then **executed it live** — see §6.

**Known design wrinkle and how it was settled:** `od_*_revision` agents *could* declare `injects: [template, design_system]`, which would collide with the 13-06 `missing_template_context` ingress guard when revising from a completed parent. Resolution (locked): dispatch passes `od_context=None`; **no revision agent declares any inject** today; the 13-06 guard lives ONLY at the `run_pipeline` ingress, which `_handle_revision → execute()` never traverses; the composed context already embeds the complete parent deck (the physical realization of the template). A future inject-declaring revision agent must FIRST persist `template_id` on `WorkflowRun` (additive migration, Q3) — made loud by an executable pin (14-01). → 14-RESEARCH.md (Settled Design Wrinkle); 14-01-PLAN.md (Task 1 step 4)

**Depends on:** Phase 13. **Wave structure:** Wave 1 = 14-01 (manifest flips + parity traps + scripted harness) ∥ 14-02 (WS queue dispatch, stub-compatible); Wave 2 = 14-03 (engine real dispatch, blocked on 14-01); Wave 3 = 14-04 (intelligence-suite rewrite + phase gate, blocked on 14-03). → ROADMAP.md "### Phase 14" (Plans)

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

- **Real-dispatch path** — `_handle_revision` now does `revision_pipeline_type = f"{target.removesuffix('_output')}_revision"` (WR-06 alias, data-only) → `get_pipeline_agents(revision_pipeline_type)` → `async for event in self.execute(agents=…, user_message=revision_context, pipeline_type=revision_pipeline_type, od_context=None, gate_agent_ids=[], parent_run_id=…)`, forwarding every pre-stamped event verbatim through the RAW `websocket_send_fn`, capturing `final_output` from `pipeline_complete` and a `terminal_failed` flag from `pipeline_failed`. → engine.py:3882 / 14-03-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments); 14-VERIFICATION.md (truth 1)
- **Pre-dispatch guards** — empty `get_pipeline_agents` → ValueError naming the unsupported target (Pitfall 7); **CR-02 review fix** added a second gate: `compile_for_run(revision_pipeline_type).planner != "skip"` → ValueError ("not revision-dispatchable"), so a `planner: run` target fails fast (mapped to `revision_validation_error`) instead of hanging at the clarify gate. Both predicates are compiled-manifest DATA — SC-001 held. → engine.py:3838/3856 / 14-REVIEW-FIX.md (CR-02); 14-VERIFICATION.md (Required Artifacts → engine.py)
- **`derived_from` lineage write** — post-dispatch, guarded `if final_output and not terminal_failed:`, `ArtifactGraph.write_ref` + `ScopedStore.write_ref` with `kind=target_artifact_type`, `content=final_output`, `derived_from=original.id`, `workspace_id=original.workspace_id`, `visibility="workspace"`, `producer_agent=agents[-1].id` (derived from resolved specs — IN-05 spirit, no literal). On persist failure: keep `state_restoration_failed` vocabulary, do NOT fail the run. → engine.py:3908-3937 / 14-03-SUMMARY.md (Exact-kind lineage); 14-PATTERNS.md (Post-dispatch lineage write)
- **`_handle_revision_execution`** (WS) — module coroutine mirroring `_handle_workflow_execution`: revision row creation, per-run event queue, inner `_run_revision_to_queue` bg engine task, sentinel-terminated drainer with 10s heartbeat. Revision runs now register in `_PIPELINE_TASKS`/`_PIPELINE_QUEUES` (cancellable via `cancel_pipeline`, reconnect-discoverable via the existing owner-gated path) and clean via `_cleanup_pipeline`. → websocket.py:1787 / 14-02-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments)
- **Manifest planner flips** — `ppt_revision` (2 steps) and `od_ppt_revision` (1 step) declare `planner: skip` so a dispatched revision bypasses the planner LLM call AND the indefinite clarify `event.wait()` (`prototype_revision`/`user_stories_revision`/`app_builder_revision` ride `run_pipeline` and are explicitly OUT OF SCOPE — untouched). → 14-01-SUMMARY.md (Files Created/Modified)
- **Terminal-status fidelity** — `pipeline_complete`→completed, `pipeline_complete` with `data.status=="degraded"`→degraded (WR-02), `pipeline_failed`/no terminal→failed, `CancelledError`→cancelled; no path leaves the row "revising". This stops a failed revision being offered as a future revision parent by the FE `status === "completed"` lookup. → websocket.py:1946-1979 / 14-02-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments)
- **Registry-derived `agent_count`** — `len(get_pipeline_agents(<derived alias>)) or 1` (ppt_revision=2, od_ppt_revision=1, unknown→cosmetic 1), never hardcoded. → websocket.py:1875 / 14-02-SUMMARY.md
- **WR-03 reconnect section contract** — live-attach drainer derives `_reattach_section` from the owner-filtered AUTHZ-03 gate row via the inverse suffix transform (`od_ppt_revision` → `od_ppt_output`); non-revision runs keep `section: None` byte-identically. (Live-confirmed at the re-pass: WR-03 PASS — 29 live-attach frames all `section='od_ppt_output'`.) → websocket.py:656-822 / 14-REVIEW-FIX.md (WR-03); REPORT-2026-06-12.md (item 8)
- **Scripted revision-agent harness** — `_scripts_for` branches `od-ppt-revision-agent`, `ppt-revision-agent` (narration-only), `ppt-revision-assembler`; the last agent of each pipeline emits an `<artifact>`-wrapped REVISED deck (sanitizer pass-through, byte-distinct from the od-ppt-validator parent deck). → 14-01-SUMMARY.md (Accomplishments)
- **Events / FE contract** — NO new event types and NO FE-contract change: drained frames keep `{type, chunk: None, section: <target_artifact_type>, data}`; ingress error vocabulary (`empty_revision_instruction`, `missing_revision_params`, `revision_validation_error`, `revision_error`, CR-01 `pipeline_already_running`) byte-preserved. The real terminal `pipeline_start`/`pipeline_complete` pair is now `execute()`'s own. → 14-02-PLAN.md (must_haves); 14-VERIFICATION.md (Data-Flow Trace)
- **Migrations / `_KNOWN`:** none — no new table, no new capability, no `_KNOWN` movement, no new dependency (Package Legitimacy Audit: zero). → 14-03-PLAN.md (Artifacts); 14-RESEARCH.md (Standard Stack)

(The local var `planning_context_unavailable` survives only as input to an INFO log; the deleted item was the terminal *payload key*.) → 14-VERIFICATION.md (Anti-Patterns Found note)

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **D-01 — `od_context=None` + `gate_agent_ids=[]` on dispatch** (the settled design wrinkle): no revision agent declares injects; the 13-06 guard lives only at the `run_pipeline` ingress; the parent deck embedded in the composed context realizes the template. `gate_agent_ids=[]` (not `None`) suppresses the static gate-set fallback — no inter-agent HITL pause on a panel revision. → 14-03-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions)
- **D-02 — failed/empty dispatch writes NOTHING under the exact kind** — a failed revision must never become a future revision parent via FR-014 chain link 1; composes with 14-02's terminal-status fidelity. (Live-confirmed: cancelled runs wrote NO exact-kind ref — no poisoned parents.) → 14-03-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions); T-14-03-05 (14-SECURITY.md); REPORT-2026-06-12.md (item 5)
- **D-03 — lineage-persist failure keeps `state_restoration_failed` but does NOT fail the completed run** (RESEARCH Open Q2): the 13-05 terminal-block `kind="deliverable"` ref keeps FR-014 chain link 2 functional. → 14-03-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions)
- **D-04 — `producer_agent=agents[-1].id`** derived from the resolved spec list (no literal — IN-05 spirit); revision pipelines are 1-2 single_shot steps and the last agent's stream is the declared `ppt`-strategy deliverable. → 14-03-SUMMARY.md; 14-PATTERNS.md
- **Two planner-parity traps must move in lockstep** — `test_manifest_parity.py` (`test_planner_run_everywhere`) AND `test_id_alias_resolver.py` (`test_compiled_planner_is_run_everywhere`) BOTH carve out exactly `{ppt_revision, od_ppt_revision}` → `skip`, every other dispatchable manifest stays `run`. (14-RESEARCH missed trap #2; planning caught it by grep.) `test_id_alias_resolver.py` keeps its OWN `_RUN_REVISION_DISPATCHED` copy with a cross-citing lockstep comment (no cross-module import). → 14-01-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions / Decisions Made)
- **Injects/ingress-guard resolution is an executable pin** — `test_run_revision_revision_agents_declare_no_template_injects` fails CI if any revision agent of either pipeline ever declares `template`/`design_system` injects; that future change requires `template_id` persistence on `WorkflowRun` first (additive, Q3). → 14-01-PLAN.md (Task 1 step 4); T-14-01-02 (14-SECURITY.md)

- **F2 is now end-to-end AND live-confirmed (mechanics).** vs Phase 13 where only routing/contract was closed and the deliverable was still the Phase-3 stub. A model now runs and the revised artifact (not the context blob) is the deliverable. Future agents: do NOT rebuild any of `_handle_revision` real dispatch, `_handle_revision_execution`, the planner flips, the lineage write, or the scripted revision harness — they exist and are live-verified.
- **LV-02 → CLOSED by Phase 15 (the most important carry-forward).** SC4 *content* ("revised deck in the preview") was blocked at the 2026-06-12 re-pass by LV-02 — a **parent-deliverable / prompt-contract** issue (`od-ppt-validator` streamed QA narration instead of re-emitting the deck), explicitly **OUTSIDE Phase 14's surface**. It was handed to **Phase 15 (Live-Pass Prompt Contract Closure)**, which added an `od-ppt-validator` AGENT.md output contract (re-emit the complete corrected deck wrapped in `<artifact>`) — AGENT.md + tests only, ZERO engine/capability edits (the resolver-fallback alternative was rejected as INV-3-sensitive). Phase 15's live re-check (2026-06-13, `PHASE-15-RECHECK.md`) marks LV-02 **CLOSED (live)**: resolved `final_output` = an 18,661-char deck (`<!DOCTYPE html>`, 5 `<section class="slide">`, NOT the 1,350-char QA narration), and an FE-exact `od_ppt_output` revision returned a genuinely revised deck — Phase-14 SC4 content thereby unblocked. **A future agent must NOT re-diagnose LV-02 against Phase-14 code — it was never a Phase-14 defect, and it is already closed.** → PHASE-15-RECHECK.md (LV-02 SC1); ROADMAP.md "### Phase 15"
- **SC4 mechanics-vs-content split is the one thing to internalize:** Phase 14 owns the revision LOOP (dispatch, lineage, terminal fidelity, cancel/reconnect — all live PASS); the CONTENT entering the loop is the PARENT pipeline's deliverable (Phase-15 territory). Do not conflate them.
- **deferred-items.md root cause UPDATED (LV-01 supersedes).** The earlier "expired AWS session / live Bedrock title call" hypothesis for `test_pipeline_cancel.py::test_cancel_marks_workflow_cancelled_and_sends_ack` is **DISPROVEN** — with VALID creds it still fails deterministically (the CPython 3.11 `wait_for` result-vs-cancel race, LV-01). Still out of scope, still NOT a Phase-14 regression (fails byte-identically at HEAD~2). Candidate fix: `asyncio.timeout()` at the three drainer sites (websocket.py:1669/802/1999). → deferred-items.md (UPDATE 2026-06-12); REPORT-2026-06-12.md (LV-01)
- **Live (iteration-2 Info) survivors — informational, none blocking; several CONFIRMED live at the re-pass:** IN-01 final bg-task wait swallows `CancelledError` (mirrored run_pipeline debt); IN-02 `pipeline_cancelled` ack typically not delivered on cancel (LIVE-CONFIRMED — driver idled out; the run row still lands `cancelled` and the durable log replays the outcome); IN-03 two `section` shapes for the revision-error family; IN-04 durable-REPLAY revision frames still carry `section: None` (WR-03 fix covered live-attach only — LIVE-CONFIRMED at the re-pass); IN-05 residual drain skipped if the bg task outlives the 5s grace. → 14-REVIEW.md (Info IN-01..IN-05); REPORT-2026-06-12.md (Known-Info debt)
- **Pitfalls baked into the contract (do not regress):** planner must stay `skip` on the two manifests or a dispatched revision hangs at the clarify `event.wait()` (Pitfall 1, now also fenced by CR-02); never re-introduce a second stamping counter (Pitfall 2 — the seq-contiguity trap fails it, proven live with contiguous 1..118/1..42); proceed-path tests MUST target only `ppt_output`/`od_ppt_output` (any other `*_output` derives a `planner: run` manifest and hangs). → 14-RESEARCH.md (Common Pitfalls); 14-04-PLAN.md (Targeting rule)
- **Workspace delta gotcha** — `execute()` mints the revision run's OWN workspace (it does NOT inherit `original.workspace_id`); only the exact-kind lineage ref carries the parent's workspace. Resolve run_events scope owner-scoped + recover the minted workspace from the persisted rows, never from the parent. → 14-04-SUMMARY.md (key-decisions); WR-04 (14-REVIEW-FIX.md)

**One-line outcome:** Five AGENT.md *prompt bodies* now carry output contracts — od-ppt-validator must re-emit the complete deck as its single `<artifact>` (so the `od_ppt` deliverable + the FR-014 revision chain carry a real deck, not QA narration), plus sdlc-governance anti-fabrication and app-infra-generator `/api/v1` contracts — with ZERO engine/capability/migration edits (SC-001).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Source: `ROADMAP.md ### Phase 15`, `15-CONTEXT.md`. Depends on **Phase 14** (the FR-014 revision chain / Phase-14 SC4 "revised deck in preview" was *blocked* by LV-02 — see `REPORT-2026-06-12.md` verdict row 6).

Five success criteria (`ROADMAP.md ### Phase 15` → "Success Criteria"):
1. **LV-02 closed** — `od-ppt-validator` AGENT.md carries an output contract (always re-emit the complete corrected deck wrapped in `<artifact>`); on a live `od_ppt` run the resolved `final_output` is the deck (`<section class="slide">`, not QA narration) and an FE-exact `od_ppt_output` revision returns a revised deck (Phase-14 SC4 content unblocked).
2. **sdlc-governance residual (F4) closed** — the `app-`/`dotnet-`/`mulesoft-sdlc-governance` bodies carry anti-fabrication contracts (begin directly with deliverable content; never emit tool-call syntax); live streams show zero `<function_calls>`/`<invoke>` preambles.
3. **infra-generator residual (F5) closed** — `app-infra-generator` makes the `/api/v1` prefix contract forceful and positionally prominent.
4. **Offline pins land** (13-03 precedent) — contract-grep tests for all five prompts + a scripted-model pin that the ppt deliverable resolution yields the validator's re-emitted deck; the 5 characterization goldens stay byte-identical.
5. **Live re-check recorded** — one `od_ppt` run + one revision (~$0.10) demonstrating criterion 1; criteria 2–3 may ride the session or defer to the next live pass (record disposition either way).

**Design wrinkle / REJECTED alternative (LOCKED — do not revisit):** the LV-02 fix is a *prompt output contract only*. The alternative — a **fallback inside the `deliverable: ppt` resolver** (scan earlier streams for the largest `<section>`-bearing artifact) — was **explicitly REJECTED** as an engine-side behavior change, INV-3-sensitive, contradicting the "streamed deck IS the deliverable" capability contract (`15-CONTEXT.md ## LV-02 fix approach (LOCKED)`). `agents/capabilities/deliverables/ppt.py` and `_artifact.py` are READ-ONLY anchors.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **Bodies-only / zero-engine-edit (SC-001) — LOCKED.** Every edit is below the closing frontmatter `---`; frontmatter is byte-frozen on all five agents (durable pin `test_contract_agents_frontmatter_frozen`, hardened by WR-03 to 8 fields × 5 agents). Do not "normalize" od-ppt-validator's intentional `tools: [workspace]` (RESEARCH Pitfall 4).
- **REJECTED: a `deliverable: ppt` resolver fallback** for LV-02 — engine-side, INV-3-sensitive (§1). `ppt.py` / `_artifact.py` are READ-ONLY. A future agent must NOT "fix" any od_ppt deliverable regression by editing the resolver; the fix lives in the validator's output contract.
- **exactly-ONE-artifact wording, never "last/final artifact"** — `unwrap_artifact` is **first-match** (`_artifact.py:34-38`, attribute-tolerant `<artifact[^>]*>`); a leading status artifact would otherwise win the unwrap and reproduce LV-02 (`15-01-SUMMARY.md` Decisions; RESEARCH Pitfall 2).
- **Anti-tool-XML line byte-identical across the 3 sdlc bodies; deliverable-start line tailored** — app = `filename:` fenced block, dotnet/mulesoft = first Markdown heading; importing the filename-block phrase into the migration prompts would change their deliverable format (Pitfall 5).
- **Forbidden tokens named exactly once per body, terse, no multi-line bad-example exemplars** — exemplars re-prime fabrication (Pitfall 7; pinned `body.count(token) == 1` per WR-04).
- **Live verification is non-blocking** (project defer-live-verification convention / D-05): phase completes on offline evidence; criteria 2–3 may be recorded as next-live-pass items.

## Phase 16 — Terminal-State Integrity and Reconnect Frame-Contract (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `…/16-terminal-state-integrity-and-reconnect-frame-contract/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-13 — `16-VERIFICATION.md` status `passed`, 5/5 must-haves, 0 overrides)  ·  **Plans:** 4/4  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — closes six root-caused issues from the 2026-06-13 Deep Root-Cause Investigation, clusters A+B: **ISS-016, ISS-017** (cluster A error-swallow), **ISS-007, ISS-002** (cluster A cancel-delivery), **ISS-008, ISS-009** (cluster B reconnect frame-contract) → see `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` top table (all six now `FIXED`) + "Deep Root-Cause Investigation (2026-06-13)" clusters A+B, and `ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 16".
**One-line outcome:** A run that did not truly succeed now *tells* the truth on the live wire (`pipeline_failed`/`status:degraded`), a real Stop *delivers* `pipeline_cancelled` so in-flight cards clear, the Preview *shows* a degraded/failed affordance instead of a neutral empty-state, and durable-replay/reconnect frames *match* live — engine + WebSocket + one FE affordance, zero new tables/migrations, INV-3 parity intact.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Goal (`16-CONTEXT.md` "Phase Boundary"; `ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 16"): make abnormal run outcomes faithful end-to-end. The 6 issues collapse into **3 mechanisms + 1 reconnect-contract pair**:

- **Cluster A — terminal-state integrity (BE+FE).** ISS-016 (a runner-surfaced model/tool error is swallowed into an empty-but-successful `agent_complete`, so the run ends `pipeline_complete` with DONE badges) and its FE face ISS-017 (Preview shows the neutral "Output will appear here" for a terminal empty run). ISS-007 + ISS-002 (ONE cancel root cause): the `pipeline_cancelled` ack never reaches the live wire, so an in-flight card stays RUNNING (007) and the cancel test is flaky (002).
- **Cluster B — reconnect frame-contract (same handler `websocket.py:763-822`).** ISS-008 (durable-replay revision frames carry `section:None`) + ISS-009 (live-attach reconnect ack omits `live`).

Five ROADMAP success criteria (`ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 16"):
1. A runner-surfaced model/tool error on **every** agent ends `pipeline_failed` with `agent_error`s — never an empty `pipeline_complete`; a **partial** failure ends `status:degraded`. (ISS-016)
2. A real `cancel_pipeline` delivers `pipeline_cancelled` on the live wire (FE clears in-flight cards), DB row `cancelled`; `test_pipeline_cancel.py` passes deterministically. (ISS-007/002)
3. The Preview shows a degraded/failed affordance (not the neutral empty-state) for a terminal run with no content, on **both** live and history-reopen, keyed on the server signal. (ISS-017)
4. Durable-replay revision frames carry the real `section` (matching live-attach) and the live-attach `pipeline_reconnected` ack carries `live:true`. (ISS-008/009)
5. INV-3 parity: the 5 characterization goldens (prototype / od_prototype / prototype_revision / od_ppt / app_builder) stay byte-identical; `lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken; zero new tables/migrations.

**Depends on:** Phase 15. **Wave structure** (`ROADMAP.md` Plans block): W1 = 16-01 (A1 engine error-arm) ∥ 16-04 (A2 FE affordance) in parallel — disjoint files; W2 = 16-02 (A3 cancel) — edits `engine.py` after 16-01; W3 = 16-03 (B reconnect-contract) — edits `websocket.py` after 16-02.

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (BUILT)

| Plan | Issue / wave | BUILT (per its SUMMARY) | Key names |
|------|--------------|--------------------------|-----------|
| **16-01** | ISS-016 · W1 | `_run_agent` consume loop gains an `elif etype == "error":` arm that records the runner's bounded error message + breaks; a post-loop `if agent_errored:` branch emits a recoverable `agent_error` and **returns** — deliberately skipping the `output` build, the result-append, and the empty `agent_complete`. The failed agent stays in `_failed_agent_ids`, so the existing F3 terminal machinery maps all-fail → `pipeline_failed`, partial → `pipeline_complete` `status:degraded`. Flipped `test_model_fallback.py::test_non_transient_propagates` (was encoding the bug); added `test_engine_runner_error_arm.py` (fault-injection via the real `execute()` + `ScriptedNonTransientError`). Runner untouched. (`16-01-SUMMARY.md`) | `etype == "error"`, `agent_error`, `pipeline_failed`, `status:degraded`, `_failed_agent_ids`, `ScriptedNonTransientError` |
| **16-02** | ISS-007/002 · W2 | `cancel_pipeline` now calls cooperative `cancel_event.set()` instead of destructive `current_pipeline_task.cancel()`; a per-run `_CANCEL_EVENTS` map + connection-scoped `_run_id_sink` resolve the run owner-scoped. The bg task survives; the engine emits `pipeline_cancelled` through the normal persisted+drained path. Engine **pre-agent cancel gap** closed (`engine.py:1509` break → transition `cancelled` + yield `pipeline_cancelled` + return, not `pipeline_complete`). The 3 queue-get drainers swapped to `asyncio.timeout()`. `pipeline_cancelled_seen` tracking persists `cancelled` on the cooperative normal-yield (auto-fixed Rule-2). Tests: `test_pipeline_cancel.py` deterministic; `test_run_revision_ws_dispatch.py::test_cancellation_lands_row_cancelled` requires the frame in `ws.sent`. | **16-03** | ISS-008/009 · W3 | Replay branch derives `_replay_section` ONCE before the `for _r in _missed:` loop from the owner-scoped run-row `type` via the **WR-06 inverse** (`f"{run_type.removesuffix('_revision')}_output"` when `*_revision`, else `None`), applied at the replay send (was hardcoded `section:None`); non-revision stays `None` (byte-identical to live-attach). Live-attach `pipeline_reconnected` ack gains `"live": True` (symmetric with the no-live-task `live:False`). Note: `RunEvent.type` is the *event* type, so an owner-scoped `WorkflowRun.type` lookup was added in the same already-open `_ws_db` session (no second round-trip, no new/unscoped session). Handler-driven cluster-B tests added to `test_ws_reconnect_replay.py`. (`16-03-SUMMARY.md`) | `_replay_section`, `removesuffix('_revision')`, `pipeline_reconnected`, `"live": True` |

History-reopen failure threaded `page.tsx` → `DashboardLayout` → `PreviewPanel` via `reopenedRunStatus` (set from `fullRun.status`). 8-case `PreviewPanel.degraded.test.tsx` added (incl. the explicit no-signal "no client `empty==failed` guess" case). (`16-04-SUMMARY.md`) | `PipelineRunState`, `failed`/`failedAgents`, `DegradedRunAffordance`, `showFailureAffordance`, `reopenedRunStatus`, `PreviewPanel` |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

No new capability, table, migration, or cross-boundary import — this phase corrects emission/consumption on existing live paths.

- **Engine arms (`backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py`):** the `error` consume-arm in `_run_agent` (verified at `engine.py:2442`, post-loop emit `:2585`, per `16-VERIFICATION.md`); the pre-agent cancel terminal (`:1539-1563`) that transitions `cancelled` + yields `pipeline_cancelled`; a module-level `_sanitize_agent_error` helper (`:105`, added in review-fix WR-02) returning a fixed leak-free client message.
- **Events on the wire (all pre-existing event TYPES; this phase makes them fire faithfully):** `agent_error` (recoverable) → F3 → `pipeline_failed` / `pipeline_complete` `status:degraded`; `pipeline_cancelled` on the cooperative-cancel path; `pipeline_reconnected` ack now carrying `live:true` on live-attach.
- **WS handlers (`backend/app/api/websocket.py`):** cooperative `cancel_event` plumbing (`_CANCEL_EVENTS` map, per-connection `_run_id_sink`, `cancel_event.set()` in `cancel_pipeline`, `cancel_event=` passed to `engine.execute(...)`/`_handle_revision(...)`); `_replay_section` derivation in the durable-replay branch; `"live": True` on the live-attach ack; `pipeline_cancelled_seen` → persist `cancelled`; `asyncio.timeout()` at the 3 queue-get drainers.
- **FE affordance (ONE, additive):** `PipelineRunState` gains optional `failed?`/`failedAgents?` (`frontend/src/types/index.ts`); `WorkflowStatus` union widened to include `"degraded"` and `"revising"` (review-fix WR-01); `DegradedRunAffordance` + `showFailureAffordance` in `PreviewPanel.tsx`; `reopenedRunStatus` / `reopenedFailedAgents` props threaded `page.tsx` → `DashboardLayout` → `PreviewPanel`; `Sidebar.tsx` status maps extended (degraded → amber AlertTriangle, revising → blue Loader2).
- **Schema/API:** **ZERO new tables, ZERO migrations.** `section` stays a WS-frame-wrapper field — `RunEvent` gains no column (the rejected DB-column hack was not taken). The reopened `degraded`/`failed` status and `agents_failed` ids are read from already-persisted run-row fields (`wr.status`, `wr.error` parsed by `parseFailedAgentIds`).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12)

Largely **additive / behavior-correcting** — no module deleted, no dual implementation introduced. Net substitutions:

- **Destructive `current_pipeline_task.cancel()` → cooperative `cancel_event.set()`** for the user-Stop (`cancel_pipeline`) path. Destructive `task.cancel()` survives ONLY on the `WebSocketDisconnect` path (no socket to ack; durable replay covers reconnect) + a defensive fallback when no cooperative event is resolvable. **Single mechanism per path** — the WR-01 residual-drain stayed a documented fallback and was NOT also wired on the cancel path (no dual cancel mechanism — `16-02-SUMMARY.md` Decisions).
- **Hardcoded `"section": None` in the replay loop → derived `_replay_section`** (WR-06 inverse). Non-revision still resolves to `None` (byte-identical to live-attach).
- **PreviewPanel's bare `!hasContent` → neutral empty-state** replaced by the three-way server-keyed branch (neutral kept only for streaming/no-signal).
- **`test_non_transient_propagates` flipped** — it previously *encoded* the ISS-016 bug as the expected contract; it now asserts the fault surfaces (`agent_error` + single-agent `pipeline_failed`).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **SC-001 / INV-1 — every new branch keys on a GENERIC event TYPE, never a name.** The error arm keys on `etype == "error"` only (the `ValidationException` / "Operation not allowed" literal was removed even from the code comment so the SC-001 grep gate returns 0); cancel branches key on the generic cooperative signal; `_replay_section` keys on the generic `run_type` `_revision` suffix transform (the same WR-06 inverse already at the gate-restore/live-attach sites). No workflow/model/provider literal added. (`16-CONTEXT.md` "INVARIANTS"; verified `grep -nE 'ValidationException|Operation not allowed'` → 0 in `engine.py`+`websocket.py`, `16-VERIFICATION.md` SC5.)
- **INV-3 / Q3 byte+event parity — the 5 goldens stay byte-identical.** The scripted model never raises or cancels and always streams non-empty output, so the new `error`/cancel/pre-agent arms are **dormant** on goldens. Proven (not assumed): 44 passed / 7 skipped, NO `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`, re-proven after the review-fix `engine.py` edit (`16-REVIEW-FIX.md` "Invariant Re-proof").
- **Zero new tables, zero migrations.** `section` is presentation-only; adding a `section` DB column was a rejected hack. No alembic/versions file added (`16-VERIFICATION.md` SC5).
- **`lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken** — engine-internal + WS-adapter + FE edits only; no new cross-boundary import.
- **The runner stays as-is (06-05 D-05 LOCKED).** Only the engine's *consumption* of the `{"type":"error"}` yield changed; the non-throttle swallow is preserved. Re-raising all runner exceptions was a rejected hack. (Note: `deep_agent_runner.py:514-516` comment was corrected in WR-03 — the engine no longer ignores `error` events.)
- **Error text is sanitized before it reaches the client.** `_sanitize_agent_error` emits a fixed generic message ("The model rejected this request."); raw `str(exc)` is logged server-side only (review-fix WR-02, T-16-01-ID). Do not widen this to echo raw exception text.
- **The FE affordance is server-keyed — never a client `if (terminal && !finalOutput) show failed` guess** (rejected: re-creates the IN-03 FE-vs-DB disagreement). The signal comes from `pipelineState.failed/.degraded` (live) or the reopened `fullRun.status` (history). Owner/connection scoping preserved on both `cancel_pipeline` (connection-local `_run_id_sink`) and the replay read (`owner_id == user.id`).
- **The WR-06 inverse / WR-03 mirror.** `_replay_section` is the *inverse* of the WR-06 revision `pipeline_type` alias; it *mirrors* the WR-03 live-attach `_reattach_section` contract onto the replay branch (the branch WR-03 left uncovered). Keep them symmetric.

## Phase 17 — Test-Infra and Verification-Gap Closure (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `…/17-test-infra-and-verification-gap-closure/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-13) — `17-VERIFICATION.md` **passed 4/4** (`status: passed`, `score: 4/4 must-haves verified`, `overrides_applied: 0`); review 0 CR / 1 WR (WR-01, fixed) / 2 IN; test-only (no `17-SECURITY.md` — no runtime/attack surface)  ·  **Plans:** 3/3  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone — closes cluster-C deep-investigation findings; maps to no plan-`§25` phase)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — closes **ISS-003 / ISS-010 / ISS-011** (cluster C, the 2026-06-13 Deep Root-Cause Investigation), test-infra only → tracked centrally in `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` (top table `ISS-003 :12`, `ISS-010 :19`, `ISS-011 :20`; cluster-C row `:74`; investigation rows `:45/:52/:53`) and pointed at by `ROADMAP.md ### Phase 17` (`:558-577`). → see §6.
**One-line outcome:** Three harness-only fixes — `_drive_live` now compiles `clarify.mode="off"` (no more clarify-gate hang) and restores both `compile_for_run` and `create_runner` in `finally` (WR-01), the OTel hook is span-verified offline via an injected `InMemorySpanExporter`, and the Phase-8 HITL live-tail tests SKIP (not FAIL) on mid-sweep SSO-token expiry — with **ZERO production code, the 5 characterization goldens byte-identical, lint-imports 4/0** (no INV-3 exposure, no SC-001 engine edit).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Source: `ROADMAP.md ### Phase 17` (`:558-577`), `17-CONTEXT.md ## Phase Boundary`. **Depends on Phase 16.** Framing: close the 3 **cluster-C** issues, all **test-harness / verification-only, ZERO production code, no INV-3 exposure** — the engine / Workspace / FE are not touched (`17-CONTEXT.md <domain> Phase Boundary`, `<decisions> INVARIANTS`). No discuss round — context sourced directly from the cluster-C investigation, code-proven file:line.

Four success criteria (`ROADMAP.md ### Phase 17` → "Success Criteria" `:565-570`):
1. **ISS-003** — `test_phase3_token_delta_live._drive_live` compiles `clarify.mode="off"` (mirroring the shared harness) instead of the dead `ALWAYS_CLARIFY` flag; a fault-injected drive RETURNS instead of hanging at the clarify `event.wait()`; offline collection still skips cleanly.
2. **ISS-010** — a deterministic offline test asserts the OTel hook emits a span with the right attributes via an injected `InMemorySpanExporter` (the hook's module-private `_TRACER` reset; `_build_span_processor` monkeypatched), plus a graceful-degrade case when the OTLP exporter pkg/endpoint is absent. **ISS-010 closed by test, not by a live collector.**
3. **ISS-011** — the Phase-8 HITL live-tail tests SKIP (not fail) on mid-sweep SSO/credential expiry via a per-test `live_skip_reason()` re-check; the collection self-check no longer asserts strict collection-vs-runtime equality; `TestOfflineHITL` stays green offline.
4. **Zero production-code changes** (test/harness files only); the 5 characterization goldens + lint-imports 4/0 unaffected; live re-runs requiring fresh AWS creds recorded as deferred follow-ups.

Out of scope (`17-CONTEXT.md <domain>` OUT OF SCOPE): any production code (`backend/app/**`, non-test `backend/agents/**`); **cluster D** (ISS-004/005/006 → Phase 19); **cluster E** (ISS-014/015/019/021 → Phase 18); and the live re-runs of ISS-003 / ISS-011 that need fresh AWS creds (recorded as deferred).

- **ISS-003:** the dead `engine_mod.ALWAYS_CLARIFY = False` line in `_drive_live` (a no-op since the flag was deleted in 07-05; `grep -rn ALWAYS_CLARIFY agents/ app/` = 0 hits) was **removed** and superseded by the `compile_for_run` clarify-off wrap. `grep -c ALWAYS_CLARIFY tests/agents/test_phase3_token_delta_live.py` == 0 (not reintroduced; comments phrased without the literal token so the count holds).
- **ISS-010:** the prior status DEFERRED → **FIXED-by-test** (closed by the in-memory-exporter test, no live collector). Otherwise additive (2 new otel tests; the existing 8 untouched).
- **ISS-011:** the self-check's strict `_LIVE_SKIP == live_skip_reason()` cross-time equality assertion was **removed** (it was the actual bug — it assumed the SSO session is immortal across a multi-hour run) and replaced by per-point-in-time gate-correctness checks. Status MONITORING → **FIXED** (by-test; Part-1 live re-run deferred). Otherwise additive.
- Everything else is purely additive (new tests, a fixture, a helper, a per-test decorator). No production code superseded.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

1. **TEST-ONLY / zero production code.** The defect in all three is in the harness; the fix lives there. No `backend/app/**`, no non-test `backend/agents/**`, no migrations, no new deps (`17-CONTEXT.md <decisions> INVARIANTS`). The 5 characterization goldens are untouched by construction and stay byte-identical; lint-imports stays 4 kept / 0 broken. Do not "fix" any of these in production code.
2. **ISS-003 — clarify-off via the shared wrap, NOT a production change.** REJECTED (do NOT do): re-add the `ALWAYS_CLARIFY` engine flag (dual-impl, INV-12); add a clarify timeout in `clarify_engine.py` (perturbs production HITL — the gate is deliberately no-timeout); flip the `prototype` manifest `clarify.mode` (breaks INV-3 + `test_manifest_parity.py`). Suppressing clarify removes a HANG, not the A/B token-delta measurement (clarify emits no `input_tokens`). **Isolation: `_drive_live` patches module globals — restore EVERYTHING it patches in `finally` (`compile_for_run` AND `create_runner`, per WR-01); a leaked `create_runner` routes later engine tests into live-Bedrock construction.**
3. **ISS-010 — closed by an in-memory exporter test, NOT a live collector.** The #1 LANDMINE: the hook caches a module-private `_TRACER` and never calls `set_tracer_provider()`, so a global-provider test would silently MISS its spans (a **false green**). Inject via the hook's OWN `_build_span_processor` factory + reset `_TRACER=None`; use `SimpleSpanProcessor` (synchronous, no `force_flush`). REJECTED: stand up a live OTLP collector / `pip install opentelemetry-exporter-otlp` to go green (env-dependent, adds a deliberately-optional dep); a global `set_tracer_provider` test. Live OTLP export remains an OPTIONAL end-of-milestone smoke, not a blocker.
4. **ISS-011 — SKIP-not-FAIL on credential expiry; transient classification UNTOUCHED.** The re-check resolves `live_skip_reason()` via the `live_harness` MODULE (so the offline simulation can monkeypatch it). REJECTED (DANGEROUS): adding "expired"/"security token" to `_TRANSIENT_SUBSTRINGS` / `model_policy.py` so the runner "retries" — an expired SSO token can't be refreshed by a botocore retry, and it would mask real `AccessDenied`/`ValidationException` faults and perturb production error semantics (INV-3-relevant). Also REJECTED: "just re-run" with no harness change (leaves the recurring fault). **The transient-error classification stays as-is — do not widen it.**

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

**BE (additive, parity-neutral):**
- `DeliverableSpec.mimetype: str | None = None` — optional **declared** deliverable shape hint (`backend/agents/workflows/plan.py`); compiler passes it through verbatim (`compiler.py _compile_deliverable`), no defaulting (INV-5).
- New import-pure helper module `backend/agents/capabilities/deliverables/_mimetype.py` (`default_mimetype(strategy, name)`) + a `default_mimetype` staticmethod on each of the four resolvers. Defaults: `single_file`→infer from name extension (`.html`/`.htm`→`text/html`, `.md`→`text/markdown`, unknown→`application/octet-stream`); `serialized_sandbox`→`application/zip`; `streamed_text`→`text/markdown`; `ppt`→`text/html`. **Derived from the DECLARED strategy/name, never content-sniffed.**
- `pipeline_complete` now carries two **unconditional** keys — `deliverable_mimetype` + `deliverable_filename` — sourced from `getattr(ectx.deliverable, …)` beside `final_output` (clean + degraded). No new cross-boundary import.

**FE (generic, SC-001):**
- `GenericDeliverablePreview` — the generic mimetype-dispatched renderer in `PreviewPanel.tsx` (HTML→sandboxed iframe `sandbox="allow-scripts"`, NO `allow-same-origin`; markdown→`MarkdownPreview`; zip→AppBuilder bundle; else→download). Reused on the history-reopen surface (`WorkflowHistory.tsx`).
- Shared `deriveDeliverableMimetype` helper (`frontend/src/types/index.ts`) — the single reopen-side mimetype source imported by both reopen surfaces (live uses the BE-declared `deliverable_mimetype`).
- One generic deliverable row in `FilesTab.tsx` (resolved mimetype + filename via the existing `downloadBlob` path); per-agent `.md` outputs preserved as "Agent outputs".
- **Model picker relocated** into the live `AgentsPopup` Agents tab; `model_overrides` threaded into the `run_pipeline` payload — **MODEL-03 is now reachable end-to-end** (backend `_validate_model_overrides` already validated + persisted it; this closed the FE gap).

**NOTE — zero schema/API churn:** **zero new tables / migrations** (`find -newermt 2026-06-13 migrations` → none). `/api/capabilities` (API-02) + `test_capabilities_api.py` **retained** untouched (8 passed). No reopen-row persistence column added — reopen mimetype is content-derived (see §7 carry-forward).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **Generic mimetype dispatch = SC-001 (LOCKED).** The deliverable renderer dispatches on the **declared mimetype** (live) / output-shape heuristic (reopen), **never a workflow name**. No FE-side workflow-name render branch (grep-clean across `PreviewPanel.tsx`/`page.tsx`/`WorkflowHistory.tsx`). REJECTED hacks (do not reintroduce): hardcode `ui_custom_proto`→iframe; route unknown→the `custom` branch (escaped HTML); BE content-sniff `final_output`.
- **The `custom` type is NOT a known branch (CR-01 fix, LOCKED).** `custom` was removed from `KNOWN_RENDER_TYPES` so a live `custom` HTML deliverable falls into the generic channel and gets the sandboxed iframe (not escaped MarkdownPreview). The 4 bespoke renderers (user_stories/ppt/prototype/app_builder) are untouched — the generic path is a FALLBACK only.
- **HTML is ALWAYS sandboxed (T-18-05, LOCKED).** Both live and reopen iframes are `sandbox="allow-scripts"` with **NO `allow-same-origin`**. `MarkdownPreview` uses NO `rehype-raw` (raw HTML escaped to text) — confirmed XSS-safe (WR-03); do not add `rehype-raw` to the generic markdown path.
- **INV-3: the 5 characterization goldens stay byte-identical via `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` (LOCKED).** The two additive `pipeline_complete` keys live there (mirroring `model_id`/`estimated_cost_usd`); `_REQUIRED_DATA_KEYS["pipeline_complete"]` is a SUBSET check so additive keys pass. Any future `pipeline_complete` key that must stay parity-neutral goes in `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` and must keep deliverable BYTES unchanged.
- **`model_overrides` empty-selection guard (LOCKED).** Only sent when ≥1 non-default model is picked → existing runs are byte-identical. Single ingress: `useWorkflow` `Object.assign(payload, context)` (no double-send).
- **WR-01 effective-strategy mimetype (LOCKED).** When `serialized_sandbox` resolves None and falls back to `streamed_text`, the emitted mimetype is derived from the **effective** strategy (the bytes actually produced), not the declared one — so the advertised mimetype matches the bytes.
- **MODEL-03 reachability decision:** the picker was RELOCATED (preferred branch), delivering MODEL-03 FE↔BE; it was NOT deferred to v2.
- **ISS-015 — WONTFIX at P18, ⚠ SUPERSEDED: reopened & CLOSED by Phase 20.** Phase 18 recorded WONTFIX for a *bare hardcoded* picker; Phase 20 then built the **data-driven** version the guardrail itself prescribed — a `WorkflowCatalog` that reads `GET /api/workflows` + the `user_launchable` flag (never a hardcoded name dropdown), launching via the existing run/wizard paths. So the rule still holds (**do not build a hardcoded-name picker**) but the catalog now IS the launch surface. → Phase 20 section. The original rationale: wizards collect template+design-system (a bare picker structurally can't); bare `prototype`/`ppt` are template-gated-unreachable; `sample_*` are test fixtures.

## Phase 19 — Prompt and Deliverable Adherence (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/19-prompt-and-deliverable-adherence/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-13) — gsd-verifier PASS, 4/4 must-haves, 0 overrides  ·  **Plans:** 3/3  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone — no source-plan id, like Phases 13–18)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — closes **cluster D** of the 2026-06-13 deep root-cause investigation (`ISS-006` / `ISS-005` / `ISS-004`) with **durable STRUCTURAL** fixes; subsumes the earlier F4/F5 live-verification residuals (ISS-004/005) + the app_builder cross-agent drift (ISS-006). Phase 15 had only added the prompt-only contracts; Phase 19 adds the deterministic backstops. → see `.planning/ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 19" + `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` "Deep Root-Cause Investigation" (cluster D) rows ISS-004/005/006.
**One-line outcome:** Three non-deterministic-model residuals get code-level guards — a shared `getDatabase` accessor literal threaded across the app_builder producer/consumer prompts (+ grep pin), a pure-stdlib `api_prefix` Validator wired as an **event-free `post_step`** on the infra step, and the existing tool-XML sanitizer extended to the **streamed `agent_chunk`** path with chunk-straddle buffering — all three landed with the 5 characterization goldens byte/event-identical.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Cluster D = the three prompt/deliverable-adherence residuals. The phase boundary (`19-CONTEXT.md` → "Phase Boundary") is explicit: close them with **DURABLE structural fixes, NOT prompt-only "ask the model nicely"**, so a non-deterministic model can't silently regress them. Depends on **Phase 18**.

Four success criteria (`ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 19"):
1. **ISS-006** — the app_builder producer (`app-code-generator`) + consumer (`app-test-implementation`) AGENT.md bodies share a single canonical `getDatabase` accessor literal (incl. the Jest/Vitest global-setup file `tests/setup.ts`/`globalSetup`), pinned by an offline acceptance-grep; reverting either side fails the pin. INV-3-safe (scripted model ignores prompt bodies).
2. **ISS-005** — a pure-stdlib `api_prefix` `Validator` capability (modeled on `spec_plan_coverage.py`) exists, self-registers, and is wired as an **event-free `post_step`** on the app_builder infra-generator step — flagging infra endpoints lacking `/api/v1` and writing a `validation_results` row, **WITHOUT adding any event to the run** (so the app_builder golden stays byte-identical). Import-pure (lint-imports 4/0).
3. **ISS-004** — the engine sanitizes the streamed `agent_chunk` path (reusing `_strip_fabricated_tool_xml`, with a chunk-straddle buffer) for tool-less agents, so fabricated `<function_calls>`/`<invoke>` XML is removed from the live UI stream, not just the accumulated output. SC-001: keys on the generic tool-less condition, never a workflow name.
4. **INV-3 parity** — the 5 characterization goldens stay byte/event-identical; lint-imports 4/0; zero new tables/migrations. The live re-confirms (004/005/006) are recorded as next-live-pass items, not phase blockers.

Added offline acceptance-grep pin `test_getdatabase_accessor_contract_shared` asserting on `load_agent_spec(id).prompt_body` for both agents; fault-injected both revert directions. Body-only edits — frontmatter untouched. | `app-code-generator/AGENT.md`, `app-test-implementation/AGENT.md`, `tests/agents/test_prompt_contracts.py` | `58f2ae09`, `d98cc421` |

Wired at the `agent_chunk` yield, **probe-gated** on the tool-less runner capability (feed a fabricated-XML probe to `sanitize_output` once → detects an active sanitizer; tool-using streams bypass entirely → byte-AND-chunk-identical). ### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

**New capability — `api_prefix` Validator** (`backend/agents/capabilities/validators/api_prefix.py`): pure-stdlib, registered `@register("validator", "api_prefix", user_allowed=True)`. Modeled verbatim on `spec_plan_coverage.py` (`Issue` dataclass, single `map_severity` source, `_worst_label`/`_SEVERITY_ORDER`/`_record` helpers, `await target.runner.record_validation_result`). Single module constant `API_PREFIX = "/api/v1"`. Import-pure (no `app.*`/`execution_engine` import), sandbox-confined, degrade-not-crash.

**New post_step — `api_prefix_audit`** (`backend/agents/capabilities/post_steps/api_prefix_audit.py`): registered `@register("post_step", "api_prefix_audit")`, mirrors `revision_validation.py` — resolves the `api_prefix` validator via the registry, builds a tiny `DeliverableContext`-shaped target from `ctx`, runs it side-effects-only, **emits NO events**, wrapped in try/except that never aborts the run.

**Two-surface registration** (`registry.py`): both `(kind,name)` pairs added to the `_KNOWN` literal (compiler `is_registered` membership) AND both module paths added to `discover()`'s `_builtin_modules` import tuple (runtime `@register` → `_IMPLS` binding). `_KNOWN` count is now **63** (was 61); the drift guard's `_EXPECTED_NAMES` mirror in `test_registry_capabilities.py` was updated in lockstep. The post_step invocation seam is `engine.py:1889-1891` (`_run_step`).

**Manifest wiring** (`app_builder/workflow.yaml`): `post_step: api_prefix_audit` on the `app-infra-generator` step with `gates: []` — NOT a `gates:[validation]` gate.

**Streamed-chunk sanitizer reuse** (`engine.py`): `_ChunkStreamSanitizer` delegates the actual stripping to the existing runner `sanitize_output` / `_strip_fabricated_tool_xml` (13-02) — no second/divergent regex; it only token-scans to find WHERE to hold an open/partial span.

**Shared accessor literal** (`getDatabase`): a prompt-contract literal threaded across the two app_builder AGENT.md bodies (13-03 shared-literal pattern), pinned by the offline grep.

**Schema:** **ZERO new tables / migrations.** The `validation_results` table already exists; the validator writes an audit row into it. No DB rows are golden-tracked.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12)

Largely **additive** — no engine subsystem was removed. The structural substance is **supersession of the Phase-15 prompt-only contracts with enforced structural backstops**, stated explicitly:
- ISS-005's prose-only `/api/v1` rule (3 AGENT.md bodies, zero code enforcement) is now backed by the deterministic `api_prefix` validator — the prompt remains, but it is no longer the sole enforcement mechanism.
- ISS-004's accumulated-output-only sanitization (`sanitize_output()` post-loop) is extended to the streamed path — the single sanitization locus now covers the live UI stream + durable-replay collector + reconnect tail, closing the gap the prior design left intentionally unfiltered.
- ISS-006's model-invented accessor name on each side is replaced by one shared canonical literal.
- Minor in-phase cleanup during the review-fix pass: dead/duplicate branches and an unused `Issue.validator` field were removed from `api_prefix.py` (IN-01/IN-02).

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **The `api_prefix` enforcement is an EVENT-FREE `post_step`, NOT a `gates:[validation]` gate.** This is the load-bearing INV-3 decision. The `validation` gate emits a `validation_warning` event when residual issues exist; the 85-event app_builder golden has no such slot → a gate breaks parity. Proven both directions: event-free → golden byte-identical; the in-test gate-wiring fault-injection emits the warning (golden would FAIL). **Do not "promote" this validator to a gate on the golden pipeline.**
- **The chunk sanitizer is a same-object no-op on clean text** and **probe-gated on the tool-less runner capability** — a tool-using agent's stream is byte-AND-chunk-identical (not merely join-identical). The authoritative `output_chunks` path stays RAW so the post-loop `sanitize_output` keeps the golden output. **Do not double-strip into the authoritative path; do not strip in the FE; do not add a second/divergent regex.**
- **WR-01 hold-threshold:** the partial-opener hold only fires on a trailing prefix of length ≥ 2 (`range(len-1, 1, -1)`), so a lone `<` — common in legit tool-less prose (HTML/JSX, `a < b`) — is never buffered and chunk granularity is preserved.
- **SC-001 keying:** the validator keys on infra-file content + the declared capability; the chunk sanitizer keys on the generic tool-less probe; the prompt literal is a shared contract. **No workflow/agent-name (`app_builder`, `sdlc-governance`, `app-infra-generator`, `prototype-`) branch in the kernel** (grep gate = 0; the sole `app_builder` token in the engine is an INV-3 rationale comment, not a branch).
- **INV-3:** the 5 characterization goldens (prototype/od_prototype/prototype_revision/od_ppt/app_builder) stay byte/event-identical. Three independent guards: (006) scripted model never reads prompt bodies; (005) the post_step is event-free + DB rows aren't golden-tracked; (004) goldens normalize `agent_chunk.chunk` to a sentinel + pin only post-sanitized `final_output`/`output_length`.
- **Import purity:** validator + post_step shim are pure-stdlib; `lint-imports` stays **4 kept / 0 broken**.
- **Additive only:** zero new tables/migrations (`validation_results` pre-exists).
- **Two-surface registration discipline:** a capability in `_KNOWN` but absent from `discover()`'s `_builtin_modules` compiles but raises `RuntimeError("known but has no bound impl")` at the runtime post_step seam. Both surfaces moved together; a post-discover `resolve()`-returns-instance test pins `_IMPLS` is bound.
