## Phase 20 — Workflow Catalog: Data-Driven Browse-and-Launch (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `…/20-workflow-catalog-data-driven-browse-and-launch-gallery-reali/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-14) — `20-VERIFICATION.md` verdict `passed`, 5/5 must-haves verified, 0 overrides  ·  **Plans:** 2/2  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone — not in the milestone plan-id ledger)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — **realizes WF-DB-01's browse/launch half** (the user-facing catalog; persistence is P21) and **closes ISS-015**, *reversing the Phase 18-05 WONTFIX*: the generic pipeline-type picker P18 dispositioned WONTFIX-by-design is now BUILT, data-driven, exactly per the guardrail P18 wrote for it. Sources: `.planning/ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 20" (goal + 5 SC + 2 plans); `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` ISS-015 rows (line 24 disposition `SHIPPED — Phase 20 / WF-DB-01`, line 59 `✓ → SHIPPED — Phase 20`, line 82 sweep note).
**One-line outcome:** The hardcoded home tiles get a data-driven sibling — a `WorkflowCatalog` in-dashboard view that fetches the live `GET /api/workflows`, two-gate-filters on a NEW additive `user_launchable` manifest flag AND the existing `canRunPipeline` tier check (never a hardcoded name list = SC-001), and launches each row through the EXISTING run/wizard paths — with the 5 INV-3 goldens byte-identical and zero new tables/migrations/engine edits.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

From `.planning/ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 20" (goal + 5 SC) and `20-SPEC.md` §1–§8 (the file:line-grounded reuse contract) / `20-CONTEXT.md` (LOCKED decisions). **Depends on Phase 19.** This is the **SC-001 dividend made visible** — the kernel already runs any workflow by manifest, so "launch any workflow" is wiring, not engine surgery (`20-SPEC.md §1` "Why it matters architecturally").

1. **(SC-1)** Catalog renders ONLY `user_launchable` ∧ tier-entitled workflows from a **live `GET /api/workflows` fetch** — no hardcoded name list; `sample_*` fixtures, `*_revision`, and `od_*` internal types never appear.
2. **(SC-2)** Idea-box workflows launch through the **existing single `run_pipeline` send site**; `prototype`/`ppt` route into the **existing template wizard** (no bare run); gated workflows show the **existing lock/upgrade affordance**.
3. **(SC-3)** Net-new UI is minimal + **reuse-bound** — the catalog is a data-driven `CreationHub` mounted as ONE new `DashboardLayout` view; each new file names the existing analog it copied (REUSE-MANDATE, first-class).
4. **(SC-4)** Backend change is **additive-only** — the 5 manifest fields surfaced through the existing endpoint; **NO new tables, NO migration**.
5. **(SC-5)** INV-3 parity holds (5 characterization goldens byte-identical) · `lint-imports` 4/0 · a **mocked Playwright e2e** asserts filter + launch + gating · a **manifest-schema unit test** covers the new optional fields while strict-key still rejects `when/if/for/expr` (INV-5).

**REUSE-MANDATE (first-class, `20-SPEC.md §3`):** every new surface bound to an existing analog — `CreationHub` rows+launch-fork+gating, `DashboardLayout` view-state machine, `AppHeader` nav, `AgentModelPicker` data-fetch shell, `entitlements.ts`, the existing `run_pipeline` + template-wizard launch paths. "No analog found: None" (`20-PATTERNS.md` §"No Analog Found" — the SC-001 reuse dividend).

Surfaced via the EXISTING `GET /api/workflows`: 4 fields added to `WorkflowSummary`, `list_workflows` additively `load_manifest(workflow_id, _WORKFLOWS_DIR)` (guarded → defaults on failure) and populates them. `user_launchable: true` set on 7 launchable `workflow.yaml`s (5 plain + prototype/ppt); prototype/ppt also carry `launch_surface: "wizard"`. | **85 passed** (5 goldens byte-identical + manifest + workflows_api, NO `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`); `lint-imports` 4/0; 0 migrations. `20-01-SUMMARY.md` "Verification Evidence". |

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

**BE (additive, parity-neutral) — the ENTIRE backend change (`20-SPEC.md §4`):**
- `WorkflowManifest` gains 5 **inert discovery-metadata** fields (`backend/agents/workflows/manifest.py`): `user_launchable` (bool, default `False`) + `display_name`/`description`/`icon`/`launch_surface` (`str | None`, default `None`). Plus two validators: `_optional_bool` (bool-accept-only) and `_optional_str`. The 5 keys added to `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS`.
- Surfaced through the **EXISTING authenticated `GET /api/workflows`** (`backend/app/api/workflows.py`): 4 fields added to `WorkflowSummary`; `list_workflows` enumerates generically over `PIPELINE_AGENTS`, additively reads the manifest, and populates them with a `display_name or _display_name(id)` fallback. The endpoint stays **DB-free** (module docstring: "this router issues no DB query at all") and **off the golden path**.
- 7 launchable manifests flagged in **data** (the `user_launchable` YAMLs): `app_builder`, `user_stories`, `custom`, `mulesoft_to_springboot`, `dotnet_to_azure` (plain-run) + `prototype`, `ppt` (also `launch_surface: "wizard"`). Everything else (revisions, `od_*`, `reverse_engineer`, `chat`, `sample_*`) stays unflagged → `user_launchable=False` → excluded by gate 1 **without any name-based filter**.

**FE (generic, reuse-pure, SC-001):**
- `getWorkflowDefinitions(token)` + `WorkflowSummary` interface in `frontend/src/lib/api.ts` (the new manifest fetcher, analog `getCapabilities`).
- `frontend/src/components/catalog/WorkflowCatalog.tsx` — the data-driven `CreationHub`; props `onSelectFeature` + `userTier` (identical to `CreationHub`, so it drops into the same mount). Friendly label via the **exported** `getWorkflowLabel(id)` (`useNotifications`) — `WORKFLOW_LABELS`/`TYPE_META` are module-private; rows carry no leading icon (CreationHub rows don't either — see §7 / `20-REVIEW.md` IN-03).
- The `"catalog"` **in-dashboard view** (one `MainView` value + one `motion.div key="catalog"` block in `DashboardLayout.tsx`) and the Catalog **nav button** (`AppHeader.tsx`, `LayoutGrid` icon).
- Tests: `WorkflowCatalog.test.tsx` (vitest two-gate filter + friendly-label fallback) and `frontend/e2e/tests/ts-z.catalog.spec.ts` (mocked Playwright; per-spec `page.route('**/api/workflows*')` reverse-registered to win over the fixtures' `**/api/**` catch-all — no fixture edit).

**NOTE — zero schema/API churn:** **NO new tables, NO migration, NO new launch endpoint, NO new Next route** (`src/app/catalog/page.tsx` absent). Q3's "every new table carries `owner_id`+`workspace_id`" is **not triggered** — there is no new table. The launch path is the existing workflow-agnostic WS `run_pipeline` (`20-SPEC.md §2`).

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12)

- **No code deleted.** Phase 20 is **purely additive** — the catalog is built **alongside** the existing hardcoded 6-tile `CreationHub` home surface, not in place of it (the catalog is a *new* `DashboardLayout` view reachable from a *new* nav button). The hardcoded `WORKFLOWS` module-const is **NOT present in the catalog** (`grep -c "const WORKFLOWS" WorkflowCatalog.tsx` → 0) — the catalog reuses `CreationHub`'s row JSX/launch/gating but replaces the const array with the live fetch (SC-001). `CreationHub.tsx`'s own `WORKFLOWS` const is untouched (home tiles still exist).
- **ISS-015 WONTFIX *reversed*.** P18-05 dispositioned ISS-015 (the generic pipeline-type picker) **WONTFIX-by-design** with a guardrail ("if ever built, a manifest-driven picker MUST read `GET /api/workflows` + a `user_launchable` flag — NOT a hardcoded dropdown"). Phase 20 **builds exactly that**, flipping ISS-015 to **SHIPPED/FIXED** (`ISSUES-REGISTER.md` line 24: `SHIPPED — Phase 20 / WF-DB-01 (was WONTFIX 18-05)`; line 59: `✓ → SHIPPED — Phase 20`). The earlier register's "Do NOT build the generic pipeline-type picker" instruction is **now obsolete** — it exists.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **SC-001 — launchability keys on a DECLARED flag, never a name list (LOCKED).** Gate 1 (`user_launchable`) is a declared manifest flag enumerated generically over `PIPELINE_AGENTS`; gate 2 is the pre-existing `canRunPipeline` tier check. The `CreationHub` `WORKFLOWS` const is genuinely **absent** from the catalog (`20-REVIEW.md` Summary, `20-VERIFICATION.md` Truth 1). **A brand-new manifest setting `user_launchable: true` joins the catalog with ZERO FE/BE edit.** To add a workflow to the catalog: set the manifest flag — do NOT add a hardcoded name. REJECTED hack (do not reintroduce): a hardcoded type dropdown (ships broken template-gated buttons + exposes test fixtures — the exact reason ISS-015 was WONTFIX'd, `ISSUES-REGISTER.md` line 59).
- **Additive-only — NO new tables/migrations in P20 (LOCKED).** Manifests are version-controlled `workflow.yaml` data; the endpoint is DB-free. Persistence of *user-authored* workflows is **Phase 21**, not here (Q5: file-backed manifests now, DB-backed user workflows later).
- **INV-3 — 5 characterization goldens byte-identical (LOCKED).** `prototype` / `od_prototype` (alias) / `prototype_revision` / `od_ppt` / `app_builder`. The 5 new fields default `False`/`None` and are **never read by the compiler/kernel** (the compiler reads only `steps/context_providers/deliverable/planner/clarify/seed_files/allowed_workers/limits`) → `CompiledWorkflow` byte-identical, run/event path untouched. Same inert posture as the shipped `model`/`limits` forward fields (D-06). The plan RAN the goldens (no `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`) to prove identity, not rewrite baselines.
- **INV-5 — strict-key still rejects `when/if/for/expr` (LOCKED).** `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS` widened ONLY with the 5 non-control-flow presentation/visibility keys; the strict-key rejection test is preserved unchanged → no DSL. Do NOT add control-flow keys to the allow-list.
- **Ports & Adapters — `lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken (LOCKED).** `manifest.py` is pure-data (gains no import); `workflows.py`'s new `_WORKFLOWS_DIR` import from `agents.execution_engine.engine` is a permitted `app → agents` edge. No source→forbidden edge created.
- **Reuse-bound UI — net-new styling is a defect (LOCKED, `20-UI-SPEC.md §0`).** The catalog introduces NO new visual language; every pixel inherits a reused component's Tailwind classes. NO raw API `name` rendered; NO `od_*`/`*_revision`/`sample_*` rows visible; in-dashboard view (not a Next route).
- **Two gates ANDed (LOCKED).** A row shows iff `user_launchable` (product workflow) AND `can_run_pipeline(tier, type)` (this user). Gated-but-launchable rows render the lock/upgrade affordance (shown-locked, NOT hidden — discoverable upgrade path). Tiers are **basic/pro/enterprise** (no `admin`).
- **Launchability is discovery metadata, NOT authorization (LOCKED, threat T-20-02).** `user_launchable` does NOT authorize a run — launch still crosses the server-side `can_run_pipeline` gate in the existing `run_pipeline` WS path. A client forging `user_launchable=true` cannot launch a workflow its tier forbids. The catalog is **not a new trust boundary**.
- **`prototype`/`ppt` route to the wizard, never a bare run (LOCKED).** They require template + design-system (the wizard `canContinue` gate); the catalog only does the `router.push` to `/workflow/{type}/templates`. `custom` seeds zero agents → lands on the idea page needing AgentsPopup assembly first (disabled-Run state, not an error).

Wants a future fixture-owning pass. All Phase-20 created/modified files are tsc-clean.
- **Do NOT rebuild:** the data-driven catalog (`WorkflowCatalog.tsx`, the `"catalog"` view, `getWorkflowDefinitions`, the 5 manifest fields, the 7 launchable YAMLs) already EXISTS — **extend by setting `user_launchable` on a manifest, never by adding a hardcoded name** (SC-001). The mocked-route reverse-registration idiom (`page.route` after `goto`, winning over the fixture catch-all) is the canonical way to mock a new endpoint per-spec without editing fixtures.
- **The one thing to know:** the catalog is **flag-driven, not name-driven** — a new workflow appears the moment its manifest sets `user_launchable: true` (and the user's tier entitles it), with zero FE/BE/engine edit. Any new `WorkflowManifest` top-level key must be added to `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS` AND must be a non-control-flow presentation/visibility key (NEVER `when/if/for/expr` — INV-5), and must default `False`/`None` and stay unread by the compiler or it breaks the 5 INV-3 goldens.

## Phase 21 — Saved Workflows: User-Authored, Named, Persisted (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/21-saved-workflows-user-authored-named-persisted-custom-workflo/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-14) — gsd-verifier PASS, 5/5 must-haves, 0 overrides + post-verify review-fix pass (WR-01..04) + live campaign ALL-GREEN  ·  **Plans:** 3/3  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone — no source-plan id, like Phases 13–20)
**Requirements delivered:** none new — **completes WF-DB-01** (DB-backed user-authored workflows; Phase 20 did browse/launch, **P21 adds persistence**) and **realizes decision Q5** ("file-backed manifests now, DB-backed user workflows later" — *later* is now). → see `.planning/ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 21" + `.planning/ISSUES-REGISTER.md` `ISS-015` lineage (SHIPPED Phase 20 → authoring layer Phase 21). This phase lands at **milestone status `verifying`, all phases complete** (count is "at P21 close" — do not treat the milestone total as fixed).
**One-line outcome:** A user composes a custom workflow (agents + per-agent models), names+saves it from BOTH the catalog AND the main-page composer, sees it in the catalog as a renameable/duplicable/deletable "Your workflows" entry, and launches it through the UNCHANGED `run_pipeline` path — persisted as a `source="user"` row in the **REUSED** dormant `workflows` table via the milestone's **first additive table-migration `0021`**, with the dead `_persist_workflow_definition` engine writer it supersedes **removed** (INV-12).

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

The authoring + persistence layer on top of the Phase 20 catalog. Phase boundary (`21-CONTEXT.md` → "Phase Boundary"; `21-SPEC.md` §1): persistence + a CRUD API + the two Save entry points + the catalog "Your workflows" section + rename/duplicate/delete + launch-a-saved-workflow. **Reuse-first** is the overriding mandate — every node copies a named existing analog; net-new is a defect. **Depends on Phase 20.**

Five success criteria (`ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 21"):
1. A user can **Save** a custom workflow (name + optional description) from the main-page composer AND from the catalog; it persists as a `source="user"` `workflows` row scoped to `owner_id`/`workspace_id`/`user_id`.
2. The catalog shows a **"Your workflows"** section (friendly name) with a per-row **Rename / Duplicate / Delete** menu; built-in (manifest) rows stay **read-only** (no kebab).
3. Launching a saved workflow **replays `{base_pipeline_type, agent_ids, model_overrides}`** through the EXISTING `run_pipeline` path; `agent_ids`+`model_overrides` are **re-validated at launch** (a since-disallowed agent/model is rejected, not smuggled).
4. Persistence **REUSES** the `workflows` table (additive migration `0021`, no new table) AND the dead `engine.py` writer it supersedes is **removed** (INV-12 — no dual write); CRUD is owner-scoped (cross-owner read/rename/delete → 404).
5. **INV-3** holds — the 5 characterization goldens stay byte-identical (the engine-writer removal is `custom`-only, dormant on the non-custom goldens) · `lint-imports` 4/0 · backend CRUD + authz tests + a mocked Playwright spec (save → appears → rename → launch) pass.

**Wave structure** (`ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 21" Plans):
- **Wave 1 — 21-01 (backend spine):** reuse the table, +nullable cols, migration `0021`, owner-scoped CRUD, REMOVE the dead writer, BE tests + goldens parity + alembic up/down + lint.
- **Wave 2 — 21-02 (FE save + catalog):** api.ts CRUD + `UserWorkflowSummary`, `NameWorkflowModal`, "Your workflows" section + kebab + "+ Create workflow", composer "Save workflow" button, catalog vitest. *(blocked on Wave 1)*
- **Wave 3 — 21-03 (FE launch wiring + e2e):** the load-bearing `IdeaInputPage` `initialAgentIds`/`initialModelOverrides` preload + guarded re-derive, `onLaunchSaved` threaded through `DashboardLayout`, mocked Playwright spec. *(blocked on Wave 2)*

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

**Schema — REUSED table, additive migration `0021` (the milestone's FIRST new table-migration).** The chain was `0014→0020` (all `0015`–`0020` were column/data migrations on existing tables); `0021_saved_user_workflows.py` (`down_revision="0020"`) is the first migration in this milestone to touch a table for a new *feature surface* — and it does so **additively, with no `create_table`**. It adds **nullable columns** to the existing `workflows` table via `batch_alter_table` + an `ix_workflows_owner_source` index. **No new table** — this is the load-bearing INV-12 decision: the dormant `WorkflowDefinition`/`workflows` table (`backend/app/models/workflow_definition.py`; future-fitted in `0014` with `source`/`owner_id`/`workspace_id`/`manifest_json`/`version`; previously 1 dead writer, 0 readers) is REUSED for `source="user"` rows. A `user_workflows` table was explicitly REJECTED as a textbook dual-implementation violation.

> **IMPORTANT — column count evolved 2 → 3.** The SPEC/ROADMAP/21-VERIFICATION all say "**2** nullable columns" (`base_pipeline_type`, `model_overrides`). The **final landed migration adds 3** nullable columns — the post-verify review-fix pass (WR-03, commit `420cea39`) added a **dedicated `description`** column rather than overloading the user's free-text description onto `constitution_ref` (whose semantic is a workflow-memory lookup key). The final state in `backend/app/models/workflow_definition.py:41-43` and `0021_saved_user_workflows.py:38-40` is `base_pipeline_type` + `model_overrides` + `description`, all `nullable=True`. A future agent: the table carries **3** Phase-21 nullable columns, and `description` is its own column — do NOT re-overload `constitution_ref`.

**API — new owner-scoped router `backend/app/api/user_workflows.py`** (`prefix="/api/user-workflows"`): `POST ""` (validate then insert, owner-stamped `user_id=owner_id=workspace_id=current_user.id`, `source="user"`, `artifact_edges="[]"`), `GET ""` (list where `user_id==me AND source=="user"`, `updated_at.desc()`), `GET "/{id}"`, `PATCH "/{id}"` (rename/edit), `DELETE "/{id}"` (→204). Cross-owner/missing → **404** via `_owned()`. Registered in `backend/app/main.py` (`include_router(user_workflows_router)`). The `_validate_model_overrides` two-check predicate is **replicated locally** in the router (identical logic to `websocket._validate_model_overrides`) to keep the app-layer router free of a websocket-module import.

**Run-launch — REUSED, no engine/run-path edit.** Launch = replay the stored `{base_pipeline_type, agent_ids, model_overrides}` triple into the existing `run_pipeline` WS handler, which **re-validates** `agent_ids` against the LIVE `allowed_custom_agent_ids` and `model_overrides` via its own `_validate_model_overrides`. No `if saved_workflow:` fork; no new pipeline name (SC-001).

**Frontend surfaces:** `UserWorkflowSummary` + 4 CRUD fetchers (`frontend/src/lib/api.ts`); `NameWorkflowModal` (`frontend/src/components/catalog/NameWorkflowModal.tsx`, reused by Save + Rename); the "Your workflows" section + per-row kebab + "+ Create workflow" (`WorkflowCatalog.tsx`); the composer "Save workflow" button + the load-bearing `initialAgentIds`/`initialModelOverrides` preload props + guarded re-derive (`IdeaInputPage.tsx`); the `onLaunchSaved`/`savedComposition`/`handleLaunchSaved` wiring (`DashboardLayout.tsx`). **No new visual language** (UI-SPEC §1 — every node copies an existing analog: `DeleteModal`, `WorkflowHistory` kebab, catalog rows, `AgentModelPicker` loading/error/empty triad).

**Entitlements:** reused unchanged — `can_run_pipeline(tier, base_pipeline_type)` gates BOTH save (fail-fast → 403, no orphan rows) AND launch; v1 saved workflows are `custom`-based → enterprise.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12)

**The one engine edit is a DELETION:** `_persist_workflow_definition` (the half-built, unscoped, best-effort, **unread** dead writer in `engine.py` — id discarded, errors swallowed, `custom`-only) **and its single call site** were removed. The new owner-scoped `/api/user-workflows` router is its replacement; keeping both would be the dual-write INV-12 violation. Verified gone: `grep -c '_persist_workflow_definition' engine.py == 0` (the only remaining `_persist_*` is the unrelated `_persist_budget_snapshot_if_active`). Because the writer was `custom`-only, it **does NOT fire on any of the 5 (non-custom) characterization goldens** — so its removal is **dormant on the goldens** and INV-3 byte-parity holds (proven, `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset). **Do NOT resurrect this writer.** Persistence now has exactly one author: the CRUD router.

Also superseded (logical, not a code deletion): decision **Q5** ("DB-backed user workflows *later*") and **WF-DB-01** persistence — both now realized; the throwaway custom composer is now a saveable, named, reusable pipeline.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **A saved workflow is PURE DATA — `{base_pipeline_type, agent_ids, model_overrides}` — never a new pipeline name (SC-001).** The kernel knows no workflow by name; launch replays the triple through the UNCHANGED `onRun`/`handleRunPipeline`/`startPipeline`/`run_pipeline` path. No `if saved_workflow:` fork anywhere (`grep 'if (saved' == 0` in `DashboardLayout`). v1 `base_pipeline_type = "custom"` only (the table/validation support other base types; exposing them is deferred).
- **REUSE the `workflows` table — do NOT add a `user_workflows` table.** `source="user"` rows live alongside `source="file"` rows. A new table = INV-12 dual-implementation violation (explicitly REJECTED).
- **Additive-migration-only.** `0021` adds **nullable** columns + an index via `batch_alter_table`; NO alter of existing columns/constraints; `owner_id`+`workspace_id` already present (stamped on create); `artifact_edges` stays NOT NULL → user rows write `"[]"` (do NOT relax it). Reversible (up/down/up proven). **Final count = 3 nullable cols** (see §3 callout).
- **Owner-scoped CRUD — IDOR → 404, default-deny (INV-8).** Every query filters `user_id==current_user.id AND source=="user"`; cross-owner read/rename/delete returns **404** (not 403 — don't leak existence). Stamp `user_id=owner_id=workspace_id=current_user.id` (self-id convention).
- **Re-validate `agent_ids` + `model_overrides` at BOTH save AND launch (load-bearing security invariant).** Save uses the EXACT launch predicates (`allowed_custom_agent_ids`, `ModelCatalog().ids()`, `SUPPORTED_PIPELINE_TYPES`, `can_run_pipeline`) so a persisted row can never carry something launch would reject; launch re-checks against the LIVE allow-list so a since-disallowed agent/model is rejected, never smuggled. `agent_ids` must be **non-empty** (WR-02 fix: `Field(min_length=1)` → 422; no unrunnable orphan rows). Name uniqueness is **API-level per-user** (409), NOT a DB constraint (the table is shared with `source="file"` rows).
- **Entitlement deny → 403; validation → 422; duplicate name → 409** — distinct fail-fast codes; the entitlement gate runs before any insert.
- **`GET /api/workflows` (Phase 20 catalog) stays DB-free** — saved workflows are served by the SEPARATE `/api/user-workflows`; the FE merges client-side.
- **INV-3 — the 5 characterization goldens (prototype/od_prototype/prototype_revision/od_ppt/app_builder) stay byte-identical.** The new columns/router/FE never touch the run/event path; the only engine edit is the `custom`-only dead-writer removal (dormant on the non-custom goldens). **Ports & Adapters:** the router lives in `app.*` (legal `app→agents`); `lint-imports` stays **4 kept / 0 broken**.
- **No new capability grants** — `exec`/`network`/`secrets`/`spawn_subagents` stay OFF.

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward

- **Seed must resolve against `ALL_LIBRARY_AGENTS` (`LIBRARY_AGENTS ∪ CUSTOM_AGENTS`), NOT base `LIBRARY_AGENTS`.** Caught as a Rule-1 bug by the launch e2e (`569aa93d`): saved `custom` workflows reference `CUSTOM_AGENTS` ids that are ABSENT from the base `LIBRARY_AGENTS`, so resolving the seed through the base list loaded **0 agents** and the load-bearing launch silently no-op'd. The composer's own `AgentLibrary` uses the superset; the launch seed must match it. **Do not narrow this back to `LIBRARY_AGENTS`.**
- **`IdeaInputPage` has no agent-preload by default — the guarded re-derive effect is mandatory.** `pipelineAgents` is always re-derived from `LIBRARY_AGENTS.filter(type)`, which is **empty for `custom`**. The `if (initialAgentIds?.length) return;` guard is what keeps the saved seed from being clobbered on the first effect run. Removing the guard breaks launch.
- **`onSelectFeature` only carries a bare `WorkflowType`** — it cannot carry a composition; that is why a distinct `onLaunchSaved` prop + `savedComposition` state exist. `handleSelectFeature` clears the seed so a normal launch starts clean (no stale-seed bleed).
- **Playwright mock glob must be `**/api/user-workflows**` (double-star)** — a single `*` does not cross a `/`, so `/{id}` PATCH/DELETE would fall to the fixtures catch-all and silently break rename/delete.
- **`model_overrides` live in a REF** (`modelOverridesRef.current`) — read `.current` on Save; the picker has no value-prop by default (WR-01 addressed the launch-replay seed; if extending the picker, seed its internal state from the saved overrides or merge-not-replace on change).
- **Column-count drift across docs:** the SPEC/ROADMAP/21-VERIFICATION say "2 nullable columns"; the FINAL landed migration has **3** (`description` added by WR-03). Trust the code (`workflow_definition.py:41-43`, `0021:38-40`), not the planning prose, on column count.
- **`deferred-items.md`:** 2 PRE-EXISTING TS2352 errors in `frontend/e2e/fixtures/mockApi.ts` (readonly-tuple casts on `CAPABILITIES`/`MODEL_CATALOG`) — confirmed identical on HEAD, untouched by Phase 21, outside its `files_modified`, do not affect any Phase 21 gate. Owned by the e2e-fixtures cleanup task, NOT a saved-workflows concern.
- **Open UI-review WARNINGs (`21-UI-REVIEW.md`, not yet fixed):** add Esc-close + focus-trap to `NameWorkflowModal`/delete-confirm/kebab; add `aria-label="Workflow options"` to the kebab button (`WorkflowCatalog.tsx:364`); make the saved-list error banner dismissable/auto-clear (mirror the composer's 2.5s `savedConfirm`). All three are inherited-analog a11y gaps the UI-SPEC named as acceptance — fix here AND ideally backport to the analogs (`DeleteModal`, WorkflowHistory kebab).
- **Carry-forward (remaining WF-DB-01 scope, all explicitly deferred — `21-CONTEXT.md` `<deferred>`):** saving non-`custom` base types; sharing/marketplace; org/workspace-shared workflows; versioning beyond the existing `version` column. These are deliberate v-next, NOT gaps.

## Phase 22 — Capability Surfacing & User Empowerment: Universal Runtime UX Completeness (post-milestone)

**Folder:** `…/22-capability-surfacing-and-user-empowerment-universal-runtime-/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-06-15) — `passed`, 17/17 must-have requirements verified (re-verified after one SURF-03 gap closure + a full review-fix pass)  ·  **Plans:** 9/9  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone)
**Requirements delivered:** SURF-01..03, EMP-01..04, WIRE-01..03, UXFIX-01..04, DECIDE-01/02, LIVE-01 (new families registered this phase) → see `ROADMAP.md` "### Phase 22" block + `REQUIREMENTS.md` (17 statement lines + 17 traceability rows; registered under ISS-012 reconciliation).
**One-line outcome:** The SC-001 dividend is cashed at the UX layer — every registered capability is now VISIBLE in a live-registry-driven palette and COMPOSABLE through a per-agent Advanced expander whose selections persist and reach execution via the EXISTING run path with zero kernel workflow-name branches, while the latent `model:`/`retry:`/`injects:` compiler drops, the four v1.0-audit UX gaps, the two open decisions (N9, N11), migration 0022, and the consolidated live-Bedrock evidence pass all close.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Driver: `.planning/v1.0-MILESTONE-AUDIT.md` + the 2026-06-14 capability-adoption audit + a deferral-ledger sweep. The kernel/registry expose ~70 capabilities but the product surfaced ~a dozen. **Depends on Phase 21** (reuses the catalog + saved-workflows composer + `/api/capabilities` + `/api/user-workflows` CRUD — extends, never forks). 8 success criteria (ROADMAP "### Phase 22"; mirrored in `22-SPEC.md` Acceptance Criteria), grouped by family:

- **Surfacing (SURF-01..03):** SC-1 — every registered capability kind visible in a UI palette sourced from a live `GET /api/capabilities` fetch, grouped by kind, showing name/description/`user_allowed`/security-gated/config-schema, with NO hardcoded capability-name array in the FE (SC-001 grep = 0). Composer shows a workflow's declared per-step capabilities from the compiled plan before composing.
- **Empowerment (EMP-01..04):** SC-2 — a user composes a workflow opting into ≥1 user-allowed capability per lever-type (validator + human gate + non-default model + retry), SAVES it, LAUNCHES it through the existing run path, and the selection demonstrably reaches execution. SC-3 — privileged caps render visible-but-locked and are server-rejected if smuggled, re-validated at SAVE and LAUNCH (CAP-03). Selecting a gate-requiring cap auto-attaches the gate.
- **Latent wiring (WIRE-01..03):** SC-4 — compiler materializes top-level + per-step `model:` and per-step `retry:` so they take effect; `injects:` is consumed-or-fails-loud; a parametrized test proves no `_ALLOWED_STEP_KEYS` entry is accepted-but-dropped.
- **UX fixes (UXFIX-01..04):** SC-5 — data-driven catalog is the home landing showing friendly `display_name`s; a custom binary deliverable re-renders faithfully on history-reopen from a persisted mimetype; the generic mimetype renderer is the primary dispatch path.
- **Decisions (DECIDE-01/02):** SC-6 — N9 (artifact retention) and N11 (premium-model policy) decided and recorded in REQUIREMENTS.md; 3 stale "confirm" labels reconciled; model picker reflects the N11 policy.
- **Live (LIVE-01):** SC-7 — one consolidated live-Bedrock pass on the `default` profile records evidence per standing deferral (or an explicit disposition).
- **Invariants (SC-8):** 5 goldens byte-identical (INV-3) · kernel name-free (SC-001 grep 0) · `create_deep_agent` adapter-only (INV-13) · import-linter 4/0 · additive migrations only.

Ambiguity 0.135 (gate ≤0.20); 17 requirements locked. The acceptance bar is **representative-per-lever**, not exhaustive per-capability E2E (locked in `22-SPEC.md` Boundaries + Interview Log).

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

- **Registry metadata seam (SURF-02):** `_META` dict sibling to `_TRUST`; `register()` gains `description=`/`config_schema=` kwargs; `registry.describe(kind, name)` accessor. Single source of truth — NO static `CAPABILITY_META` map in the API layer (D-08; a newly `@register`'d cap appears with metadata, no FE/API edit — SC-001).
- **`/api/capabilities` additive fields:** each entry now carries `description`, `security_gated` (derived `not user_allowed`, not separately stored), and a populated per-cap `config_schema` (JSON-Schema-lite; legitimately `{}` for no-config caps). Auth (`Depends(get_current_user)`) + permission scopes + all prior keys preserved (API-02 intact). FE `CapabilityEntry` mirrors the fields.
- **Compiler materialization (WIRE):** top-level `model:` → `CompiledWorkflow.model`; per-step `model:`/`retry:`/`injects:`/`fix:`/`depends_on:` → typed `Step` fields, via strict-key coercion helpers (`_compile_model_policy`/`_compile_retry_policy`/`_compile_fix_policy`) that reject unknown nested keys (INV-5 at every level). `_ALLOWED_STEP_KEYS` is the consumed-or-raises contract, enforced by `test_allowed_step_keys.py` (D-17). Per-step injects consumed via `AgentContext.step_injects` merged order-stable at `factory._compose_system_prompt`.
- **Migration 0022 (UXFIX-02):** `0022_workflow_run_deliverable_mimetype.py`, `down_revision = "0021"`, single-head, additive nullable `deliverable_mimetype`/`deliverable_filename` on `WorkflowRun` (already owner_id+workspace_id-scoped); reversible.
- **Saved-selection persistence (EMP-03):** the dormant **`manifest_json`** column on the `workflows` table reused — zero migration — to store the compact `{agent_id:{validators?,gates?,model?,retry?}}` map (reserved `__workflow__` key for workflow-level limits). Synth seam `agents/workflows/selections.py` shared by save + launch.
- **FE capability palette + Advanced expander:** `CapabilityPaletteSection` (grouped-by-kind, locked rows) and per-agent `AdvancedExpander` (Validator → Gate → Model → Retry), both embedded-and-exported inside `AgentsPopup`, both sourcing options purely from the live `/api/capabilities` payload. `single_shot` is now a `user_allowed=True` strategy.
- **Data-driven catalog as home:** the home landing is the `WorkflowCatalog` reading `GET /api/workflows` (UXFIX-03); the generic-primary `FIRST_PARTY_RENDERERS` dispatch table (UXFIX-04).
- **SURF-03 compiled-plan projection (added at gap-closure):** `getWorkflowDetail(token, id)` → `GET /api/workflows/{id}`; `IdeaInputPage` maps each step's `strategy`/`gates`/`validators`/`compaction`/`task_source.kind` to declared caps (no hardcoded names) and feeds the AgentsPopup "Declared by this workflow" strip.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12)

- **`AgentModelPicker` tier filter dropped** (`AgentModelPicker.tsx:76`, DECIDE-02/D-23) — the `model_catalog.filter(m => m.user_allowed)` removed; every catalog entry is now offered to every tier (server `_validate_model_overrides` + the new `validate_selection_model_ids` are the authoritative allow-list).
- **First-party deliverable branches collapsed into the generic renderer** (UXFIX-04/D-21) — the 4 first-party per-type branches (`user_stories`/`app_builder`/`ppt`/`prototype`) became routed entries in the `FIRST_PARTY_RENDERERS` table; the generic mimetype renderer is the primary route, not fallback-only.
- **Redundant Catalog nav tab + `catalog` view block removed** (`AppHeader.tsx`, `DashboardLayout.tsx`) — home IS the catalog; a second identical tab would re-create the dual-surface smell UXFIX-03 was chartered to remove. The `CreationHub.WORKFLOWS` hardcoded array no longer drives the default landing.
- **The accepted-but-dropped key behavior removed** — `model:`/`retry:`/`injects:`/`fix:`/`depends_on:` were allow-listed but silently dropped by both compiler constructors; now materialized (no key accepted-but-dropped). The `{}` `config_schema` stub at `capabilities.py` removed (now registry-projected).
- Everything else is **additive** (registry kwargs, API fields, migration 0022 columns, `manifest_json` reuse). No tables dropped, no destructive alter.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **N9 (DECIDE-01 / D-22) RESOLVED → keep-by-default.** Run artifacts retained indefinitely; `run_ttl`/`days:N` are opt-in overrides. Recorded in `REQUIREMENTS.md` ART-04. **Carry-forward:** the code/config default is still `run_ttl` in 4 sites (`artifacts/graph.py:101/133`, `models/artifact_ref.py:52`, `models/workspace.py:33`, `authz.py:202`) — flagged as a follow-up (a server_default/migration flip is INV-3-sensitive; not done in a docs plan). The product decision of record is keep-by-default.
- **N11 (DECIDE-02 / D-23) RESOLVED → premium-open-to-all-tiers.** Global default stays Haiku with an ordered fallback chain on throttle/error; premium (`cost_class=premium`) models selectable by ALL tiers, no per-tier gating. Recorded in MODEL-05; the model-picker tier filter is dropped.
- **trust=user at SAVE and LAUNCH (CAP-03)** — a smuggled/tampered `user_allowed=False` capability (security/approval gate, exec/spawn/secrets, `user_allowed=False` MCP) is server-rejected at both sites; `engine._apply_selections` adds a third defense-in-depth re-compile. **The FE lock is advisory only** — the server `_check_trust` is authoritative. Selection-supplied **model ids** are also catalog-validated via `validate_selection_model_ids` (`ModelCatalog().is_allowed`, honoring `user_allowed`) at BOTH chokepoints (CR-01/WR-04 fix) — never trust a selection model id.
- **Privileged caps visible-but-locked** — exec/`spawn_subagents`/`security`+`approval` gates/powerful MCP render but are never user-composable; this phase makes them visible, NOT enabled. The exec ⇒ `gates:[security,approval]` coupling (D-01) stays compiler-enforced.
- **SC-001** — the user composer routes through the capability registry + `trust=user/db`; NEVER a workflow-name branch. The `_apply_selections` overlay keys on `agent_id` (generic); palette/expander options derive from payload `kind`. Kernel name-free grep stays 0.
- **INV-3** — the 5 characterization goldens (prototype, od_prototype, prototype_revision, od_ppt, app_builder) stay byte/event-identical. `deliverable_mimetype`/`deliverable_filename` stay in `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`; empty selections/injects → byte-identical no-op (the mechanical proof). Authored `display_name` is inert presentation metadata.
- **INV-5** — no DSL; `_ALLOWED_STEP_KEYS` strict-key rejection preserved (WIRE-03 did NOT loosen it into silent-accept; the consumed-or-raises guard is durable).
- **INV-13** — `create_deep_agent` only inside the `langchain_deepagents` adapter (sole site `app/agents/deep_agent_runner.py:291`). Untouched.
- **Ports & Adapters** — import-linter 4 kept / 0 broken; adding a capability = add a module + `@register`, no kernel edit.
- **Additive migrations only** — 0022 is additive; `manifest_json` reuse needs no migration; every new column rides existing owner_id+workspace_id scope.

## Phase 23 — Redo Gate Action — Human-Gate Redo with Instructions (post-milestone)

**Folder:** NOT a `.planning/phases/<NN>/` GSD phase — a STANDALONE review-hardened plan executed in 2 waves on branch `new-workflow-engine` (no `_register-parts/` file): `.planning/REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` (v2, the review-hardened plan) + `.planning/REDO-GATE-PLAN-REVIEW.md` (senior-architect adversarial review, findings F1–F10, verdict **NEEDS-REVISION** → folded into v2) + `.planning/REDO-GATE-WAVE1-SUMMARY.md` (backend) + `.planning/REDO-GATE-WAVE2-SUMMARY.md` (frontend)  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-01) — verified offline (redo safety **7 passed**, lint-imports 4/0, SC-001 grep clean, goldens **4/5 byte-identical** — the 5th is a pre-existing branch red, see §6) AND **LIVE-validated on real Bedrock** (eu-central-1 Haiku 4.5, run `97277945`)  ·  **Plans:** 2 waves — Wave-1 backend (8 commits `802cb6fe`…`d66df568`) + post-test fix `9efee5f8` + Wave-2 frontend (6 commits `07e47041`…`20655c86`)  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone, no plan-id)
**Requirements delivered:** None new — a new UX capability (the **Redo** action) layered onto the EXISTING human review gate; no REQUIREMENTS.md ids. → `REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` §1 (the 4 locked product decisions) + §0 (the v1 = INLINE-ONLY scope lock).
**One-line outcome:** The human review gate gains a generic, name-free **Redo (with optional additional instructions)** action covering every LIVE (inline) human gate — it re-runs the gated agent in place as a flat `while True:` loop on a FRESH `:redo{N}` checkpoint thread (so the live model regenerates instead of replaying its rejected turn), injects any extra note via a consume-once `=== ADDITIONAL INSTRUCTIONS (REVISE) ===` block, keeps the rejected output as a prior `ArtifactGraph` version with `derived_from` lineage (never deleted), and re-pauses at the SAME gate showing the new output — with declared/user-composed `gate:human` safely fenced off the un-wired path (no Redo button; any stray `_gate_redo` consumed, never PASSed), **no migration**, and the 5 characterization goldens dormant/byte-identical.

### 1. Goal & Success Criteria (what was PLANNED)

Driver: a user request for a "Redo with additional instructions" control on the human review gate, scoped + hardened into `REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` (v2). At a human review gate (today the FE offers Approve / Edit / Cancel via the `approve_review` WS message), add a **Redo** action: re-run **the gated agent in place** (a fresh model call), optionally with extra free-text instructions, then **re-pause at the SAME gate** showing the new output; the old output is kept as a prior version. **Depends on** the whole milestone gate machinery (`_run_review_gate`, the `set_review_response`/`get_review_event` resume channel, `ArtifactGraph` versioning, `_run_agent` dispatch — Phases 2/5/7/13).

**The 4 locked product decisions** (designed-to, not re-decided — `REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` §1):
1. **Unbounded** redos per gate (human-paced; no cap) ⇒ the re-run MUST be a **loop, not recursion** (review finding F2).
2. **Optional** instructions — empty = plain regenerate (same context, fresh sample); with text = the note is injected as an extra REVISE directive for the re-run.
3. **Generic / all human gates (SC-001)** — the redo path keys on the generic gate decision + the generic `_should_gate` predicate, NEVER a workflow-name / agent-id literal in the kernel.
4. **Keep old output as a prior `ArtifactGraph` version** — supersede via a new version with `derived_from` lineage to the rejected one; NEVER delete.

**Scope lock — v1 = INLINE-ONLY** (`REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` §0): v1 wires Redo for the **inline human-gate path only**, which is the *full* live reach today — the live prototype gates (`prototype-specify`/`prototype-plan`) both declare `gate: Human_Gate` AND `gates:[human]`, and the WR-02 dedupe (`engine.py:3727`) skips the declared `human` gate whenever the inline gate fires, so **every live human gate routes through the inline consumer**. Declared / user-composed `gate:human` (addable via the Phase-22 AdvancedExpander, `user_allowed=True`) is fenced OUT (no Redo button, stray signal consumed) and a real `GateOutcome "redo"` that re-runs the gated *producer* step is a documented FOLLOW-UP. This satisfies decision #3 for all *live* gates name-free.

**Adversarial review folded in** (`REDO-GATE-PLAN-REVIEW.md`, verdict NEEDS-REVISION → all addressed in v2): **F1** (BLOCKER — the shared `_run_review_gate` would leak/`GATE_PASS` `_gate_redo` on the declared path → T-human consume-arm + the `redoable` discriminator fence), **F2** (HIGH — recursion → `while True:` loop), **F3** (HIGH — `derived_from`/directive leak on empty/error → unconditional consume-once loop locals), **F4** (durable-resume of a paused-with-pending-redo gate is LOST, not "handled" — documented limitation), **F5** (`_latest_typed_content` by `tree()` order could serve the rejected version → select by `max(version)`), **F6–F10** (retry×gate×redo edge note, optional cancel-aware wait, FE idempotency, honest audit row, prose accuracy).

**Invariant bar:** SC-001/INV-1 kernel name-free · INV-2 per-run state only · INV-3 5 goldens byte/event-identical · INV-12 reuse-no-fork · INV-13 deepagents-only · additive migrations only (here: **none**) · import-linter 4/0.

### 2. What Was Implemented — per plan (BUILT)

No GSD plan rows (standalone plan); rows are the plan's atomic tasks across 2 waves, mapped to the as-built commits (`REDO-GATE-WAVE1/2-SUMMARY.md`).

### 3. Capabilities, Modules, Schema & API Added

- **WS protocol — `approve_review` EXTENDED (no new message type, INV-12):** two optional backward-compatible fields — `action` (`"approve"` default | `"redo"`) and `instructions` (string). A `redo` rides the SAME owner-gated handler (`_review_gate_owned_by`) and the SAME `set_review_response`/`get_review_event` resume channel a new message type would have had to duplicate. Outbound is an untyped inline literal (no FE type-union edit beyond the inbound shape).
- **`redoable` discriminator (generic FE fence):** `_run_review_gate` gains a `redoable: bool = False` param emitted on `review_gate_ready.data`; the **inline** consumer passes `redoable=True`, the **declared** consumer (`kernel_services.run_human_gate → _run_review_gate`) passes nothing → `False`. The FE renders the Redo control iff `onRedo && redoable`. Keyed on the inline call site (a structural path), NOT a workflow/agent name (SC-001). Added to `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` so the goldens stay byte-identical (the `deliverable_mimetype`/`deliverable_filename` precedent).
- **`_gate_redo` internal signal:** mirrors the existing `_gate_rejected`/`_gate_edited` shape — consumed inside the consumers, NEVER put on the WS queue. Consumed on BOTH consumers: the inline `_run_agent` loop (re-runs) AND the declared `HumanGate`/`ApprovalGate` (safe non-PASS).
- **`while True:` redo loop in `_run_agent`:** the single-shot run+gate became a flat loop — on `_gate_redo` set loop locals + `continue`; on approve/edit `break`; on reject `return`. Flat stack, O(1) per event for unbounded redos (F2). The redo directive + `derived_from` are loop locals, drained and nulled unconditionally at iteration entry (consume-once, F3) so an empty-output/exception path cannot leak onto the next agent.
- **Instructions seam:** `ectx.redo_directive` (additive scratch) → a `=== ADDITIONAL INSTRUCTIONS (REVISE) ===` block in `_compose_context_message` (signature UNCHANGED — rides the additive field; no test blast radius; dormant when empty).
- **Artifact lineage:** `derived_from` threaded onto the main handoff write (`_dual_write_artifact`, already accepts it) so the redo version (N+1) records lineage to the rejected ref; `_latest_typed_content` now selects the **max-`version`** ref (F5) so a rejected prior version can never win after persist + rehydrate.
- **Fresh checkpoint thread per redo:** `{run}:{agent}:redo{N}` thread_id for `redo_attempt>0` (the LangGraph checkpointer otherwise replays the prior turn — see §6 THE BUG). Same class as the 06-05 CR-02 throttle-fallback `:retry{n}` fix.
- **Declared-path fence (T-human):** `HumanGate.evaluate_stream` + `ApprovalGate.evaluate_stream` gain a `_gate_redo` consume-arm → terminal outcome `GATE_WAIT_HUMAN` (the plan's recommended non-PASS option; `GATE_BLOCK` was the bounded alternative) + an honest audit row. No `GateHandler` Protocol change.
- **FE:** Redo control (button + textarea) + idempotent latch on `ReviewGatePanel`; `redoable?` on the inbound type; pass-through on `DashboardLayout`; capture+send on `page.tsx`.
- **Schema / migration: NONE.** `artifact_refs` already has `version` + `derived_from` + `parents` + `owner_id` + `workspace_id` (migration 0014); `gate_events.outcome` is a free `String` (migration 0016) so the optional `outcome="redo"` audit row needs no migration; the redo decision lives only in the in-memory `ArtifactStore` resume dict.

### 4. What Was Deleted / Superseded (INV-12 move-don't-copy)

Phase 23 is **almost entirely additive** (a new UX action on a completed milestone), so — like Phase 13 — there are **no single-impl collapses and no table drops**. The only "removal-equivalent" is **none** (no production code was deleted). The move-don't-copy / no-fork discipline still shows up:

- **No second HITL mechanism, no forked gate, no second dispatch path** — redo reuses the SAME `_run_review_gate` pause/resume, the SAME `set_review_response`/`get_review_event` channel, the SAME `approve_review` owner-gated handler, the SAME `_run_agent` (`create_runner`/`astream_events`) dispatch, and the SAME `ArtifactGraph` versioning. The re-run is a plain re-iteration of the existing loop, not a side channel — so re-emitted events still pass `execute()`'s single `itertools.count(1)` seq boundary.
- **T-human removes the v1 "half-fork":** the review (`REDO-GATE-PLAN-REVIEW.md` F1/INV-12) flagged that changing the *shared* `_run_review_gate` without teaching the declared consumer would create a second, BROKEN behavior on that consumer. The consume-arm makes the shared primitive behave correctly on both consumers — a clean reuse, not a fork.
- **Loop replaced recursion *in the plan*, not in shipped code** — v1 of the plan proposed recursive `_run_agent` re-invocation; v2 specified the `while True:` loop and that is what was BUILT, so there is no recursive implementation to delete.

INV-3 gates stayed armed throughout: no golden/snapshot files were re-baselined (the one new golden-path key `redoable` is stripped by `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`, not re-recorded); banned-pattern (SC-001) + import-linter green.

### 5. Key Decisions & Locked Constraints (do NOT contradict)

- **The 4 locked product decisions** (§1): unbounded redos · optional instructions · generic/all-(live)-human-gates (SC-001) · keep the rejected output as a prior `ArtifactGraph` version (`derived_from`, never delete).
- **v1 = INLINE-ONLY (LOCKED).** Redo is wired for the inline human-gate path = every *live* human gate today (WR-02 dedupe routes them through the inline consumer). Declared / user-composed `gate:human` shows **no Redo button** (`redoable=false`) and its stray `_gate_redo` is consumed (never PASS, never leaked). **Full declared-path redo** (a real `GateOutcome "redo"` re-running the gated *producer* step from the dispatch loop) is a documented FOLLOW-UP, NOT v1.
- **Extend `approve_review`, not a new WS message (INV-12)** — reuses the one owner gate (`_review_gate_owned_by`) and the one resume channel (`set_review_response`/`get_review_event`); a new type would duplicate both. Owner check runs BEFORE the write (IDOR mitigation + `test_approve_review_ownership.py` ratchet preserved).
- **Loop, not recursion (F2)** — `while True:` inside `_run_agent`; flat stack + O(1) per event for an explicitly-unbounded operation.
- **Consume-once, unconditional (F3)** — the redo directive (via `ectx.redo_directive`, dirty only across the two adjacent compose lines, cleared unconditionally) and `redo_derived_from` (a pure `_run_agent` loop local — intentionally NOT an `ectx` field, so it cannot leak across agents) are drained + nulled every iteration.
- **Latest-by-version (F5)** — `_latest_typed_content` selects `max(version)`; a rejected prior version can never be served post-resume. The rejected ref is KEPT (decision #4).
- **Fresh `:redo{N}` checkpoint thread (the post-test fix)** — required for the live model to regenerate; the suffix is added **ONLY for `redo_attempt>0`** so the first/non-redo run AND all 5 goldens keep their exact thread_id (INV-3 dormant). Do NOT remove the `redo_attempt` gate.
- **No migration (Q3 additive)** — `artifact_refs.version`/`derived_from` + free-`String` `gate_events.outcome` already support redo; the resume decision is in-memory only. Do not add a DB column for redo state.
- **SC-001 / INV-1** — redo keys on `action=="redo"` (`_run_review_gate`), the event type `_gate_redo` + `_should_gate` (inline consumer), the structural `redoable` flag (inline call site), and `ectx.redo_directive` (injector); the declared fence keys on the event type. **No workflow-name / agent-id literal added to the kernel** (banned-pattern grep clean). The FE shows Redo purely off the server `redoable` flag (no name check).
- **INV-3 / INV-12 / INV-13** — goldens dormant (`action` defaults `"approve"`; `redo_directive==""`; `derived_from=None`; `redoable` stripped); reuse-no-fork (above); the re-run is a plain `_run_agent` loop re-iteration → `create_deep_agent` adapter-only, no new agent loop.

### 6. Status, Verification & Evidence (what HAPPENED)

- **Offline verification (`REDO-GATE-WAVE1-SUMMARY.md`):** `test_redo_gate_safety.py` **7 passed** (F1 declared-gate fence over `HumanGate`+`ApprovalGate`, F2 flat-stack over ~80 redos, F3 no-lineage-leak ×2 [empty + exception], F5 latest-by-version, + the new `test_redo_uses_fresh_checkpoint_thread_per_attempt`); `test_artifact_store.py` 11, `test_approve_review_ownership.py` 7 (incl. the redo IDOR pin), `test_declared_gate_streaming.py` 3; `lint-imports` **4 kept / 0 broken**; **SC-001 grep clean** over `agents/execution_engine/` (only `action=="redo"`/`_gate_redo`/`redoable`/`redo_directive`, no workflow/agent literal); dispatch-loop pin = exactly 1.
- **INV-3 goldens: 4/5 byte-identical** (prototype, od_prototype, prototype_revision, app_builder). **The 5th — `test_characterization_od_ppt::test_od_ppt_event_snapshot` — is a PRE-EXISTING branch red**, verified failing at the pre-work merge `1327acce` (stash-and-retest confirmed), diverging at the od_ppt `agent_input.context_message` template block — **NOT caused by this feature** and not touched by the redo path (the od_ppt *deliverable* snapshot passes).
- **Frontend (`REDO-GATE-WAVE2-SUMMARY.md`):** `ReviewGatePanel.test.tsx` **7/7**; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean on all 4 changed source files (the only project-wide tsc errors are pre-existing in untouched files); full FE suite adds **0 new failures** vs clean pre-work HEAD (identical pre-existing red set: WorkflowCatalog ×5, AgentProgressPanel ×1, IdeaInputPage.declaredCapabilities ×1 — none in files this wave touched).
- **THE post-test bug + fix (commit `9efee5f8`).** The redo loop initially reused the same `{run}:{agent}` checkpoint thread_id → the LangGraph checkpointer **replayed the prior turn** → the live model "remembered" its rejected output and replied "the dashboard is complete…" in ~3s instead of regenerating. Fix = a fresh `{run}:{agent}:redo{N}` thread per `redo_attempt` (suffix only for attempt>0 so goldens/first-run thread_ids are unchanged — INV-3 preserved); same class as the 06-05 CR-02 throttle-fallback `:retry{n}` fix. The new regression asserts distinct per-attempt threads (catchable offline only via thread identity, since the scripted test model has no conversational memory).
- **LIVE-VALIDATED 2026-07-01 on real Bedrock** (eu-central-1 **Haiku 4.5**, run `97277945`): Redo (no instructions) → fresh `:redo1` thread → **~80s full regeneration** (vs the ~3s replay bug) → re-paused at the plan gate → user approved → build ran. Graph verified in `artifact_refs`: `task_list` **v1 (rejected, KEPT)** + **v2 (approved, latest, `derived_from`=v1)**; the build consumed **v2** (7 `## Task` tasks). This is the end-of-feature live confirmation per the defer-live-verification convention.

Wave-2 FE (6) `07e47041` · `f93ae1bf` · `ce1a36f4` · `59087c59` · `a35feda4` · `20655c86` (docs). Adjacent boot fix `3f2645a1` (see §7). Branch `new-workflow-engine`, **NOT pushed**.

### 7. Gotchas, Survivors & Carry-Forward

- **F4 — pending redo is in-memory; a mid-gate process restart LOSES it (accepted limitation).** `restore_non_terminal_runs` classifies a `waiting_for_user` run and marks it **`failed`** (`engine.py:4007-4025`) — it does NOT re-arm the review event or re-emit `review_gate_ready`. Pre-existing parity with today's gates (clarify + review both); any already-written redo `ArtifactRef` persists as a benign orphan (no corruption). NOT "handled" by resume; do not claim otherwise. (The pre-existing `test_restart_resume::test_waiting_for_user_run_is_rearmed_not_driven` red on this branch is exactly this limitation.)
- **F7 — Stop-while-paused is a pre-existing gate-wait no-op that redo AMPLIFIES.** `_run_review_gate`'s `await event.wait()` (`engine.py:3918`) waits only on the review event, not `cancel_event`; unbounded redos widen the open-panel window. The optional cancel-aware wait (race `cancel_event`) was **DEFERRED** (T8/B9, skipped) — a high-value follow-up that would also fix the long-standing gap.
- **Declared-path redo DEFERRED (v1 inline-only).** A real `GateOutcome "redo"` that re-runs the gated *producer* step from the dispatch loop (the declared `human` gate is a PRE-step gate over the PREVIOUS step's output) is a genuinely different mechanism and is the documented follow-up. v1 fences it safely (no Redo button + consume-arm) — do NOT treat the declared path as redo-capable.
- **Minor lineage gap (cosmetic).** `derived_from` is stamped only on the main consumed kind (e.g. `task_list`); the duplicate producer-id-named kind (e.g. `prototype-plan`) versions additively but its v2 lacks the back-link. Consumption + latest-selection (max-version) are correct, so decision #4 holds for what the gate reviews and what downstream consumes; the missing back-link on the secondary kind is benign.
- **The gsd-executor `git stash` hazard (survivor).** An executor's verification stash swept the uncommitted `app/core/config.py` AWS_BEARER fix and crashed the live backend; it was restored + committed (`3f2645a1`). Lesson: do not run a `git stash` verification sweep with unrelated uncommitted boot fixes in the tree.
- **Pre-existing branch reds (NOT this feature):** the `od_ppt` event-snapshot golden (diverges at the od_ppt context_message template, failing at `1327acce`) and `test_waiting_for_user_run_is_rearmed_not_driven` (the F4 limitation). Both pre-date this work and are out of scope.
- **Adjacent boot fix `3f2645a1` (footnote).** `app/core/config.py` did not declare `AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK` (`config.py:26`), yet `main.py`'s lifespan reads it to report the provider mode → `AttributeError` crashed every Bedrock-IAM boot (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY empty). Declared with an empty default → falls through to the IAM/SSO credential chain (AWS_PROFILE). A run-config/boot fix committed during this work, not part of the redo feature.

### 8. File Index (the standalone-plan docs + as-built files)

- **Planning docs (`.planning/REDO-GATE-*.md`):** `REDO-GATE-PLAN.md` (v2, the review-hardened plan — §0 scope lock, §1 the 4 decisions, §2 current-state grounding cited to file:line, §3 end-to-end design, §4 exact changes B1–B10 + T-human, §5 invariant compliance, §7 test plan, §8 task/wave breakdown, §9 risks, §10 compliance matrix); `REDO-GATE-PLAN-REVIEW.md` (senior-architect adversarial review — findings F1–F10, per-invariant matrix, verdict NEEDS-REVISION, "what's GOOD / keep"); `REDO-GATE-WAVE1-SUMMARY.md` (backend actuals, verification table, pre-existing-failure log); `REDO-GATE-WAVE2-SUMMARY.md` (frontend actuals, exact wire contract, pre-existing FE red set).
- **Backend code (as-built):** `agents/execution_engine/context.py` (`redo_directive`), `agents/execution_engine/engine.py` (`_run_review_gate` redo branch + `redoable`; `_run_agent` `while True:` loop + `_gate_redo` consumer + fresh `:redo{N}` thread; `_compose_context_message` REVISE block; `_latest_typed_content` max-version; redo audit row), `agents/artifact_store/store.py` (`set_review_response` kwargs), `app/api/websocket.py` (`approve_review` redo, owner-check unchanged), `agents/capabilities/gates/human.py` + `…/approval.py` (consume `_gate_redo`), `tests/agents/characterization/_normalize.py` (`redoable` ∈ `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`), `app/core/config.py` (adjacent AWS_BEARER fix).
- **Backend tests:** `tests/agents/test_redo_gate_safety.py` (F1×2 / F2 / F3×2 / F5 / fresh-thread), `tests/unit/test_artifact_store.py` (redo round-trip), `tests/unit/test_approve_review_ownership.py` (redo IDOR pin).
- **Frontend (as-built):** `frontend/src/types/index.ts` (`redoable?`), `…/components/preview/ReviewGatePanel.tsx` (+ `ReviewGatePanel.test.tsx`), `…/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`, `…/app/dashboard/page.tsx`.
- **Live evidence:** real-Bedrock run `97277945` (eu-central-1 Haiku 4.5) — `task_list` v1(rejected)+v2(approved, `derived_from`=v1) in `artifact_refs`; build consumed v2.

## Phase 24 — Prototype Nav-Validation Hardening (render_check + static_check) (post-milestone)

**Folder:** NOT a `.planning/phases/<NN>/` GSD phase — **5 sequential GSD quick tasks** on branch `new-workflow-engine` (each with PLAN + SUMMARY + VERIFICATION under `.planning/quick/260701-<id>-*/`; no `_register-parts/` file): `260701-bob` (round 1) → `260701-erg` (round 2) → `260701-go2` (round 3) → `260701-hqa` (round 4) → `260701-kml` (round 5).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-01) — each round plan-checked + verified; the two producers' final behavior **verified by direct orchestrator reproduction AND an independent Chromium probe that converged on both files** (broken A → 17/17 dead/None; fixed B → 17/17 correct). Offline gates green each round (targeted suite 150→191 passing, `lint-imports` 4/0, 4 stable characterization goldens byte/event-identical, NO SNAPSHOT_UPDATE).  ·  **Plans:** 5 quick tasks, 18 code/test commits (`4b65c66c`…`0d8429ac`) + 5 docs commits.  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone, no plan-id).
**Requirements delivered:** None new — a correctness + coverage hardening of the prototype HTML validators. → the per-round SUMMARY/VERIFICATION files.
**Driver:** a real broken prototype (`imc-inventory-certificate-management.html`, "navigation didn't work properly") that BOTH validators passed green — plus expert review feedback across the arc.

**One-line outcome:** The two prototype validators — `static_check` (stdlib) + `render_check` (headless Chromium) — went from **fail-open** (shipping a broken-nav prototype green) → **no-false-positive** (correctly passing a well-built alias/parametric router) → **honest** (labeling *why* a route fails), pinned by a four-fixture regression floor. The kernel/engine seam changes are additive and INV-3-safe (goldens byte/event-identical; no migration; `lint-imports` 4/0).

### The 5 rounds (each a quick task)

- **R1 `260701-bob` — nav-coverage + render-unavailable seam.** Broadened `render_check._check_nav` beyond the `.nav-item[href]`-only heuristic (also `a[href^='#']` + `onclick` `location.hash`/`navigateTo(` discovery); added correct-section assertion (`NavResult.expected`) + a coverage-0 P0 finding; `static_check` flags dynamic-nav (onclick/navigateTo) dead-links as ERRORS. Added the single `render_coverage_status` policy helper in kernel-pure `severity.py` routing the 3 render-availability consumers (`html_render.py`, `_select_issues_to_fix`, `_run_validation_fix_loop`); a per-step `require_render` manifest knob (compiler→Step→DeliverableContext) with a `PROTOTYPE_REQUIRE_RENDER=False` default; a distinct `validator_skipped` audit row (SKIPPED severity sentinel). Corrections vs external advice baked in: no `GATE_ERROR` (fail-closed rides the existing ValidationGate block-critical), `require_render` per-step not global, fix-loop stays fire-and-forget. Commits `4b65c66c`/`242a1e38`/`7ff4b6e5`.
- **R2 `260701-erg` — un-dedup + router-dead + require_render load-bearing.** Per-route un-dedup so each concrete malformed route reports (not collapsed by first-segment), `${…}` template routes excluded from browser exercise, configurable `nav_settle_ms`; `static_check` routes-map RESOLUTION cross-check (router-dead when a section exists but has no map entry, guarded on map presence); `require_render: true` on the prototype **build** + **revision** steps (revision genuinely fails closed via `gates:[validation]`; build is record-the-skip only — `gates:[]`, discarded validator returns — documented as INV-3-neutral); golden-harness pin `require_render=False` via `copy.deepcopy` in `_patched_compile_for_run` (also fixed a latent `@lru_cache` shared-mutation of `compile_for_run`). Full 414KB file committed as regression fixture. Commits `7370d750`/`cdb25817`/`e8e4f336`.
- **R3 `260701-go2` — route-table-aware (the false-positive fix).** New shared `backend/app/agents/route_table.py`: `parse_routes_table` (object `{pattern:page}` AND array `[{pattern,page}]`; returns None for href-valued objects — the disambiguation guard that keeps goldens/scenario tests on the legacy path) + `resolve_route` (exact/alias/`:id`, `re.escape`d base, first-match-wins). Both tiers RESOLVE each nav route through the table before judging it (render `expected = resolve_route(...)`, static checks the resolved page's section), with a graceful first-segment fallback when no table. This eliminated the alias/parametric false-positives: fixture **B** (correct alias/`:id` router) now PASSES both tiers; **A** still FAILS (genuinely). Commits `4f176004`/`298da23c`/`9878a821`.
- **R4 `260701-hqa` — blank→null render honesty.** `render_check` reads the active **page section** excluding nav/anchors (`[data-page].is-active:not(a):not(.nav-link):not(.nav-item):not(.nav-submenu-link), .page-section.is-active, .section.is-active`) → returns `activated=None` on a blank page instead of coercing to a nav-link's `data-page` (the old `[data-page].is-active` matched the sidebar `<a>`). Shared `_dead_nav_line` now distinguishes *"activated NOTHING (blank page)"* from *"activated '<Y>' but expected '<X>'"* so the fix-loop gets the accurate defect class. `blank-nav.html` guard fixture. A render repin 16→17 dead (the spurious `#/dashboard` pass closed). Commits `01b62986`/`0877ab33`.
- **R5 `260701-kml` — wrong-section fixture C (coverage close-out).** `mis-route.html` — a SPA that activates a real-but-WRONG section (swap map, deliberately NOT named `routes` so `parse_routes_table`→None→`expected`=first-segment) — plus scenario-15 LIVE integration test firing the `activated is not None AND activated != expected` branch end-to-end (was `_nav_ok`-unit-only). TEST-ONLY (no production diff). Commits `b54159bf`/`0d8429ac`.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Producers:** `backend/app/agents/render_check.py` (`_check_nav` broadened discovery + `_exercise_route` section-scoped read → None + `nav_settle_ms` + `_nav_ok`/`_coverage_finding`/`_first_path_segment`), `backend/app/agents/static_check.py` (dynamic-nav dead-links + routes-map resolution cross-check + orphan reconciliation), `backend/app/agents/route_table.py` (NEW shared resolver).
- **Seam/consumers:** `backend/agents/capabilities/validators/severity.py` (`render_coverage_status` + SKIPPED sentinel), `backend/app/agents/validators/html_render.py` (routes through helper; P0 on skipped_blocked; `_record_skip` audit), `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` (`_select_issues_to_fix` + `_run_validation_fix_loop` render-availability via the helper; shared `_dead_nav_line`), `kernel_services.py` + `workflows/plan.py` + `workflows/compiler.py` (`require_render` threading), `capabilities/gates/validation.py` + `capabilities/strategies/task_loop.py` (require_render pass-through), `backend/app/core/config.py` (`PROTOTYPE_REQUIRE_RENDER`).
- **Manifests:** `backend/agents/workflows/prototype/workflow.yaml` + `prototype_revision/workflow.yaml` (`require_render: true`).
- **Tests/fixtures:** `backend/tests/agents/test_nav_coverage.py` (scenarios 1–15) + `test_route_table.py`; the **four regression fixtures** under `backend/tests/agents/fixtures/`: `imc-…-full.html` (A, must-FAIL: 5 genuine static dead + 17/17 render dead/None), `imc-…-fixed.html` (B, must-PASS: 0 static, 17/17 render correct incl. aliases/`:id`), `blank-nav.html` (must-FAIL, every route `activated=None`), `mis-route.html` (must-FAIL, wrong-section `activated != expected`). Harness pin in `tests/agents/_scripted_model.py` (`_patched_compile_for_run` deepcopy + `require_render=False`); wording repins in `test_phase5_fixloop_selection.py`.

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-3:** the 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical every round with NO SNAPSHOT_UPDATE (goldens have no parseable routes table + no nav → the new branches are inert on them; baseline-proven by reverting the producers). The one standing red is **`test_characterization_od_ppt` — a PRE-EXISTING/ENVIRONMENTAL failure** (a local `skills/opendesign/.../example.html` `TEMPLATE EXAMPLE` event-golden drift, first observed in `260701-erg`, baseline-proven independent of the validators; od_ppt is a deck pipeline that runs no HTML validators) → routed to a CI/clean-env re-confirm (see ISSUES-REGISTER / the per-round VERIFICATION `human_verification`).
- **INV-1/12/13:** no `if pipeline_type ==`; the producers/wrappers were extended in place (the ONE new module is the shared `route_table.py`); no `deepagents` change. Additive only — **no migration**. `lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken every round (`route_table.py` + `render_coverage_status` stay app→app / kernel-pure — no kernel→app edge).
- **Fix register:** logged as **FIX-028** (`.planning/FIX-REGISTER.md`).

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- A **direct-DOM `onclick`** that toggles a section with NO route at all is still less-covered than hash / `:id` / `navigateTo` nav (render exercises route-shaped nav).
- The A/B/blank/mis-route fixtures are 4 calibration points (totally-broken / fully-correct / blank / mis-routed), not a proof of universal correctness on arbitrary agent output — keep glancing at raw `activated` values on real runs for new false +/− patterns.
