## Phase 25 — Revision Families & Run-Inputs Surfacing (post-milestone)

**Folder:** NOT a `.planning/phases/<NN>/` GSD phase — **8 GSD quick tasks in 3 workstreams (A/B/C)** on branch `new-workflow-engine`, driven by a single plan-of-record `.planning/REVISION-FAMILY-AND-RUN-INPUTS-PLAN.md` (POR; each task has PLAN+SUMMARY+VERIFICATION under `.planning/quick/260702-{s3p,u2o,uos,vff,wag,wwx}-*/` + `260703-{08q,174}-*/`; the two FE-heavy workstreams also carry `WORKSTREAM-{B,C}-UI-SPEC.md` design contracts + `WORKSTREAM-{B,C}-UI-REVIEW.md` 6-pillar audits; no `_register-parts` file).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-03) — each task plan-checked + verified offline (A 6/6 · B1 6/6 · B2 7/7 · B3 6/6 · C1 7/7 · C2 6/6; two UI audits 21/24→fixed & 23/24→fixed); the two UI-review fix passes (wag, 174) verified by re-run.  ·  **Plans:** 8 quick tasks / 25 code+test commits + 8 docs commits (`45552896`…`63a63e31`).  ·  **Plan-id → §25:** (post-milestone, no plan-id).
**Requirements delivered:** None new (a UX/read-surface effort — no REQ-IDs). Local requirement tags in the POR: A §3/§4, B §5 D1-D7, C §5 D3/D4/D7 + the parser/fetcher/retention foundation.
**Driver:** three user-reported product gaps — (1) a revision workflow shows ONLY the revised artifact and reads as a disconnected orphan run (the source must be dug out of history); (2) the user's original prompt/brief is invisible after launch (buried in the 60–100k-char agent input blob); (3) clarify questions & answers are wiped from FE state the instant they're submitted, visible nowhere afterward.

**One-line outcome:** All three are **read-surface gaps, not storage gaps** — the data model already held everything (`WorkflowRun.parent_run_id` indexed self-FK, `WorkflowRun.input` verbatim brief, per-round `kind="clarifications"` artifact refs, `derived_from` lineage, the never-consumed `/artifacts` endpoint). So the fix is a backend read surface + FE surfacing/wiring with **ZERO migrations and ZERO kernel edits**: revisions become one versioned **workflow family**, the brief becomes a first-class **Starting-point card + `prompt.md`**, and clarify Q&A is retained live + fetched on reopen into a **Clarifications card + `clarifications.md`**.

### The 8 quick tasks (3 workstreams)

- **A `260702-s3p` — backend read surface (app-layer only: `runs.py` + `websocket.py`).** (1) **IDOR fix** — a single `_resolve_owned_parent_run_id(db, id, user.id)` helper hardens BOTH parent-link ingress sites (`run_pipeline` :1731, `run_revision` :2286) to link `parent_run_id` only when the source row exists AND is owned (keyed on `user_id` — the Phase-13 CR-01 precedent — NOT the nullable backfilled `owner_id`; engine `assert_owns` stays the content gate, dispatch untouched). (2) additive `parent_run_id` + server-computed `root_run_id` on `WorkflowRunResponse` (list+get) via a memoized owned-ancestor walk with a foreign/missing sentinel terminator + cycle guard (zero extra queries for NULL-parent pages) + a `_run_response` `model_fields` builder that solves the `from_attributes` computed-field gap. (3) NEW `GET /api/runs/{id}/family` — owner-scoped (404-not-403), BFS over owned children, `(created_at ASC, id ASC)` order, 1-based `revision_index`, **out-of-family parent pointers nulled so no foreign metadata leaks**. (4) additive `kind` filter on `/artifacts` (filter BEFORE `_build_lineage_tree` → filtered refs surface as roots; no-param byte-identical; unknown→empty not 422). 52 unit tests; goldens 9/1-baseline (od_ppt env); lint 4/0.
- **B1 `260702-u2o` — FE foundation + universal linkage.** `getRunFamily` fetcher + raw `RunFamily`/`FamilyMember` types (snake-shape, like `ChainContext`) + REQUIRED `WorkflowRun.parentRunId`/`rootRunId` mapped in `normalizeWorkflowRun`; a new `contentSourceRunId` state (page.tsx: set on `pipeline_complete.pipeline_run_id` + reopen `fullRun.id`, cleared on fresh run) that **replaces + deletes** the fragile `currentWorkflowRunId` heuristic (with its `prototype_revision_revision` never-match bug); universal revision linkage — all 4 inline revise handlers (Ppt via `parent_run_id` WS field, the 3 run_pipeline paths via `source_workflow_run_id`) + all 4 history `onRevise*` callbacks (now thread `selectedRun.id` — previously sent NOTHING → orphan runs).
- **B2 `260702-uos` — history family grouping + detail version timeline** (per UI-SPEC Surfaces 1+2). New `RevisionFamilyView.tsx` sibling: `groupRunsByFamily` by `rootRunId` → one root card per family (`v{N}` pill + expandable chronological version rows with `↳ revises v{n}`); single-member/legacy-NULL-parent families render byte-identical to today's flat row (delete menu preserved via threaded props — plan-check W1). Detail-view `VersionTimeline` radiogroup chip row above the tab bar (Arrow-key nav + managed focus); click-to-switch via `getWorkflow` + `AnimatePresence mode="wait"` cross-fade. **Verifier caught + orchestrator fixed a cross-suite regression**: the new detail-open `getRunFamily` crashed 3 pre-existing WorkflowHistory suites lacking the mock export (the B2 lesson — undefined-mock-export) → empty-family mock added.
- **B3 `260702-vff` — live version chip + base-version Files** (UI-SPEC Surfaces 3+4). New `LiveVersionChip.tsx` in the PreviewPanel header — `v{n} ▾` pill (≥2-member family only); dropdown loads an older version READ-ONLY via `getWorkflow` (content routed into the `renderType` slot, `onRevise*` forced undefined) with an amber "Viewing v{k} (read-only) — Back to latest →" banner; pulse-ticks on revision-complete. Family fetched in DashboardLayout (owns `contentSourceRunId`). FilesTab "From v{n-1}" collapsed lazy base-version section; the per-type file-build branches extracted into a module-level `deriveDeliverableFiles` helper (INV-12 net-negative). All new props OPTIONAL → zero regression.
- **wag `260702-wag` — Workstream-B closing UI audit + a11y sign-off.** gsd-ui-auditor scored the B surface **21/24, reuse-fidelity HONORED, SHIP** (1 BLOCK + 4 FLAG, all a11y/consistency). Fixes: (BLOCK) the LiveVersionChip listbox was keyboard-dead (handler on a non-focusable div) → roving-tabIndex `role=option` buttons + a **declarative focus effect keyed on `highlightIdx`** (mirrors the auditor-praised VersionTimeline radiogroup; the imperative `.focus()` stranded focus on BODY — an executor Rule-1 fix); (FLAG) history child rows → native `<button>` w/ `aria-label`; (FLAG) `groupRunsByFamily` sorts `(createdAt ASC, id ASC)` = the backend `/family` tie-break so list v-numbers == timeline `revision_index` for present members; (FLAG) unified lowercase "↳ revises v{n}"; (FLAG) `[revision-family]` console.warn on 3 swallowed fetch sites.
- **C1 `260702-wwx` — run-inputs foundation.** Shared `lib/runInput.ts` `parseRunInput(input) → {brief, attachments[], revisionInstruction?, existingArtifactBlock?, chainContext?, preferences?}` — the SINGLE exact (non-heuristic) parser for every FE-composed `=== marker ===` family; handles two real gotchas: **ASYMMETRIC** `EXISTING {X}`/`END EXISTING {Y}` labels (independent open/close match, NOT a backreference — a backreference silently drops real inputs) and **UNCLOSED** `REVISION REQUEST` (capture to next `===`/EOF). Replaces the DashboardLayout `safeCleanBrief` dup AND B2's `extractRevisionInstructionPreview` shim (now delegates) — **INV-12 one parser project-wide**. `getRunArtifacts` fetcher (first FE consumer of A's kind filter) + `RunArtifactsResponse`/`ArtifactNode` types (node has NO created_at). `ClarifyRound` type + `PipelineRunState.clarifications?` + live retention: `retainClarifyRound` folds the answered Q&A into pipelineState BEFORE the questionnaire panel clears (survives unmount — gap-3 closed), reset per run; `submittedBrief` captured on the live launch path (gap-2).
- **C2 `260703-08q` — StartingPointCard + ClarificationsCard + Files "Run input"** (UI-SPEC Surfaces 1-3; the FINAL build unit). New `StartingPointCard.tsx` BEFORE PlannerCard (clones the PlannerCard shell; props-driven so it renders on reopen; variant by PARSED shape via C1 `parseRunInput`, NEVER `workflowType` — SC-001): normal → brief + attachment chips; revision → instruction primary + "revision of v{n-1}" chip + a lazy "Original brief (v1)" expander (`getWorkflow(rootRunId)` via the B3 dynamic-import idiom, so no render-time api call); chained → chip. New `ClarificationsCard.tsx` AFTER PlannerCard — per-round Q&A (question semibold + emerald answer) + amber(high)/gray(medium) impact badges; renders NOTHING for a PROCEED run; live=`pipelineState.clarifications`, reopen=`getRunArtifacts(kind=clarifications)` → new `lib/clarifications.ts` `parseClarificationArtifacts` (JSON→ClarifyRound[], NOT a 2nd run-input parser). FilesTab "Run input" section: `prompt.md` (always) + `clarifications.md` (rounds only) via `renderFileRow`+`downloadBlob`. Wired on BOTH mounts. Timeline narrative order **Starting point→Planner→Clarifications→Agents→Complete**. Regression guard: `getRunArtifacts` added to all 4 WorkflowHistory api-mocks (the B2 lesson, done in-plan).
- **174 `260703-174` — Workstream-C closing UI audit + polish.** gsd-ui-auditor scored the C surface **23/24, reuse-fidelity HONORED, a11y 4/4** (C did NOT repeat B's keyboard gaps — every expander a native button w/ aria-expanded, badges carry text, fetch regions aria-busy), SHIP — 0 BLOCK / 3 FLAG (all non-visual). Fixes (wiring, not restyle): the "revision of v{n}" chip was AUTHORED in StartingPointCard but `revisionParentVersion` was **never threaded** → a dead spec feature; computed it (1-based family index of the active run's PARENT via `parent_run_id` lookup) + threaded at BOTH AgentThinkingTab mounts (live PreviewPanel from `runFamily`, reopen WorkflowHistory from the fetched `family`); threaded `originalBriefRootRunId` at the live mount too (live/reopen expander symmetry); ClarificationsCard timeline dot recolored to the solid-navy sibling convention (inherited token). 88-test audited regression re-run green.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Backend (A, app-layer only — ZERO `backend/agents/` edits):** `backend/app/api/runs.py` (`_run_response`/`_compute_root_ids` owned walk; `get_run_family` endpoint + `RunFamilyResponse`/`FamilyMemberResponse`; `parent_run_id`/`root_run_id` on `WorkflowRunResponse`; `kind` param on `get_run_artifacts`), `backend/app/api/websocket.py` (`_resolve_owned_parent_run_id` helper wired at both parent-link ingress sites).
- **FE new components:** `frontend/src/components/history/RevisionFamilyView.tsx` (B2 — grouping + VersionTimeline + statusDotClass), `frontend/src/components/preview/LiveVersionChip.tsx` (B3 — chip + dropdown + read-only banner), `frontend/src/components/results/StartingPointCard.tsx` + `ClarificationsCard.tsx` (C2).
- **FE new libs:** `frontend/src/lib/runInput.ts` (C1 `parseRunInput` — the ONE marker parser), `frontend/src/lib/clarifications.ts` (C1/C2 — `parseClarificationArtifacts` + `renderClarificationsMarkdown`). New fetchers in `frontend/src/lib/api.ts`: `getRunFamily`, `getRunArtifacts`.
- **FE wired surfaces:** `AgentThinkingTab.tsx` (places both cards, narrative order), `FilesTab.tsx` ("Run input" section + `deriveDeliverableFiles` + base-version section), `WorkflowHistory.tsx` (family grouping list + detail timeline + reopen clarify fetch + both card mounts), `PreviewPanel.tsx` (live chip + read-only override + both card mounts), `DashboardLayout.tsx` + `app/dashboard/page.tsx` (`contentSourceRunId` + `submittedBrief` + `getRunFamily` seam + universal revision linkage), `types/index.ts` (`RunFamily`/`FamilyMember`/`ClarifyRound`/`RunArtifactsResponse` + `WorkflowRun.parentRunId`/`rootRunId` + `PipelineRunState.clarifications`).

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **D1 — child-run model KEPT; unify at the read/UX layer.** A revision stays its own terminal `WorkflowRun` (folding into the parent would fight the state machine/seq counter/checkpointer/Phase-14 terminal-status fidelity). The "family" is a read-time concept over the `parent_run_id` chain. **`parent_run_id` is the ONLY lineage column** — the dead `source_run_id` column stays untouched, NOT repurposed (a future cleanup-migration decision, not this effort).
- **D2 — version = 1-based CHRONOLOGICAL index within the family** (`created_at ASC, id ASC` — the exact backend `/family` tie-break; list and timeline coincide for present members). Branching-safe (members carry `parent_run_id` for "revises v{n}" badges). NOT `artifact_refs.version` (that's per-`(run_id,kind)`).
- **D3 — brief single-sourced in `run.input`; D4 — clarifications single-sourced in the existing `kind="clarifications"` refs.** No new storage (INV-12). Surfacing only: `parseRunInput` derives the brief; `getRunArtifacts(kind=clarifications)` reads the Q&A on reopen; live retention holds it in `pipelineState.clarifications`.
- **D5 — ZERO new WS event types** (a new event enters the golden event multiset — the one confirmed INV-3 tripwire). D6 — ownership keyed on `user_id` (the nullable backfilled `owner_id` is NOT the authz key). D7 — `root_run_id` computed server-side (FE groups by a plain field, pagination-safe).

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-3:** the 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical throughout — A is app-layer + DB/API fields (never golden-tracked; the harness drives the engine directly, asserts deliverable bytes + normalized event multiset only), B/C are FE-only (no engine/websocket/manifest touched). Proven each task with `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset; the one standing red is the **pre-existing `test_characterization_od_ppt` env failure** (P24 baseline), unrelated.
- **SC-001/INV-1:** kernel untouched — A/B/C touch ZERO files under `backend/agents/`; family logic keys on `parent_run_id` + the generic `_revision` suffix idiom; every FE card variant is chosen by parsed shape, never a `workflowType` string (grep-gated 0 in both cards).
- **INV-12 (net-negative):** one `parseRunInput` replaced the DashboardLayout `safeCleanBrief` dup + B2's `extractRevisionInstructionPreview` shim; `deriveDeliverableFiles` extracted the FilesTab per-type branches (deleted from body); the `currentWorkflowRunId` heuristic replaced by `contentSourceRunId`. No dual storage anywhere.
- **Q3 / Ports & Adapters:** ZERO migrations (every column/table pre-existed); `lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken (app-layer + FE only). tsc identity gate GATE_OK on every FE task (3 pre-existing `mockApi`/`IdeaInputPage` errors, zero new — verify by identity, tsc emits `file(line,col)`).
- **Regression discipline (the load-bearing lesson):** a new fetch inside a shared component (`getRunFamily` in B2, `getRunArtifacts` in C2) crashes any sibling test suite that mocks `@/lib/api` without the new export → every task ran the FULL audited sibling-suite set (up to 17 files) and added the missing mock export; B2's verifier caught one that the executor's self-check missed (proved by bisect).

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **Live pass NOT done.** Everything is offline-verified (unit/DOM/goldens/lint) per the project's defer-live-verification convention — a live Bedrock/Playwright pass on the `/family` endpoint + the revision-chip render + the read-only version load is the outstanding confirmation (SSO login is interactive). **The branch is NOT pushed** (33 commits on `new-workflow-engine` ahead of origin).
- **Workstream D parked** (POR §9, a SEPARATE decision): retire the FE content-inlining for `user_stories_revision`/`app_builder_revision`/ppt-fallback (server-side parent seeding via the `previous_run` provider + FR-014, instruction-only `run.input`), which would kill the 40k/60k truncation and let `parseRunInput` retire the `=== marker ===` convention entirely. **Hard fence:** `prototype_revision` is a characterization golden with pinned `context_message` bytes — its extraction mechanism is untouchable without a deliberate re-baseline; D scopes to the non-golden revision types only.
- **Legacy revision runs with NULL `parent_run_id`** (linkage never captured) render as standalone families — no backfill is possible (honest); new revisions link correctly.
- **Window-vs-authoritative version count:** the history list card counts family members present in the ≤100-run `getWorkflows` window; the detail `/family` timeline is the authoritative full count. They coincide for recent clustered families; a family with members beyond the fetch window shows fewer in the list card (documented, by design — POR §11).

## Phase 26 — Cost, Caching, Cache-Token Accounting & Pricing Single-Source (post-milestone)

**Folder:** NOT a `.planning/phases/<NN>/` GSD phase — **5 GSD quick tasks** on branch `new-workflow-engine` (each with PLAN+SUMMARY+VERIFICATION under `.planning/quick/260704-{p10,t2x,ttk,uvs}-*/` + `.planning/quick/260705-ed8-*/`; no plan-of-record — a single-session cost/caching sweep driven directly by a real Bedrock-bill investigation). One of them (ed8) is a self-correction of a regression another (t2x) introduced in the same window.  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-05) — each task plan-checked + independently gate-re-run offline (p10 5/5 · t2x pass · ttk pass · uvs 12/12 · ed8 6/6 hard gates); the one deferred item is a live Bedrock `cache_read>0` confirmation, held per the project's defer-live-verification convention.  ·  **Plans:** 5 quick tasks / ~18 code+test commits + 5 docs commits (`5346c242`, `4d949f9a`, `16b89f03`, `3d8ff806`, `102135ed`).  ·  **Plan-id → §26:** (post-milestone, no plan-id).
**Requirements delivered:** None new (a telemetry/cost-correctness + caching-enablement effort — no REQ-IDs). Fix-register coverage: **FIX-034** (p10) · **FIX-035** (t2x) · **FIX-036** (ttk) · **FIX-037** (uvs) · **FIX-038** (ed8); issues **ISS-031** (closed by FIX-034), **ISS-032** (RESOLVED by FIX-036), **ISS-033/ISS-034** (OPEN follow-ups).
**Driver:** a user-reported high AWS Bedrock bill → a gut-feel "are we burning tokens?" investigation confirmed **~21.46M input tokens ≈ $24.85 per build is REAL** (read straight from Bedrock `usage_metadata`, not a counting artifact). Five compounding root causes: (1) prompt caching silently OFF on Bedrock (deepagents' `AnthropicPromptCachingMiddleware` caches only `ChatAnthropic`; prod runs `ChatBedrockConverse`), (2) run cost under-reported 4x–20x (both sites hardcoded Claude-3-Haiku `$0.25/$1.25`/M for every model), (3) cache tokens unaccounted (the runner dropped the `input_token_details` split), (4) no UI visibility of caching, and (5) t2x's own fix regressed INV-12 by adding a second model-id list.

**One-line outcome:** Caching is turned **ON** for Bedrock (config-gated, default ON) via a deepagents middleware, cost is corrected to the **real per-model + regional rate** and **cache-discounted** through **one shared `estimate_cost_usd`**, the cache split is surfaced end-to-end (runner → engine → both cost sites → FE), and the pricing table is folded back into the **single model-id source** — all **golden-neutral** (cost + model_id are stripped volatile keys) with **zero kernel edits** (app-layer + shared runner/engine + a kernel-pure capability module + FE).

### The 5 quick tasks

- **p10 `260704-p10` — Bedrock prompt caching ON + extended-thinking knob (FIX-034 / ISS-031; commit `5346c242`).** New provider-agnostic `_BedrockCachePointsMiddleware` (mirrors `_ToolFilterMiddleware`: sync `wrap_model_call` + async `awrap_model_call` + `request.override`) fires **only** on `isinstance(request.model, ChatBedrockConverse)`, gated on `BEDROCK_PROMPT_CACHE_ENABLED` (read at CALL time, default ON, `BEDROCK_PROMPT_CACHE_TTL` "5m"), setting `model_settings['cache_control']` so `langchain_aws._apply_cache_points` appends cachePoints — registered in the existing `create_deep_agent(middleware=[...])` list, no-op on ChatAnthropic/scripted (no double-apply). Plus an enable-only `THINKING_BUDGET_TOKENS` (default 0) threading `thinking={"type":"enabled","budget_tokens":…}` into **both** `build_model` provider branches, budget clamped to `[1024, resolved max_tokens − 1]` with `temperature=1` forced in the enabled branch ONLY (disabled path byte-identical). 3 new Settings fields; 10 offline tests.
- **t2x `260704-t2x` — per-model / region-aware run cost (FIX-035 / ISS-032; commit `4d949f9a`; INV-12 regression later fixed by ed8).** Both cost sites previously computed an inline Claude-3-Haiku `(_tok_in·0.00000025)+(_tok_out·0.00000125)` for EVERY model → under-report 4x (Haiku 4.5) to ~20x (Opus). New kernel-pure stdlib-only `model_pricing.py` (per-family rates + `_regional_premium` +10% for eu./us./apac + cache read/write-5m/write-1h tiers) routed through ONE shared `estimate_cost_usd` at both the engine `pipeline_complete` event and the persisted `workflow_runs.token_usage`; NULL `model_id` prices against `BEDROCK_INFERENCE_PROFILE_ID`. Reconciliation pin `$24.85`. Cache args left at default 0 (deferred → ISS-032). Note: this task introduced a **second** model-id list, superseded internally by ed8.
- **ttk `260704-ttk` — capture prompt-cache tokens for costing (ISS-032 → RESOLVED, FIX-036; ISS-033 logged; commit `16b89f03`).** langchain_aws sets `usage_metadata.input_tokens` to the TOTAL (incl. cache) with the split in `input_token_details={cache_read, cache_creation}`. New pure runner helper `_cache_token_counts(meta) → (cache_read, cache_write)` (guards every absent/None/partial shape → `(0,0)`, never raises) surfaces the split into the usage event + text-only `TokenUsage`; the engine accumulates per-agent → `results` + `agent_complete` + run totals on `pipeline_complete`; BOTH cost sites price the **UNCACHED** split `input_tokens=max(0, total − cache_read − cache_write)` + cache_read (0.1x) + cache_write (1.25x/2x) + `cache_ttl` via the ONE shared `estimate_cost_usd` — input telemetry stays the TOTAL, only the cost is discounted, no double-count. Golden-neutral via 4 ADDITIVE `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`. Direct-call agents deliberately out of scope → ISS-033.
- **uvs `260704-uvs` — cache-token breakdown in the UI (FIX-037 / ISS-034; commit `3d8ff806`).** FRONTEND-ONLY consume of the already-emitted FIX-036 fields: `TokenUsageSummary` renders a `·`-separated `⚡ {N} cached ({X}%)` amber segment after the input figure, gated on `cacheRead > 0` (`pct = round(cacheRead / max(1, input) · 100)`, optional `· {N} written` when `cache_write > 0`) — byte-identical render when cache is 0/undefined (the common case until a live cached run). Threaded via optional type fields on `WorkflowRun.tokenUsage` + `PipelineRunState`, parsed in `useWorkflow` (`pipeline_complete` authoritative; `agent_complete` preserves prev). **No FE dollar / per-model math** (INV-12) — cost is already discounted backend-side; dollar-savings deferred → ISS-034.
- **ed8 `260705-ed8` — model_pricing INV-12 single-source restore (FIX-038; commit `102135ed`).** t2x's `MODEL_PRICING` was a SECOND hand-maintained model-id list → it violated INV-12 and broke `test_model_catalog::test_single_source_grep` (green at baseline `eb3ccced`, red at t2x HEAD). Proper fix (no test-loosen): a frozen `Pricing` dataclass (`input, output, cache_read, cache_write_5m, cache_write_1h`) + a `pricing` field on `ModelEntry` added to `model_catalog.py` (the single source; all 5 entries populated); `model_pricing.py` now DERIVES each rate via a three-tier `_resolve_pricing` (exact catalog `get` → region/version-normalized fallback → `cost_class=="cheap"` default) and holds **ZERO** `claude-(haiku|sonnet|opus)-4` literals — the deleted `MODEL_PRICING`/`Price`/`_FALLBACK`/`_canonical` machinery all carried literals. `estimate_cost_usd` signature + math + the `$24.85` pin preserved; engine.py / websocket.py untouched.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Caching + thinking (p10):** `backend/app/agents/deep_agent_runner.py` (`_BedrockCachePointsMiddleware`, registered in the `create_deep_agent` middleware list), `backend/app/agents/model_factory.py` (clamped thinking budget threaded into both `build_model` branches), `backend/app/core/config.py` (`BEDROCK_PROMPT_CACHE_ENABLED` / `BEDROCK_PROMPT_CACHE_TTL` / `THINKING_BUDGET_TOKENS`).
- **Pricing + cost math (t2x, ttk, ed8):** `backend/agents/capabilities/model_pricing.py` (kernel-pure — `estimate_cost_usd` + `_resolve_pricing` + `_regional_premium`, zero model-id literals) + `backend/agents/capabilities/model_catalog.py` (frozen `Pricing` dataclass + `pricing` field on `ModelEntry` = the single source). Both cost sites: `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` (`pipeline_complete`) + `backend/app/api/websocket.py` (persisted `workflow_runs.token_usage` + the `agent_complete` per-agent cache collector).
- **Cache-token producer/accumulation (ttk):** `backend/app/agents/deep_agent_runner.py` (`_cache_token_counts`, usage-event emit, `astream_with_usage` sum) → `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` (per-agent accumulation + run totals) → `backend/tests/agents/characterization/_normalize.py` (`_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` +4 additive keys).
- **FE cache surfacing (uvs):** `frontend/src/components/workflow/TokenUsageSummary.tsx` (the `⚡ N cached (X%)` segment), `frontend/src/hooks/useWorkflow.ts` (parse), `frontend/src/types/index.ts` (optional cache fields on `WorkflowRun.tokenUsage` + `PipelineRunState`).

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Pricing DERIVES from `model_catalog.py`'s frozen `Pricing` field — one model-id source.** `model_pricing.py` holds ZERO `claude-(haiku|sonnet|opus)-4` literals; `test_model_catalog::test_single_source_grep` (assertion `hits == {"model_catalog.py"}`) enforces it. Do NOT add a 2nd model list OR a parallel pricing table — this EXTENDS the Phase-06 locked single-source decision; ed8 restored it after t2x broke it (FIX-038). Adding a rate = edit the catalog entry's `Pricing`, never a new dict.
- **(b) Caching is Bedrock-only via `_BedrockCachePointsMiddleware`** (no-op on ChatAnthropic/scripted — deepagents' `AnthropicPromptCachingMiddleware` already covers ChatAnthropic, so this avoids double-apply), gated on `BEDROCK_PROMPT_CACHE_ENABLED` (default ON), TTL 5m. Cache injection is a per-call `model_settings['cache_control']` (a middleware `request.override`), NOT a `ChatBedrockConverse` constructor field (`extra=forbid`).
- **(c) Cost math SUBTRACTS cached tokens.** langchain_aws sets `input_tokens` = the TOTAL (incl. cache), split in `input_token_details`; the ONE shared `estimate_cost_usd` prices `input_tokens=max(0, total − cache_read − cache_write)` + cache_read/write tiers. Input telemetry stays the TOTAL; only the cost is discounted — never double-count by subtracting twice (the runner keeps the total; the split happens only at the cost sites).
- **(d) Extended thinking is enable-only, default OFF** (`THINKING_BUDGET_TOKENS=0`) — a dormant operator knob with zero behavioral drift; `temperature=1` is forced ONLY when enabled.
- **(e) Regional premium is +10% for `eu.` / `us.` / `apac.`** inference-profile prefixes (matched on region strings, NOT model-id literals) — lives in `_regional_premium`, applied on top of the base per-family rate.

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-3 (golden-neutral):** all 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical across every task, `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset, NO regen — `cost`/`model_id` are `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` (so a rate swap is invisible by construction) and ttk's 4 new cache keys were added ADDITIVELY to the strip set. Under the scripted model there is no `input_token_details` → cache=0 → uncached=total → math identical.
- **INV-12 (single source, one cost fn):** exactly ONE `estimate_cost_usd` at both cost sites; the single model+pricing source restored by ed8 (a genuine INV-12 regression caught + fixed in-window).
- **INV-13 (deepagents):** caching + thinking are middleware / model-kwargs only — no hand-rolled loop, no cachePoints re-implementation.
- **SC-001 (kernel knows no workflow by name):** caching dispatch is `isinstance(ChatBedrockConverse)`; cost dispatch keys off `model_id` + region only; the FE segment gates on `cacheRead > 0` (a value, not a `workflowType`).
- **Evidence:** goldens 10/10 (each task); `model_pricing` + `iss032` 39 passed; `model_catalog` 9 incl. `single_source_grep` GREEN; bedrock cache/thinking 10; FE `TokenUsageSummary`/`useWorkflow` 12/12; `lint-imports` 4 kept / 0 broken throughout.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **ISS-033 (OPEN) — the last caching-everywhere gap.** SmartPlanner (`smart_planner.py:389`), ClarifyEngine (`clarify_engine.py:492-493`), and the handoff Test/Compliance agents call the model DIRECTLY (bypassing the shared deep-agent runner) → they get NO Bedrock prompt caching (the p10 middleware runs only in the runner path) AND their tokens are UNCOUNTED in run cost (they never emit `usage` events into the engine's per-agent accumulation). Each direct call can be up to ~150k tokens against a ~450k brief. Fix = a shared cached-invoke helper routing direct calls through the same cachePoints + `usage_metadata` path.
- **ISS-034 (OPEN) — dollar-savings display.** Show "saved $Y (Z%)" by having the backend also emit `estimated_cost_full_usd` (cost priced as-if-uncached) so the FE can render the delta; explicitly NOT built in FIX-037 (which shows only the cache token count + %).
- **Live Bedrock `cache_read>0` confirmation DEFERRED** to the end-of-milestone Bedrock pass (offline structural proof landed now; SSO login is interactive) — per the project's defer-live-verification convention.
- **Adjacent same-window tasks (separate scope):** `er8` (FIX-033 — Files-tab run-input file for revision runs), `byv` (FIX-029 — prototype Thinking-tab StartingPoint/Clarifications preamble + build-checklist flash), and `d4v` (FIX-030 — prototype run-endpoint brief-cap raise) also landed on `new-workflow-engine` this window; see the STATE quick-tasks table.

## Phase 27 — Spec Revision Loop: "Update the Specs" on Analyze Gate (KAN-101, post-milestone)

**Folder:** NOT a `.planning/phases/<NN>/` GSD phase — a single-session feature implementation driven by KAN-101. Fix-register entry: **FIX-048** (2026-07-07, commit `d3a311d`). Branch: `new-workflow-engine`.  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-07) — live-verified on real Bedrock (revision sub-pipeline fired, specify re-ran with analysis context, plan re-ran, analyze re-opened gate; no errors).  ·  **Plan-id → §27:** (post-milestone, no plan-id).

**Requirements delivered:** KAN-101 — after the Spec Kit Analyzer completes, users can trigger an AI-driven spec revision cycle ("Update the Specs") that re-runs prototype-specify → prototype-plan → prototype-analyze with the analysis report as context, then re-opens the analyze gate. The loop repeats unlimited times. Only "Accept & continue to build" advances to the build agents.

**Driver:** The analyze gate only offered Approve (ignore issues) or Reject (lose all work). Users needed an actionable path to iteratively fix spec issues found by the analyzer before committing to a full build.

**One-line outcome:** A new `action="update_specs"` on the `approve_review` WS handler triggers a `_gate_update_specs` engine signal; a new `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline` helper re-runs the three preceding agents with the analysis report injected via `ectx.spec_revision_context`; the Thinking tab shows a revision banner + version chips (v2, v3…) on Phase 1/2/3 cards. Stop-button cancel-awareness inherited from KAN-100. INV-1/INV-3/INV-12/SC-001 all held.

### What was built

**Backend (3 files):**

- **`backend/app/api/websocket.py`** — new `elif action == "update_specs"` branch in the `approve_review` handler reads `analysis_report` from the WS message and stores it via `store.set_review_response(action="update_specs", instructions=analysis_report)`. Rides the same owner-gated IDOR boundary as the existing `redo` branch (13-REVIEW CR-01).
- **`backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py`** — four changes:
  1. `_run_review_gate`: new `if action == "update_specs"` branch transitions state to `generating` and yields `{"type": "_gate_update_specs", "analysis_report": instructions}` — a new internal signal mirroring `_gate_redo` (SC-001: keyed on generic action discriminator, no agent/pipeline-type literal).
  2. `_run_agent` while-loop locals: added `spec_revision_attempt = 0` alongside `redo_attempt`.
  3. `_run_agent` while-loop top: a `spec_revision_pending_output` sentinel check — if `ectx.spec_revision_pending_output` is set after a revision cycle, skip the model call and re-open the gate directly with the new analysis output (consume-once, F3 pattern).
  4. Inline gate consumer: new `elif gate_event.get("type") == "_gate_update_specs"` branch calls `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline`, collects the new analysis output, stores it on `ectx.spec_revision_pending_output`, and `break`s to re-enter the while-loop.
  5. New method `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline`: position-based (not name-based — INV-1) re-run of `ordered_agents[index-2]` (specify) → `ordered_agents[index-1]` (plan) → `spec` (analyze) using the existing `_run_agent` path (INV-12). Sets `ectx.spec_revision_context = analysis_report` before running; clears it in `finally` (consume-once). Yields all WS events upstream. Returns the new analyze output via `_revision_analyze_output` internal signal. Cancel-aware throughout.
  6. `_compose_context_message`: new `elif revision_context := getattr(ectx, "spec_revision_context", "")` block appending a `=== SPEC KIT ANALYSIS REPORT (REVISION CONTEXT) ===` block — dormant on every normal run (INV-3), generic scratch field (SC-001).
- **`backend/agents/prompts/prototype-specify/AGENT.md`** — new `## REVISION MODE` section: when the system prompt contains a `SPEC KIT ANALYSIS REPORT` block, the spec writer fixes identified issues rather than regenerating from scratch. Output contract (`<spec>...</spec>`) unchanged.

**Frontend (6 files):**

- **`frontend/src/components/preview/ReviewGatePanel.tsx`** — added `onUpdateSpecs?: (gateKey, analysisReport) => void` prop; `handleUpdateSpecs` callback; "Update the Specs" button (rendered only when `isAnalysis && !!onUpdateSpecs`); approve button label changed to "Accept & continue to build" for the analysis gate. Panel clears on action (same as Redo).
- **`frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`** — added `onUpdateSpecsReview` prop to interface + destructuring; wired into `<ReviewGatePanel onUpdateSpecs={onUpdateSpecsReview}>`.
- **`frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx`** — added `onUpdateSpecsReview` callback that sends `{type:"approve_review", gate_key, action:"update_specs", analysis_report:output}` and clears `reviewGateData`.
- **`frontend/src/types/index.ts`** — added `specRevisionCount?: number` field to `PipelineRunState`.
- **`frontend/src/hooks/useWorkflow.ts`** — in `agent_start` handler: detects a spec revision re-run (when `prototype-specify` fires `agent_start` and is already `"done"`), resets its output/thinking/context for live display, and increments `pipelineState.specRevisionCount`.
- **`frontend/src/components/results/PrototypePipelineView.tsx`** — added `versionChip?: string` prop to `PhaseCard`; computes `versionLabel = revisionCount > 0 ? "v${currentVersion}" : undefined` from `pipelineState.specRevisionCount`; adds a violet revision banner ("Spec Revision Cycle N · Spec & plan are being revised") above the phase cards when `revisionCount > 0`; passes `versionChip` to Phase 1/2/3 cards; imports `RefreshCw` icon.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **WS handler:** `backend/app/api/websocket.py` — `approve_review` handler `elif action == "update_specs"` branch
- **Engine signals + sub-pipeline:** `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` — `_run_review_gate`, `_run_agent` (loop top + inline gate consumer + loop local), new `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline`, `_compose_context_message`
- **Revision prompt:** `backend/agents/prompts/prototype-specify/AGENT.md` — `## REVISION MODE` section
- **FE gate panel:** `frontend/src/components/preview/ReviewGatePanel.tsx`
- **FE wiring:** `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx`
- **FE state + Thinking tab:** `frontend/src/types/index.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useWorkflow.ts`, `frontend/src/components/results/PrototypePipelineView.tsx`

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Generic action discriminator only — no pipeline_type/agent_id literals (INV-1 / SC-001).** The `update_specs` action is keyed on the `action` string in `set_review_response`; the sub-pipeline helper is position-based (`index-2`, `index-1`, `index`), never `if agent_id == "prototype-specify"`. The `ectx.spec_revision_context` and `ectx.spec_revision_pending_output` scratch fields are generic (any workflow with the right shape can use this).
- **(b) Sub-pipeline reuses `_run_agent` — no second agent runner (INV-12 / INV-13).** `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline` calls `_run_agent` for each of the three agents. No hand-rolled loop, no duplicate deepagents runner.
- **(c) Goldens stay byte-identical (INV-3).** `_gate_update_specs` and `_revision_analyze_output` are internal signals never forwarded on the WS wire. The `spec_revision_context` block in `_compose_context_message` is dormant when the scratch field is empty. The `update_specs` action is never sent by the characterization scripted model. No regen needed.
- **(d) Cancel-awareness (KAN-100 precedent).** The sub-pipeline checks `cancel_event.is_set()` before each agent run; if set, yields `_gate_rejected` and returns immediately.
- **(e) Unlimited revision cycles by design.** No hard cap on `spec_revision_attempt`. Each cycle increments the counter; the FE shows the version number. Stop button is the only hard abort.

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-1:** All engine branching on `action == "update_specs"` string and generic ectx scratch fields. Zero `pipeline_type` or `agent_id` literals in the new code.
- **INV-3:** No new WS event wire keys (internal signals only). 5 characterization goldens unaffected, no regen.
- **INV-12:** `_run_spec_revision_sub_pipeline` calls `_run_agent`. No duplicate runner.
- **SC-001:** Position-based sub-pipeline. `ectx.spec_revision_context` read by `_compose_context_message` generically.
- **Evidence:** Backend started clean (startup log `Backend ready`, no import errors). Frontend TypeScript diagnostics 0 errors on all 6 changed files. Live Bedrock verification: revision cycle fired at 16:42:42 (`Review gate update_specs: has_report=True`), `prototype-specify` re-ran in 84s with analysis context, gate re-opened, `prototype-plan` started — no errors in trace.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **Full versioned history in Thinking tab** — the current implementation (Option B) updates Phase 1/2/3 cards in-place and shows a version chip + revision banner. A full history showing v1 spec collapsed + v2 spec expanded with per-iteration verdict badges is a follow-on enhancement (would require suffixed agent IDs or a `runs[]` per agent in state).
- **`AgentProgressPanel` version annotation** — the left panel agent rows show the current run state but do not yet show the version number (e.g. "Spec Writer Agent v2"). Minor enhancement.
- **Analysis report stored as artifact** — the analysis report passed via `instructions` field is currently in-memory only. Persisting it as a typed artifact with `derived_from` lineage is a future enhancement for the audit trail.

## Phase 28 — Chat Contracts & Guards [A0]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/28-chat-contracts-guards-a0/`  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-07) — `28-VERIFICATION.md` verdict `passed`, 9/9 must-haves verified, 0 overrides; no human verification required (docs/tests-only phase, no UI or runnable endpoint)  ·  **Plans:** 3/3  ·  **Plan-id → CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md (milestone v2.0 POR):** A0

**Requirements delivered:** CHAT-06 — "Golden neutrality: new event types in `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES`, volatile keys in `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`, characterization proof that chat never fires on golden paths" (REQUIREMENTS.md line 452, the only REQ-ID this phase maps to). The phase's other four deliverables (Steps-L2 artifact-derivation contract, figure-to-field pinning, D-12 live-state contract, mockWs chat-driver contract, resolved-decisions record) are not separately REQ-ID'd — they are covered by the phase's own must-haves (`28-VERIFICATION.md` truths 6-9) rather than REQUIREMENTS.md.

**Driver:** every later v2.0 phase (29 chat backbone, 31 chat lane, 32 run redesign, …) needed a locked, code-verified contract to build against instead of ad-hoc mock interpretation. New chat event types had to be declared legal in the documented WS vocabulary and normalizer *before* any code emitted them, or later phases would either trip the forward-vocabulary guard or risk perturbing the 5 characterization goldens (POR §7 landmine). The Steps artifact-derivation rules and every run-screen figure needed pinning to an exact backend source so downstream UI work doesn't invent numbers, and the POR's autonomous-run decisions needed one citable, DECIDED record so the auto-chain never re-opens them.

**One-line outcome:** Five additive, tests+docs-only deliverables landed with zero kernel/runtime edits: `chat_message`/`chat_reply`/`stream_attached` registered in `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES` + `message_id`/`replayed_through_seq` in `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`, guarded by a standing `test_chat_event_neutrality.py` proof; three UI-SPEC contract docs (Steps-L2 artifact derivation, figure-to-field pinning, D-12 live-state table); the mockWs chat-driver e2e contract; and `RESOLVED-DECISIONS.md` (LOCK-A..G + the full ND-1..ND-13 set — a superset of the roadmap one-liner's "ND-1..ND-9" wording, confirmed not-a-gap by the verifier). All 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical (22/22 offline green, no fixture touched), lint-imports 4 kept/0 broken, INV-1/INV-3/SC-001 all held.

### What was built

**Vocabulary & golden guards (28-01 — 2 modified, 1 new):**

- **`backend/tests/agents/test_phase3_cutover_verify.py`** — added `chat_message`, `chat_reply`, `stream_attached` to `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES` with a documenting comment matching the existing "declared-gate dispatch events" convention, making the three run-chat event types legal in the documented outbound WS vocabulary before any code emits them.
- **`backend/tests/agents/characterization/_normalize.py`** — added `message_id` (FE idempotency key) and `replayed_through_seq` (replay cursor) to `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`, following the existing `image_count` belt-and-suspenders precedent. `stream_attached.live` and the `chat_reply` card discriminators were deliberately left unstripped (deterministic/stable); `_REQUIRED_DATA_KEYS` untouched.
- **`backend/tests/agents/test_chat_event_neutrality.py`** (new, 90 lines) — parametrized, paired proof: (a) none of the three chat event types appears in any of the 5 golden event streams, (b) all three are members of `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES`, so an accidental removal of either fails loudly. Reads committed fixtures only; never drives the engine.

**UI-SPEC contract docs (28-02 — 3 new, under `contracts/`):**

- **`STEPS-ARTIFACT-DERIVATION-CONTRACT.md`** — pins each of the four Steps-L2 artifact kinds to its exact backend source: `pages` ← the prototype route table (`route_table.py`), `tasks` ← the `task_list` typed artifact, `checks` ← `validation_results`, `construction` ← dual-sourced from `task_progress` (task loop) plus `wave_*`/`subagent_*` (fan-out) events — including the KAN-99 N-1 checklist cap and the L3 per-task shape.
- **`FIGURE-TO-FIELD-PINNING.md`** — maps every run-screen figure to its exact producing emit field (per-agent duration/tokens/cache split ← `agent_complete`; run totals/cache/`estimated_cost_usd`/`model_id`/deliverable ← `pipeline_complete`; construction checklist ← `task_progress`; waves/workers ← `wave_*`/`subagent_*`; version ← `/family` `revision_index`). Verifies in code that `agent_input.context_sources` carries name via `agent_name` and size via `full_output_length`/`summary_length` (no literal name/size keys, no cache-% sub-field); cache/reduction %, uploaded-file context rows, and live interim token counts are explicitly marked UNVERIFIED rather than fabricated.
- **`LIVE-STATE-CONTRACT.md`** — the full D-12 ten-state table (real run state → chat-lane render → composer mode → Steps behavior) plus all five post-merge deltas (`update_specs` 4th gate action, reject-confirm, terminal fence, spec-revision loop-back + terminology rule, planner window KAN-97, N-1 cap KAN-99, event-driven gates KAN-94).

**e2e driver + decision record (28-03 — 2 new):**

- **`MOCKWS-CHAT-DRIVER-CONTRACT.md`** — pins the e2e chat-driver API as an ADDITIVE extension of the live `frontend/e2e/fixtures/mockWs.ts` `{type, data:{…, event_id, seq}}` envelope (same seq/event_id counters, same connection); catalogues the three chat frame types (`chat_message` user-turn echo, `chat_reply` narrator cards, `stream_attached` reattach handshake replacing `pipeline_reconnected`); records the LOCK-B additive-only transport constraint and the 123-existing-mocked-spec preservation bar (RUNUI-05 consumer).
- **`RESOLVED-DECISIONS.md`** — transcribes the POR §3 AUTONOMOUS-RUN DECISION LOCK: LOCK-A..G verbatim-in-substance, plus a 13-row ND-1..ND-13 disposition table (each RESOLVED / RESOLVED-deferred / DEFERRED with its lock source) — the single citable authority so later phases never re-open a locked decision.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Event vocabulary guard:** `backend/tests/agents/test_phase3_cutover_verify.py` — `_DOCUMENTED_EVENT_TYPES`
- **Volatile-key normalizer:** `backend/tests/agents/characterization/_normalize.py` — `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`
- **Golden-neutrality proof:** `backend/tests/agents/test_chat_event_neutrality.py`
- **UI-SPEC contracts:** `.planning/phases/28-chat-contracts-guards-a0/contracts/{STEPS-ARTIFACT-DERIVATION-CONTRACT,FIGURE-TO-FIELD-PINNING,LIVE-STATE-CONTRACT}.md`
- **e2e driver + decision record:** `.planning/phases/28-chat-contracts-guards-a0/contracts/{MOCKWS-CHAT-DRIVER-CONTRACT,RESOLVED-DECISIONS}.md`
- **Verified-in-code emit sites the docs cite:** `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` — `_build_context_sources` (name/size fields, spot-checked at lines 5115-5140), plus the `agent_complete`/`pipeline_complete`/`task_progress` emit sites (spot-checked at lines 3470-3482, 2316-2345, 2769-2780) and `wave_scheduler.py`/`fanout.py` for the construction dual-source.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Additive-only, zero kernel/runtime edits (INV-3).** All three plans are tests/docs only. New chat event types are pinned to the vocabulary/normalizer *before* any code emits them; the golden-neutrality proof is the standing guard Phase 29's chat wiring must keep passing without a fixture regen.
- **(b) Only two subkeys are volatile-stripped.** `message_id` and `replayed_through_seq` go into `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`; `stream_attached.live` and the `chat_reply` card discriminators stay unstripped (deterministic/stable) — do not add them later without cause.
- **(c) `agent_input.context_sources` describes upstream AGENT outputs, not uploaded files.** Name = `agent_name`, size = `full_output_length`/`summary_length`; there is no cache/reduction-% sub-field on it. Cache/reduction % must come from separate P26 cache telemetry (`cache_read_tokens`/`cache_write_tokens`), never synthesized onto `context_sources`.
- **(d) Terminology rule.** The intra-run spec-revision loop (KAN-101 / Phase 27) is NOT a "revision run" (family child, D-02) — later chat-lane/UI work must not conflate the two.
- **(e) mockWs chat driver is ADDITIVE ONLY (LOCK-B).** Reuses the existing single seq counter, `nextEventId()`, and one connection; `stream_attached` supersedes `pipeline_reconnected` for chat/stream reattach without removing it; the 123 existing mocked e2e specs must stay green; `useWebSocket.ts` untouched.
- **(f) All ND-1..ND-13 are DECIDED, not re-derivable.** `RESOLVED-DECISIONS.md` is the single citable authority — Concierge always-on (ND-2, Haiku); LOCK-E defers team-sharing / resume-from-failed-step / per-agent prompt-override persistence / image-persistence-on-reopen; output name = "Deliverable" (ND-13.6); draft-run persistence (ND-1) explicitly NOT built (client-side chat only).

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-3:** 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical. Independently re-run: neutrality test + cutover-verify + 5 `test_characterization_*.py` → **22 passed, 1 warning in 51.44s**; `git status --porcelain` on `backend/tests/agents/characterization/golden/` empty (zero fixture changes), no `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`.
- **INV-1 / SC-001:** every guard reasons on generic event/key membership only — no workflow-name or pipeline-type literal introduced.
- **Import-linter:** `/opt/homebrew/bin/lint-imports` (from `backend/`) → **4 contracts kept, 0 broken**.
- **CHAT-06:** REQUIREMENTS.md line 452 marks it "Phase 28 [A0] / Complete" — the only REQ-ID mapped to this phase, no orphans.
- **Anti-patterns:** grep `TBD|FIXME|XXX` across all 8 phase-created/modified files → 0 matches; 0 fenced code blocks across the 5 contract docs (honors the "contract, not implementation" constraint).
- **Diff scope:** the full phase window (`826a011d..HEAD`) is 3 additive test files + 5 additive doc files + planning-doc updates only — zero production backend/frontend code touched, no golden fixture regenerated, no decision re-opened.

## Phase 29 — Transport Cutover + Chat Backbone [A1]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/29-transport-cutover-chat-backbone-a1/` — 10 plans (29-01..29-10) across 6 waves. Branch: `feat/ui-2`.  ·  **Status:** `human_needed` (2026-07-07 → 2026-07-08) — `29-VERIFICATION.md`: 26/28 observable truths VERIFIED (0 FAILED), 2 PARTIAL (both explicitly-documented, LOCK-B-sanctioned deferrals to the live pass — not gaps); every offline binding gate (wire-parity, the 5 INV-3 characterization goldens, the attach/replay matrix, banned-patterns, import-linter, all ported unit suites, `tsc`, the SSE e2e specs) was independently re-run and passed by the verifier. A same-day, post-verification deep code review (`29-REVIEW.md`, 8 files) then found 3 Critical bugs — all 3 fixed before Phase 30 began (commits `5dc1ce31`/`55a5cef3`/`89a19d40`, plus migration `0024`); 3 Warnings + 2 Info were left open (see Known follow-ups). `29-UAT.md` records 4 human-verification items, all `[pending]` (0 run).  ·  **Plan-ids → CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md (milestone v2.0 POR) §A1:** 29-01..29-10.

**Requirements delivered:** CHAT-07 (the transport cutover itself: SSE stream + REST commands built ADDITIVELY alongside `/ws/chat` per D-13, wire-parity proven, D-14 resilience, ported inbound-handler suites), CHAT-01 (`POST /api/runs/{id}/messages` persists chat turns as idempotent `run_events` rows, zero new tables), CHAT-02 (the mechanical intent router — zero model calls, routes clarify/gate/steering/revision incl. `update_specs`→KAN-101, the KAN-100 terminal fence, KAN-94 event-driven gate detection), CHAT-03 (the steering seam — consume-once `ectx.steering_notes` → `=== USER GUIDANCE ===`, sticky vs one-shot), CHAT-04 (narrator `chat_reply` milestone cards, offline-proven), CHAT-05 (family-anchored chat transcript, reusing the pre-existing `GET /api/runs/{id}/family`). CHAT-06 (event-vocabulary seeding) shipped in Phase 28, not this phase.

**Driver:** The milestone's chat-and-UI-convergence plan calls for the transport to become SSE-down + REST-up in full and for a user message to reach a run, persist durably, route by run state, and shape the next agent's dispatch, with browser-native reconnect/reopen fidelity — replacing the WS-only `/ws/chat` path. The AUTONOMOUS-RUN DECISION LOCK (POR §3, LOCK-B) overrides the ROADMAP goal's literal "WS run-path deleted at exit" wording for this unattended run: build the new transport and chat backbone strictly additively, prove wire-parity against the existing WS frames, and defer the actual `/ws/chat` deletion to a separate, supervised follow-up after a human validates the live cutover.

**One-line outcome:** Shipped a per-run SSE stream (`GET /api/runs/{id}/events/stream`) plus six REST command endpoints alongside an untouched `/ws/chat`, proven wire-parity-identical to the recorded WS frame sequences for all 5 golden pipelines; layered the chat backbone on top — `POST /api/runs/{id}/messages`, a pure mechanical router, the `ectx.steering_notes` seam, and narrator `chat_reply` card projections — all offline-verified (172 backend tests + 6 e2e specs + `tsc` clean + lint-imports 4/0, independently reproduced by the verifier), then hardened by a same-day deep code review that caught and fixed 3 real concurrency/data-integrity bugs (a double-emitted gate event, a silently degraded REST-launch history, and an unserialized `run_events` seq race) via a new additive migration before Phase 30 began.

### What was built

**Backend (15 files/artifacts):**

- **`backend/tests/agents/characterization/_sse_projection.py`** (29-01, created) — the pure `run_events`→SSE projection contract: `ws_frame_from_engine_event`, `run_events_from_engine_events`, `run_event_to_sse_frame`, `project_events`, `assert_wire_parity`. Dependency-free of `app.*`/the kernel (imports only the characterization normalizer), so production's own SSE endpoint could re-implement its rendering against this contract without a `tests/` import.
- **`backend/tests/agents/test_wire_parity.py`** (29-01, created) — the CHAT-07 binding, non-vacuous wire-parity gate: 5-pipeline parity + 1 injected-drift case (6 tests).
- **`backend/tests/agents/characterization/golden/*.wsframes.json`** (29-01, 5 created) — recorded, volatile-stripped, order-canonical WS frame sequences for `prototype`/`od_prototype`/`od_ppt`/`prototype_revision`/`app_builder`, chat-dormant by construction.
- **`backend/app/api/run_stream.py`** (29-02, created) — `GET /api/runs/{id}/events/stream`: two-layer owner check (→404, never 403), durable replay from `Last-Event-ID`, the `stream_attached{live,replayed_through_seq}` handshake, and the D-14g `review_gate_ready` re-arm on attach; attaches to the existing `_PIPELINE_QUEUES` via a read-only import of `websocket.py`'s `_get_or_create_queue` (no symbol added to `websocket.py`).
- **`backend/app/core/config.py`** (29-02, modified) — `SSE_TRANSPORT_ENABLED` flag + the D-14h `SSE_KEEPALIVE_PING_SECONDS`/`SSE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` knobs.
- **`backend/app/main.py`** (29-02/29-03, modified) — two additive `include_router` lines (`run_stream_router`, `run_commands_router`) beside the untouched `websocket_router` include.
- **`backend/app/api/run_commands.py`** (29-03/29-04/29-09, created then extended) — the REST up-channel: `POST /{id}/gate` (4 actions incl. `update_specs`→KAN-101, the KAN-100 terminal fence, the KAN-94 pending-gate check), `POST /{id}/answers`, `POST /{id}/cancel`, `POST /api/runs` (launch + image caps), `POST /{id}/revisions` (family-child dispatch), `POST /api/runs/user_message` (a POST+SSE shim driving the untouched `ChatRunner`), and `POST /{id}/messages` (wired to `chat_router.route_chat_turn`). Reuses `websocket.py`'s owner/terminal predicates and cancel registry by read-only import; `_drive_launch_to_queue`/`_drive_revision_to_queue` are a sanctioned LOCK-B *duplication* (not an import) of the WS closures, which are `nonlocal`/socket-coupled and not extractable.
- **`backend/tests/agents/test_attach_replay_matrix.py`** (29-05, created) — the 7-scenario, 12-test SSE durability gate (fresh / mid-stream / live / terminal / cross-owner / restart / **SC-2** gate-answer-while-stream-down), bound to the 29-01 projection for contiguity/dedup/ordering/projection-exact assertions.
- **`backend/agents/execution_engine/context.py`** (29-08, modified) — `ectx.steering_notes`, a consume-once `list[{text, sticky}]` queue, added beside `redo_directive`.
- **`backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py`** (29-08, modified) — a new `=== USER GUIDANCE ===` block in `_compose_context_message`, read+cleared during composition (one-shot dropped, sticky retained).
- **`backend/app/api/chat_router.py`** (29-09, created) — `route_chat_turn(run_state, turn) -> Dispatch`, the pure, zero-model mechanical router: `clarify_waiting→answers`, `gate_paused→gate`, `running→steering`, `terminal→revision`; `derive_open_gate` re-derives gate state from the durable `run_events` vocabulary, confirmed armed via the store (KAN-94).
- **`backend/app/agents/chat_narrator.py`** (29-10, created) — `project_milestone_card`/`persist_milestone_card`/`consume_deep_link`: pure projections of clarify/gate/pipeline/deliverable/**spec_revision** (KAN-101, D-02-terminology-distinct) milestones into `chat_reply` cards, persisted via `ScopedStore.append_event`, with a single-use deep-link nonce.
- **`backend/agents/authz.py`** (post-review fix CR-03, modified) — new `ScopedStore.append_event_next_seq()`: optimistic serialized `seq`/idempotency allocation with `IntegrityError` retry, replacing the racy read-`max(seq)+1`-then-write pattern in the two new chat writers.
- **`backend/alembic/versions/0024_run_events_uniqueness.py`** (post-review fix CR-03, created) — additive migration (`down_revision="0023"`): two owner+workspace-scoped UNIQUE constraints on `run_events` — `uq_run_events_scope_event` (`run_id, owner_id, workspace_id, event_id`) and `uq_run_events_scope_seq` (`run_id, owner_id, workspace_id, seq`); no new table, no column altered; reversible via `batch_alter_table`.
- **`backend/app/models/run_event.py`** (post-review fix CR-03, modified) — ORM-side mapping for the two new unique constraints.

**Frontend (8 files):**

- **`frontend/e2e/fixtures/mockSse.ts`** (29-06, created) — mock-SSE transport driver: intercepts `GET /api/runs/{id}/events/stream` via `page.route`, serves `id:{seq}`/`data:{json}` frames, honors `Last-Event-ID` for replay-from-cursor, supports `drop()`/reattach; shares `MockWs`'s `SeqSource` (one monotonic seq/event_id space).
- **`frontend/e2e/fixtures/mockWs.ts`** (29-06, modified, purely additive) — exported `nextEventId()` + `SeqSource` interface; added `chatMessage`/`chatReply`/`streamAttached`/`waitForChatCommand` helpers beside the existing, byte-identical ones; `pipeline_reconnected` kept alongside the new `stream_attached`.
- **`frontend/e2e/tests/ts-sse.spec.ts`** (29-06, created) — 2-test smoke: attach → replay-from-cursor → drop → reattach, dedup-by-`event_id` + resume-by-`seq`.
- **`frontend/src/hooks/useRunStream.ts`** (29-07, created) — the SSE transport twin of `useWebSocket`: `fetch`+`ReadableStream` (not `EventSource`, so `Authorization`/`Last-Event-ID` headers can be sent), a max-seen-seq dedup cursor, exponential-backoff reconnect capped at 30s, a `connecting→replaying→live→reconnecting` state machine, and silent pre-expiry JWT refresh (D-14a/c/d/f). Dispatches the same `{type,data}` envelope as the WS `onmessage` handler, so the existing dashboard reducer binds unchanged.
- **`frontend/src/providers/RunConnectionProvider.tsx`** (29-07, created) — app-level connection ownership meant to survive route changes, server-derived reattach (queries non-terminal `GET /api/runs`, D-14b), a persisted per-run replay cursor (D-14c), reconnect on `visibilitychange`/`online` (D-14e), and a REST up-channel `sendCommand` + fan-out `subscribe`. `useRunConnection()` has an inert default so `useWorkflow` compiles/runs byte-identically when the provider is absent.
- **`frontend/src/lib/env.ts`** (29-07, modified) — `NEXT_PUBLIC_SSE_TRANSPORT` flag, OFF by default (WS path intact).
- **`frontend/src/hooks/useWorkflow.ts`** (29-07, modified) — an additive, flag-selected transport branch: SSE-on routes `run_pipeline`/`submit_questionnaire` over REST via the provider; SSE-off is the existing `websocketSend`, byte-for-byte unchanged.
- **`frontend/e2e/tests/ts-sse-resilience.spec.ts`** (29-07, created) — 4 offline driver-level specs: reload-resume, route-change survival, auto-reconnect (past-cursor-only replay), multi-tab shared sequence.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) LOCK-B additive-only override is binding for this phase.** The ROADMAP goal's "WS run-path deleted at exit" wording is explicitly overridden: `/ws/chat`, `backend/app/api/websocket.py`, `backend/app/api/websocket_handoff.py`, and `frontend/src/hooks/useWebSocket.ts` stayed byte-identical across the ENTIRE phase diff (`git diff --stat` empty for all four, confirmed by the verifier over the full commit range) — no deletion, no ratchet, no migration-ledger deletion row. The WS deletion + INV-12 exit gate are a separate, supervised follow-up after a human validates the live cutover.
- **(b) ND-11 resolved COEXIST, not unify (written BEFORE any 29-08 engine/context edit).** `steering_notes` is a generic THIRD consume-once injection seam beside the two shipped seams (`redo_directive` [P23 Redo] and `spec_revision_context` [KAN-101]); both shipped seams stay byte-stable. Steering thread-id policy: **no fork** — inject into the next dispatch on the agent's normal base thread. This sidesteps both the Redo `:redo{N}` fork rationale and the KAN-101 base-thread replay hazard (the latter is explicitly OUT of scope here — see Known follow-ups).
- **(c) The mechanical router is pure and name-free (SC-001/INV-1).** `route_chat_turn` keys only on generic run-state signals (`LIVE-STATE-CONTRACT.md` §1) — never `pipeline_type`/`spec.id`/an agent-id literal — lives in the app layer (not the kernel), and makes zero model calls on any branch.
- **(d) Production re-implements the SSE frame rendering in-line rather than importing the test-support module.** `run_stream.py` does not import `_sse_projection.py` (that would make `app/` boot depend on `tests/` being deployed); parity is instead proven IN THE TEST, which imports the real `assert_wire_parity` against the live endpoint's output.
- **(e) The launch/revision REST drivers are a SANCTIONED LOCK-B duplication, not an import — and the two copies must stay behaviorally identical.** `_drive_launch_to_queue`/`_drive_revision_to_queue` duplicate the WS closures because those closures are `nonlocal`/socket-coupled and not extractable. This is exactly the invariant CR-02 found broken (4 event-handler branches silently missing) and fixed — a future change to the WS original must be mirrored here by hand until the two are de-duplicated.
- **(f) Zero new tables held (Q3).** Chat turns and narrator cards ride the existing `run_events` table through the single `ScopedStore.append_event` stamping boundary. Migration `0024` (added post-review, CR-03) is additive-only — two UNIQUE constraints on an existing table — never a new table, never a destructive alter; it chains cleanly after `0023`.
- **(g) Two-layer owner check on every new endpoint.** `user_id` filter → `ScopedStore` default-deny; cross-owner and unknown-run both resolve 404, never 403 (IDOR-safe by construction, T-29-02-1/T-29-09-1).
- **(h) Terminology: the KAN-101 intra-run spec-revision loop is NOT a D-02 family "revision run."** The narrator's `spec_revision` card kind is checked FIRST in classification (keyed on the generic `spec_revision_attempt`/`revision_index` discriminator) specifically so the two are never visually conflated.

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-1 / SC-001:** `test_banned_patterns.py` green throughout (11/11 at every checkpoint); zero `pipeline_type==`/`spec.id==`/workflow-name literals introduced in the router, steering composition, or narrator.
- **INV-3:** all 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical for the entire phase (chat/steering/narrator dormant on scripted golden runs; no `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`, no golden fixture edited). CHAT-07's wire-parity golden is the NEW binding, non-vacuous gate proving the SSE projection ≡ the recorded WS frames.
- **INV-12:** no duplicate agent runner, no duplicate merge/artifact writer, no duplicate `ChatRunner`. The one sanctioned LOCK-B duplication (launch/revision drivers, decision (e) above) was audited by code review and repaired to stay behaviorally identical to its WS original.
- **INV-13:** `deepagents`/`DeepAgentRunner` untouched; the chat backbone and narrator are app-layer and model-call-free (the router and the narrator both make zero model calls).
- **Verifier's own offline evidence (independently reproduced, not taken from SUMMARY claims):** 172 backend tests + 6 e2e specs + lint-imports 4 kept/0 broken + `tsc --noEmit` clean (the verifier notes this differs in suite-selection scope from, but is consistent with, the executors' own cumulative claim of 187 passes across all 10 plans).
- **Post-verification code review (`29-REVIEW.md`, deep, 8 files):** 3 Critical + 3 Warning + 2 Info found. All 3 Criticals were fixed the same day, each with a new regression test landing in the same commit (confirmed via `git diff --stat 5dc1ce31~1..89a19d40`: `test_sse_stream.py` +37 lines, `test_rest_run_launch.py` +89 lines, new file `test_run_events.py` +100 lines) — CR-01 (`run_stream.py`'s gate re-arm no longer double-emits `review_gate_ready` when the durable replay already covered it), CR-02 (`run_commands.py`'s REST launch driver regained the 4 dropped WS event-handler branches so persisted `agent_outputs` history matches the WS path), CR-03 (migration `0024` + `append_event_next_seq` close the `run_events` seq/idempotency race). Pass/fail of these specific regression tests was not independently re-run for this register entry — the fix commits' own messages report wire-parity, the 5 goldens, and the new tests green, and the migration applying/reversing cleanly on SQLite.
- **Threats mitigated (register):** T-29-02-1 cross-owner SSE replay→404; T-29-07-1 spoofed stale-sessionStorage run ids (mitigated by server-derived reattach); T-29-07-2 mid-run forced-logout DoS (mitigated by silent JWT refresh); T-29-09-1/T-29-09-2/T-29-09-3/T-29-09-4 chat-endpoint info-disclosure / replay / terminal-fence / kernel-name-branch-creep; T-29-10-1/T-29-10-2 narrator scoped-read / replayed-nonce defense.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **`RunConnectionProvider` built + correct but not mounted.** `frontend/src/app/layout.tsx` is outside the 5-file LOCK-B allow-list, so the live app does not yet consume the SSE transport even with the flag on; mounting it is the deferred integration step (`29-UAT.md` test 1, `[pending]`).
- **Steering live in-process delivery deferred (DEF-29-09-1).** The router computes the `CHANNEL_STEERING` dispatch and `apply_steering()` appends to `ectx.steering_notes` — both proven offline — but the live endpoint→running-`ectx` handle lookup (an engine-side drain at the next dispatch) needs an `engine.py` edit outside 29-09's allow-list (`29-UAT.md` test 2, `[pending]`). Review finding **WR-01** additionally flags that today's API response (`{"ok":true,...,"channel":"steering"}`) can't tell the caller "durably recorded" apart from "delivered live" — left open.
- **Narrator not yet invoked from any production code path.** `project_milestone_card`/`persist_milestone_card` are proven correct by 23 offline unit tests but have zero production call sites; live wiring lands with Phase 31/32's chat-lane rendering (`29-UAT.md` test 3, `[pending]`).
- **WR-02 (open):** `chat_narrator`'s deep-link nonce registry (`_ISSUED_NONCES`) is an in-memory, unbounded, unpersisted, unscoped global `set()` — it will invalidate every unconsumed nonce on a routine restart or in any multi-worker deployment, and does not itself verify `run_id` ownership at consumption. Needs a DB-backed, ownership-checked nonce before Phase 31/32 exposes it live.
- **WR-03 (open):** gate actions routed through `/messages` (the chat-turn path) can never carry `edited_content` — only the dedicated `POST /{id}/gate` endpoint forwards it. Documented feature-parity gap, not a bug; either thread the field through `ChatTurn`/`Dispatch` or document gate-edit as `/gate`-only.
- **IN-01/IN-02 (open, info-level):** `SSE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` is declared in `config.py` but never read/enforced anywhere; `_gate_is_pending` reaches into `ArtifactStore`'s private `_resume_events` dict from two call sites in `run_commands.py` instead of through a public accessor.
- **KAN-101 base-thread unbounded-replay risk over many `update_specs` cycles** — acknowledged in `ND-11-SEAM-DECISION.md` as a standing audit item for the mechanical-router work or the Phase 34 live pass; this phase neither widens nor fixes it.
- **DEF-29-06-1 / DEF-29-04-1 — pre-existing `feat/ui-2` baseline breakage, confirmed unrelated.** ~120/123 mocked Playwright specs fail on pure home-render assertions (the branch's redesigned dashboard home removed the legacy CreationHub button labels the specs target, commits `9c7b78bb`/`fe7a351b`) and 12/50 `test_run_pipeline_validation.py` cases fail (a widened custom-agent-pool). Both confirmed pre-existing via `git diff --name-only` showing zero Phase-29 edits to the affected files; owned by separate workstreams, not this phase (`29-UAT.md` test 4, `[pending]` sanity re-check).
- **Live/Bedrock confirmation deferred to the milestone-end pass (D-24), per `29-UAT.md` (4 items, all `[pending]`, 0 run):** live SSE attach + `RunConnectionProvider` mount surviving reload/route-change; a steering note actually landing in the next agent's live composed prompt; a `chat_reply` narrator card actually appearing on the live down-channel as a milestone fires; and the DEF-29-06-1/DEF-29-04-1 baseline-failure-count sanity re-check.
