## Phase 30 — Uploads & Multimodal [A2]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/30-uploads-multimodal-a2/` — 5 plans (30-01..30-05, planned + executed 2026-07-08). Branch `feat/ui-2`. · **Status:** Complete — VERIFIED 2026-07-08T02:34:02Z (`30-VERIFICATION.md`, 9/9 must-haves, 0 overrides). `30-REVIEW.md` (deep, 22 files) found 7 issues (1 HIGH/HI-01, 4 MEDIUM/WR-01..04, 2 LOW-INFO/IN-01..02); a same-day fix pass closed 4 of 7 (HI-01, WR-01, WR-02, WR-03 — commits `f98c8763`/`07c1b3b6`/`9d860970`/`4037775c`, all 2026-07-08); the remaining WR-04 and IN-01 are the SAME two items already tracked as this phase's own pre-declared deferrals (see Known follow-ups). · **Supersedes:** the standalone "Phase 30 (cluster) — Image-Input" entry that previously stood at the end of this register (written by 30-05) — folded in below as section **A**; nothing is lost.

**Requirements delivered:** UPLD-01 (owner-scoped, capped document upload → `RunSandbox`); UPLD-02 (run-entry vision images — pre-landed pre-milestone, waves `edw`/`frv`/`gvq` — plus the per-turn/mid-chat image carrier, 30-03); UPLD-03 (uploaded-document text sticky in every subsequent `agent_input`, 30-01+30-02); UPLD-04 (launch-time image attach — pre-landed pre-milestone — plus client-side resize + the ND-10 reopen placeholder, 30-04); and the ND-10/LOCK-E disposition itself (image persistence for replay/reopen is an explicit non-goal, not a gap).

**Driver:** Runs could only ever be launched from a text brief. Phase 29 built the chat message path but gave agents no way to receive a reference document or an image; a vision-capable agent had nothing to look at, and a user with a spec PDF had to paste its content into the brief by hand. The run-entry image half (vision at launch) had already landed pre-milestone via a separate IMAGE-INPUT-PLAN; Phase 30 closes the remaining gaps — a real document-upload endpoint, sticky extracted-text context for uploaded docs, images attached mid-conversation riding the Phase-29 message path, and client-side/reopen polish — while explicitly refusing to build durable image storage (ND-10/LOCK-E).

**One-line outcome:** Two payload shapes now enter a run: DOCUMENTS (`POST /api/runs/{id}/files`) land as real bytes under the run's `RunSandbox` and their extracted text is sticky context via the new `context_provider:uploaded_files` capability (zero engine edits, SC-001); IMAGES stay payload-transient end-to-end — at launch via the pre-landed `run_images` input_provider, and now also mid-conversation via a per-turn carrier riding the Phase-29 `/messages` path — never touching sandbox/DB/`run_events`, so the honest "image not retained" placeholder (ND-10/LOCK-E) is the reopen disposition, not a gap. Goldens stay byte-identical (INV-3).

### What was built

**A — Image-input cluster (run-entry images; pre-landed pre-Phase-30, waves `260707-edw`/`frv`/`gvq`; folded in from the superseded standalone entry):**
- Capability `input_provider:run_images` — `backend/agents/capabilities/input_providers/run_images.py` (`RunImagesProvider`, `name="run_images"`, `async def load(ctx)`): reads the transient `ctx.run_images` base64 carrier, composes multimodal content-blocks, returns `[]` when empty (dormant). Registry lockstep in `backend/agents/capabilities/registry.py` (`@register` decorator, `_KNOWN` membership tuple, `discover()` import tuple).
- Ingress caps + vision guard — `_validate_images(...)` in `backend/app/api/websocket.py`: mime allow-list `{png,jpeg,webp,gif}`, ~3.75 MB/image, ≤20 images, ~8 MB aggregate, a vision-capable-model guard; gated by `settings.IMAGE_INPUT_ENABLED`; validated PRE-MINT on both the WS `run_pipeline` path and `POST /api/runs` (`backend/app/api/run_commands.py`) — the SAME validator the per-turn carrier (C, below) reuses.
- Engine seams (`backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py`): `_normalize_run_images(images)` seeds `ectx.run_images` at run entry; `_dispatch_payload(context_message, input_blocks)` (split-transport — bare string when no blocks, INV-3 byte-parity); `_compose_input_blocks(spec, ectx)` (the per-agent `injects:[images]` gate, locally derived from `spec.injects ∪ step.injects`, never the stale `current_spec_injects`, to avoid leaking images to a later non-opted agent).
- Workflow opt-in (declared, name-free — SC-001): `backend/agents/workflows/prototype/workflow.yaml` (`input_providers:[run_images]`); `backend/agents/prompts/prototype-specify/AGENT.md` (`injects:[template, design_system, images]`). No other workflow opts in.
- Frontend: `frontend/src/components/workflow/IdeaInputPage.tsx` (picker/preview; `attachedImages` sent as the out-of-band `images` field, never concatenated into the brief — D3); `dashboard/page.tsx` plus the two template launch surfaces (`workflow/prototype/templates/page.tsx`, `workflow/ppt/templates/page.tsx`) thread a length-guarded `images` spread; `useWorkflow.ts` carries the payload through launch.
- Tests (7, offline): backend `test_input_providers_run_images.py`, `test_image_input_wiring.py`, `test_prototype_image_optin.py`, `test_image_ingress_validation.py`, `test_image_ws_ingress.py`; frontend `IdeaInputPage.imageInput.test.tsx`, `useWorkflow.imagePayload.test.ts`.
- Locked disposition: D-07 as-built — images are PAYLOAD-TRANSIENT (message → `ectx.run_images` → base64 blocks; never sandbox/DB/`run_events`; only DOCUMENTS go to the `RunSandbox`, correcting the POR's earlier "images → RunSandbox" wording).

**B — Document upload endpoint + sticky context (30-01 UPLD-01, 30-02 UPLD-03):**
- `POST /api/runs/{id}/files` — `backend/app/api/run_files.py` (~240 lines): the same two-layer owner check as the Phase-29 message path (`WorkflowRun.user_id` ORM filter → `ScopedStore.get_run` default-deny, both → 404, IDOR never 403); ext allow-list `{pdf,docx,pptx,txt,md,csv,json}` (image mimes/exts → 415 — images stay on the payload-transient path); caps — per-file 10 MB (single-sourced from `file_extract._MAX_FILE_BYTES`), count ≤20, aggregate 40 MB, all validated up front so a rejection writes nothing; registered in `backend/app/main.py`.
- Single extraction impl (INV-12) — `extract_upload_text()` factored into `backend/app/api/file_extract.py`; the pre-existing `/api/files/extract-text` endpoint now delegates to it instead of duplicating.
- The `.uploads/` contract — a reserved sandbox prefix (`sandbox._UPLOADS_PREFIX`) holds raw bytes + a `.txt` extraction sidecar per document + a `manifest.json` (`{name, mime, has_text}`); `backend/app/agents/sandbox.py`'s `_collect_deliverable_relpaths` excludes the prefix so uploads never pollute the deliverable (dormant on goldens, INV-3).
- `context_provider:uploaded_files` — `backend/agents/capabilities/context_providers/uploaded_files.py` (`UploadedFilesProvider`): kernel-pure (registry decorator + stdlib only, import-linter enforced), reaches the run's OWN sandbox via the `ctx.runner.sandbox` handle (no cross-owner accessor exists by construction), reads the manifest + sidecars, composes an `uploaded_files_context` block (`## Uploaded Files` header), self-gates on the declared `uploaded_files` inject token, degrades to `{}` on any miss. Registry lockstep moved 65→66 (`_KNOWN`, `discover()`, both drift-guard count asserts).
- SC-001 proof: a dedicated test asserts `engine.py`'s source carries zero textual reference to `"uploaded_files"` — the capability rides the pre-existing, unmodified `_compose_context_message` provider loop purely by registration.
- Sticky-context proof: uploaded document text is present in EVERY subsequent `agent_input` of an opted-in workflow (re-read on every dispatch, not consume-once) — proven end-to-end against a 3-agent fixture workflow. Not yet opted into any shipping workflow (`prototype` or otherwise) — a future workflow opts in via `context_providers:[uploaded_files]` on its manifest + `injects:[uploaded_files]` on the consuming agent, no kernel change required.

**C — Per-turn image carrier (30-03, UPLD-02 residue):**
- `MessageCommand.images` on the Phase-29 `POST /api/runs/{id}/messages` endpoint (`backend/app/api/run_commands.py`) — validated by the SAME `_validate_images` caps as the launch path, pre-persist (400 `invalid_image_input` on violation, nothing queued or written).
- `chat_router.apply_turn_images()` (`backend/app/api/chat_router.py`) — the image analogue of `apply_steering`; normalizes to `{mime_type,data}` and appends to `ExecutionContext.pending_turn_images` (new consume-once scratch field, `backend/agents/execution_engine/context.py`, the same D-03 idiom as `steering_notes`).
- Engine drain — `_drain_turn_images(ectx)` (module-level helper, `engine.py`), called before `_compose_input_blocks` at each dispatch.
- **Post-review fix (HI-01, commit `f98c8763`, same day):** the original 30-03 landing drained `pending_turn_images` by appending onto the sticky, never-cleared `run_images` list — reachable, not dormant, because `prototype-specify` already declares `injects:[...,images]` and `prototype/workflow.yaml` already declares `input_providers:[run_images]` (both pre-date this phase and are live), and a Human-Gate redo re-dispatches `prototype-specify` in a loop, so a stale image would silently re-attach to every later dispatch forever. The fix introduced a DISTINCT one-shot `ExecutionContext.turn_images_once` field: `_drain_turn_images` moves pending images onto it, and `_compose_input_blocks` renders it into exactly that one dispatch's blocks and clears it immediately after (read-then-clear co-located at the render site, mirroring the `steering_notes` idiom) — verified directly against the current `engine.py`/`context.py` source. Proven fail-before/pass-after with a two-sequential-dispatch regression test; the 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical.
- ND-10/LOCK-E lock: per-turn images are stamped `{"kind":"image","retained":false}` with no `data` field in the persisted `chat_message` row; no base64 anywhere in DB/`run_events`; gone on replay/reopen (test-locked).
- Live in-process delivery to an already-running run is deferred — see Known follow-ups (DEF-30-03-1 / review WR-04).

**D — Client-side resize + ND-10 reopen placeholder (30-04, UPLD-04 residue):**
- `resizeImage()` — `frontend/src/lib/resizeImage.ts`: `createImageBitmap` decode with an `<img>` fallback, aspect-preserving downscale to `maxEdge` 1568 (default; vision-optimal; comfortably under the server's ~3.75 MB/image cap), png stays lossless, jpeg/webp re-encode at quality 0.85, no-upscale passthrough, degrade-not-block (any decode/canvas/encode failure falls through to the original file's base64, never throws). Wired into `IdeaInputPage.tsx`'s image attach branch before the out-of-band `images` payload is built; server caps stay authoritative — the resize is a client optimization only.
- ND-10 "image not retained" placeholder — `frontend/src/components/results/StartingPointCard.tsx`: a new `AttachmentRef` type + optional `attachmentRefs` prop; a `{kind:"image", retained:false}` ref renders an `ImageOff` icon + muted text, no `<img>`, no byte fetch, no durable storage; no-ref/retained/non-image paths are visually unchanged.
- The component-level contract is proven by tests; wiring a REAL reopened run's persisted refs into `attachmentRefs` is an accepted, pre-declared out-of-file-scope deferral — see Known follow-ups (review finding IN-01).

**E — Register-first back-fill (30-05):** the plan that originally wrote the standalone "Phase 30 (cluster) — Image-Input" register entry this consolidated entry now supersedes; docs-only, no code.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Document upload:** `backend/app/api/run_files.py`, `backend/app/api/file_extract.py` (`extract_upload_text`), `backend/app/agents/sandbox.py` (`_UPLOADS_PREFIX`), `backend/app/main.py`.
- **Sticky context provider:** `backend/agents/capabilities/context_providers/uploaded_files.py`, `backend/agents/capabilities/registry.py`.
- **Per-turn images:** `backend/app/api/run_commands.py` (`MessageCommand.images`, `_live_ectx_for_run`), `backend/app/api/chat_router.py` (`apply_turn_images`), `backend/agents/execution_engine/engine.py` (`_drain_turn_images`, `_compose_input_blocks`), `backend/agents/execution_engine/context.py` (`pending_turn_images`, `turn_images_once`).
- **Run-entry images (folded-in cluster):** `backend/agents/capabilities/input_providers/run_images.py`, `backend/app/api/websocket.py` (`_validate_images`), engine seams `_normalize_run_images`/`_dispatch_payload`/`_compose_input_blocks`, `backend/agents/workflows/prototype/workflow.yaml`, `backend/agents/prompts/prototype-specify/AGENT.md`.
- **Frontend:** `frontend/src/lib/resizeImage.ts`, `frontend/src/components/results/StartingPointCard.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/workflow/IdeaInputPage.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/workflow/prototype/templates/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/workflow/ppt/templates/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/hooks/useWorkflow.ts`.
- **Tests:** `backend/tests/unit/test_run_files_upload.py`, `backend/tests/agents/test_uploaded_files_provider.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_run_message_images.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_mechanical_router.py`, `backend/tests/agents/test_sandbox_deliverable.py`, plus the 7 image-input tests listed in section A.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Documents → sandbox, images → payload-transient — never the reverse (D-07 as amended).** `POST /api/runs/{id}/files` rejects image mimes/exts with 415; images never land on disk/DB, only in `ectx.run_images`/`turn_images_once` → base64 content-blocks.
- **(b) `context_provider:uploaded_files` is kernel-pure and self-gating (SC-001/INV-1).** Registry decorator + stdlib only; reaches disk via `ctx.runner.sandbox`, never by importing `app.agents.sandbox.RunSandbox` directly (would cross the import-linter's capability→app boundary); gates on the declared inject token, never a workflow name/id.
- **(c) ND-10/LOCK-E: image persistence for replay/reopen is a permanent non-goal, not a gap.** The "image not retained" placeholder IS the disposition. Do not re-plan durable image storage.
- **(d) Per-turn images render exactly once (HI-01 fix — do not regress).** `turn_images_once` is distinct from the sticky `run_images`; any future change to `_drain_turn_images`/`_compose_input_blocks` must keep the read-then-clear co-located at the render site, or the stale-image-reattachment bug returns.
- **(e) The two-layer owner check is reused verbatim, never re-derived.** `run_files.py`'s owner check is the same pattern as `run_commands.post_message` (ORM `user_id` filter → `ScopedStore` default-deny, both → 404, never 403).
- **(f) Caps are validated fully before any write/persist.** Both the upload endpoint (ext/size/count/aggregate) and the per-turn image path (`_validate_images`) reject before touching disk or queuing anything.

### Invariants & verification

- **INV-1:** All new branching keys on declared capability names / generic scratch fields (`pending_turn_images`, `turn_images_once`, the `uploaded_files` inject token) — zero `pipeline_type`/agent-id literals.
- **INV-3:** 5 characterization goldens (`prototype`, `prototype_revision`, `od_prototype`, `od_ppt`, `app_builder`) — byte/event-identical, no `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE`; `test_sandbox_deliverable.py` green (uploads-exclusion dormant with no `.uploads/` tree); empty `pending_turn_images`/`turn_images_once` ⇒ `_dispatch_payload` returns the bare `context_message` (same object).
- **INV-12:** `extract_upload_text()` is the single PDF/DOCX/PPTX extraction impl; `/api/files/extract-text` delegates rather than duplicating.
- **INV-13 / import-linter:** `uploaded_files.py` imports only the registry decorator + stdlib; `lint-imports` held 4 kept / 0 broken throughout the phase.
- **Q3:** additive only — zero new migrations/tables this phase (uploads live on the sandbox filesystem + existing `run_events`/`chat_message` rows).
- **SC-001:** the phase's `engine.py` diffs — 30-03's image drain (`57f7a41d`) plus the same-day HI-01 fix (`f98c8763`) — are both image-carrier-only; zero lines attributable to the `uploaded_files` capability, which is picked up purely via registration into the pre-existing `_compose_context_message` provider loop.
- **Evidence (re-run at verification time, not trusted from summaries):** 13 (upload endpoint) + 113 (provider + registry + run_images drift-guard) + 39 (per-turn carrier + router) + 16 (FE resize/placeholder vitest) + 13 (sandbox deliverable) + 10 (5 goldens) = 204 offline tests green, plus the 7 pre-existing image-input tests (section A) unaffected; registry `_KNOWN` live count = 66.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **DEF-30-03-1 (== DEF-29-09-1; matches review finding WR-04) — live in-process delivery of a per-turn image to an ALREADY-RUNNING run.** `run_commands.py`'s `_live_ectx_for_run(run_id)` unconditionally returns `None` today (no live-ectx registry exists yet), so `apply_turn_images` on the mid-run steering channel is a no-op in production even though the image was cap-validated and durably logged — the endpoint still returns 200 with no signal that the image was dropped. The seam is fully proven offline; live wiring is deferred to Phase 34 (LIVE-02).
- **`StartingPointCard.attachmentRefs` has no production caller yet (matches review finding IN-01).** The placeholder contract is proven by tests, but the only production call site (`AgentThinkingTab.tsx`) never passes `attachmentRefs` — dead code in production until a reopen surface threads a run's real `retained:false` refs through. Phase 31 "Chat Lane MVP" (CHATUI-03, attachment UI) was the named candidate to close this at verification time.
- **IN-02 (accepted, no action planned).** `run_files.py`'s `_read_manifest` and `uploaded_files.py`'s manifest reader are two independent implementations of "read `.uploads/manifest.json`, degrade to `[]`/`{}` on any parse error" — a deliberate consequence of the app-layer/kernel-pure import boundary, flagged only as a maintainability note.
- **Live-Bedrock multimodal confirmation** for the document and per-turn image paths was deferred to the milestone-end live pass (Phase 34) per this phase's own execution-viability note; all 9 `30-VERIFICATION.md` truths are proven offline.
- **Live regression found and fixed post-phase (quick-260710-ftq, 2026-07-10, commits `a4a00682`/`ba159678`, `feat/ui-2`).** Live Bedrock UI testing found the pre-landed run-entry image spine (section A) was actually dead on the prototype/ppt launch surfaces: `dashboard/page.tsx`'s `setPendingOdProtoParams`/`setPendingOdPptParams` rebuilt their state objects with an explicit field list that omitted `images` (the backend carrier and the `prototype` opt-in were correct — a pure FE state-setter omission, not a Phase 30 defect). Fixed with a length-guarded `images` spread mirroring the existing `selections`/`agentIds` spreads (INV-3-safe: absent when image-less). Live-proven on Bedrock: the outbound frame went from 300 B/no-images to 47797 B with `images:[1]`, and `prototype-specify` recorded `agent_input.image_count=1` — the spec-writer now actually receives the attached image end-to-end. A second, unrelated defect (the Home "Recent runs" chip not opening the run) was fixed in the same quick task.
- **Documentation lag (informational, non-blocking).** `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md`'s traceability table still shows `UPLD-04 | Pending` and `ROADMAP.md`'s Phase 30 checkbox is unchecked even though all 5 plans are individually marked complete — a docs-lag artifact `30-VERIFICATION.md` itself calls out, not a code gap.

## Phase 31 — Chat Lane MVP [A3]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/31-chat-lane-mvp-a3/` — 7 plans (31-01..31-07) across 4 waves. Branch: `feat/ui-2`.  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-08) — `31-VERIFICATION.md` verdict `passed`, 15/15 must-haves verified, 0 overrides; code review (`31-REVIEW.md`) found 0 Critical / 2 High / 3 Medium / 3 Low, landed as advisory, not fixed in-phase (commit `54df085d` "verifier PASSED 15/15; review advisory").  ·  **Plan-id → CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md (milestone v2.0 POR):** A3.

**Requirements delivered:** CHATUI-01 (revived chat kit: family-anchored transcript, streaming markdown, `aria-live`/`role="log"`, open-design borrow-list 1–7 w/ Apache-2.0 attribution), CHATUI-02 (in-lane gate/clarify quick-actions mirroring Steps), CHATUI-03 (attachment UI — paste/drag-drop/resize — + token-usage widget) — all 3 marked Complete in `REQUIREMENTS.md` (lines 222-226 + traceability table lines 458-460); also closes the UPLD-04 "remaining: client-side resize, paste/drag-drop (Phase 31 UI)" residue.

**Driver:** Phase 29 had already wired the WS/SSE chat-frame transport (`chat_message`/`chat_reply`/`stream_attached`) and `DashboardLayout`/`page.tsx` already threaded `messages`/`streamingContent`/`onSendMessage` props — but nothing rendered them, and the dead in-repo chat kit (`ChatPanel`/`MessageBubble`/`ChatInput`: streaming bubbles, markdown, cursor, auto-scroll already built) sat unmounted while the run screen offered only the Steps UI. Users needed a working conversational lane — transcript, gate/clarify actions, attachments, deliverable deep-links — reachable in the CURRENT skin before the Phase-32 reskin, firing the SAME backend channels the Steps panels already use.

**One-line outcome:** `RunChatLane` mounts LIVE in the run screen's execution left column (`DashboardLayout.tsx`), fed a family-anchored (D-02) transcript by the new transport-agnostic `useRunChat` hook (SSE `RunConnectionProvider` when mounted+enabled, else the legacy WS chat-frame fan-out — byte-identical flag-OFF, LOCK-B) with streaming markdown + `aria-live`/`role="log"`; five-kind `ResultCard`s deep-link into `PreviewPanel` tabs via a nonce'd seam (borrow #6); `InlineGateActions`/`InlineClarifyActions` fire the SAME `approve_review`/`submit_questionnaire` channels the Steps panels use; `ChatAttachments`/`useChatAttachments` add paste+drag-drop on top of the existing picker, images routed through Phase-30's `resizeImage`; `ChatTokenWidget` surfaces P26 telemetry; and all 7 open-design borrow-list mechanisms (partial-json repair, streaming-json extractor, tool-renderer registry, `buildBlocks` reducer, measured virtualizer, nonce'd deep-link, Thinking/Todo/FileOps blocks) land as clean-room Apache-2.0-attributed reimplementations under `frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/`. SC-001/INV-3/FIX-039/LOCK-B all held; verified BY DELTA (new specs pass, zero green→red regression against the pre-existing `feat/ui-2` baseline, DEF-29-06-1).

### What was built

**Backend:** none — FE-only phase (INV-3 held: zero backend files, no golden fixture touched across all 7 plans).

**Frontend — open-design borrow primitives (`chat/runtime/`, plan 31-01, 9 files):**

- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/partial-json.ts`** — tolerant truncated-JSON repair/parse (borrow #1).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/streaming-json.ts`** — single named-string-field streaming extractor (borrow #2).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/blocks.types.ts`** — `AgentEvent`/`ChatBlock` unions, generic-kind discriminators (SC-001).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/buildBlocks.ts`** — pure events→blocks coalescing reducer (borrow #4).
- **`frontend/NOTICE`** + **`frontend/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`** — Apache-2.0 attribution to nexu-io/open-design for all 7 borrow mechanisms (D-09); zero new npm dependencies across the whole phase.
- (+ 3 vitest suites, 30 cases.)

**Frontend — rendering mechanics (`chat/runtime/` + `chat/blocks/`, plan 31-02, 8 files):**

- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/tool-renderers.tsx`** — `registerToolRenderer`/`renderToolBlock`/`GenericToolCard`: 3-tier dispatch, per-renderer try/catch isolation, name-only lookup (borrow #3); ships empty, no pre-populated tool vocabulary.
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/useMeasuredVirtualWindow.ts`** — `VIRTUALIZE_THRESHOLD=80`, pure measured virtual window over a scroll ref (borrow #5).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/blocks/ThinkingBlock.tsx`, `TodoCard.tsx`, `FileOpsSummary.tsx`** — collapsed-reasoning / plan-progress / files-this-turn blocks (borrow #7); first `data-testid`s in the codebase.
- (+ vitest suites, 48 cases cumulative.)

**Frontend — transcript data layer (plan 31-03, 4 created + 1 modified):**

- **`frontend/src/hooks/useRunChat.ts`** — transport-agnostic, family-anchored (D-02) transcript reducer: append-only over `chat_message`/`chat_reply`/`stream_attached`, `event_id` dedup, optimistic `sendMessage` reconciled by client-minted `message_id`.
- **`frontend/src/hooks/useTabDeepLink.ts`** — nonce'd deep-link seam (borrow #6): `requestOpenTab`/single-use `consume()`, module-level monotonic nonce.
- **`frontend/src/types/index.ts`** (modified) — `ChatMessage` gains optional `attachments`/`cardKind`/`deepLink`/`runId`/`threadId`; new `ChatAttachment` (ND-10 payload-transient, `retained:false` default) + `DeepLinkTarget` types.

**Frontend — composition root + result cards (plan 31-04, 4 created + 2 modified):**

- **`frontend/src/components/chat/ResultCard.tsx`** — narrator `chat_reply` card, switch on generic `cardKind` (clarify/gate/pipeline/deliverable/spec_revision); `deliverable` labeled **"Deliverable"** (LOCK-F); `spec_revision` reads "Revising spec — cycle N" (KAN-101).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/RunChatLane.tsx`** — the composition root: header + transcript + a D-12 mode-switched composer (idle/building/clarify/gate/complete/terminal) absorbing Stop/revise/suggestions, mounting plan-05's quick-actions and plan-06's attachments/token widget.
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/MessageBubble.tsx`** (modified) — narrator turn renders `ResultCard`; assistant turn renders the plan-01 agent-block strip above the kept react-markdown body + streaming-cursor.
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/ChatPanel.tsx`** (modified) — `role="log"`/`aria-live="polite"`/`aria-relevant="additions text"` + `data-testid="chat-transcript"`; plan-02 measured virtualizer engages >80 msgs; `hideComposer` flag so the lane owns the single composer.

**Frontend — in-lane gate/clarify quick-actions (plan 31-05, 4 files):**

- **`frontend/src/components/chat/InlineGateActions.tsx`** — 4 gate actions (approve/reject-confirm/redo/`update_specs`) firing the SAME `approve_review` channel; terminal fence (KAN-100), retained edit (KAN-98), reject-confirm (KAN-95).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/InlineClarifyActions.tsx`** — quick-reply chips/option picker/freeform firing the SAME `submit_questionnaire` channel, canonical `[{question_id, answer}]` shape.

**Frontend — attachments + token widget (plan 31-06, 6 files):**

- **`frontend/src/hooks/useChatAttachments.ts`** — picker/paste/drag-drop intake; images routed through Phase-30's `resizeImage` before base64 (UPLD-04); client allow-list + size cap mirroring the server.
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/ChatAttachments.tsx`** — drop zone + paste handler + removable preview chips + ND-10 reopen placeholder (no `<img>` for a non-retained ref).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/ChatTokenWidget.tsx`** — compact P26 token-usage widget (pinned `totalTokens`/`estimatedCostUsd`/`cacheReadTokens`).

**Frontend — live integration (plan 31-07, 2 created + 3 modified):**

- **`frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx`** (modified) — instantiates `useRunChat`/`useTabDeepLink`; additive WS chat-frame routing (`chat_message`/`chat_reply`/`stream_attached`, previously unhandled no-ops) into a local pub-sub when the SSE flag is off (LOCK-B).
- **`frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`** (modified) — mounts `RunChatLane` in the execution left column (`data-testid="execution-chat-lane"`), wires gate/clarify/attachment/token callbacks, derives the generic `runState` (gate > clarify > building > terminal > complete > idle, SC-001).
- **`frontend/src/components/preview/PreviewPanel.tsx`** (modified) — becomes the nonce'd deep-link consumer for ALL panel tabs (was preview/files-only).
- **`frontend/e2e/tests/ts-chat.spec.ts`** + **`ts-chat-cards.spec.ts`** — first data-testid-driven mocked chat e2e specs (7 tests), DELTA-verified.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Borrow primitives (#1/#2/#4):** `frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/{partial-json,streaming-json,blocks.types,buildBlocks}.ts`
- **Rendering mechanics (#3/#5/#7):** `frontend/src/components/chat/runtime/{tool-renderers.tsx,useMeasuredVirtualWindow.ts}`, `frontend/src/components/chat/blocks/{ThinkingBlock,TodoCard,FileOpsSummary}.tsx`
- **Transcript data layer:** `frontend/src/hooks/useRunChat.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useTabDeepLink.ts` (#6), `frontend/src/types/index.ts`
- **Composition root + cards:** `frontend/src/components/chat/RunChatLane.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/chat/ResultCard.tsx`, revived `frontend/src/components/chat/{MessageBubble,ChatPanel}.tsx`
- **Quick-actions:** `frontend/src/components/chat/{InlineGateActions,InlineClarifyActions}.tsx`
- **Attachments + token widget:** `frontend/src/hooks/useChatAttachments.ts`, `frontend/src/components/chat/{ChatAttachments,ChatTokenWidget}.tsx`
- **Live wiring:** `frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/preview/PreviewPanel.tsx`
- **Attribution:** `frontend/NOTICE`, `frontend/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`
- **e2e:** `frontend/e2e/tests/ts-chat.spec.ts`, `frontend/e2e/tests/ts-chat-cards.spec.ts`
- **Code review (advisory, unfixed):** `.planning/phases/31-chat-lane-mvp-a3/31-REVIEW.md`

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) D-09 — no external chat framework.** Every borrow-list mechanism is a clean-room reimplementation from a behavioral spec (no build-time dep on / bundled copy of open-design) WITH Apache-2.0 attribution (`NOTICE` + `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` + a per-file "Adapted from nexu-io/open-design" header); zero new npm dependencies across all 7 plans.
- **(b) SC-001 — every dispatch keys on a GENERIC discriminator, never a workflow/agent-name literal.** `ResultCard` switches on `cardKind`; `RunChatLane`'s composer switches on `RunLaneState` (D-12); the tool-renderer registry keys on tool name only; `InlineGateActions`' `update_specs` action is driven by a caller-supplied `updateSpecsEligible` flag, not an agent-id comparison. Grepped 0 in every plan; 31-05 additionally asserts it via a source-reading vitest case.
- **(c) LOCK-F — the deliverable card/label reads "Deliverable."**
- **(d) D-02 family-anchored transcript** — a child (revision) run's frames stitch into the SAME transcript array via `runId`/`threadId`; a state-change frame never wipes a prior turn.
- **(e) One backend channel either way.** `InlineGateActions`/`InlineClarifyActions` fire the identical `approve_review`/`submit_questionnaire` payloads the Steps `ReviewGatePanel`/`QuestionnairePanel` already send — no second command channel introduced.
- **(f) INV-3 / LOCK-B.** FE-only phase: zero backend files, no golden fixture, `useWorkflow.ts`/`useWebSocket.ts` untouched (FIX-039 preserved); the newly-routed `chat_message`/`chat_reply`/`stream_attached` frames were previously unhandled no-ops, so the flag-OFF legacy WS path stays byte-identical.
- **(g) a11y shipped from day one.** `role="log"` + `aria-live="polite"` + `aria-relevant="additions text"` on the transcript region — the open-design-documented failure mode, not repeated.
- **(h) ND-10 — attachments/images are payload-transient** (`retained:false` default); a reopened non-retained ref renders a "not kept on reopen" placeholder, never durable storage.
- **(i) D-15 — current skin, behavior over restyle;** Phase 32 owns the reskin.
- **(j) Verify BY DELTA, not absolute-green** — the pre-existing `feat/ui-2` e2e baseline is red (DEF-29-06-1); the phase's bar is new-specs-pass + zero green→red regression, never absolute-green.

### Invariants & verification

- **SC-001:** grep for `"prototype"|od_ppt|app_builder|user_stories|prototype-analyze|prototype-specify` across every new/modified chat production file = 0 hits in every one of the 7 plans; the only 2 source hits anywhere are inside the tests' own grep-regex strings, and `ArtifactCard.tsx`'s pre-existing `"prototype"` type-union member is untouched and predates this phase.
- **INV-3:** `git diff --name-only f9f759b1~1..217bf7df` (the verifier's phase-window spot-check) touches only `frontend/src/**`, `frontend/e2e/**`, `frontend/NOTICE`/`THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`, `.planning/**`; zero backend files; no golden fixture regenerated.
- **FIX-039 / LOCK-B:** `useWorkflow.ts` absent from every plan's diff; `useWebSocket.ts` untouched; the flag-OFF legacy WS path is byte-identical by construction.
- **tsc-identity:** `npx tsc --noEmit | grep -v mockApi.ts | grep -c "error TS"` → `0` after every one of the 7 plans' tasks, independently re-verified at phase close.
- **a11y:** `role="log"` + `aria-live="polite"` on `data-testid="chat-transcript"`, asserted by both `RunChatLane.test.tsx` and `ts-chat.spec.ts` (TS-CHAT-02).
- **Evidence:** `31-VERIFICATION.md` scored 15/15 must-have truths + all 14 required artifacts present (`status: passed`, 0 overrides, verified 2026-07-08T11:44:19Z). Independently re-ran (not just trusted): `tsc --noEmit` (0 new errors), 114+22 vitest cases across chat runtime/component/hook suites, both new mocked Playwright specs (`ts-chat.spec.ts` + `ts-chat-cards.spec.ts`, 7/7 pass), plus a delta spot-check of 2 baseline files (`ts-a.auth.spec.ts`, `ts-sse.spec.ts`) confirming the 2 observed failures (TS-A-05/06) are pre-existing red in `.planning/TEST-REGISTER.md`, not a regression.
- **Code review (`31-REVIEW.md`, deep, 41 files):** 0 Critical / 2 High / 3 Medium / 3 Low, status `issues_found`, landed as ADVISORY — none fixed in-phase (commit `54df085d`). Neither High finding breaks the currently-ACTIVE legacy-WS path (both are gated behind conditions false today).

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **HI-01 (code review) — `useRunChat`'s `runId` is wired to the clarify-only `activePipelineRunId`, not the live run id.** The moment the SSE transport flag flips ON, any chat message sent while a run is building (the common case) would POST to `/api/runs` with `runId=null` and spawn a brand-new, unrelated run instead of appending to the in-progress one (`page.tsx:917-923`, `useRunChat.ts:281-283`). Inert today only because `NEXT_PUBLIC_SSE_TRANSPORT` defaults OFF. Fix: thread `pipelineState.pipelineRunId` instead.
- **HI-02 (code review) — the LIVE-STATE-CONTRACT "Cancelled" state is unimplemented.** `pipeline_cancelled` never sets a `cancelled`/`failed` flag, so `runLaneState` falls through to `"idle"` (plain composer) instead of the contract's mandated "Cancelled by you" + relaunch mode, and no cancellation message is ever pushed to the transcript (`DashboardLayout.tsx:1290-1296`, `useWorkflow.ts:534-567`).
- **MD-02 (code review) — sent attachments never render in the transcript.** `ChatMessage.attachments` is populated by `useRunChat` but never read by `MessageBubble`/`ChatPanel`/`ResultCard` — a picked/pasted/dropped file disappears from the transcript the moment it's sent, a regression against the phase's own attachment deliverable. Also open: MD-01 (a failed `sendCommand` is silently swallowed, no error surfaced to the transcript), MD-03 (the page-level transcript never clears on `handleSelectWorkflowRun`, so switching to an unrelated historical run can bleed a prior run's chat messages into the new view), and 3 Low findings (`TodoCard` borrow-#7 is dead code — no `"todo"` block-kind wired through `buildBlocks`; `useTabDeepLink().consume()` is unused/undocumented dead code, single-use-ness actually comes from nonce-diffing; `InlineGateActionsProps.agentId` is accepted but never used).
- **`RunConnectionProvider` app-level mount (SSE-transport activation)** — built/tested/imported by both `useWorkflow.ts` (Phase 29) and `useRunChat` (Phase 31) but not mounted in `frontend/src/app/layout.tsx`; `NEXT_PUBLIC_SSE_TRANSPORT` is inert app-wide, the legacy WS path is the only ACTIVE transport today. Pre-existing Phase-29 deferral, explicitly out of this phase's 3-file scope (31-07-PLAN.md Task 1), deferred to the milestone-end live pass.
- **`THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` stale local-path cells** — 4 of 7 borrow-mechanism rows (#3/#5/#6/#7) still read `_planned (31-02)_`/`_planned (31-04)_` even though all four shipped with their own per-file attribution headers; the substantive Apache-2.0 attribution requirement is independently satisfied (NOTICE + 10 per-file headers) — cosmetic doc-debt only.
- **Live end-to-end (streaming SSE, multimodal send)** remains milestone-end live-deferred (DEF-29-09-1, needs the live-ectx registry); absolute-green e2e stays explicitly out of scope (the `feat/ui-2` baseline is pre-existing red, DEF-29-06-1) — delta-verify only.

## Phase 32 — Run-Screen Redesign [A4]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/32-run-screen-redesign-a4/` — 10 plans (32-01…32-10) across 4 dependency-ordered waves (W1 tokens+primitives → W2 backend SC-001 flag + Audit endpoints + chat-lane state → W3 reskin + Steps drill-down + Audit tab → W4 e2e hardening). Branch `feat/ui-2`.  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-08) — VERIFIED **passed**, 10/10 observable truths (`gsd-verifier`, 2026-07-08T15:36:47Z); a same-day code review + 6-pillar UI audit then surfaced 2 medium/3 low + 1 UI BLOCKER, **all fixed same-day** (see "Review + UI-audit arc" below).  ·  **Plans:** 10/10  ·  **Depends on:** Phase 31  ·  **Plan-id → POR §72:** [A4] (`.planning/CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md`).

**Requirements delivered:** RUNUI-01..05 (`REQUIREMENTS.md`; all flipped `Pending`→`Complete` same-day as ISS-043) ≙ ROADMAP Phase-32 SC-1..4. Also closes 2 Phase-31 review HIGHs living on this exact chat-lane surface: ISS-035 (no "Cancelled by you" ack) and ISS-036 (latent SSE-only `runId` bug).

**Driver:** D-10 (POR) sequences the v2.0 reskin "run screens first (Phase 32), shell after (Phases 35–38)" — the token layer is a hard dependency for every later shell phase, so it had to land clean before anything else — plus the 2 Phase-31 review HIGHs (ISS-035/036) that live on this exact surface.

**One-line outcome:** The run screen converges on the Hexaware Run design: a canonical `@theme`/`:root` token layer (one-chroma brand `#3C2CDA`, Manrope/Heebo, resolved radius ladder button=10/card=14) + 5 token-consuming primitives (Button/Card/Tabs/Badge/Pill) land first (SC-1); left becomes the Phase-31 `RunChatLane` fully absorbing `AgentProgressPanel`'s controls with faithful cancelled/failed/degraded terminal cards (SC-4); right becomes Preview/Steps/Files/Audit, with Steps rebuilt into a single GENERIC 3-level drill-down (dual-source L3 merging `task_progress` with the `wave_*`/`subagent_*` tree, the KAN-99 N-1 checklist cap, inline gate/clarify via the reused `InlineGateActions`/`InlineClarifyActions`) that supersedes and DELETES the bespoke `PrototypePipelineView` (INV-3) and closes the SC-001 literal leak by stamping a generic `update_specs_eligible`/`artifact_kind` flag on `review_gate_ready.data`, mirroring the `redoable` precedent (SC-2); Audit gains **3 new owner-scoped read endpoints** (`gate-events`/`validation-results`/`exec-runs`, IDOR→404) + client-side CSV/JSON export (SC-3); and the 5 run-screen e2e specs are re-anchored off brittle color-class assertions onto durable role/structural selectors, verified BY DELTA against the pre-existing 128-red DEF-29-06-1 baseline (SC-4). All 5 backend characterization goldens stay byte-identical throughout (INV-3).

### What was built

**Wave 1 — token layer + primitives:**
- **32-01** — rewrote `globals.css` `@theme inline` + `:root` to the full canonical evidence-11 §B2/§B3/§B4 palette (brand one-chroma, warm ink ramp, beige surfaces, status ramp, severity ladder, resolved radius ladder, elevation/motion/scrim); swapped `layout.tsx` fonts Inter/Fraunces→Manrope/Heebo; added `token-layer.test.ts`, an offline PRESENT/ABSENT guard (9 assertions). Resolved the 2 open evidence-11 §C human decisions (button radius=10, card radius=14) and locked running-status = blue `#3C2CDA` (the Handoff mock's amber "running" is a documented bug, ignored). Retired-palette removal scoped to the token layer only — the other ~370 `#1B2A4A` component sites are intentionally deferred to Phase 35+.
- **32-02** — net-new `frontend/src/components/ui/{Button,Card,Tabs,Badge,Pill}.tsx`; every value resolves to a plan-01 token class or `var()`, zero raw hex; a generic variant/status API keyed on presentational props, never a workflow name (SC-001); 28-test vitest suite.

**Wave 2 — SC-001 backend flag + Audit endpoints + chat-lane state:**
- **32-03** — 3 additive, owner-scoped, read-only endpoints on `backend/app/api/runs.py`: `GET /api/runs/{id}/gate-events`, `/validation-results`, `/exec-runs`, sharing a new `_owner_gate_or_404` two-layer gate (`WorkflowRun.user_id`, never the nullable `owner_id`) cloned from `get_hook_runs`; `exec-runs` surfaces only the truncated `output_digest`, no raw output field; no migration (the 3 tables already exist, alembic 0016/0018); 17/17 tests.
- **32-04** — closed the SC-001 literal leak at its source: `_run_review_gate` stamps `update_specs_eligible: bool` + `artifact_kind: str` on `review_gate_ready.data`, derived structurally from `_artifact_kind_for` via a new `_UPDATE_SPECS_ELIGIBLE_KINDS` frozenset (originally `{spec, task_list, summary}` — narrowed post-review, see "Key locked decisions"), mirroring the `redoable` precedent exactly; both keys added to `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS`; the 5 goldens + the new `test_sc001_gate_flag.py` all byte-identical (19 passed).
- **32-05** — FE state plumbing: `pipeline_cancelled` now stamps a `cancelled: true` terminal marker (ISS-035, symmetric with the existing `failed: true`); `useRunChat` now targets the live `pipelineState.pipelineRunId ?? activePipelineRunId` (ISS-036; SSE stays dormant per LOCK-B); the `prototype-specify` literal is gone from `useWorkflow.ts` (`isSpecRevisionRerun = wasAlreadyDone`, an ND-11 generalization); `page.tsx` parses the plan-04 `update_specs_eligible`/`artifact_kind` flags into `reviewGateData`, the single FE feed plans 06/08 consume. FIX-039's unconditional accumulator reset proven byte-unchanged throughout.

**Wave 3 — reskin + Steps drill-down + Audit tab:**
- **32-06** — `RunChatLane` reskinned to tokens/primitives (raw hex count 0); absorbs `AgentProgressPanel`'s Stop/revise controls and DELETES its duplicate run-lane mount (INV-3 — one stop/revise implementation; `AgentProgressPanel.tsx` itself is retained for its own test suite + a plan-08 relocation target); renders faithful cancelled ("Cancelled by you" + Run again) / failed ("What went wrong" + `agents_failed[]` + a `sanitizeError`-scrubbed first-line message + Edit brief & run again) / degraded ("Completed with issues") terminal cards off the generic plan-05 markers. `DashboardLayout` derives a terminal `runLaneState` + a `laneGate.updateSpecsEligible`/`approveLabel` mapping (mirrors the `redoable` idiom).
- **32-07** — right tab host reskinned onto the plan-02 underline `Tabs` primitive; "Thinking" relabelled "Steps" (internal id kept `thinking`, so testid/deep-link targets stay stable); a MANUAL typed-renderer switcher layered on top of — never replacing — the generic `FIRST_PARTY_RENDERERS` dispatch, gated to generic/mimetype-routed deliverables only (avoids an a11y option-role collision with `LiveVersionChip`); `PreviewPanel` forwards the plan-06 gate/clarify props to `AgentThinkingTab` as dormant passthroughs for plan 08.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **D-10 (reskin order).** Run screens first (Phase 32), shell after (Phases 35–38) — the token layer is the hard dependency the rest of v2.0 gates on.
- **D-15 (reskin-look, keep-behavior).** Adopt the DS mock's visual language; KEEP the product's richer live-data behavior and REUSE existing components (`AgentThinkingTab`, `WaveTreePanel`, `PreviewPanel`/`FIRST_PARTY_RENDERERS`, the Phase-31 `RunChatLane`) rather than rebuilding from the mock's stale/hardcoded versions — a face-value rebuild REGRESSES. Shared-surface truth is `evidence 11 §B`, not any single mock.
- **D-12 (live-state contract).** Real event → lane render + composer mode + Steps behavior, per state; the transcript ACCUMULATES across states/revisions; the mock's phase scrubber is excluded. Cancelled/degraded/planner-running/fix-loop are real states the mock omits — they render here.
- **LOCK-F / LOCK-B held.** Output name stays "Deliverable"; the Audit governance palette (green/amber/red) is the SOLE one-chroma exception elsewhere. Legacy WS stays the ACTIVE transport — SSE stays dormant (ISS-036 wires `runId` correctly but does not activate it).
- **KAN-95/98/100 reused verbatim, not reimplemented.** Reject two-step confirm + navigate-home (KAN-95), the `isPipelineRunning` guard + terminal auto-dismiss + the cancel-aware gate wait (KAN-100), and `retainAgentEdit` (KAN-98) all come from Phase-31's `InlineGateActions`/`InlineClarifyActions`, mounted inline in Steps — no second implementation.
- **KAN-99.** The Steps construction checklist caps at N-1 until the build agent truly finishes (a later agent starts OR the run ends) — the final `task_progress` precedes the fix-loop, so `completed_count == total-1` must never be read as a stall.
- **KAN-101 scope is analyze-gate-only (corrected mid-phase).** `update_specs_eligible` derives from `_UPDATE_SPECS_ELIGIBLE_KINDS`, which plan 32-04 originally set to `{spec, task_list, summary}` — a broadening that also lit the affordance up on the specify/plan gates. Code review flagged this as an unconfirmed behavior change against the locked KAN-101 scope; it was narrowed to `{summary}` same-day (ISS-038, commit `bab42693`). Do not re-widen without an explicit product decision.
- **`PrototypePipelineView` DELETED, not generalized (INV-3).** `AgentRunState` carries no `artifactKind`, so its 3 positional phase-agent lookups could not be generalized off a declared flag; the generic `AgentThinkingTab` path is a complete superset renderer, so the bespoke view was deleted rather than parallel-maintained.

## Phase 33 — Concierge + Compaction [A5]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/33-concierge-compaction-a5/` — 5 plans (33-01..33-05) across 4 waves. Branch: `feat/ui-2`.  ·  **Status:** VERIFIED — **PASS-WITH-CONCERNS** (2026-07-09, offline, tree clean at HEAD `5e20984f`) — all 4 ROADMAP SC proven at the phase's offline-provable bar (193 tests green, 5 goldens byte-identical, SC-001 grep-gated 0 hits), **but the Concierge is fully DORMANT end-to-end in the shipped app** (no live caller wires it in yet) and code review surfaced 1 High + 4 Medium findings (H1/M2/M3 load-bearing). Live-wiring + these findings + 6 live-deferred checks are explicitly carried to **Phase 34**.  ·  **Plan-ids → §33:** 33-01..33-05.

**Requirements delivered:** ROADMAP Phase 33 SC 1–4 — `chat:concierge` (proposal-only tools, confirm chips, Haiku default via `deep_agent_runner`, INV-13) answers run questions from real run data and its proposals execute only through existing channels; `compaction:chat_history` + `context_provider:conversation` bound the composed history under budget with recent turns verbatim; post-run chat turns produce revision runs stitched into the family transcript (D-02); a brand-new custom workflow gets lane + router + Concierge with zero new code (SC-001). Realizes D-04 (mechanical-router-first escalation), D-05 (the ONE per-run Concierge capability, proposal-only), D-08 (backend-owned compaction + conversation context provider).

**Driver:** Every run needed free-form Q&A and steering beyond the Phase-29 mechanical router's structured turns (clarify/gate/steering/revision/`update_specs`), without hardwiring chat behavior per workflow and without letting an unbounded transcript blow the context budget — and the milestone's core value (SC-001) required this to work for a workflow the kernel has never heard of.

**One-line outcome:** Three new registered capabilities — `chat:concierge` (per-run `DeepAgentRunner`/Haiku orchestrator, read + proposal-only tools), `compaction:chat_history` (pure budget-bounded transform), `context_provider:conversation` (compacted chat-history block) — plus a zero-model router escalation branch, seam-reusing proposal disposal with a confirm-chip hold, and FE confirm/reject/compact UX land fully offline-proven (registry drift-guard 66→68→69, 193 tests, 5 goldens byte-identical). But the subsystem ships **wired to no caller**: no non-test backend code sets `concierge=True` and no non-test frontend code mounts the Phase-33 props — so live activation, server-enforcing the confirm-hold (H1), serializing ORM rows for the model (M2), and threading the compiled manifest onto the live Concierge (M3) are Phase-34 work.

### What was built

**Backend (10 files, Waves 1–3 + Wave 4 proof plan):**

- **`backend/agents/capabilities/compaction/chat_history.py`** (Plan 01, created) — `ChatHistoryCompaction` (`name="chat_history"`, duck-typed, no `base.py` port). `compact(transcript, *, budget, keep_recent) -> str`: under budget returns the transcript verbatim; otherwise the `keep_recent` most-recent turns stay byte-verbatim and the older tail collapses to one short summary marker sized to fit `budget`. Recent-turn fidelity always wins if the safety net is hit.
- **`backend/agents/capabilities/context_providers/conversation.py`** (Plan 01, created) — `ConversationProvider` (`ContextProvider` port). Self-gates on the `"conversation"` inject token (INV-1, dormant on goldens = INV-3); reads owner-scoped chat `run_events` via `ctx.scoped_store.read_events` (default-deny, no raw ORM); bounds the rendered transcript through `registry.resolve("compaction","chat_history")`; returns one `{conversation_context: ...}` block; degrades to `{}` on any missing store/empty transcript/read error.
- **`backend/agents/capabilities/registry.py`** (modified twice, non-overlapping) — Plan 01 lockstep 66→68 (`compaction/chat_history`, `context_provider/conversation`); Plan 02 lockstep 68→69 (`chat/concierge`, `app.agents.chat` added to `_forward_packages`).
- **`backend/app/agents/chat/concierge.py`** (Plan 02, created) — `ConciergeCapability` (`name="concierge"`, duck-typed chat kind). `async converse(ctx, user_message) -> str` reaches the model EXCLUSIVELY through `DeepAgentRunner` (Haiku default; P26 cache-points + base-stack summarizer inherited; zero `create_deep_agent` references — INV-13). READ tools (owner-scoped `ScopedStore` wrappers over `read_events`/`list_refs`/`get_ref`/`read_gate_events`, IDOR→404) are disjoint from PROPOSAL-ONLY tools (`propose_steering_note`/`propose_revision`/`propose_gate_action` incl. `update_specs`) that return a frozen `ProposalIntent(channel, params)` and self-execute nothing. System prompt composed from DATA only (no `pipeline_type`/`spec.id`/workflow-name branch — INV-1).
- **`backend/app/agents/chat/__init__.py`** (Plan 02, created) — the `app.agents.chat` package; imports `concierge` so `@register` fires via `_forward_packages`.
- **`backend/app/api/chat_router.py`** (Plan 03, modified) — `CHANNEL_CONCIERGE` constant + a generic `ChatTurn.concierge` opt-in marker + a pure, additive escalation branch at the top of `route_chat_turn` (zero-model; fires only when a turn is explicitly marked free-form AND has no routable discriminator).
- **`backend/app/api/run_commands.py`** (Plan 03, modified) — `MessageCommand.concierge`/`confirm_proposal` fields; `_ConciergeCtx`; `_resolve_concierge`; the `CHANNEL_CONCIERGE` dispatch branch (invoke Concierge, project a `chat_reply` row); `_dispose_concierge_proposal` (routes each `propose_*` intent through the SAME Phase-29 seams: `steering_note→apply_steering`, `gate_action→set_review_response`, `revision→_mint_revision_row+_drive_revision_to_queue`); `_CONCIERGE_GATE_ACTION_MAP` (`request_changes→redo`); the `concierge_proposal` confirm-chip hold (a durable `run_events` row for consequential proposals, executed only via the FE confirm round-trip `{concierge:true, confirm_proposal:{channel,params}}`).
- **`backend/agents/workflows/manifest.py`** (Plan 05, modified) — optional `WorkflowManifest.chat` DATA field (`_optional_dict`, allow-listed in `_ALLOWED_TOP_KEYS`).
- **`backend/agents/workflows/plan.py`** (Plan 05, modified — Rule-3 addition beyond original plan scope) — `CompiledWorkflow.chat` field, needed because the compiler builds a fresh `CompiledWorkflow` and a manifest-only field would dead-end.
- **`backend/agents/workflows/compiler.py`** (Plan 05, modified — Rule-3 addition) — carries `chat=dict(getattr(manifest,"chat",{}) or {})` verbatim onto the compiled plan; pure data, zero branching (INV-5).

**Frontend (2 files, Wave 4):**

- **`frontend/src/components/chat/RunChatLane.tsx`** — `LaneProposal` type + `proposals`/`onConfirmProposal`/`onRejectProposal`/`compactAvailable`/`onCompact` props; a confirm/reject chip pair (`chat-proposal-confirm`/`chat-proposal-reject`) for held consequential proposals, rendered above the composer so it is visible in any live state; a header `chat-compact` affordance surfacing on `compactAvailable` OR high composed-context usage. All callbacks are presentational (mirrors `onSuggestion`/`onApprove`) — the confirm POST transport is caller-owned, deliberately NOT wired into `RunChatLane` or `useWorkflow.ts` (protects FIX-039).
- **`frontend/src/components/chat/ChatTokenWidget.tsx`** — `ComposedContextTelemetry` (local optional extension, no shared `PipelineRunState` schema change) + `composedContextUsage()` helper + `COMPACT_THRESHOLD_PCT` + a "🧠 N% context" sub-display (`chat-context-usage`) that degrades to hidden when the stream omits the fields.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Compaction capability:** `backend/agents/capabilities/compaction/chat_history.py`
- **Conversation context provider:** `backend/agents/capabilities/context_providers/conversation.py`
- **Concierge capability (app-side):** `backend/app/agents/chat/concierge.py`, `backend/app/agents/chat/__init__.py`
- **Registry lockstep (two bumps):** `backend/agents/capabilities/registry.py` (66→68 then 68→69)
- **Router escalation:** `backend/app/api/chat_router.py`
- **Proposal disposal + confirm-chip hold:** `backend/app/api/run_commands.py`
- **Manifest `chat:` DATA field + propagation:** `backend/agents/workflows/manifest.py`, `plan.py`, `compiler.py`
- **FE confirm/reject/compact + composed-context telemetry:** `frontend/src/components/chat/RunChatLane.tsx`, `ChatTokenWidget.tsx`
- **SC-001 + INV-3 proof suite:** `backend/tests/agents/test_sc001_lane_router_concierge.py`, `test_chat_event_neutrality.py`, `test_phase3_cutover_verify.py`

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) ONE `chat:concierge` capability, per-run instances (D-05, SC-001).** Workflow-specificity comes from CONTEXT (compiled manifest `chat:` data, artifacts, events, conversation) — never per-workflow code.
- **(b) Proposal-only, app disposes through the SAME Phase-29 seams (D-05).** No forked execution path; consequential proposals (`gate_action`, `revision`) held behind a `concierge_proposal` confirm-chip row; non-consequential answers/`steering_note` project immediately as `chat_reply`.
- **(c) Escalation is OPT-IN via a generic `ChatTurn.concierge` marker, NOT plain-text-by-default (D-04; realizes ND-2's "per-run toggle initially").** A literal "plain text in running/terminal escalates" predicate would have flipped the Phase-29 default routing and broken four frozen, out-of-scope tests — deviation forced by the diff contract + INV-12, documented as an auto-fix in `33-03-SUMMARY.md`.
- **(d) Compaction is backend-owned, measured in characters, recent-turn fidelity always wins over budget (D-08).** In-graph message growth stays the deepagents `SummarizationMiddleware`'s job; `chat_history` is the separate composed-transcript-bounding layer.
- **(e) `request_changes` (Concierge vocabulary) reconciles to `redo` (gate-seam vocabulary)** via `_CONCIERGE_GATE_ACTION_MAP`; `approve`/`reject`/`update_specs` pass through unchanged.
- **(f) Manifest `chat:` is pure DATA (INV-5)** — carried `WorkflowManifest.chat → CompiledWorkflow.chat`, never branched on anywhere in the compiler or engine.
- **(g) FE confirm/reject/compact are presentational callback props only.** The confirm round-trip transport and composed-context stream fields are caller-owned ("plan 07" / Phase-34) — this is WHY the app-side wiring in (h) below is still open.
- **(h) Registry lockstep landed in two non-overlapping bumps** (66→68 in Plan 01 for the 2 kernel capabilities; 68→69 in Plan 02 for the Concierge) to avoid a shared-file double-edit across waves.

### Invariants & verification

- **Registry drift-guard:** 66 → 68 (Plan 01) → 69 (Plan 02); `len(_KNOWN)==69` = 3 hits, `==68`/`==66` = 0, re-run independently at verification.
- **INV-13 (Concierge model ONLY via `DeepAgentRunner`):** `test_banned_patterns.py` 11 passed; `grep create_deep_agent concierge.py` = 0, `DeepAgentRunner` = 6.
- **INV-12 (`ChatRunner` not forked):** `test_chat_contract.py` 11 passed; `git diff --name-only` confirms no `ChatRunner`/chat_contract edit.
- **INV-1 / SC-001 (name-free lane + router + Concierge):** a throwaway `sc001_throwaway_wf` manifest routes through the name-free `route_chat_turn`, resolves the one shared `chat:concierge` impl, compiles its `chat:` block, is read by the Concierge; `test_sc001_lane_router_concierge.py` 9 passed; grep gate = 0 hits for the throwaway name in engine/router/concierge and 0 hits for `pipeline_type==`/`spec.id==`/`workflow_name==` in the chat surface.
- **INV-3 (5 goldens byte/event-identical, Concierge dormant on golden paths):** `concierge_proposal` is the only new event type, fires on zero golden paths, needed no `_VOLATILE_STRIP_KEYS` change; `git diff --stat golden/` empty with `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset.
- **INV-5 (manifest `chat:` stays data):** `grep "if .*\.chat\b|chat ==" manifest.py compiler.py` = 0.
- **import-linter:** 4 kept / 0 broken.
- **Owner-scoping (IDOR→404):** all Concierge reads + the conversation provider go through `ScopedStore` only (18 refs, 0 raw ORM); cross-owner denial asserted in `test_concierge_capability.py`.
- **Total independently re-run offline:** 193 tests green across the phase's suites (116 + 46 + 31 per `33-VERIFICATION.md`), plus grep/git/lint gates.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope) — ALL carried to Phase 34

- **Gating concern (verifier finding NEW-33-01) — the Concierge is fully DORMANT end-to-end in the shipped app.** No non-test backend code sets `MessageCommand.concierge=True` (the `chat_router.py` escalation branch is never taken live); `DashboardLayout.tsx` (~line 1602) mounts `RunChatLane` WITHOUT any of `proposals`/`onConfirmProposal`/`onRejectProposal`/`onCompact`/composed-context telemetry, and no non-test FE code POSTs `concierge:true`/`confirm_proposal`. Every piece is proven in isolation + at the seam/contract level by explicit phase policy ("build + offline-prove here; live wiring is Phase-34"), but SC-1/SC-3 have ZERO live end-to-end reachability until this caller wiring lands — the single dependency gating the milestone core value from becoming observable.
- **Code review (`33-REVIEW.md`, deep, 10 files: 1 High + 4 Medium + 4 Low, 0 Critical, none FIX-039-sensitive):**
  - **H1 (High) — confirm-chip hold is client-trust, not server-enforced** (`run_commands.py:825-841` confirm branch, `:538-549` hold write). The durable `concierge_proposal` "pending" row is written but never read back, verified, or resolved on confirm — the confirm turn disposes purely from client-supplied `body.confirm_proposal`, leaving a permanent "pending" ghost row with no check the proposal was ever actually held for this run. Bounded by the existing mechanical channels (not a new privilege escalation) but defeats the hold's audit/integrity purpose.
  - **M2 (Medium) — Concierge READ tools return raw SQLAlchemy ORM rows** (`concierge.py:220-243`); a LangChain `@tool` coerces them to `str()` object reprs, so the live model receives no usable content and cannot ground answers in run data (masked offline by the scripted-fake model).
  - **M3 (Medium) — the manifest `chat:` block never reaches the live Concierge.** `_ConciergeCtx` (`run_commands.py:848-851`) never sets `compiled`, so `getattr(compiled,"chat",{})` in `_compose_system_prompt` is always `{}` on the production path — the 33-05 manifest→compiler→plan propagation is proven at the data layer but inert live.
  - **M1 (Medium, minor) — a missing `action` defaults to `approve`** (`run_commands.py:571`); combined with H1, a malformed/partial `confirm_proposal` can silently approve an armed gate.
  - **M4 (Medium) — no idempotency short-circuit on a replayed `message_id`** — a replayed confirm turn can re-mint a revision child run or re-invoke `set_review_response` (mirrors pre-existing `CHANNEL_REVISION` behavior, widened by the Concierge confirm path).
  - **L1–L4 (Low):** a dead `_DEFAULT_CONTEXT_BUDGET` constant; `propose_gate_action` degrades an invalid action to a consequential `request_changes` rather than an inert marker; `DeepAgentRunner`'s `thread_id` is inert without a checkpointer; `chat_history.compact` can exceed budget in narrow edge cases (`keep_recent==0`, a negative budget).
- **Live-deferred (Phase-34 live pass, expect `human_needed` — from `33-VERIFICATION.md`):** (1) live Concierge Q&A against real Bedrock, grounded/non-hallucinated; (2) multi-turn P26 cache-point placement across a Concierge conversation; (3) live mid-run steering delivery — `_live_ectx_for_run` returns `None` today (DEF-29-09-1); (4) live model tool-result → proposal surfacing — `converse()` returns `str` only so `_drain_concierge_proposals` yields `[]` against the current impl (disposal+confirm contract proven offline via a test-double); (5) FE confirm-chip round-trip + compact affordance on real usage — needs the caller wiring above ("plan 07"); (6) `ChatTokenWidget` composed-context sub-display — hidden until the run stream emits `composedContextTokens`/`contextBudgetTokens`.
- **Pre-existing, unrelated (not attributable to Phase 33):** 9 `test_clarify_defaults_match_engine`-family failures (7 in `test_manifest_parity.py` + 2 in `test_manifest.py`) — a `plan.clarify.defaults` vs engine `_pipeline_defaults` mismatch, confirmed identical on the clean baseline before and after every 33-0x plan. ~120 CreationHub/home-redesign Playwright failures (DEF-29-06-1) — out of scope, untouched.
- **Deferred design scope (`33-CONTEXT.md`, LOCK-E):** per-agent prompt-override, team-sharing, resume-from-failed Concierge sessions — not part of this phase's SC.

## Phase 35 — Shell Chrome + Reskin Pages [B1] (SHELL-01, milestone v2.0)

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/35-shell-chrome-reskin-pages-b1/` — 7 plans (35-01..35-07) across 2 waves (Wave 1: 35-01 foundation; Wave 2: 35-02..35-07, blocked on Wave 1 completion). Branch: `feat/ui-2` (milestone v2.0 — Universal Run Chat & VelocityAI UI Convergence).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-09) — `35-VERIFICATION.md` verdict `passed`, 16/16 observable truths (21/21 counting per-plan must-haves), 0 overrides; FRONTEND-ONLY / OFFLINE, verified BY DELTA (DEF-29-06-1); live/pixel/e2e sign-off explicitly deferred to the end-of-milestone live pass.  ·  **Plans:** 7/7  ·  **Plan-id → §35:** 35-01..35-07 (POR `.planning/CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md` §82, the Phase-35 brief).

**Requirements delivered:** SHELL-01 — "Shell chrome (dark top bar, nav pill Home·Library·My Workflows, profile menu, notifications) + reskin-only pages (Settings, pickers, Library) on the token layer" (REQUIREMENTS.md line 248, the only REQ-ID this phase maps to). Decomposed into 7 plan-local sub-tags B1-01..B1-07 (Requirements Coverage table in `35-VERIFICATION.md`; no separate REQUIREMENTS.md rows for them). Also realizes ND-12 (Login/Register + Admin reskin scope, identity-trap drop). Note: REQUIREMENTS.md's own checkbox (L248, still `[ ]`) and its coverage-table status (L470: "Pending") are both stale tracking artifacts per `35-VERIFICATION.md`'s own note — the implementation is complete and independently verified, not a gap.

**Driver:** the Phase-32 `@theme` token layer + `ui/*` primitives (Button/Card/Tabs/Badge/Pill) had already landed, but the shell (top bar/nav/profile menu/notifications) and every page-level surface in scope were still on the pre-Phase-32 retired palette (navy `#1B2A4A`, `#2563eb`, `#f5f5f0`, Inter/Fraunces/JetBrains) — Phase 35 is the token layer's first real consumer. The v2.0 mocks (evidence `02`/`10`/`11`) also carried UX fictions (SSO, password-reset, email-invite, account-suspension, a fabricated "150 design systems" count) that needed to be explicitly rejected rather than built, since the shipped product's wiring on most of these surfaces was already richer than the mock.

**One-line outcome:** 16 frontend source files across shell chrome (`AppHeader`, `DashboardLayout`, `NotificationPanel`) and the reskin-only page/flow surfaces (Account Settings, Template picker + 3 sibling modals, Design-System picker + 2 sibling modals, Review-gates, Library, Login/Register, Admin) were migrated onto the Phase-32 token layer and `ui/*` primitives with every existing wired behavior preserved byte-for-byte (D-15) — retired-palette grep = 0 and stray-stock-palette grep = 0 on every touched file, LOCK-F's real ~14 design-system count held (no fabricated "150"), the nav relabelled Catalogue→My Workflows without touching the `saved-workflows` route (D-11/SC-001), a full keyboard/ARIA menu-button contract added to the profile menu + notifications bell + admin `TierDropdown`, and the ND-12 identity-trap backends (SSO/Forgot/invite/suspend) explicitly dropped rather than built. 8 new test suites (40 tests) plus a pre-reskin mocked-Playwright baseline pin the reskin-by-delta contract; `35-VERIFICATION.md` scored the full must-have set PASSED, with live/pixel sign-off deferred to the end-of-milestone pass.

### What was built

**Wave 1 — shell chrome (35-01, foundation for every later plan — 3 files modified, 3 created):**
- **`frontend/src/components/layout/AppHeader.tsx`** — dark near-black shell (`bg-surface-near-black`) replacing the white-fill nav pill with a purple-underline active state (`border-brand` + `aria-current="page"`); nav relabelled `Catalogue`→`My Workflows` (D-11, text-only — the `saved-workflows` page key + `onNavigate` handler are untouched, the `WorkflowCatalog` component rename is Phase 36); full keyboard/ARIA on the profile menu (`aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded`, `role="menu"`/`"menuitem"`, Escape-close+refocus via a wrapper `onKeyDown`).
- **`frontend/src/components/ui/NotificationPanel.tsx`** — chrome-only token reskin (bell, unread badge, dropdown, empty-state, rows, progress bar); same menu-button a11y pattern as the profile menu; status label switched to the `Badge` primitive (chrome only — the live feed is Phase 38).
- **`frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`** — light-touch: only the page-shell body background inline style swapped for `bg-surface-paper`; no routing/page-key restructure. Its remaining pre-existing stock-palette lines (loading screen, error banner, a modal) were left out of scope, flagged by the verifier as a candidate for a later reskin phase.
- New: `AppHeader.a11y.test.tsx` (7 tests), `NotificationPanel.a11y.test.tsx` (4 tests) — RED-first then GREEN. `frontend/e2e/.baseline-35.txt` — the pre-reskin mocked-Playwright pass/fail snapshot every later wave verifies against BY DELTA (captured partial: the offline Next `webServer` times out ~45s/spec before a full run completes).

**Wave 2 — reskin-only pages (35-02..35-07, all built on the 35-01 token/a11y idiom):**
- **Account Settings (35-02) — `frontend/src/components/settings/AccountSettings.tsx`** (the heaviest single file, 21 retired-palette lines): migrated onto tokens + `Tabs`/`Card`/`Button`/`Badge`; section nav moved from a vertical sidebar to the `Tabs` primitive; all four already-wired sections (`getMe`; password via `changePassword`; AI model via `getPreferences`/`updatePreferences`, kept as the existing native `<select>` rather than rebuilt into chip cards; constitution via `fetch('/api/settings/constitution')` GET/PUT/DELETE) preserved; no unbacked fields added (fiction guard — no Full name/Role/Organization inputs). New: `AccountSettings.render.test.tsx` (5 tests).
- **Template picker flow (35-03) — `frontend/src/components/workflow/prototype/{TemplateGallery,TemplateDetailModal,CustomTemplateModal,TemplateCard}.tsx`**: the 505-line `TemplateGallery` reskinned with `role="tab"`/`role="tablist"` category chips (kept as inline scrollable buttons, not the `Tabs` primitive, to preserve horizontal scroll + a Custom count badge); `TemplateDetailModal` kept on the dark-surface token idiom (not the light `ui/*` primitives — it is a full-bleed dark preview); `CustomTemplateModal` + `TemplateCard` consume `Button`/`Pill`; real search/category-filter/custom-upload/localStorage/`onSelect` wiring untouched; dead `UploadCustomCard` removed. New: `TemplateGallery.reskin.test.tsx` (5 tests).
- **Design-System picker + Review-gates (35-04) — `frontend/src/components/workflow/prototype/{DesignSystemPicker,DesignSystemDetailModal,CustomDesignSystemModal}.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/workflow/ReviewGatesSection.tsx`**: same dark/light idiom split as the template flow; `DesignSystemPicker` LOCK-F-enforces the real `systems.length`/`totalVisible` count (no fabricated "150"); the custom-DS placeholder DESIGN.md example was de-branded (retired font names + raw fenced hexes → generic families + `<...-hex>` tokens) so the data content also clears the grep gates; `ReviewGatesSection` reskinned classNames-only — `onChange(gateAgentIds, touched)` + `aria-expanded`/`sr-only` byte-preserved. New: `DesignSystemPicker.reskin.test.tsx` (4 tests); logged `deferred-items.md` for a pre-existing, unrelated `ReviewGatesSection.test.tsx` count-drift (see Known follow-ups).
- **Library (35-05) — `frontend/src/components/library/LibraryPage.tsx`**: the Agents/Skills/Hooks tab bar moved to the `Tabs` primitive (real catalog counts carried in the tab label string); cards → `Card`, tag chips → `Pill`; real local-data search/category filters and the Skill/Hook copy-to-clipboard detail modals preserved (no invented backend — `fetch(|/api/` grep = 0). New: `LibraryPage.reskin.test.tsx` (5 tests).
- **Login + Register (35-06) — `frontend/src/app/login/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/register/page.tsx`**: login restructured into a two-column split with a NEW ~46% dark brand panel (`bg-surface-near-black`, Manrope wordmark + brand dot, static non-trap value-prop tags); `login()`→redirect-by-`is_admin`, 401 handling, loading/disable state, and the invite-only footer preserved; every e2e selector kept verbatim (`#email`, `#password`, submit name "Sign in", heading "Welcome back"); the SSO button and "Forgot?" link DROPPED (ND-12 — no backend exists for them). Register: single `#f5f5f0`→`bg-surface-paper` token swap; the `router.replace("/login")` redirect stub is unchanged (B1-06). New: `login.reskin.test.tsx` (5 tests).
- **Admin (35-07) — `frontend/src/app/admin/page.tsx`**: near-black header shell + `Card` stat cards/table + a `Badge`-based `TierBadge` (tier→status-ramp map: basic→queued/pro→running/enterprise→done, explicit label) + `Pill` role/count chips; `TierDropdown` gained the same menu-button a11y contract as the 35-01 profile menu; `getMe().is_admin` gate+redirect, `adminListUsers`/`UpdateTier`/`CreateUser`/`DeleteUser`, optimistic tier-revert, the Runs/Role/Joined columns, and the password-create modal (email+password+plan+grant-admin) all preserved; the mock's Status/Last-active columns + email-invite flow DEFERRED — `AdminUser` has no backing `status`/`last_active_at`/`display_name` fields — asserted by a negative `columnheader` test. New: `admin.reskin.test.tsx` (5 tests).

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Shell chrome:** `frontend/src/components/layout/AppHeader.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ui/NotificationPanel.tsx` — tests `AppHeader.a11y.test.tsx` / `NotificationPanel.a11y.test.tsx`; delta baseline `frontend/e2e/.baseline-35.txt`.
- **Account Settings:** `frontend/src/components/settings/AccountSettings.tsx` — `AccountSettings.render.test.tsx`.
