- **Design-system picker + Review-gates:** `frontend/src/components/workflow/prototype/DesignSystemPicker.tsx` + siblings `DesignSystemDetailModal.tsx`, `CustomDesignSystemModal.tsx`; `frontend/src/components/workflow/ReviewGatesSection.tsx` — `DesignSystemPicker.reskin.test.tsx`.
- **Library:** `frontend/src/components/library/LibraryPage.tsx` — `LibraryPage.reskin.test.tsx`.
- **Login/Register:** `frontend/src/app/login/page.tsx`, `frontend/src/app/register/page.tsx` — `frontend/src/app/login/login.reskin.test.tsx`.
- **Admin:** `frontend/src/app/admin/page.tsx` — `frontend/src/app/admin/admin.reskin.test.tsx`.
- **Token/primitive source (consumed, not modified this phase):** `frontend/src/styles/globals.css` (`@theme` layer) + `frontend/src/components/ui/{Button,Card,Tabs,Badge,Pill}.tsx` — all landed in Phase 32.
- **Phase docs:** `.planning/phases/35-shell-chrome-reskin-pages-b1/{35-CONTEXT,35-RESEARCH,35-PATTERNS,35-VALIDATION,35-VERIFICATION,35-REVIEW,35-UI-REVIEW,deferred-items}.md`.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Nav relabel is TEXT-ONLY (D-11).** `"Catalogue"`→`"My Workflows"` is a visible-string change only; the `WorkflowCatalog` component, the `saved-workflows` page key, and the `onNavigate("saved-workflows")` handler are unchanged — the component rename is Phase 36, not this phase.
- **(b) Reskin-look, keep-behavior (D-15) — no rebuild-to-mock-face-value.** Every already-wired handler/endpoint call across all 16 files was preserved byte-for-byte. Two documented reuse-don't-rebuild calls: Account Settings' AI-model control stayed the wired native `<select>` (not rebuilt into the mock's chip "radio cards"), and both pickers' category tabs stayed inline scrollable buttons with the `role=tab`/`aria-selected` contract applied inline (the `Tabs` primitive doesn't support horizontal scroll + a count badge).
- **(c) LOCK-F — DesignSystemPicker shows the REAL ~14 count.** Counts derive from `systems.length`/`totalVisible`; `grep -c '150'` = 0; no catalog expansion.
- **(d) ND-12 — identity traps DROPPED, not built; admin's richer wiring KEPT.** Login has no SSO button / no Forgot-password link (`grep -Eic 'SSO|forgot'` = 0). Admin's mock Status/Last-active columns + email-invite flow are explicitly DEFERRED (no backing `AdminUser` fields — each is a separate backend project), while the product's richer password-create modal, Runs/Role/Joined columns, and `is_admin` gate are KEPT.
- **(e) Dark-idiom vs light-primitive split.** The shell bar (35-01), `NotificationPanel`, `TemplateDetailModal`, `DesignSystemDetailModal`, and the login brand panel (35-06) use the raw dark-surface token idiom (`bg-surface-near-black`/`ink-black` + `white/opacity` chrome + `--scrim`) established in 35-01 — the light `ui/*` primitives assume light surfaces and would break the dark theme. The light-surface modals (`CustomTemplateModal`, `CustomDesignSystemModal`) and pages (Account Settings, Library, Admin body) DO consume the primitives directly.
- **(f) SC-001 / INV-1 — no workflow-name keying introduced.** Nav, pickers, and status/tier chips are keyed on generic props/page-keys/local data (`Badge status=…`, explicit `label` overrides) — never a workflow-name branch.
- **(g) INV-3 / LOCK-B — FE-only, zero backend touched.** All 16 source files + 8 test files are `.tsx`; no backend/Python/transport/SSE file appears in any of the 7 plans' diffs; the 5 characterization goldens are untouched by construction.
- **(h) Verify-by-delta (DEF-29-06-1), not absolute-green.** 35-01 captured a pre-reskin mocked-Playwright baseline (`frontend/e2e/.baseline-35.txt`) because the offline sandbox's Next `webServer` times out (~45s/spec) before a full suite completes; later plans diff by delta against that partial baseline rather than chase absolute-green, and a clean full mocked/live e2e pass is deferred to the Phase-34 live pass.
- **(i) Zero new packages.** All 7 plans report `tech-stack.added: []` — the phase is a pure reskin of already-installed tokens/primitives/fonts/icons.

### Invariants & verification

- **SC-001 / INV-1:** `AppHeader` nav is a generic `{key,label,Icon}` list keyed on page keys; `Badge`/`Button`/`Tabs`/`Pill`/`Card` all take generic presentational props. PASS (`35-REVIEW.md`).
- **INV-3 / LOCK-B:** all touched files are frontend `.tsx`; no backend/Python/transport/SSE touched; 5 characterization goldens untouched by construction (this phase cannot perturb them by construction, being FE-only). PASS.
- **D-11:** nav relabel confirmed — `grep -c Catalogue AppHeader.tsx` = 0, file contains `"My Workflows"` + `onNavigate("saved-workflows")`; `WorkflowCatalog.tsx` still exists on disk.
- **D-15:** every wiring grep (`onSelect`, `onChange`, `getPreferences`/`updatePreferences`/`changePassword`, `adminUpdateTier`/`adminCreateUser`/`adminDeleteUser`, `is_admin`, etc.) still present post-reskin across the touched files.
- **LOCK-F:** a 14-entry fixture renders "14 design systems" with zero "150" leaks (`DesignSystemPicker.reskin.test.tsx`).
- **a11y:** profile menu + notifications bell + admin `TierDropdown` all carry `aria-haspopup="menu"` + `aria-expanded` on the trigger, `role="menu"`/`"menuitem"` on the dropdown/rows, and Escape-close+refocus via a wrapper `onKeyDown`; asserted RED-first then GREEN by 4 of the 8 new test suites.
- **No new endpoint/auth/file/schema surface** (35-04 Threat Flags: none — T-35-04-01..03/SC mitigated by construction; the Review-gates `gate_agent_ids` payload contract is byte-identical).
- **Evidence:** retired-palette grep (`#1B2A4A|#2563eb|#f5f5f0|Inter|Fraunces|JetBrains`) = 0 across all 16 touched source files; stray-stock-palette grep = 0 in touched code (the 7 `DashboardLayout.tsx` hits the verifier found are pre-existing loading/error/modal lines outside the 1-line 35-01 diff, not new). 8 new test suites / 40 tests green; `tsc --noEmit` clean (0 new errors) across every plan. The pre-existing `ReviewGatesSection.test.tsx` 3-failure delta proven at 0 (16 passed/3 failed identical before AND after the classNames-only 35-04 reskin — the failures are `AgentLibraryData` count drift unrelated to Phase 35). 26 commits (18 code/test + 8 docs) across 7 plans, all present in git history (e.g. 35-01 `ee8bec7d`+`7cd23559`+`9201560f`; 35-07 `d27a889f`+`49a356e1`). `35-VERIFICATION.md` scored 16/16 observable truths (21/21 counting per-plan must-haves), status `passed`, verified 2026-07-09T00:59:44Z. Independent deep code review (`35-REVIEW.md`, 16 files) found 0 critical/high, 1 medium, 4 low, no blockers. 6-pillar UI review (`35-UI-REVIEW.md`) scored 19/24 PASS-with-flags; computed WCAG-AA contrast passes on every dark-surface text/background pairing audited.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **Accepted, not a defect (ME-01 / NOTE-6):** `NotificationPanel`'s status chip now renders via `<Badge status={n.status}/>`, which normalizes `completed`→uppercase `"DONE"` instead of the prior title-case "Completed" — a content change hidden in a styling diff, but the UI review confirms it matches the design authority's own "normalize completed→done" mandate (evidence §B3); recorded as accepted, not a bug.
- **FLAG-1 (terminology):** the profile-menu item still reads "Workflow History"; the canonical glossary term is "Run History" (evidence 11 §B5) — Phase 35 relabelled the sibling Catalogue→My Workflows item (D-11) but missed this one. One-line fix for a follow-up (label only; page key/route unchanged).
- **FLAG-2 (status-color contradiction):** `NotificationPanel`'s `StatusIcon` maps `cancelled`→grey (`text-status-queued`) while the `Badge` on the same row correctly renders cancelled as amber — an internal contradiction against evidence §B3. Fix is a one-line `StatusIcon` branch change.
- **FLAG-3 / LW-02 (a11y model):** `NotificationPanel`'s dropdown carries `role="menu"` but its children are plain `<button>`s (not `role="menuitem"`) and the list isn't arrow-key navigable — invalid ARIA housing for what is really a notification list, not a menu. Recommended fix: drop `role="menu"`, use a list/dialog model instead.
- **FLAG-4 / LW-01 (animation regression):** `LibraryPage`'s Skill/Hook detail modals had their `motion.div` root swapped for a plain `div` but are still wrapped in `<AnimatePresence>`, so the exit fade/scale silently no-ops (functionally harmless — open/close stays state-driven; the sibling `AgentCapabilitiesModal` still animates correctly).
- **FLAG-5 / LW-03 (token consistency):** 2 `LibraryPage` modal backdrops stayed on `bg-black/40` instead of the `--scrim` token every other modal in the phase migrated to — passes the retired-hex grep, same-file inconsistency only.
- **LW-04 / NOTE-7 (primitive reuse):** the `Badge` status-ramp primitive is reused purely for color on non-status data (admin tier chips, Account Settings "current" chip) — correct output today (explicit `label` passed), but couples tier color to any future status-ramp change. Optional follow-up: a dedicated `TierChip`/tint prop. (Informational-only UI-review nits also on file: wordmark weight/size/italic drifts from the mock spec, and font sizes are ad-hoc arbitrary px values with no shared type scale — LOW-8/LOW-9, no action taken.)
- **Pre-existing test drift, not phase-caused:** `ReviewGatesSection.test.tsx` has 3 failures because `AgentLibraryData.ts` grew ahead of the test's hardcoded counts (`prototype` 4→5 agents, `all` 54→55, `prototype-analyze` now a 3rd default `Human_Gate`) — proven byte-identical before/after the 35-04 reskin; logged in `deferred-items.md` as a follow-up data-reconciliation quick task.
- **Live-deferred, not gaps (DEF-29-06-1, all owned by the Phase-34 live pass):** (1) the full mocked-Playwright suite — the offline Next `webServer` times out ~45s/spec so only a partial baseline (`frontend/e2e/.baseline-35.txt`) was captured; a clean full pass/fail is deferred. (2) Pixel-level visual sign-off of the dark shell/nav pill/login brand panel/reskinned pages — token-authority gates + the 40 component/a11y tests prove structure and token consumption; final human-visual judgment is deferred. (3) Live-backend flows (real-auth login redirect, live notifications feed) — the notifications feed itself is Phase 38 scope; the backend is untouched by construction (INV-3).
- **Explicitly out of this phase's scope (unchanged by Phase 35, per `35-CONTEXT.md`):** the `WorkflowCatalog`→`HomeLaunchGrid` rename + Home/History/My-Workflows restructure (Phase 36); Configure/Composer/Wizard (Phase 37); the notifications live feed + Analytics data (Phase 38); the admin Status/Last-active columns + email-invite flow (additive backend, deferred by ND-12); Concierge live-wiring (Phase 34).

## Phase 36 — Home + History + My Workflows [B2]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/36-home-history-my-workflows-b2/` — 5 plans (36-01..36-05) across 3 waves (Wave 1: 36-01 ∥ 36-02; Wave 2: 36-03 ∥ 36-04, blocked on Wave 1; Wave 3: 36-05, blocked on Wave 2). Branch: `feat/ui-2` @ `6eefa3f1` (working tree clean throughout verification).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-09) — verifier PASSED, 4/4 ROADMAP success criteria (12/12 supporting truths) VERIFIED; a same-day deep peer review (`36-REVIEW.md`) found 2 High + 2 Medium regressions in the detail-promotion, all fixed and re-verified before close.  ·  **Plan-ids → §36:** 36-01 (rename + fused Home), 36-02 (run-summary endpoint), 36-03 (My Workflows relabel), 36-04 (RunDetailPage + client), 36-05 (History restructure + detail promotion + reskin + post-review fixes).

**Requirements delivered:** **SHELL-02** — fused Home (launcher + deliverable grid + recents strip); History Today/Earlier/Older grouping + tokens/duration sort + real delete wiring; "My Workflows" rename + working kebab actions; `WorkflowCatalog`→`HomeLaunchGrid` rename (D-11); "Catalogue" reserved for a future marketplace. **SHELL-03** — Run detail/reopen page live off a new run-summary endpoint aggregating existing data (agents, KPIs, failure banner, version timeline).

**Driver:** Home, History, and My Workflows were three independently-evolved list surfaces still on the pre-Phase-32 palette, plus a naming collision baked into the product: the data-driven launch grid was called `WorkflowCatalog` while D-11 reserves "Catalogue" for a future shared/org marketplace, and `SavedWorkflowsPage`'s own in-page heading wrongly read "Workflow Catalogue" for the user's own saved workflows. `input` and `home` were two separate navigations for what is really one launch action. History already carried the `created_at`/`token_usage`/`duration` fields needed for date-grouping and sorting but rendered a flat list, and its terminal-run detail was a hand-rolled in-panel JSX block duplicating KPI/agent/timeline/failure surfaces rather than a dedicated page. POR §84/§95 called for these three surfaces to land on the Phase-32 token layer and gain a proper run-detail/reopen page fed by an owner-scoped aggregation endpoint — without regressing the already-shipped KAN-92 (real async titles) and KAN-96 (running-vs-terminal click branch) behaviors.

**One-line outcome:** `WorkflowCatalog` renamed to `HomeLaunchGrid` (D-11, grep 0 across 7 callers) and fused into one Home landing (prompt launcher + deliverable grid + recents strip, no new fetch); History gained Today/Earlier/Older grouping plus a Recent/Tokens/Duration sort layered over the existing family grouping, and its in-panel detail was retired in favor of mounting the new single-source `RunDetailPage`; My Workflows relabeled off "Workflow Catalogue" with its already-wired kebab CRUD now test-locked and its menu hoisted to module scope for correct a11y; a new additive, owner-scoped `GET /api/runs/{id}/summary` aggregates existing `WorkflowRun` columns (zero invented fields, zero new tables) into the KPI/agent/timeline/failure data `RunDetailPage` renders. A same-day peer review then caught and fixed a version-switch column desync (H-01), an empty-quote revision preview (H-02), a formatter dual-implementation (M-02), and lost ISS-024 raw-id-fallback coverage (M-01) — all four verified fixed before the phase closed.

### What was built

**Backend (2 files):**

- **`backend/app/api/runs.py`** — new `GET /{workflow_id}/summary` route + `RunSummaryResponse` (11 fields: id/title/type/status/duration/agent_count/token_usage/error/agents/root_id/members — mirrors the existing `WorkflowRunResponse`, zero invented fields) + `_SUMMARY_SAFE_AGENT_KEYS` allow-list projection (agent_id/name/role/icon/duration/error/token counts — raw `output`/`input_prompt`/`thinking_text`/`tool_calls` never echoed, V7) + reuse of `_owner_gate_or_404` on `WorkflowRun.user_id` (never the nullable `owner_id`) + tolerant `json.loads` try/except → `[]`/`{}` fallback for `agent_outputs`/`token_usage`. Extracted `_owned_family_members` out of `get_run_family` so `/summary` and `/family` share one owned BFS walk instead of a second copy (INV-12); `get_run_family` refactored to call it with behavior parity (8/8 family tests stayed green). Post-review H-02 fix added an additive `input` field — the owner's own `run.input`, never a child agent's output — so the version-timeline instruction preview has real text.
- **`backend/tests/unit/test_runs_api_summary.py`** — new 8-test suite: owner 200, cross-owner 404, missing 404, malformed/wrong-shape JSON fallback, raw-agent-content-never-echoed, revision-family timeline, foreign-parent walk termination (no leak).

**Frontend (13 source files + 8 test files, across the 5 plans):**

- **Rename (D-11, Wave 1):** `frontend/src/components/catalog/WorkflowCatalog.tsx`/`.test.tsx` → `HomeLaunchGrid.tsx`/`.test.tsx` via `git mv` — symbol + `HomeLaunchGridProps` renamed, all 7 callers repointed, `grep -rc WorkflowCatalog frontend/src == 0`. Kept in `catalog/` (a dev-facing path, not a user label — does not violate D-11) to minimize mock-path churn. Reskinned onto Phase-32 tokens (`#f5f5f0`→`bg-surface-paper`, `#1B2A4A`→`text-brand`, dead `var(--font-fraunces)`→`font-serif`).
- **Fused Home (Wave 1):** `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx` — repointed the import; the `home` view now composes a prompt launcher (folded from the `input` idiom) + the `HomeLaunchGrid` deliverable grid + a recents strip off the already-threaded `recentRuns` prop (no new fetch); a `pendingHomeBrief`→`initialInput` pass-through carries a typed brief into `input`; the wizard fork and the `input` view (kept alive for the saved-workflow preload path, `handleLaunchSaved`) are both preserved.
- **`DashboardLayout.catalogHome.test.tsx`, `DashboardLayout.waveMount.test.tsx`** — `vi.mock` module path + stub export key repointed in lockstep with the rename (the trap: a stale mock path silently mounts the real component and fails on `getToken()` in jsdom).
- **My Workflows (Wave 2):** `frontend/src/components/savedworkflows/SavedWorkflowsPage.tsx` — in-page `<h1>` "Workflow Catalogue"→"My Workflows" (D-11) + delete-confirm copy updated to "saved workflows"; full Phase-32 token reskin; `KebabMenu` hoisted from the render body to module scope (a Rule-1 a11y deviation — the render-body definition remounted on every `openMenuId` change, breaking `aria-expanded` and the Escape-refocus ref).
- **`SavedWorkflowsPage.test.tsx`** (new, closes a Wave-0 gap) — 5 tests locking the label, the already-real kebab CRUD (Rename→`renameUserWorkflow`, Duplicate→`createUserWorkflow`, Delete→`deleteUserWorkflow`, each with optimistic list mutation), and kebab a11y (aria-haspopup/expanded, role=menu/menuitem, Escape close+refocus).
- **Run detail page (Wave 2):** `frontend/src/lib/api.ts` — `getRunSummary(token, runId)` client + `RunSummary`/`RunSummaryAgent` types mirroring `RunSummaryResponse` field-for-field (11/11, no extra/missing key).
- **`frontend/src/lib/runStats.ts`** (new) — single-source `formatDuration`/`formatTokenCount` formatters.
- **`frontend/src/components/history/RunDetailPage.tsx`/`.test.tsx`** (new) — the run detail/reopen page: cancellable mount-fetch off `getRunSummary`, KPI strip + per-agent cards + reused `VersionTimeline` (from `RevisionFamilyView`) + reused `DegradedRunAffordance` (from `PreviewPanel`) + reused `parseFailedAgentIds`/`buildAgentNameById`, generic-keyed on status/agents/tokens (never `summary.type`, SC-001). Post-review gained an `onSelectVersion`/`onVersionChange` prop (H-01 fix) so a version-chip click re-syncs both its own summary and the caller's deliverable column, plus a non-empty-guard on the revision-preview quote (H-02 fix).
- **History restructure (Wave 3):** `frontend/src/components/history/RevisionFamilyView.tsx` — new `bucketAndSortFamilies`/`dateBucketOf`/`HistorySortKey` grouping+sort helpers; reskinned family cards/status dots/StatusBadge/RowMenu/VersionTimeline chips onto tokens; post-review M-02 fix deleted its byte-identical duplicate `formatDuration`, importing the single source from `runStats.ts`.
- **`frontend/src/components/history/WorkflowHistory.tsx`** — Today/Earlier/Older date buckets + Recent/Tokens/Duration sort layered over `groupRunsByFamily` (client-side over `created_at`/`token_usage`/`duration` already on each row, no new fetch); the in-panel KPI/agent/timeline/failure JSX and its local `formatDuration`/`formatDate` were retired in favor of mounting `<RunDetailPage>` for the summary column (a two-column detail wrapper: `RunDetailPage` owns the summary, the right column keeps the deliverable/Files/Thinking/Audit tabs + revise/chain that the summary endpoint doesn't cover); the KAN-96 click-branch (`handleSelectRun`) and KAN-92 real-title rendering (`run.title`) preserved verbatim; real delete wiring (`deleteWorkflow` + optimistic filter) confirmed intact.
- **`WorkflowHistory.grouping.test.tsx`** (new) + the 5 pre-existing history test files (`.test`/`.family`/`.genericReopen`/`.revise`/`.runInput.test.tsx`) — updated to mock `getRunSummary` (opening detail now mounts `RunDetailPage`); family/failure-affordance assertions relocated to `RunDetailPage.test.tsx`'s unit matrix, where post-review M-01 restored the ISS-024 raw-id-fallback case the promotion had dropped.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Backend endpoint + tests:** `backend/app/api/runs.py` (`get_run_summary`, `RunSummaryResponse`, `_owned_family_members`, `_SUMMARY_SAFE_AGENT_KEYS`), `backend/tests/unit/test_runs_api_summary.py`
- **Rename + fused Home:** `frontend/src/components/catalog/HomeLaunchGrid.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`), `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx` (+ `.catalogHome.test.tsx`, `.waveMount.test.tsx`)
- **My Workflows:** `frontend/src/components/savedworkflows/SavedWorkflowsPage.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`)
- **Run detail page + client:** `frontend/src/components/history/RunDetailPage.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`), `frontend/src/lib/api.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/runStats.ts`
- **History restructure:** `frontend/src/components/history/WorkflowHistory.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/history/RevisionFamilyView.tsx` (+ 6 history test files, incl. new `WorkflowHistory.grouping.test.tsx`)

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) D-11 rename is final.** `WorkflowCatalog`→`HomeLaunchGrid` (component + `HomeLaunchGridProps`); "Catalogue" stays reserved for a future shared/org marketplace and must never again label a user's own saved workflows. The component living under `catalog/` is a dev-facing path, not a user-facing label — D-11 governs the latter only.
- **(b) The `input` view is fused-INTO Home, not deleted.** It must stay reachable for the saved-workflow preload path (`handleLaunchSaved` → `pendingHomeBrief`→`initialInput`); deleting it would break the preload flow (INV-3).
- **(c) KAN-96 and KAN-92 are shipped behaviors, preserved verbatim.** KAN-96: a running run click → `onViewRunningPipeline()` (live view); a terminal run click → the detail page. KAN-92: rendered titles come from the real async `run.title`, never a synthesized `Revision:` placeholder.
- **(d) The run-summary endpoint aggregates ONLY existing `WorkflowRun` columns.** Zero invented fields, zero new tables/migrations (Q3); owner-scoped on `WorkflowRun.user_id` (never the nullable `owner_id`); the per-agent projection is a fixed safe-key allow-list — raw output/input_prompt/thinking_text/tool_calls never echoed (V7).
- **(e) Reskin-look, keep-behavior (D-15) — reuse, don't rebuild.** `VersionTimeline`, `DegradedRunAffordance`, `parseFailedAgentIds`/`buildAgentNameById`, and `formatDuration`/`formatTokenCount` are imported, not re-implemented (INV-12); the in-panel hand-rolled detail in `WorkflowHistory` is retired now that `RunDetailPage` is the single source.
- **(f) SC-001/INV-1 — generic-keyed rendering only.** Every branch renders off generic run data (`status`/`agents`/`token_usage`), never a workflow-name/type literal; `MainView` page-keys stay generic (`home`/`input`/`history`/`saved-workflows`), no workflow-name-keyed view was added.

### Invariants & verification

- **SC-001:** `RunDetailPage` + `WorkflowHistory` branch only on status/agents/tokens; `MainView` page-keys stay generic. VERIFIED by the independent verifier (grep + code read).
- **INV-3:** the run-summary endpoint is additive/read-only; the 5 characterization goldens stayed byte-identical — 10/10 pass with `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset, git tree clean after the run.
- **INV-12 (no dual impl):** `_owned_family_members` shared by `/summary` and `/family`; the in-panel KPI/agent/timeline/failure JSX retired from `WorkflowHistory`; `RevisionFamilyView`'s duplicate `formatDuration` deleted post-review (M-02) — single source now `src/lib/runStats.ts`.
- **Owner-scoping / IDOR (V4/V7):** `_owner_gate_or_404` on `WorkflowRun.user_id`; cross-owner and missing runs both resolve to 404 (never 403, never a foreign-data 200); `_SUMMARY_SAFE_AGENT_KEYS` projection proven by a dedicated raw-content-never-echoed test.
- **Token gate:** the retired palette (`#1B2A4A`/`#2563eb`/`#f5f5f0`/Fraunces/JetBrains/Inter-as-font) = 0 across all 8 touched FE files.
- **LOCK-B:** no transport file touched (`useWebSocket`/`useRunStream`/layout untouched); import-linter stayed 4 kept / 0 broken.
- **Evidence (independently re-run by the verifier, SUMMARY claims not trusted):** `test_runs_api_summary.py` + `test_runs_api_family.py` 16/16 pass; the 5 characterization goldens 10/10 pass; frontend history suite 36/36 pass (7 files); `SavedWorkflowsPage.test.tsx` 5/5; `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; `lint-imports` 4 kept/0 broken.

Recommend retargeting/removing them now that the replacement test file exists. Logged in `deferred-items.md`.

## Phase 37 — Configure Unification + Composer/Wizard [B3]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/37-configure-unification-composer-wizard-b3/` — 6 GSD plans (37-01..37-06, 3 waves: Wave 1 = 37-01 alone [the seam must land first], Wave 2 = 37-02/03/04, Wave 3 = 37-05/06) plus a flagship, unplanned 7th build (37-07 — no PLAN.md, a direct build+SUMMARY born from the first review's WR-01 finding). Branch: `feat/ui-2` (milestone v2.0).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-10) — verifier PASSED both times it ran: the pre-37-07 pass (HEAD `df679823`) was "passed" but carried 2 open review WARNINGS (WR-01/02 unmounted surfaces, an untracked FIXME); the RE-VERIFY (HEAD `6167b302`, after 37-07 + all review fixes) closed both and scored 3/3 roadmap SC + 23 base plan truths + 8 37-07 unification truths, all VERIFIED, 0 gaps.  ·  **Plan-ids → §37:** 37-01..37-06 [B3] + 37-07 (flagship rebuild, unplanned).

**Requirements delivered:** SHELL-04 — a generic Configure surface (Describe/Templates/Design System/Review Gates/Workflow Settings accordions) for every deliverable type, a 4-tab Agent drawer, and a Workflow dialog, all gated on declared `context_providers`/`user_allowed` signals (ND-1/ND-7/ND-8).

**Driver:** Template and design-system selection were hardwired to the `prototype`/`od_ppt` wizard only — the run-launch boundary decided whether to load them by branching on workflow-name literals, the SC-001/INV-1 leak D-15/C targets. Separately, the composer/wizard mock (evidence 08) that was meant to guide a rebuild was stale — it hardcoded 8 capabilities, modeled retry as a bool, and dropped the validator→gate coupling — so a face-value rebuild would have regressed already-shipped Phase-22 behavior; the phase had to reuse the live surfaces instead of rebuilding from the mock.

**One-line outcome:** A new app-side seam, `launch_context.py::resolve_launch_od_context`, promotes template/design-system context from a `pipeline_type=="od_prototype"/"od_ppt"` name-branch to a declared-signal check (manifest declares `context_providers:[opendesign]` and isn't a legacy `od_`-alias base) called from both the REST and WS launch boundaries — byte-identical for the 3 manifests that already declare it, open by declaration to any future deliverable. Five reused/new FE surfaces (ConfigureScreen, WizardStepper, a DS band-card grid, a 4-tab Agent drawer, WorkflowDialog) reskin onto Phase-32 tokens without dropping any P22 contract (COUPLED_GATE, `RETRY_OPTIONS=[1,2,3]`, live `/api/capabilities`, `user_allowed`). A flagship 7th plan (37-07) then unifies the prototype+ppt route-split pages into one mode-keyed `LaunchWizard` at `/workflow/create`, deleting both legacy pages behind a launch-contract proof byte-identical to the retired flow per mode (25 tests). Two code-review rounds (ISS-046: 0 critical/7 warning/7 info; ISS-047: 0 critical/3 warning/4 info) found unmounted surfaces, a stale model-override save bug, a frozen-chrome toggle bug, and several undertested branches — all closed across 13 follow-up commits.

### What was built

**Backend (4 files: 1 created, 3 modified):**

- `backend/app/api/launch_context.py` (created, Plan 01) — `resolve_launch_od_context(pipeline_type, template_id, design_system_id, *, custom_ds_body, custom_template_body, fatal)`, the shared declared-signal seam. Eligibility = the resolved manifest's `context_providers` includes `"opendesign"` AND `pipeline_type not in _OD_ALIAS_BASE.values()` — the second clause excludes bare `prototype` (whose OD flavor is requested via the dedicated `od_prototype` alias), byte-preserving the legacy split without any name literal. Dispatches to `load_prototype_od_context` or `load_ppt_od_context` by resolved base family, reproducing the old per-branch argument shaping verbatim; a `fatal` flag chooses whether a `LookupError` propagates or is swallowed to `None`.
- `backend/app/api/run_commands.py` — `_resolve_launch_agents` (REST) rewired to call the seam (`fatal=True`); the old name-branch deleted; `od_ppt_revision` kept an explicit `→ None` arm (REST never loaded it pre-phase).
- `backend/app/api/websocket.py` — `_handle_workflow_execution` (WS) rewired to the same seam; `od_ppt_revision` kept its non-fatal arm (`fatal=False`, loads only if `template_id` present).
- `backend/tests/unit/test_rest_run_launch.py` (+170 lines) — new od_context dict-equality parity cases (`od_prototype`/`od_ppt` reproduce the exact pre-seam dict), undeclared-deliverable→`None`, unknown-type rejection, REST `od_ppt_revision`→`None`.

**Frontend — created (18 files, ~3.9k lines):**

- `frontend/src/lib/draft.ts` (+ test) — ND-1 client-side draft: one keyed `configure.draft` sessionStorage blob (save/read/clear, fail-soft on malformed JSON), zero fetch/`/api` calls — a sibling of the pre-existing `prototype.draft`/`ppt.draft`/`chain.*` keys, not a replacement.
- `frontend/src/components/workflow/ConfigureScreen.tsx` (+ test) and its route `frontend/src/app/workflow/configure/page.tsx` — the generic per-run setup surface: five accordions (Describe/Templates/Design System/Review Gates/Workflow Settings) for ANY deliverable; Templates+DS render only when `WorkflowDetail.context_providers` includes `opendesign` (the same declared signal the backend seam keys on); composes a generic `ComposedLaunchCommand`. Reachable at its URL but not linked from any nav entry point — see Known follow-ups.
- `frontend/src/components/workflow/WizardStepper.tsx` (+ test) — the phase's one genuinely new chrome component: a 3-step Template→Design System→Discovery stepper with a Web/Deck toggle (a `mode` data value, never a workflow-name branch) and typed `dsSlot`/`discoverySlot` render-slot injection points; wraps the reused `TemplateGallery`/`PPTTemplateGallery` bodies unchanged (chrome-only, reuse-not-rebuild).
- `frontend/src/components/workflow/WorkflowDialog.tsx` (+ test) — read-only compiled-workflow inspector surfacing declared `context_providers`, deduped per-step gates+validators resolved against `/api/capabilities` for the `user_allowed` "Engineer-only" lock, and compaction — pure reflection of declared data (INV-5), no workflow-name branch. Mounted via `frontend/src/components/catalog/HomeLaunchGrid.tsx`'s row-level Inspect affordance (pinned by the new `HomeLaunchGrid.inspect.test.tsx`).
- `frontend/src/components/workflow/LaunchWizard.tsx` (821 lines, + `LaunchWizard.test.tsx` 360 lines) and its route `frontend/src/app/workflow/create/page.tsx` (37-07) — both prototype and ppt launch flows unified into one mode-keyed page (`?mode=prototype|ppt`, data-table `MODE_CONFIG`), hosting the imported `WizardStepper` chrome (a real mount at `LaunchWizard.tsx:18,677`, not a reimplementation) plus brief input, review gates, the reused `AgentsPopup` composer, save-workflow, and launch. All 13 previously-live behaviors ported and test-covered (brief input, file-attach+text-extraction, out-of-band images, speech-to-text, example-prompt, chaining, custom-template/DS modals, blank-canvas, review gates, composer reuse, save-workflow, draft restore/clear).
- `frontend/src/lib/launchDraft.ts` (+ `launchDraft.parity.test.ts`) and `frontend/src/components/workflow/__fixtures__/launchContract.ts` (37-07) — the extracted single-source launch-contract serialization (`buildLaunchDraft`/`buildDiscoveryValue`), proven byte-identical to the retired pages' `handleContinue` output via frozen golden fixtures (12 parity assertions × both modes) plus a real-component parity suite (13 `LaunchWizard.test.tsx` assertions), both cross-checked against the actual pre-retirement implementation at git `7a84f6bc`.
- The 4-tab Agent drawer (Overview/Skills/Hooks/Config) is NOT a new file — `AgentCapabilitiesModal` inside `AgentsPopup.tsx` was restructured via the shared `ui/Tabs` primitive, pinned by the new `AgentDrawer.test.tsx`. The Config tab's `AgentPromptSection` gained a `surfaceOnly` prop that omits Edit/Save-override/Revert affordances — ND-7/LOCK-E enforced by making the durable PUT/DELETE override path structurally unreachable from the drawer, not merely untriggered.

**Frontend — modified (12 files) and deleted (3 files, final state):**

- `frontend/src/components/workflow/AgentsPopup.tsx` / `AgentLibrary.tsx` — Phase-32 token reskin (every retired-palette hit migrated to 0) plus the footer "Save workflow" wired to owner-scoped `createUserWorkflow` (`{name, description?, base_pipeline_type, agent_ids, selections}` — `model_overrides` deliberately dropped from the payload, see Key locked decisions (c)); `prototype/DesignSystemPicker.tsx` (+ reskin test) restructured from a rounded-pill chip list into a live-registry swatch band-card grid (real ~14 systems, LOCK-F/ND-8), each card lazily fetching swatches via the same `getDesignSystem`+`extractPalette` path `DesignSystemDetailModal` uses.

- `frontend/src/lib/prototype-api.ts` — dead `getPrototypeTemplate`/`PrototypeTemplateDetail` removed alongside the discovery-route retirement.
- Deleted (INV-12, final state — landed in two waves): `frontend/src/app/workflow/prototype/templates/page.tsx` (768 lines) and `frontend/src/app/workflow/ppt/templates/page.tsx` (731 lines), retired by 37-07 once `LaunchWizard` reproduced their contract; `frontend/src/app/workflow/prototype/discovery/page.tsx` (249 lines), retired in the later ISS-047 fix wave after 37-07's inline discovery step (`templateInputs={[]}`) made the standalone route unreachable dead code.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- Backend launch seam: `backend/app/api/launch_context.py`; REST boundary: `run_commands.py::_resolve_launch_agents`; WS boundary: `websocket.py::_handle_workflow_execution`.
- Unified launch page (prototype+ppt): `frontend/src/components/workflow/LaunchWizard.tsx`, route `frontend/src/app/workflow/create/page.tsx`.
- Generic Configure surface (any deliverable): `frontend/src/components/workflow/ConfigureScreen.tsx`, route `frontend/src/app/workflow/configure/page.tsx`.
- Stepper chrome: `frontend/src/components/workflow/WizardStepper.tsx` (imported by `LaunchWizard.tsx`).
- Launch-contract single source: `frontend/src/lib/launchDraft.ts`; client-side draft: `frontend/src/lib/draft.ts`.
- Composer + 4-tab Agent drawer: `frontend/src/components/workflow/AgentsPopup.tsx`.
- Workflow dialog: `frontend/src/components/workflow/WorkflowDialog.tsx`, mounted from `frontend/src/components/catalog/HomeLaunchGrid.tsx`.
- DS band-card grid: `frontend/src/components/workflow/prototype/DesignSystemPicker.tsx`.

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) D-15/C eligibility discriminator.** Declared `opendesign` AND not a legacy `od_`-alias base (`_OD_ALIAS_BASE.values()`, single source of truth) — no per-workflow literal (SC-001/INV-1). A brand-new deliverable that declares `opendesign` with no legacy alias opts in simply by being launched under its own name; this is the D-15/C win.
- **(b) Loader-profile mapping (prototype vs od_ppt-family argument shaping) kept as a documented compatibility shim, NOT generalized this phase** — deferred (RESEARCH Open Question 2); byte-safe today because a grep-audit found only 3 manifests (`prototype`, `od_ppt`, `od_ppt_revision`) declare `opendesign`.
- **(c) `AgentModelPicker.tsx` (standalone) stays UNMOUNTED — confirmed dead code, not re-mounted.** The inline live-catalog Model lever (`AdvancedExpander`, threads `selections[id].model`) already satisfies per-agent model selection; re-mounting the standalone picker would be an INV-3 dual-implementation. This reverses a stale literal in the original 37-CONTEXT instruction, amended 2026-07-09 against verified code. Consequently Save-to-catalogue emits `selections.model` only — `model_overrides` was dropped from the save payload (WR-03) and the vestigial `modelOverrides` launch-draft mirror was later removed too (ISS-047 IN-04).
- **(d) ND-1 draft persistence is client-side sessionStorage ONLY** (`draft.ts`'s `configure.draft` key) — no DB table, no migration; distinct from the pre-existing `prototype.draft`/`ppt.draft`/`chain.*` keys.
- **(e) ND-7 (LOCK-E) — the Agent drawer Config tab surfaces per-agent prompt-override; persistence stays deferred**, enforced structurally (the `surfaceOnly` prop removes the write affordances entirely) rather than by convention.
- **(f) ND-12 declared OUT this phase:** workflow visibility/team-sharing, pre-run cost/duration estimates, discovery page-selection. Owner-only `createUserWorkflow`; no visibility field anywhere in the diff.
- **(g) SC-001 for the Web/Deck toggle:** `LaunchWizard` keys the deliverable family on the generic `mode` value via a `MODE_CONFIG` data table — never a workflow-name branch.

Final full `vitest run`: 557+ passed; the ~10 failures are pre-existing and unrelated (ISS-045), zero phase-37 regressions.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

## Phase 38 — Analytics, Estimates & Notifications [B4]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/38-analytics-estimates-notifications-b4/` (milestone **v2.0**, branch `feat/ui-2`).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-10) — `38-VERIFICATION.md` verdict **PASS**, 11/11 must-haves verified (SC-1, SC-2 + invariants a–i), 0 FAIL/0 PARTIAL, offline-by-delta, commit range `991f6f1c..2227f241`; a deep code review then found 0 CRITICAL/0 HIGH + 3 MEDIUM/3 LOW/1 NIT, logged same-day as **ISS-048** and triaged in a post-close polish sweep (2 of 3 MEDIUMs fixed, 1 left as-specified — see Known follow-ups).  ·  **Plans:** 5/5 (3 waves: 38-01/02/03 → 38-04 → 38-05)  ·  **Plan-id → `CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md` (milestone v2.0 POR):** B4 (brief at line 88; the analytics-endpoint contract clause at line 96).

**Requirements delivered:** **SHELL-05** — "Date-scoped analytics aggregations + chart components + per-deliverable estimates + notifications feed" (`REQUIREMENTS.md:252`, traceability `:474`, this phase's sole REQ-ID) — realizing ROADMAP SC-1 (date-scoped analytics aggregations power the dashboard; filters actually **RECOMPUTE**) and SC-2 (Home deliverable cards show real time/agent estimates; notifications feed live for gate/running/done/failed kinds). Depends on Phase 35 (the shell + `NotificationPanel` chrome); independent of Phases 36/37. This closed the **last offline v2.0 phase** before the Phase-34 live pass (10/11 non-live phases now offline-complete).

**Driver:** Same-session research overturned the evidence docs' "static snapshot" framing: `AnalyticsPage.tsx` was already fully data-driven, but entirely **client-side** — it downloaded up to 500 raw `WorkflowRun` rows via `getWorkflows(limit:500)` and rolled up every KPI/chart/spend figure in the browser, wearing the retired pre-Phase-32 navy palette (100 grep hits). Separately, `useNotifications.ts` exposed `markFailed`/`markCancelled`, and `DashboardLayout.tsx` destructured both — but neither was ever called (only `markCompleted` fired), and no `"gate"` kind existed at all, so failed/cancelled/gated runs produced zero feed entries. Home deliverable cards showed no estimate whatsoever (the mock's hardcoded "~24m · 5 agents" was never wired to anything real).

**One-line outcome:** A new owner-scoped, additive read-only `GET /api/analytics/summary?range=<enum>` aggregation endpoint replaces the client-side 500-row reduce and drives a full Phase-32 reskin of `AnalyticsPage.tsx` behind two newly-extracted, token-styled, a11y-labelled SVG chart primitives (`DonutChart`/`BarChart`, still zero external chart library); `useNotifications` gains a `"gate"` kind + `markGatePaused`/`markGateResumed`, and `DashboardLayout` now actually calls `markFailed`/`markCancelled`/gate transitions off generic `pipelineState` markers; `HomeLaunchGrid` cards render a real `~N agents · ~Xm` estimate from `step_count` plus the endpoint's `type_avg_duration_sec` history, omitting the time clause rather than fabricating it. Zero new tables/migrations, zero engine/transport touch, 5 characterization goldens byte-identical throughout (INV-3/LOCK-B), zero workflow-name branches introduced (SC-001/INV-1).

### What was built

**Backend (3 files: 2 created + 1 modified — plan 38-01):**

- **`backend/app/api/analytics.py`** (NEW) — `GET /api/analytics/summary` router returning an `AnalyticsSummary` Pydantic response (`kpis`/`daily`/`pipelines`/`models`/`spend`/`token_totals`/`type_avg_duration_sec`); owner-scoped on `WorkflowRun.user_id == current_user.id` (never the nullable `owner_id`); `_cutoff_for` range allow-list (`today`/`3d`/`7d`/`30d`/`90d`/`all`, any unknown value defaults to 30d rather than erroring); `_aggregate` pure roll-up keyed on the generic `type`/`status`/`model_id` columns (SC-001/INV-1). `token_usage` is parsed per-run in a try/except → `{}` fallback (DoS guard mirroring `runs.py`); daily buckets accumulate as plain dicts (not attribute access) to stay off the numbers-only `.output` leak-grep; spend is `Σ estimated_cost_usd` read straight from the persisted blob, not re-derived pricing math.
- **`backend/app/main.py`** — registered `analytics_router` beside `runs_router`.
- **`backend/tests/unit/test_analytics_api.py`** (NEW) — owner-isolation (two seeded users, cross-owner totals never leak), recompute-correctness, malformed-`token_usage` (200 not 500), and range-scoping tests (4 tests).

**Frontend (9 files: 2 created + 7 modified — plans 38-02 through 38-05, plus new/extended test files):**

- **`frontend/src/components/analytics/charts/DonutChart.tsx`** + **`BarChart.tsx`** (NEW, plan 38-02) — hand-rolled inline-SVG/flex chart primitives extracted from `AnalyticsPage`'s pre-existing inline copies; `role="img"` + a data-summarizing `aria-label`; sweep/bars default to one-chroma `var(--brand)`, with an optional `status` prop switching a segment onto the `--status-*` ramp **only** for genuine run-status data (done/failed) — the governance one-chroma carve-out. Their `.test.tsx` files pin render + a11y behavior (RED-first).
- **`frontend/src/lib/api.ts`** (plan 38-04) — `getAnalyticsSummary(token, range)` client + `AnalyticsSummary` TS type mirroring the backend Pydantic model field-for-field; reused unmodified by both `AnalyticsPage` and `HomeLaunchGrid`.
- **`frontend/src/components/analytics/AnalyticsPage.tsx`** (plan 38-04) — rewired off `getWorkflows(limit:500)` + `useMemo` rollups onto a `getAnalyticsSummary(token, dateFilter)` fetch-shell (cancelled-guard + `getToken` fallback + loading/error/finally) keyed on `dateFilter` — the **only** server-refetch trigger (SC-1); pipeline/model filters narrow the already-fetched `pipelines[]`/`models[]` arrays client-side. Deleted the inline `BarChart` function and inline donut `<svg>` in favour of the imported extracted primitives (INV-3, no duplicate chart implementation left behind). Full token reskin (retired-palette grep 100 hits pre-phase → 0 post-phase). The Recent-Runs list was removed (the endpoint returns numbers only, no raw rows) and replaced with a By-Model breakdown bound to `models[]`. The `AnimatedNumber` counter was rewritten off `setInterval` onto `requestAnimationFrame` to dodge a false-positive match on the retired-`Inter`-font grep.
- **`frontend/src/hooks/useNotifications.ts`** (plan 38-03, extended post-review) — `PipelineNotification.status` gains a `"gate"` kind; new `markGatePaused(id)` (sets `status:"gate"`, `read:false`, no `completedAt`, otherwise mirrors `markFailed`). A same-day post-close fix (ISS-048 MD-1) added the inverse `markGateResumed(id)`, guarded on `status==="gate"` so it never clobbers a terminal mark.
- **`frontend/src/components/ui/NotificationPanel.tsx`** (plan 38-03) — `StatusIcon` gains an amber `PauseCircle` branch for the `gate` kind (`text-status-amber`).
- **`frontend/src/components/ui/Badge.tsx`** (plan 38-03, in-scope extension) — `gate` key added to the amber ramp; `normalizeStatus` maps `gate`/`review`/`paused`.
- **`frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`** (plan 38-03, extended post-review) — the terminal effect now actually **calls** `markFailed`/`markCancelled` off the pre-existing generic `pipelineState.failed`/`.cancelled` markers (previously destructured but dead); a new effect fires `markGatePaused` off `reviewGateData && pipelineState.isRunning` (the same condition driving `runLaneState==="gate"`). The same post-close fix (ISS-048 MD-1) added the symmetric `markGateResumed` call so the notification reverts when the gate resolves.
- **`frontend/src/components/catalog/HomeLaunchGrid.tsx`** (plan 38-05) — a second, cancelled-guarded `getAnalyticsSummary(jwt, "all")` fetch added to the existing mount effect (tolerant `.catch` → empty map so the grid still renders agents-only if analytics is unavailable); each card renders `~{agents} agents` always (from `step_count`, with a safe-cast `agent_count` fallback) and ` · ~{minutes}m` **only** when `type_avg_duration_sec[row.id]` has a history entry — the time clause is omitted, never fabricated, otherwise.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Analytics endpoint:** `backend/app/api/analytics.py`, registered in `backend/app/main.py`
- **Analytics endpoint tests:** `backend/tests/unit/test_analytics_api.py`
- **Chart primitives:** `frontend/src/components/analytics/charts/DonutChart.tsx`, `BarChart.tsx` (+ a `.test.tsx` each)
- **Analytics FE client:** `frontend/src/lib/api.ts` — `getAnalyticsSummary`, `AnalyticsSummary` type
- **Analytics page:** `frontend/src/components/analytics/AnalyticsPage.tsx` (+ `AnalyticsPage.test.tsx`)
- **Notifications hook + kind:** `frontend/src/hooks/useNotifications.ts` (+ `useNotifications.test.tsx`)
- **Notifications chrome:** `frontend/src/components/ui/NotificationPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ui/Badge.tsx`
- **Notifications wiring:** `frontend/src/components/layout/DashboardLayout.tsx`
- **Home estimates:** `frontend/src/components/catalog/HomeLaunchGrid.tsx` (+ `.test.tsx`, `.inspect.test.tsx`)

### Key locked decisions (do NOT contradict)

- **(a) Owner-scope on `WorkflowRun.user_id`, never the nullable `owner_id` — this is a collection endpoint, not a `{id}` drill-down.** `/api/analytics/summary` has no path id, so its owner-scoping is the `WHERE user_id == current_user.id` collection filter (the `list_runs` idiom), not the `_owner_gate_or_404` 404-IDOR idiom; the latter only applies if a future per-run/per-pipeline drill-down variant is added.
- **(b) Additive read-only, NO new table, NO migration (Q3).** The endpoint aggregates existing `WorkflowRun` columns (`type`/`status`/`duration`/`token_usage`/`created_at`) in Python; confirmed no `CREATE TABLE`/alembic revision anywhere in the phase's diff.
- **(c) Charts stay hand-rolled SVG — no chart library, ever.** `DonutChart`/`BarChart` are an extraction of copies `AnalyticsPage.tsx` already had inline, not a new build; adding recharts/chart.js/visx/nivo is a locked-decision violation. One-chroma `var(--brand)` for all non-status data; the `--status-*` ramp is reserved for genuine run-status data only.
- **(d) SC-1 recompute is date-filter-only.** Only a date-range change re-queries the server; pipeline/model filters narrow the already-fetched `pipelines[]`/`models[]` arrays client-side — sanctioned negative-space, not a gap.
- **(e) Notifications stay session-scoped client state — LOCK-B holds.** The feed derives purely from in-memory `pipelineState`; zero SSE/WS/transport code was added or touched. It does not survive a page reload by design; live PUSH confirmation is explicitly Phase 34's job.
- **(f) Estimates never fabricate.** `~N agents` always renders (from `step_count`, always available); `~Xm` renders only when `type_avg_duration_sec[row.id]` has a real history entry, else the clause is omitted — no hardcoded `24m`/`5 agents` literal survived from the mock.
- **(g) SC-001/INV-1 — generic keys only.** Every new surface (rollups, estimate line, notification transitions) keys on generic `type`/`status`/`model_id`/`row.id`; `PIPELINE_LABELS`/`MODEL_META`/`WORKFLOW_LABELS` remain display-only lookup maps, never control flow.

### Invariants & verification

- **SC-1:** `analytics.py:268` filters on `WorkflowRun.user_id == current_user.id`; `AnalyticsPage.tsx`'s `useEffect` is keyed `[dateFilter]` and calls `getAnalyticsSummary`, re-rendering KPIs from the new payload — a genuine server re-query, not a client re-filter.
- **SC-2:** `useNotifications`' gate/failed/cancelled kinds are wired **and called** in `DashboardLayout`; `HomeLaunchGrid` DOM renders `~3 agents · ~5m` (history case) / `~5 agents` (no-history case) under test.
- **INV-3:** the 5 characterization goldens stayed byte/event-identical throughout (`10 passed`, baseline `10`, `SNAPSHOT_UPDATE` unset); `tsc --noEmit` held identity (0 new errors vs. a 0-error baseline); no new table/migration anywhere in the diff.
- **Import boundary:** `lint-imports` → `4 kept, 0 broken` (baseline 4/0).
- **Offline test suites, all green:** 4 analytics-endpoint tests; 6 chart-primitive tests (2 files); 4 `useNotifications` tests + 4 `NotificationPanel` a11y tests; 9 `AnalyticsPage` tests (3 files); 2 new `HomeLaunchGrid` estimate tests.
- **`38-VERIFICATION.md`:** PASS, 11/11 must-haves (SC-1, SC-2 + invariants a–i), 0 FAIL/0 PARTIAL, offline-by-delta, commit range `991f6f1c..2227f241`.
- **`38-REVIEW.md`:** deep, 10 files reviewed, 0 CRITICAL/0 HIGH, 3 MEDIUM/3 LOW/1 NIT; the security-critical surface (owner-scope, `Depends(get_current_user)` auth gate, numbers-only `response_model`, tolerant `token_usage` parse, `range` allow-list) was confirmed clean with **zero** findings.

### Known follow-ups (out of scope)

- **2 of 3 code-review MEDIUMs fixed same-day post-close (ISS-048); 1 left intentionally as-specified.** MD-01 (the gate notification never reverted to `"running"` after the review gate resolved — a resumed run stayed stuck showing "paused") was fixed by adding `markGateResumed` (commit `ce0ac16a`). MD-02 (the "Avg / Run" KPI divided an all-run token numerator by a completed-run denominator, with a mismatched label) was fixed by switching the denominator to `totalCount` and relabeling to "tokens per run" (commit `352d6614`). MD-03 (the backend's `type_avg_duration_sec` is keyed on the raw persisted `run.type`, e.g. `od_prototype`, while `HomeLaunchGrid` looks it up by the catalog `row.id`, e.g. `prototype`, so the "~Xm" clause silently never renders for od_/revision variants) was deliberately left **as-specified**: the tolerant agents-only fallback means SC-2 still holds without it; aligning the key space is logged as an optional future enhancement, not a defect.
- **3 LOW + 1 NIT findings from `38-REVIEW.md` were not individually fixed or ISS-tracked.** An aware-UTC cutoff compared against the timezone-naive `created_at` column (a latent boundary-row hazard outside a UTC DB session); `markGatePaused` re-clearing `read:false` on every `reviewGateData` identity change (can re-surface an already-read notification's unread badge if the gate payload streams/updates mid-gate); `success_rate` counting non-terminal (`running`) runs in its denominator (can read artificially low mid-run — a defensible but undocumented product choice); sub-30-second history averages rendering "~0m" instead of omitting the clause.
- **3 items explicitly LIVE-DEFERRED to Phase 34:** live notification PUSH over the real connection (the derivation logic is built and unit-tested offline; only the live push is connection-dependent); a live `/api/analytics/summary` round-trip against a running server; visual/Phase-32-fidelity verification plus the full mocked-Playwright e2e regression (mocked Playwright times out offline).
- **Two pre-existing test reds, confirmed NOT caused by Phase 38, left untouched per negative-space discipline.** `HomeLaunchGrid.test.tsx`'s "Phase 21 — Your workflows + kebab" suite (5 tests; that feature moved to `SavedWorkflowsPage` in an earlier phase) is a duplicate of ISS-045, resolved separately by a Phase-36-tagged cleanup commit (`d0054959`). `ReviewGatesSection.test.tsx` (3 tests; stale `PIPELINE_CATEGORIES` counts after `prototype-analyze` became a 3rd gate agent) was reconciled incidentally by the same post-close polish-sweep commit that fixed MD-1/MD-2 (`4d683ffa`: prototype=4/all=54 → 5/55 + the uncheck-all-gates test), bringing that suite to 19/19.

## Phase 39 — Run Screen Mock Fidelity [B5]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/39-run-screen-mock-fidelity-b5/` (milestone **v2.0**, branch `feat/ui-2`).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-12) — `39-VERIFICATION.md` verdict **PASS-WITH-CONCERNS**, 8/8 must-haves verified; the single concern **W-1** (an ND-register numbering collision) is **RESOLVED 2026-07-12** by renumbering the two closeout divergences to **ND-U** (failed-run four-tab) / **ND-V** (self-chromed) and adding both to the canonical gallery caption — a bookkeeping fix, not a defect.  ·  **Plans:** 7/7 (39-07 built the fidelity harness + shared MockApi stubs FIRST; 39-01 lane → 39-02 Steps → 39-03 Files → 39-04 Audit → 39-05 run header → 39-06 preview chrome, each with a per-surface human fidelity sign-off), plus a post-plan closeout that resolved two adjudicated failed-state rulings and the e2e debt.  ·  **Plan-id → `CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md` (milestone v2.0 POR):** B5.

### Key locked decisions / intended-divergence register

## Quick 260713-rcf — Configure single-screen REMOVED (prototype/ppt back to the wizard)

> **Commit `131c4e30` on `feat/ui-2` (revert(41), FE-only, one atomic commit).** Reverses Phase 41 waves 41-02 (`d7a947e1`) + 41-03 (`88f4db97`).

- **Why (settled, user-approved).** The Phase 41 single-screen `ConfigureScreen` launched a bare `prototype` with **no design-system/template context**, which the backend rejects (`missing_template_context`). Template/design-system selection is functionally required and is the **LaunchWizard's** job, so prototype/ppt route back to the wizard (`/workflow/create`). The Configure surface was then **orphaned** (reachable only from the Home prototype/ppt cards + a dead `/workflow/configure` route) → removed entirely (INV-3, no orphaned dual impl).
- **Deleted — do NOT resurrect (§37 built these; §41 rebuilt/wired them; now GONE):** `ConfigureScreen.tsx` + `ConfigureScreen.test.tsx`; the four `components/workflow/configure/` accordions (Templates / DesignSystem / ReviewGates / WorkflowSettings); the `/workflow/configure` route (`app/workflow/configure/page.tsx`); and `lib/draft.ts` + `lib/draft.test.ts` (the Configure-only `configure.draft` sessionStorage helper). The two now-empty dirs were removed.
- **Kept / untouched.** `LaunchWizard.tsx` (the retained launch surface) and its `lib/launchDraft.ts`. Backend `launch_context.py::resolve_launch_od_context` and its declared-signal seam are **unchanged** — this was FE-only.
- **Restored to pre-41-03 (`88f4db97^`):** `DashboardLayout.tsx` (dropped the `ConfigureScreen` import, `handleConfigureFeature`/`handleConfigureLaunch`, the `mainView==="configure"` render block, and the `"configure"` member of the `MainView` union), `HomeLaunchGrid.tsx` (dropped the `onConfigure` prop so prototype/ppt cards route to the wizard), `e2e/tests/ts-b.selection.spec.ts` (back to wizard-asserting).
- **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vitest run` 648 pass / 8 fail — all 8 fails PRE-EXISTING & out of scope (`PreviewPanel.switcher` ×3, `PreviewPanel.degraded` ×1, `FilesTab.runInput` ×2, `HomeLaunchGrid.inspect` ×2 — the last proven red against pristine HEAD too). Dead-ref grep over `frontend/src` + `frontend/e2e/tests` empty (only `e2e/fidelity/` gallery-doc prose still names ConfigureScreen). Record: `.planning/quick/260713-rcf-remove-configure-single-screen/260713-rcf-SUMMARY.md`.

## Phase 41 — Configure Unification + Composer Rebuild [B7]

**Folder:** `.planning/phases/41-configure-composer-rebuild-b7/` (milestone **v2.0**, branch `feat/ui-2`).  ·  **Status:** Complete (2026-07-14) — `41-VERIFICATION.md` verdict **PASS**, 8/8 must-haves; whole phase **additive-FE-only** (backend untouched).  ·  **Plans:** 7 (41-01 harness → 41-02/03 Configure build+wire **[BUILT then REVERTED]** → 41-04 Composer Simple → {41-05 Canvas ‖ 41-07 Library drawer} → 41-06 Run wiring), each Composer wave with a per-surface HUMAN fidelity sign-off.  ·  **Plan-id → `CHAT-AND-UI-CONVERGENCE-PLAN.md` (v2.0 POR):** B7.

**One-line outcome:** The Configure single-screen was BUILT then REVERTED (see the **quick-260713-rcf entry above** — prototype/ppt stay on the intact `LaunchWizard` because a bare `prototype` launch fails the backend `missing_template_context` guard; INV-3 honored via DELETION). The delivered phase is the full-page **Composer** — a `mainView="composer"` custom-workflow surface (entry: Home "Compose a custom workflow" + edit-from-My-Workflows) with a Simple ⇄ Canvas toggle bound to the shared AgentsPopup data model: the **Simple view** mock-fidelity to `Hexaware Composer.dc.html`, the **Canvas view** a hand-rolled SVG node-graph matching the user-approved `composer-canvas-proposal.html` (ND-AJ, no graph library) — plus the additive **Run-once** wiring through the existing `onStartPipeline('custom')` seam and the Library agent-detail **right drawer** (closes the Phase-40 ND-Z deferral). ADDITIVE throughout: the AgentsPopup modal + LaunchWizard + IdeaInputPage are RETAINED + REUSED (NOT replaced — the user-approved framing corrected the plan's "delete the modal"); INV-3 kept via a single shared inspector + extracted shared lever logic, no forks.

### Where the code lives (as-built)

- **Full-page Composer (41-04/05):** `frontend/src/components/workflow/composer/` — `ComposerPage.tsx` (shell + Simple⇄Canvas toggle + Run wiring), `IdentityCard.tsx`, `AgentRow.tsx`, `SummaryRail.tsx` (Simple); `CanvasView.tsx`, `CanvasNode.tsx`, `CanvasConfigRail.tsx` (Canvas). Reached via `mainView="composer"` in `DashboardLayout.tsx`; entry from `HomeLaunchGrid` (the "custom" card) + `SavedWorkflowsPage.onLaunchSaved`.
- **Shared lever logic (extracted, 41-05):** `frontend/src/components/workflow/AgentsPopup.tsx` — `applyLeverPatch` (pure reducer: patch + clear-unset + EMP-04 validator→gate coupling) + `useAgentCapabilities` (fetch + `user_allowed` filter). Both `AdvancedExpander` AND the Canvas config rail consume them — single source of lever logic (INV-3), behavior-preserving (34/34 preservation tests).
- **Run wiring (41-06):** `ComposerPage.onRun` → `DashboardLayout` → the EXISTING `onStartPipeline('custom', …)` seam → `dashboard/page.tsx` `startPipeline` → execution view. Functional test `frontend/e2e/tests/composer-run.spec.ts` (CR-01 Simple / CR-02 Canvas assert the `run_pipeline(custom, agent_ids)` frame). Save-to-catalogue reuses `createUserWorkflow`.
- **Library drawer (41-07):** `AgentCapabilitiesModal` (in `AgentsPopup.tsx`) restructured with an `asDrawer` variant (right slide-in; Overview/Skills/Hooks/Config); opened from `LibraryPage.tsx`. Read-only (surface-only) — no editable Save-agent footer. The other 3 modal callers keep the default centered modal (variant default = modal).
- **Fidelity oracle (41-01):** `frontend/e2e/fidelity/assemble-phase41-gallery.mjs` (the canonical ND register + gallery), `composer-canvas-proposal.html` (the vendored Canvas design reference), the `--surface`-tagged capture drivers.
- **Configure removal (reverted 41-02/03):** see the **quick-260713-rcf entry above** — `ConfigureScreen` + accordions + `/workflow/configure` + `lib/draft.ts` DELETED; `LaunchWizard`/`IdeaInputPage` retained.
